# RFC-317 证据台账（131 条）

> 生成自 2026-08-23 的 18-agent 并行审计（8 路公共内核族审计 × 逐族对抗复核 + 1 路守卫机制元审计 + 1 路完整性批判）。
> 每条 family finding 都经过一轮独立的对抗复核（默认立场「先证伪」）：`REFUTED` 的已在此表之外被剔除，
> `ADJUSTED` 表示锚点或严重度被复核者修正后保留，`CONFIRMED` 表示原样成立。
> 守卫元审计与完整性批判两路未过第二道复核，标为 `UNVERIFIED`——引用它们前须自行开源码复核。
> **锚点一律纯文本 `path:line`，不得改写为外链**（CI 的 markdown link check 会逐条请求外链，见 CLAUDE.md）。

| 严重度 | 条数 | 处置 |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| P1 | 6 | 本 RFC 修复 + 各配红→绿回归 |
| P2 | 46 | 本 RFC 修复 + 各配红→绿回归 |
| P3 | 79 | 本 RFC **不修**；登记进精确债务账本，由棘轮锁死不再增长 |


## EK — 执行内核公共层（10 条：P1 0 / P2 3 / P3 7）

### EK-01 · P2 · duplicate-implementation · ADJUSTED

**Two RFC-310 runners re-implement the kill chain with no process group and no tree kill, and one reads stdout unbounded before checking its cap**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/infrastructure/verificationRunner.ts:137-166` · `packages/backend/src/modules/integration/infrastructure/developmentAdapterRunner.ts:355-397` · `packages/backend/src/util/platformExec.ts:130-132` · `packages/backend/src/services/execution/managedProcess.ts:284-296` · `packages/backend/src/services/execution/managedProcess.ts:225-233` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet.test.ts:95-110`
- **证据**：verificationRunner.ts:137-149 spawns build/test programs WITHOUT `detached: true`, then at :151-165 hand-rolls `proc.kill('SIGTERM')` → 2s → `proc.kill('SIGKILL')` — direct child only; killProcessTree is never imported. `await proc.exited` (:166) has no reap deadline. developmentAdapterRunner.ts:355-370 likewise omits `detached`, and its timeout (:374-377) sends a bare `proc.kill('SIGKILL')` with no TERM grace and no tree kill. Compare managedProcess.ts:295 (`detached: true`, "Without it a fork()ed grandchild survives every kill we send") and :225-232 (killTree → killProcessTree). Separately, developmentAdapterRunner.ts:379-383 does `new Response(proc.stdout).text()` — a fully unbounded read — and only then checks `if (stdout.length > STDOUT_LIMIT)` at :393 (STDOUT_LIMIT = 256*1024, :41); the cap is enforced after the bytes are already in the daemon's heap.
- **架构影响**：Both spawn programs whose whole purpose is to fork children (npm/bun/cargo build+test; external adapter executables), which is exactly the grandchild-leak case managedProcess exists for. verificationRunner then rmSyncs the temp output dir (:188) and its caller discards the workspace while a surviving grandchild may still be writing into it. The 256KB "limit" provides no memory protection at all — a chatty adapter OOMs the daemon before the check runs.
- **现有守卫**：none for the defect — packages/backend/tests/rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet.test.ts:43-118 registers both files in the ALLOWLIST with a prose `why`, so the SITE is tracked, but the allowlist asserts only a hit count. No test asserts that a spawn site sets `detached`, calls killProcessTree, or bounds its stdout read. packages/backend/tests/rfc098-process-governance.test.ts exercises the adversarial grandchild fixture only against runNode/reapOrphanRuns.
- **拟建守卫**：Upgrade the ratchet's allowlist value from {count, why} to {count, why, governance: 'managed' | 'tree-killed' | 'exempt-<reason>'} and add an AST assertion: any file whose entry is not exempt-* must, in the same function that calls Bun.spawn, (a) pass `detached: true` and (b) reference killProcessTree. Assert the violation list is exactly []. Add a second assertion that no `new Response(proc.stdout).text()` appears in a file that also declares a byte-cap constant.
- **证伪方式**：Add a third module doing `Bun.spawn({cmd, stdout:'pipe'})` + `setTimeout(()=>proc.kill('SIGKILL'))`, add its one-line allowlist entry with any `why` string, and run `bun run gate:local`. Green — the ratchet accepts any prose justification for an ungoverned kill chain.

### EK-02 · P2 · escape-hatch · ADJUSTED

**spawnVersionProbe's governance is opt-in via timeoutMs, and two production call sites opt out**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/util/process.ts:376-423` · `packages/backend/src/util/process.ts:289-291` · `packages/backend/src/cli/start.ts:400-402` · `packages/backend/src/cli/doctor.ts:41` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc284-spawn-version-probe.test.ts:24-28`
- **证据**：`const detached = opts.timeoutMs !== undefined` (process.ts:380) gates the process group; the kill timer is `detached ? setTimeout(… killProcessTree …) : undefined` (:383-396); the finally reap is `if (detached) await reapDetachedGroup(...)` (:421). With no timeoutMs the probe has no group, no tree kill, no timeout, and both `await outPromise` (:408, an unbounded `new Response(stdout).text()`) and `await proc.exited` (:409) are unbounded. The module header (:293-295) states this plainly: "detached 仅在给了 timeoutMs 时开（无-timeout 保持历史 flat spawn byte-for-byte）". Both drivers forward the option conditionally (`...(opts.timeoutMs !== undefined ? { timeoutMs: opts.timeoutMs } : {})` — opencode/util.ts:71, claudeCode/probe.ts:59), and two production callers pass no opts at all: cli/start.ts:401 `await ccDriver.probe(ccDriver.defaultBinary(config)[0]!)` on the daemon boot path, and cli/doctor.ts:41 `await ocDriver.probe(...)`.
- **架构影响**：A wedged runtime binary (--version on a wrapper script that reads stdin, a stalled network mount) hangs daemon startup forever with an unkillable, un-grouped child — the exact failure class the probe kernel was consolidated to eliminate. The legacy-preservation flag turned the shared kernel's invariant into a caller opt-in, which is precisely the pattern that lets one call site bypass the kernel.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/rfc284-spawn-version-probe.test.ts exercises only the timeoutMs path (every case at :17, :31, :39, :52, :62, :70, :83 passes timeoutMs); the no-timeout mode is never asserted and no test pins that production callers supply one.
- **拟建守卫**：Make timeoutMs required on VersionProbeOpts with a named default constant, deleting the flat-spawn mode entirely (delete > deprecate, per CLAUDE.md). If a genuine unbounded caller must exist, keep the mode but add a source ratchet asserting every `.probe(` / `spawnVersionProbe(` call site in src/** passes a timeoutMs, with an exact-equality allowlist of exempt sites each carrying a `why`.
- **证伪方式**：Point config.claudeCodePath at a script that blocks on stdin, set defaultRuntime:'claude-code', and start the daemon: boot hangs at cli/start.ts:401. No test covers this; `bun run gate:local` is green today with both no-timeout call sites in place.

### EK-03 · P2 · missing-guard · ADJUSTED

**RFC-294's architecture ratchets scan only src/modules — the entire execution kernel is out of range**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:18` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:120-131` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:1250-1277` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:26-38` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet.test.ts:120-136` · `eslint.config.js:29-45`
- **证据**：`const MODULES_ROOT = resolve(REPO_ROOT, 'packages','backend','src','modules')` (:18) and the collector's single entry point `visit(MODULES_ROOT)` (:129). Every ratchet in the file (crossContextViolations, publicSurfaceViolations, capabilityForgeViolations, godSurfaceViolations — :1250-1277) runs over productionModuleUnits() only. Everything in this audit's scope — services/execution/*, services/schedulerAssembly.ts, services/nodeRunMint.ts, services/nodeRollback.ts, services/scheduler.ts (10,640 lines), util/process.ts, util/platformExec.ts — lives under src/services and src/util and is therefore never scanned for cross-context edges, public-surface exactness, forgeable capabilities, or god-ports.
- **架构影响**：The target-architecture contracts RFC-294 defines are enforced only on the pilot modules (two debt edges, one non-exact entrypoint), while the code that actually runs the execution chain — the part RFC-294 names TaskEngine → WrapperRuntime → NodeExecutor → ExecutionKernel — is entirely unpoliced. Findings EK-01, EK-02 and EK-07 are direct consequences: they are exactly the violation classes the scanner detects, in files it does not read.
- **现有守卫**：none — this IS the guard, and its root is src/modules. Nothing else fills the gap: eslint.config.js has no intra-backend boundary rules, and .dependency-cruiser.cjs:62-148 carries only four coarse layer rules plus no-circular.
- **拟建守卫**：Point the same scanner at a second root with a per-directory ratchet: treat each top-level services/<family>/ directory (execution, workgroup, clarify, codeHost, codeIntel, …) as a pseudo-context and snapshot its current cross-family internal-import edges as an exact-equality list that may only shrink — the same discipline as the existing crossContextViolations snapshot at :1253-1262. That makes today's debt visible and freezes it without requiring the W4 file moves first.
- **证伪方式**：Add a brand-new cross-domain value import from services/execution/managedProcess.ts to services/workgroup/state.ts and run `bun run gate:local`. Green — no scanner reads that file.

### EK-04 · P3 · business-special-case · ADJUSTED

**The "runtime-neutral" observation kernels take per-runtime-named source payloads, and the module header claims the opposite**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/execution/inventoryObservation.ts:3-6` · `packages/backend/src/services/execution/inventoryObservation.ts:36-46` · `packages/backend/src/services/execution/inventoryObservation.ts:75-82` · `packages/backend/src/services/execution/inventoryObservation.ts:85-113` · `packages/backend/src/services/execution/startupVerification.ts:143-181` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc282-c0c2-runtime-fence.test.ts:69-89`
- **证据**：inventoryObservation.ts:4-6 states the module "完全不知道 claude 有个 init 事件、opencode 有个 dump 文件——若哪天有人在这里加了一个 if (runtime === …)，那就是抽象又漏回调用方了". Thirty lines below, the exported signature is `buildRuntimeInventoryObservation(input: { …, claudeInit: ClaudeInitLike | null, snapshot: SnapshotLike | null, … })` (:36-47), `interface ClaudeInitLike { tools?; agents?; skills?; mcpServers? }` is claude's init-event schema declared verbatim in the shared module (:75-82), and `observedFacesOf` (:85-113) hard-codes the translation order: opencode snapshot first (`inventoryFacesFromSnapshot`), then claude init field-by-field. startupVerification.ts:143-158 repeats the shape: the capability switch is exhaustive, but its `sources` bag is `{ claudeInit: ClaudeInitObservation | null; loadSnapshot: () => InventorySnapshot | … }` and each `case` body picks the source belonging to that runtime (:178-185).
- **架构影响**：Adding a third runtime driver cannot be done by "register a driver" as RFC-282/RFC-143 promise: it requires editing two shared execution-layer files to add a third named source and a third translation body. The per-runtime knowledge that the eslint fence (RFC-282 §4.2) keeps out of callers has instead been hoisted INTO the commons, where the fence does not point. The stale header also actively misleads the next editor into believing the abstraction is source-agnostic.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:249-250 is the whole-tree runtime-discrimination ratchet, but its regex is `/\b(?:runtime|protocol|kind|defaultRuntime)\s*[!=]==\s*['"](?:opencode|claude-code)['"]|\bisClaude\b/`. A `geminiInit:` parameter, a `ClaudeInitLike` interface, or an `if (input.snapshot !== null)` source branch matches none of those alternations.
- **拟建守卫**：AST rule over packages/backend/src/services/execution/** and packages/backend/src/services/*.ts (excluding services/runtime/**): fail if any exported declaration's parameter names, property names, type names or type aliases contain a case-insensitive registered runtime token (derive the token list from RUNTIME_KINDS at test time so it grows with the registry; normalize 'claude-code'→'claude'). Assert the offender list is exactly []. Pair with an inverted mutation self-check (a fixture string containing `geminiInit` must be flagged) so the rule cannot be silently weakened. Correct code shape: one `observationSource` discriminated union owned by RuntimeDriverCapabilities, each driver supplying its own normalizer.
- **证伪方式**：Add `readonly geminiInit: { tools?: string[] } | null` to the buildRuntimeInventoryObservation input plus a branch for it in observedFacesOf, then run `bun run test:backend` and `bun run lint`. Both stay green, proving nothing guards the shape.

### EK-05 · P3 · escape-hatch · ADJUSTED

**MCP stdio probe spawns arbitrary local commands through the SDK, invisible to the whole-repo spawn ratchet**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/mcpProbe.ts:461` · `packages/backend/src/services/mcpProbe.ts:516-527` · `packages/backend/src/services/mcpProbe.ts:147-148` · `packages/backend/src/services/mcpProbe.ts:619-632` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet.test.ts:127-135` · `docs/audit-backlog.md:672-679`
- **证据**：`new stdioMod.StdioClientTransport({ command: cmd, args, cwd, env, stderr: 'pipe' })` (mcpProbe.ts:516) starts a user-configured local command via @modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/stdio.js (dynamically imported at :461). Termination is only `transport.close()` (:627-628, :640-641) plus a handshake-timeout race (:495-508) — no `detached`, no killProcessTree, no TERM→KILL escalation, no reap deadline. The RFC-284 ratchet's six patterns (rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet.test.ts:26-42) are Bun.spawn, spawnSync, bare `spawn(`, the child_process module string, Bun.$, and the exec/fork family. I ran that exact pattern set over src/services/mcpProbe.ts: the ONLY hit is line 300, which sits inside a doc comment and is therefore skipped by countSpawnHits (:122-125). The file registers zero hits and is absent from the ALLOWLIST — the ratchet is green while the file spawns.
- **架构影响**：The repo's headline claim (design/system-commons-unification-audit-2026-08-12.md:16 — "spawn 层：是") rests on a lexical ratchet that a library-mediated spawn walks straight past. This path is user-triggerable from the MCP settings page and repeatable; an MCP server that forks a grandchild leaks it on every probe timeout with no daemon-side backstop. docs/audit-backlog.md:672-676 registers the reliability half, but nothing prevents a SECOND SDK-mediated spawn from appearing tomorrow with the same invisibility.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet.test.ts:138-146 checks lexical spawn tokens only; no test asserts which modules may import a process-spawning transport.
- **拟建守卫**：Extend the ratchet from lexical tokens to a dependency assertion: fail if any src/** file statically or dynamically imports a module known to spawn (@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/stdio.js, node:child_process, execa-likes) outside the same explicit allowlist, and require each such entry to declare `governedBy: 'runManagedProcess' | 'documented-exemption'`. Put the import-specifier list next to SPAWN_PATTERNS so both grow together, with the same mutation self-check the file already uses.
- **证伪方式**：Add a second `new stdioMod.StdioClientTransport({...})` in another service file (or import @modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/stdio.js there and spawn). `bun run test:backend` stays green and the ratchet's "every spawn site is allowlisted" test still passes.

### EK-06 · P3 · business-special-case · ADJUSTED

**managedProcess's drain-timeout semantics default to one caller family's legacy behaviour**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/execution/managedProcess.ts:82-89` · `packages/backend/src/services/execution/managedProcess.ts:513-523` · `packages/backend/src/services/execution/agentProcess.ts:166-170` · `packages/backend/src/services/scriptRun.ts:481-495` · `packages/backend/tests/scheduler-audit-s15-sigterm-no-escalation-pid-unused.test.ts:108`
- **证据**：`keepExitedOnDrainTimeout?: boolean` (managedProcess.ts:89) with the doc "The historical relabel remains the default for existing consumers (script nodes): their contract treats an undrainable pipe as an unsafe child" (:84-88). The branch is managedProcess.ts:517-523: `if (req.keepExitedOnDrainTimeout === true) drainTimedOut = true; else outcome = 'child-unkillable'`. agentProcess.ts:169 hard-codes `keepExitedOnDrainTimeout: true` for the whole agent domain; scriptRun.ts:481-495 passes it nowhere, so the script family gets the legacy relabel. The kernel does not branch on caller identity — but its DEFAULT is one family's semantics.
- **架构影响**：A future consumer of runManagedProcess silently inherits script-node semantics: a child that exited 0 but left a grandchild holding the pipe is reported 'child-unkillable', which callers map to a hard failure. The flag is a per-caller legacy preservation threaded into the shared kernel, with the wrong polarity — the safe generic default ("exit code is trustworthy, report evidence loss separately") is the one you must ask for.
- **现有守卫**：none — grep over packages/backend/tests/** for keepExitedOnDrainTimeout returns zero hits; the only related assertion is packages/backend/tests/rfc280-startup-verification.test.ts:342, a source-text lock on a downstream `drainTimedOut === true` read.
- **拟建守卫**：Make the field required and non-optional (`drainTimeoutPolicy: 'keep-exit-code' | 'treat-as-unsafe-child'`) so every caller states its contract at the type level and a new caller cannot inherit a default. Back it with one behavioural test per policy using the stubborn-grandchild fixture already at packages/backend/tests/fixtures/stubborn-opencode.ts.
- **证伪方式**：Add a new runManagedProcess caller without the flag and assert its outcome for a child that exits 0 while a grandchild holds the pipe: it comes back 'child-unkillable'. No test turns red when that happens.

### EK-07 · P3 · boundary-blur · ADJUSTED

**The unified outcome projection reaches directly into the workgroup domain's table and service**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/execution/outcome.ts:25-30` · `packages/backend/src/services/execution/outcome.ts:240-263` · `packages/backend/src/services/execution/outcome.ts:313-330` · `packages/backend/src/services/execution/outcome.ts:266-270`
- **证据**：outcome.ts:25 imports `workgroupMessages` from @/db/schema; :30 imports `loadWorkgroupTaskState` from @/services/workgroup/state; :28-29 import AGENT_HOST_AGENT_NODE_ID from @/services/agentLaunch and CODE_ROUND_NODE_ID from @/services/codeRoundContract. `getExecutionOutcome` then runs a workgroup-specific query inline (:313-330: loadWorkgroupTaskState → select workgroupMessages.bodyMd). The workgroup arm of the pure projection (:240-263) is an ad-hoc nested if-chain on mode / wg.dwPhase / wg.resultMessageBody with a hard-coded output port literal 'result' at :253 and :257. NOTE the top-level kind dispatch is NOT the problem — `const unhandled: never = kind` at :266-270 makes a fifth ExecutionKind a build error, the approved registry-shaped pattern, added precisely because the workgroup arm used to be a bare `else` (:229-238 records that incident).
- **架构影响**：RFC-294 requires cross-context traffic to go through exact public/{commands,queries,…} contracts. Here the shared executor projection owns three other contexts' node-id constants and one context's SQL. Any workgroup schema change (rename result_message_id, split workgroup_messages) breaks the execution commons, and a new execution kind's result anchor will land as a fifth arm of the same if-chain rather than as a per-context projector.
- **现有守卫**：none — .dependency-cruiser.cjs declares only no-services-to-routes (:62-70), no-util-to-upper (:100-116), no-auth-to-services (:118-126) and no-circular (:144-148); there is no rule constraining services/execution/**. eslint.config.js:111-119 restricts only cross-package imports and the runtime-driver fence.
- **拟建守卫**：Add a dependency-cruiser rule no-execution-commons-to-domain: from ^packages/backend/src/services/execution/ to ^packages/backend/src/services/(workgroup|agentLaunch|codeRound|clarify|review) with dependencyTypesNot:['type-only'] → error, plus a snapshot of the current violating edges that may only shrink. Correct code shape: an ExecutionOutcomeProjector port per execution kind, resolved from a Record<ExecutionKind, Projector> registry assembled at bootstrap.
- **证伪方式**：Add `import { someOtherDomainTable } from '@/db/schema'` plus a fifth reach-in query to getExecutionOutcome. `bun run lint`, `bun run test`, and the dependency-cruiser gate all stay green.

### EK-08 · P3 · business-special-case · ADJUSTED

**Corrupt node_runs.runtime silently reads as opencode in the inventory read path, the exact behaviour RFC-282 决策 13 banned**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/execution/inventoryRead.ts:155-157` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/index.ts:24-39` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/index.ts:41-46` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/index.ts:59-61`
- **证据**：`// NULL runtime = 早于 RFC-111 的行，那时只有 opencode` then `const kind: RuntimeKind = isKnownRuntimeKind(run.runtime) ? run.runtime : 'opencode'` (inventoryRead.ts:155-156). isKnownRuntimeKind returns false for NULL *and* for any non-null value not in the DRIVERS registry, so a corrupt or future-downgraded protocol string takes the same branch as legacy-NULL and is projected through the opencode driver's capabilities. The comment justifies only the NULL case. Contrast getRuntimeDriver (runtime/index.ts:128-136): "an unknown kind throws loudly instead of silently running as opencode (a corrupt or future runtime value used to be executed by the wrong driver with zero signal)" — and tryGetRuntimeDriver (:141-143) exists precisely so read paths can degrade one dirty row instead of defaulting it.
- **架构影响**：A dirty row's inventory is rendered against the wrong driver's capability table (caps.startupObservation decides which observation source is consulted at :169-200), producing a confident-looking but wrong answer instead of a visible degradation — the same misattribution class RFC-304 paid for in outcome.ts:229-238.
- **现有守卫**：none — the rfc143 whole-tree ratchet (packages/backend/tests/rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:249-250) only matches comparison forms (`X === 'opencode'`); a ternary default `: 'opencode'` matches no alternation.
- **拟建守卫**：Add a third alternation to that ratchet's regex covering default/coalesce forms — `/(?:\?\?|:)\s*['"](?:opencode|claude-code)['"]/` — and extend the existing self-check block (:275-292) with `const k = x ?? 'opencode'` and `cond ? y : 'opencode'` as strings that MUST match, so the regex cannot be weakened later. Fix the code by splitting the NULL case (legacy → opencode, as documented) from the unknown-value case (→ tryGetRuntimeDriver null-degradation + a warn, matching the resolveFrozenRuntime precedent at nodeRunMint.ts:534-539).
- **证伪方式**：Run `UPDATE node_runs SET runtime='gemini' WHERE id=…` and call getRuntimeInventory: it returns an opencode-shaped projection with no warning. Add a second `?? 'opencode'` default anywhere in src/ — `bun run test:backend` stays green.

### EK-09 · P3 · stale-assertion · ADJUSTED

**The 2026-08-12 ledger's "runtime 分支清零" verdict is an artifact of the ratchet's regex shape, and its weak-spawn family list has drifted in both directions**

- **锚点**：`docs/audit-backlog.md:680-682` · `docs/audit-backlog.md:684-687` · `docs/audit-backlog.md:688-689` · `packages/backend/src/services/pluginInstaller.ts:775-799` · `packages/backend/src/services/scriptRun.ts:275-279` · `packages/backend/src/util/process.ts:376`
- **证据**：Ledger line 16 concludes "判定层/状态机层/spawn 层：是——RFC-280「唯一 spawn 点」、RFC-282「ACL 单源 / buildSpawn 单面 / runtime 分支清零」经独立复核全部兑现". Verified against source: runtime-specific code is NOT zero in the shared layer — see EK-01 (claudeInit/ClaudeInitLike at execution/inventoryObservation.ts:42,75) and EK-02 (renderOpencodeMcpEntry/renderClaudeMcpServerEntry at execution/agentInjection.ts:124,144); the "清零" verdict holds only for the comparison forms the rfc143 regex can see. Separately, docs/audit-backlog.md:665-694 (the RFC-280-out-of-range weak-spawn family) is stale in both directions: it lists pluginInstaller.ts:791-797 as hand-rolled (it now goes through runManagedProcess, pluginInstaller.ts:780) and "三份几乎逐字相同的 --version 探针骨架" (merged into spawnVersionProbe, util/process.ts:375), while it does NOT list verificationRunner.ts:137 or developmentAdapterRunner.ts:355, both created after the audit and both weaker than the entries that ARE listed (EK-04).
- **架构影响**：The one sentence a future RFC author reads before deciding "the spawn/runtime layer is done, I can skip it" is wrong in a way that compounds: it licenses adding the next per-runtime special case to the commons, and the hand-maintained backlog family has no mechanism that notices new members. Under CLAUDE.md's own rule (「代码比账本新」), a ledger claim that outlived its evidence is itself the defect.
- **现有守卫**：none — no test reads either markdown file; the only mechanical check on docs is the CI markdown link check, which validates URLs, not claims.
- **拟建守卫**：Make the claim derive from a machine check instead of prose: have the ratchet tests emit their offender lists into a generated snapshot the ledger cites by path, and add a test asserting every file path named in docs/audit-backlog.md's weak-spawn family still exists AND still matches the SPAWN_PATTERNS set (a converted entry turns red so it must be struck; a new allowlisted spawn site with no backlog entry turns red so it must be registered) — the same only-shrinks discipline packages/backend/tests/rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet.test.ts:148-151 already applies to its own allowlist.
- **证伪方式**：Grep docs/audit-backlog.md:684-686 for the three --version twins and open packages/backend/src/util/opencode.ts / util/opencode-models.ts: neither path exists any more (converged into util/process.ts:375). Then grep the same section for verificationRunner / developmentAdapterRunner: zero hits, though both are in the spawn ratchet's allowlist.

### EK-10 · P3 · boundary-blur · ADJUSTED

**RunTaskOptions is a 47-key cross-domain config bag threaded into the execution kernel by name**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/scheduler.ts:308-429` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc284-t20-child-inheritance.test.ts:36-78` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc284-t20-child-inheritance.test.ts:84-86`
- **证据**：`export interface RunTaskOptions` spans scheduler.ts:308-736 and carries 47 fields mixing every domain that touches execution: scriptInterpreters / scriptDepsInstallTimeoutMs (script nodes, :347-352), maxConcurrentCodeHostCalls / codeHostRequestTimeoutMs / codeHostResponseMaxBytes / codeHostConnections / codeHostFetch (code-host), commitPushModel / commitPushRuntime / commitPushMaxRepairRetries / commitPushDiffMaxBytes / commitPushLang / commitPushExcludePatterns (commit-push), mergeAgentModel / mergeAgentRuntime (merge agent), fanoutMaxShardTotal, ensureWorkspaceProfiles, subagentLiveCapture. The child-inheritance guard's disposition table (rfc284-t20-child-inheritance.test.ts:37-78) enumerates all of them `as const satisfies Record<keyof RunTaskOptions, Disposition>`.
- **架构影响**：Under RFC-294 each of these belongs to its bounded context and should reach the kernel through that context's port, not as a named key on the engine's options. Today adding a domain knob means widening the shared execution surface, and the kernel's signature is a running index of every feature that ever needed a setting — the classic god-port RFC-294's godSurfaceViolations ratchet exists to prevent, in a file that ratchet never scans (EK-08).
- **现有守卫**：packages/backend/tests/rfc284-t20-child-inheritance.test.ts:78 — `as const satisfies Record<keyof RunTaskOptions, Disposition>` is a real compile-time exhaustiveness guard and it WILL turn red on a new key until a disposition is declared. But it guards "declared", not "belongs": adding `myNewDomainThing?: X` plus one 'inherit' row compiles and passes. Nothing caps the bag's size or challenges a key's placement.
- **拟建守卫**：Add a size ratchet to the same test — `expect(Object.keys(DISPOSITION).length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(47)` with a comment that the number may only shrink — so every new key forces an explicit decision to grow the shared surface rather than an unnoticed one-liner. Longer term, group the domain knobs into per-context option objects (script, codeHost, commitPush, mergeAgent) resolved from that context's public contract at bootstrap, which also shrinks the disposition table to one row per context.
- **证伪方式**：Add `myDomainThing?: string` to RunTaskOptions and `myDomainThing: 'inherit'` to the DISPOSITION table. `bun run gate:local` is green; the bag is now 48 keys and no assertion noticed.

## LC — 生命周期与状态机公共层（15 条：P1 0 / P2 6 / P3 9）

### LC-01 · P2 · forgeable-contract · ADJUSTED

**The task/node_run CAS writers never consult their own transition tables; ≥5 production sites perform transitions the shared table declares illegal**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:442-491` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:175-229` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:728-747` · `packages/shared/src/lifecycle.ts:312-318` · `packages/shared/src/lifecycle.ts:338-339` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycleRepair/options-T1.ts:114-118` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycleRepair/options-T2.ts:105-109` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycleRepair/options-T3.ts:51-56` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycleRepair/options-T3.ts:105-110` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycleRepair/options-CR1.ts:86-91` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycleRepair/options-S1.ts:243-247` · `packages/backend/src/services/task.ts:3957`
- **证据**：`setTaskStatus` reads the row, checks `isTerminalTaskStatus(from) && !allowTerminal`, then `if (!args.allowedFrom.includes(from)) throw` — it never imports or calls `nextTaskStatus`. Only the thin wrapper `transitionTaskStatusByEvent` (lifecycle.ts:728-747) derives allowedFrom from the table, and it has exactly ONE production caller outside lifecycle.ts (services/task.ts). Meanwhile the shared table's docstring asserts caller sets are "a NARROWER … subset; this oracle is the superset". They are not: `targetForTaskEvent` maps `to:'interrupted'` only to the `interrupt` event, whose allowed-from is `['pending','running']` (packages/shared/src/lifecycle.ts:338-339), yet options-T1 writes `to:'interrupted', allowedFrom:['awaiting_review']`, options-T2 `['awaiting_human']`, options-CR1 `['failed']`, options-S1 `['awaiting_review']`, options-T3 `['done']` (+ `to:'failed', allowedFrom:['done']`). `nextTaskStatus(from, {kind:'interrupt'})` throws IllegalTaskTransition for every one of those. Same structural hole at node level: `setNodeRunStatus` never calls `nextNodeRunStatus`, and it has 49 production call sites vs 25 for the event path; the events `mark-done` / `mark-failed` / `mark-canceled` / `iterate-review` / `reject-review` / `mark-exhausted` have ZERO production constructors.
- **架构影响**：The declared state machine is decorative for the majority of writes. RFC-294 places the lifecycle writer in `task-execution/domain` with "transition + classified committed events" as its contract (design.md:3022); today the domain invariant is not owned by the domain — it is re-decided per call site, so a bounded-context extraction would be lifting a name, not a rule. It also means the machine cannot be used as an oracle for anything (reachability analysis, UI affordances, repair preflight) because production reaches states the machine says are unreachable.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/shared/tests/rfc108-task-transition-table.test.ts:38-90 and packages/backend/tests/lifecycle-transition-table.test.ts only prove the table is self-consistent with itself; nothing checks any call site against it.
- **拟建守卫**：AST rule (or a bun test that TS-parses backend/src): for every `setTaskStatus({...})` / `setNodeRunStatus({...})` / `setNodeRunStatusTx({...})` object literal with statically-known `to` + `allowedFrom` array literals, assert `allowedFrom ⊆ union over events e with targetFor*(e)===to of allowedFromFor*(e)`, allowing only entries listed in an explicit, per-site, justified deviation table that may only shrink. Invariant asserted: every persisted (from→to) pair is derivable from the shared transition table or is a registered, named exception.
- **证伪方式**：Run `bun -e "import {nextTaskStatus} from './packages/shared/src/lifecycle'; nextTaskStatus('awaiting_review',{kind:'interrupt'})"` — it throws IllegalTaskTransition. Then apply repair option `T1.demote-task` to an awaiting_review task: it commits awaiting_review→interrupted and every test stays green. If instead `setTaskStatus` internally validated via `nextTaskStatus`, that repair would 409.

### LC-02 · P2 · escape-hatch · ADJUSTED

**node_runs.status blind-write guard is an unbounded comment-marker escape hatch, unlike the tasks.status and merge_state hard allowlists**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/lifecycle-grep-guard.test.ts:86-91` · `packages/backend/tests/lifecycle-grep-guard.test.ts:115-117` · `packages/backend/tests/lifecycle-grep-guard.test.ts:142` · `packages/backend/tests/lifecycle-grep-guard.test.ts:154-182` · `packages/backend/src/services/terminalSweep.ts:94-99` · `packages/backend/src/services/terminalSweep.ts:112-117` · `packages/backend/src/services/terminalSweep.ts:120-125` · `packages/backend/src/services/clarify/seal.ts:344-352` · `packages/backend/src/services/workgroup/lifecycle.ts:348-359` · `packages/backend/tests/scheduler-audit-s14-tasks-status-blind-write-inventory.test.ts:38-40` · `packages/backend/tests/scheduler-audit-s14-tasks-status-blind-write-inventory.test.ts:197` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc144-merge-state-blind-write-inventory.test.ts:29-31`
- **证据**：The guard scans backend/src for `.update(nodeRuns)` + `.set({...status:...})`, then: `if (/rfc053-allow-direct-status-write/.test(preceding)) { lookahead = 0; continue }`. Any file, any status, any number of times — writing the marker comment on one of the 5 preceding lines makes the write invisible. Offenders are then filtered only by `!m.file.endsWith('services/lifecycle.ts')`, so there is no per-file allowlist and no count. Five production blind writes already sit outside the kernel behind this marker (terminalSweep ×3 → 'canceled', clarify/seal → 'done', workgroup/lifecycle → 'canceled'). Contrast the two sibling ratchets, which are hard allowlists with exact counts: `STATUS_WRITE_ALLOWLIST = { 'services/lifecycle.ts': 1 }` (scheduler-audit-s14…test.ts:38-40, with `expect(counts.status['services/lifecycle.ts']).toBe(1)` to prevent a hollow green) and `MERGE_STATE_WRITE_ALLOWLIST = { 'services/lifecycle.ts': 2 }` (rfc144-merge-state-blind-write-inventory.test.ts:29-31).
- **架构影响**：The node_run machine — the one the scheduler drives — has the weakest of the three enforcement layers. Every marker-escaped write also bypasses `assertNodeRunSourceTerminationAdmission` (the RFC-303 MR/PR fence) and the terminal-overwrite gate. Today's five writes happen to target unfenced statuses ('canceled'/'done'), so nothing is broken — but the guard would not notice a sixth write to 'running' or 'awaiting_review', which is exactly the class RFC-303 exists to stop.
- **现有守卫**：none for adding a NEW instance — packages/backend/tests/lifecycle-grep-guard.test.ts:88 is the escape hatch itself. packages/backend/tests/rfc303-node-revival-fence.test.ts:49-80 locks the fence on the three kernel APIs only, not on marker-escaped writers.
- **拟建守卫**：Rewrite lifecycle-grep-guard.test.ts as an s14-style per-file exact-count allowlist: `{ 'services/lifecycle.ts': 3, 'services/terminalSweep.ts': 3, 'services/clarify/seal.ts': 1, 'services/workgroup/lifecycle.ts': 1 }`, counts may only ratchet DOWN, any new file or increment is red. Keep the marker as documentation, drop it as authorization. Additionally assert every non-kernel entry's UPDATE carries a `WHERE status = <non-terminal literal>` predicate and a target outside SOURCE_TERMINATION_BLOCKED_NODE_STATUSES.
- **证伪方式**：Add `// rfc053-allow-direct-status-write` above a new `db.update(nodeRuns).set({ status: 'running' })` in any backend/src file and run `bun run test:backend` — green. Do the same for `db.update(tasks).set({ status: 'running' })` — scheduler-audit-s14 turns red immediately.

### LC-03 · P2 · escape-hatch · ADJUSTED

**`allowTerminal` is documented as fixup-scripts-only / 5 named holders; 21 production sites pass it**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:181-182` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:432-441` · `packages/backend/src/services/review.ts:2765-2782` · `packages/backend/src/services/review.ts:3043-3050` · `packages/backend/src/services/scheduler.ts:1316-1329` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycleRepair/options-T3.ts:51-56` · `packages/backend/src/services/task.ts:4659` · `packages/backend/src/services/task.ts:5415`
- **证据**：lifecycle.ts:181 on `setNodeRunStatus.allowTerminal`: "Default false. Set true ONLY for fixup scripts — never in normal flows." lifecycle.ts:434-437 on `setTaskStatus`: "terminal sources are refused unless the caller holds the `allowTerminal` escape hatch (holders: resumeTask, retryNode, repair CR-1, repair T3, and RFC-109 syncTaskWorkflow)". `rg -n "allowTerminal: true" packages/backend/src` returns 21 non-comment production sites across scheduler.ts (7), lifecycleRepair/options-* (9), task.ts (3), review.ts (2) — including normal user flows (review supersede at review.ts:2770 rewrites a `done` node_run to `canceled`; review sibling cascade at review.ts:3048 rewrites `done` → `pending`). The shared table is categorical here: `nextNodeRunStatus` throws for every terminal `cur` (packages/shared/src/lifecycle.ts:88-90) and `nextTaskStatus`'s only terminal-source events are `retry`/`sync-workflow` → `pending`.
- **架构影响**：Terminal-means-terminal is the load-bearing invariant of both machines (it is what makes `isTerminalNodeRunStatus` usable as a settled-gate anywhere downstream). A boolean parameter that any caller may set turns it into a suggestion, and the doc that names five holders no longer describes reality, so nobody reviewing a 22nd site has a baseline to push back from.
- **现有守卫**：none — no test counts, allowlists, or otherwise constrains `allowTerminal: true` call sites.
- **拟建守卫**：Grep ratchet in the s14 style: per-file exact counts of `allowTerminal: true`, ratchet-down-only, each entry annotated with which terminal→X rewrite it performs. Stronger form: replace the boolean with a non-forgeable capability token minted by a small allowlist module (`terminalRewriteGrant('review-supersede')`), so the set of legal terminal rewrites is enumerable and typed instead of a bare `true`.
- **证伪方式**：Add `allowTerminal: true` to any existing setNodeRunStatus call site and run the backend suite — green, and the write now overwrites `done`/`failed`/`canceled` rows the shared table calls unreachable.

### LC-04 · P2 · business-special-case · CONFIRMED

**The generic task-status writer knows about webhooks: it selects webhook/event-subscription identity columns and mints the `'webhook-terminal'` origin literal itself**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:392-406` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:459-471` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:550-570` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:591-593` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:601-607` · `packages/backend/src/services/webhook/terminalWorkspaceCleanup.ts:25-37` · `packages/backend/src/cli/start.ts:465-470` · `packages/backend/src/db/schema.ts:1113`
- **证据**：`TerminalWorkspacePrunePolicy` (lifecycle.ts:396-406) is typed to receive `webhookTriggerId`, `eventSubscriptionId`, `spaceKind`, and the three workspace-prune tombstone columns; setTaskStatus's own SELECT pulls those columns (lines 463-468) purely to feed it, and the policy is only invoked when `args.to === 'done' || args.to === 'canceled'` (line 550). The decision function is correctly injected (services/webhook/terminalWorkspaceCleanup.ts, wired at cli/start.ts:465-470) — but the *cause value* is not: the kernel hardcodes `workspacePruneCause: 'webhook-terminal' as const` (line 592) and re-checks the same three tombstone columns in the CAS WHERE (lines 601-607). The port returns a bare boolean, so a second origin cannot express its own cause without editing lifecycle.ts.
- **架构影响**：RFC-294 puts the lifecycle writer in `task-execution/domain`; a domain writer that names an integration (`webhook`) in its type signature and mints that integration's enum value cannot be extracted without carrying the integration with it. The port shape (boolean in, literal chosen by the kernel) is a half-inversion: the policy was moved out, the vocabulary was not. Note this is a genuine port, not an if/switch on origin — which is why the fix is small (widen the port's return to the cause) and worth doing before a second origin arrives.
- **现有守卫**：none — nothing prevents a second `? { workspacePruneCause: 'schedule-terminal' } :` branch being added inside setTaskStatus. `text('workspace_prune_cause', { enum: ['webhook-terminal'] })` (schema.ts:1113) is a drizzle type-level enum only; adding a literal is a one-line schema edit with no migration CHECK to fight.
- **拟建守卫**：Change `TerminalWorkspacePrunePolicy` to return `{ prune: false } | { prune: true; cause: WorkspacePruneCause }` and have the kernel write `cause` verbatim; then add a source-text assertion that `services/lifecycle.ts` contains no `workspacePruneCause: '` literal and no identifier matching `/webhook|eventSubscription|schedule|intent|mission/i`. Invariant: the lifecycle writer names zero integration origins.
- **证伪方式**：`rg -n "webhookTriggerId|eventSubscriptionId|webhook-terminal" packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts` → 5 hits in the generic writer. Add `workspacePruneCause: 'devbox-terminal'` behind a new `row.spaceKind === 'devbox'` branch at line 591 and run the suite — green.

### LC-05 · P2 · duplicate-implementation · CONFIRMED

**The "task is still preparing its repository" lifecycle predicate is hand-duplicated in three modules with three different definitions, two of which claim to be identical to the third**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/task.ts:3378-3390` · `packages/backend/src/services/autoResume.ts:136-138` · `packages/backend/src/services/stuckTaskDetector.ts:339-341` · `packages/backend/src/services/task.ts:6555-6561` · `packages/shared/src/lifecycle.ts:231`
- **证据**：Three predicates, all deciding "is this task in the RFC-287 G7 repo-prep phase": (1) `assertWorktreePresentForResume` — `task.worktreePath === '' && (task.workspaceState ?? 'available') === 'available' && hasPrepRow` where hasPrepRow is a `nodeId = REPO_PREP_NODE_ID` lookup; (2) autoResume — `t.worktreePath === '' && t.workspacePrunedAt === null`; (3) stuckTaskDetector — `c.worktreePath === '' && c.workspacePrunedAt === null`. Both (2) and (3) carry a comment asserting "判据与 assertWorktreePresentForResume / autoResume 同源" ("the criterion is the same source as…"). They are not: (1) additionally requires a `__repo_prep__` node_run row, and uses `workspaceState` which is `'pruning'` when `workspacePruningAt !== null` (task.ts:6555-6560), whereas (2)/(3) look only at `workspacePrunedAt`.
- **架构影响**：Three modules on the same lifecycle question disagree by construction. The concrete divergence: a task with `worktreePath=''` and NO `__repo_prep__` row (legacy pre-G7 rows, or a task whose prep row was never minted) is "not prep-incomplete" to task.ts — which then falls through to `existsSync('')` and returns 410 "reclaimed by worktree GC" — while autoResume routes the same row to `retryRepoPrep()` and the stuck detector silences its S4 alert for 45 minutes. The comments guarantee sync by convention, which is exactly the drift class the commons exist to remove.
- **现有守卫**：none — the predicate has no shared function, and `rg -ln "preparingRepo|DEFAULT_REPO_PREP" packages/backend/tests` returns nothing.
- **拟建守卫**：Extract one predicate — e.g. `taskWorkspacePhase(row): 'preparing' | 'available' | 'pruning' | 'pruned'` in shared or in the task-execution domain — and have all three import it; then add a source assertion that `worktreePath === ''` never appears adjacent to `workspacePrunedAt`/`workspaceState` outside that module. Invariant: exactly one definition of the repo-prep phase.
- **证伪方式**：Seed a task with `worktreePath=''`, `workspacePrunedAt=null`, and NO `__repo_prep__` node_run, then call `resumeTask` (410 task-worktree-missing) and `autoResumeInterruptedTasks` (routes to retryRepoPrep). Two modules, two verdicts, both green.

### LC-06 · P2 · duplicate-implementation · CONFIRMED

**The cancelable / non-terminal task-status set is re-enumerated as a literal array in at least six modules, one deliberately "kept local so restore doesn't couple to the lifecycle module", and two disagree about `interrupted`**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/task.ts:3588-3597` · `packages/backend/src/modules/task-execution/domain/sourceTermination.ts:61-66` · `packages/backend/src/modules/task-execution/application/applySourceTerminationEffect.ts:30-35` · `packages/backend/src/services/restore.ts:301-309` · `packages/backend/src/services/worktreeBackup.ts:29-35` · `packages/backend/src/services/worktreeBackup.ts:103` · `packages/backend/src/services/stuckTaskDetector.ts:125` · `packages/shared/src/lifecycle.ts:336-337` · `packages/shared/src/lifecycle.ts:203-208`
- **证据**：Six independent literal enumerations of statuses derivable from the shared machine: `CANCELABLE_TASK_STATUSES` (task.ts, 4 entries, comment says "aligned with the shared lifecycle table's `cancel` event" — but copied, not derived), `CANCELABLE_STATUSES` (sourceTermination.ts, ReadonlySet, same 4), `CANCELABLE` (applySourceTerminationEffect.ts, same 4), `NON_TERMINAL_TASK_STATUSES` (restore.ts, 5 entries incl. `interrupted`, with the explicit rationale "Kept local so restore doesn't couple to the lifecycle module"), `NON_TERMINAL_TASK_STATUSES` (worktreeBackup.ts, same 5 but typed `string[]`, forced in with `as never` at line 103), and an inline `inArray(tasks.status, ['pending','running','awaiting_review','awaiting_human'])` (stuckTaskDetector.ts:125). The two `NON_TERMINAL_*` copies count `interrupted` as non-terminal while `TERMINAL_TASK_STATUSES` (packages/shared/src/lifecycle.ts:203-208) counts it as terminal — same name, opposite meaning.
- **架构影响**：`allowedFromForTaskEvent({kind:'cancel'})` already computes the cancelable set from the table, and `TERMINAL_TASK_STATUSES` already exists in shared and is correctly imported by the frontend. Six hand-copies mean a 5th task status is a six-site manual sweep with no compiler help (the arrays are literal-typed and merely assignable to `TaskStatus[]`; nothing forces coverage). The colliding `NON_TERMINAL` semantics also make the codebase unreadable at a glance — "non-terminal" means two different things two files apart.
- **现有守卫**：none — no test or type forces any of these sets to agree with TASK_STATUS or with the transition table. `worktreeBackup.ts:103`'s `as never` cast additionally suppresses the only type feedback available.
- **拟建守卫**：Export `CANCELABLE_TASK_STATUSES = allowedFromForTaskEvent({kind:'cancel'})` and `RECOVERABLE_TASK_STATUSES` from packages/shared/src/lifecycle.ts, migrate all six sites, then add a grep ratchet asserting no backend/src file other than shared re-declares an array literal containing `'awaiting_review'` and `'awaiting_human'` and `'pending'`. Complement with an exhaustive test iterating `TASK_STATUS` and asserting each status is classified by exactly one of {terminal, recoverable, active} — so adding a status fails until classified.
- **证伪方式**：Add `'paused'` to `TASK_STATUS` in packages/shared/src/schemas/task.ts and run `bun run typecheck` + full suite: nothing red, yet cancelTask, restore suspension, worktree backup, source-termination and the stuck detector all silently ignore paused tasks.

### LC-07 · P3 · stale-assertion · ADJUSTED

**Kernel header cites an ESLint rule `no-direct-node-run-status-write` that does not exist anywhere in the repo**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:18-19` · `eslint.config.js:1-143` · `packages/backend/tests/lifecycle-grep-guard.test.ts:137-152`
- **证据**：lifecycle.ts:18-19 reads: "ESLint rule `no-direct-node-run-status-write` enforces that direct `db.update(nodeRuns).set({ status: ... })` only appears inside this file." `rg -n "no-direct-node-run-status-write" packages/ eslint.config.js` returns exactly one hit — that comment. eslint.config.js contains only js/tseslint recommended, react rules, and three `no-restricted-imports` blocks; there is no custom plugin, no rules directory, no local rule package.
- **架构影响**：A reader (or a future RFC author) auditing whether the node_run machine is sealed will read this line and conclude a lint-level, non-bypassable guard exists. The actual guard is LC-02's marker-opt-out grep test. This is precisely the "代码比账本新" failure mode inverted — the code comment is the stale ledger, and it is the first thing anyone touching the kernel reads.
- **现有守卫**：none — no test asserts the named rule is configured.
- **拟建守卫**：Either (a) delete the sentence and point at the real guard file, or (b) implement it: a flat-config `no-restricted-syntax` entry matching `CallExpression[callee.property.name='set']` inside a `.update(nodeRuns)` chain outside `services/lifecycle.ts`. If (b), add a mutation test (same shape as tests/rfc282-a1-eslint-boundary.test.ts) that proves the rule actually fires, so it cannot be silently dropped by a flat-config block override.
- **证伪方式**：`rg -n "no-direct-node-run-status-write" .` → one hit, the comment itself. `bun run lint` on a file containing a naked `db.update(nodeRuns).set({status:'running'})` → passes.

### LC-08 · P3 · business-special-case · ADJUSTED

**The generic node_run CAS carries code-host (MR/PR closed|merged) vocabulary in its admission gate and error messages**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:45-50` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:52-62` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:143` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:202` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:261` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:505-512` · `packages/backend/src/db/schema.ts:1050` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc303-node-revival-fence.test.ts:49-91`
- **证据**：`assertNodeRunSourceTerminationAdmission(taskId, fence: 'closed' | 'merged' | null, to)` lives at the top of the kernel and is called by all three node_run writers plus the task revival gate. It throws `ConflictError('task-source-terminal-closed' | 'task-source-terminal-merged', `task ${taskId} is fenced by an MR/PR ${fence} event; …`)`. `'closed' | 'merged'` is the code-host domain's vocabulary (schema.ts:1050 `enum: ['closed','merged']`), and the human-readable message hardcodes "MR/PR". The gate itself is generic ("is this task fenced"); only its value universe and wording are code-host-specific.
- **架构影响**：A second termination source (schedule stop, intent abandon, parent-cascade fence) either inherits an error message that lies to the user about MR/PR, or adds a third branch inside the kernel. The fence *concept* is a legitimate lifecycle primitive and belongs here; the *cause taxonomy* belongs to whoever raised it — cf. the neighbouring RFC-300 policy which correctly took the composition route.
- **现有守卫**：none for adding a third fence value — packages/backend/tests/rfc303-node-revival-fence.test.ts:49-80 pins that the three lifecycle APIs honour `sourceTerminationFence='closed'`, but nothing constrains the value set or asserts the kernel stays cause-agnostic. Marker-escaped writers (LC-02) bypass the gate entirely with no test noticing.
- **拟建守卫**：Replace the two-literal fence with `{ code: string; messageKey: string }` resolved by the raiser (modules/task-execution/domain/sourceTermination.ts already owns that mapping at lines 50-58), and add a source assertion that `services/lifecycle.ts` contains no `'MR/PR'`, no `'closed'`/`'merged'` literals. Pair with an exhaustive test that every fence cause the domain can mint produces a distinct error code through the kernel.
- **证伪方式**：`sed -n '45,62p' packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts` shows the literals in the generic helper. Add a third fence value `'stale'` to schema.ts:1050 and to the fence column: the kernel's ternary at line 59 silently classifies it as `'task-source-terminal-merged'`, and no test fails.

### LC-09 · P3 · missing-guard · ADJUSTED

**S4's 45-minute repo-prep exemption is a business special case in the stuck detector with zero test coverage; the S1/S2 workgroup exemption likewise**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/stuckTaskDetector.ts:75-76` · `packages/backend/src/services/stuckTaskDetector.ts:341-346` · `packages/backend/src/services/stuckTaskDetector.ts:358-371` · `packages/backend/src/services/stuckTaskDetector.ts:405-407` · `packages/backend/tests/stuck-task-detector.test.ts:162-180`
- **证据**：`checkOne`'s S4 branch is now a three-arm ternary of stacked business exemptions: `preparingRepo ? max(threshold, 45min) : c.parentTaskId === null ? threshold : max(threshold, 30min)`, each with its own conditional `detail` hint key (`repoPrepWaitHint`, `childBudgetWaitHint`). The child-task arm (audit decision D11) has a test at stuck-task-detector.test.ts:169. The repo-prep arm has none: `rg -ln "preparingRepo|repoPrepWaitHint|DEFAULT_REPO_PREP" packages/backend/tests packages/frontend/src` returns zero files — the symbols exist only in src. Separately, S1/S2 are skipped wholesale for workgroup tasks (`if (isWorkgroupTask(c) && (c.status === 'awaiting_review' || c.status === 'awaiting_human')) return out`) with no test either.
- **架构影响**：The stuck detector is the only thing that notices a wedged task. Each untested exemption is a permanently-silenced alert class, and the ad-hoc ternary shape invites a fourth arm ("digital-employee tasks queue longer", "intent tasks…") rather than a table. Contrast the sanctioned pattern one file over: REPAIR_OPTIONS is an exhaustive `satisfies Record<Rule, …>`; this is the same author's ad-hoc alternative for thresholds. Also violates CLAUDE.md §Test-with-every-change, which admits no "tests later" tier.
- **现有守卫**：none for the repo-prep exemption and none for the workgroup S1/S2 exemption. packages/backend/tests/stuck-task-detector.test.ts:169-180 covers only the D11 child-task arm.
- **拟建守卫**：Replace the ternary with an exhaustive exemption registry — `const S4_EXEMPTIONS: ReadonlyArray<{ id, applies(c): boolean, thresholdMs, hintKey }>` — and add a test that iterates the registry and asserts each entry has (a) a positive case that suppresses below its threshold, (b) a negative case that still fires above it, (c) a `detail` hint key present in the i18n bundle. Adding an entry without those three fails. Same registry treatment for the S1/S2 status-family exemptions.
- **证伪方式**：`rg -ln "preparingRepo" packages/backend/tests` → empty. Delete lines 341-346 of stuckTaskDetector.ts (dropping the exemption entirely) and run `bun run test:backend` — green.

### LC-10 · P3 · boundary-blur · ADJUSTED

**The generic repair engine hard-branches on `isTurnEngineWorkgroupTask`, and the flag that gates the branch is optional with only a source-heuristic guard**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/lifecycleRepair.ts:154-168` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycleRepair.ts:252-257` · `packages/shared/src/diagnose-repair.ts:50-59` · `packages/shared/src/dynamicWorkflow.ts:143-150` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc165-agent-launch.test.ts:671-696` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc165-agent-launch.test.ts:698`
- **证据**：Both entry points of the otherwise registry-driven engine open with `if (isTurnEngineWorkgroupTask(task) && def.revivesExecution === true)` — the list side synthesizes an unavailable option with a hardcoded i18n key `'diagnose.repair.common.workgroupUnsupported'`, the apply side throws `ValidationError('workgroup-repair-unsupported')`. `isTurnEngineWorkgroupTask` (packages/shared/src/dynamicWorkflow.ts:143) is a business-shape discriminator (has workgroupId AND config mode is not dynamic_workflow). The per-option half is registry-ish, but `revivesExecution?: boolean` is OPTIONAL in the shared type, so the classification is opt-in. Its only guard is a source-text heuristic: `revives = block.includes('resumeAfterApply') || block.includes('mintNodeRun') || id.includes('resurrect')`.
- **架构影响**：The engine is otherwise the repo's model registry (`REPAIR_OPTIONS satisfies Record<LifecycleAlertRule, [Def, ...Def[]]>` with a runtime id cross-check). This one branch is the ad-hoc counterpart: a second engine family needing a similar carve-out (dynamic_workflow already had to be excluded from it by comment; code-round / digital-employee are plausible next) adds a second `&&` clause rather than a row. And because `revivesExecution` is optional, an option that revives execution through any mechanism the heuristic doesn't name — calling `resumeKick`, `dispatchFrontier`, `startTask`, or a helper that wraps `mintNodeRun` — is unstamped, so the workgroup refusal silently fails open (the RFC-165 comment records that `T2.resurrect` was exactly that caught case).
- **现有守卫**：partial — packages/backend/tests/rfc165-agent-launch.test.ts:671-697 forces the stamp for options matching three source substrings. It catches nothing outside those three, and it cannot catch an option that revives via an indirection.
- **拟建守卫**：(a) Make `revivesExecution: boolean` REQUIRED on RepairOptionDef so every new option must state it at compile time. (b) Replace the `isTurnEngineWorkgroupTask` inline branch with a table on RepairContext — `repairApplicability(task): ReadonlySet<RepairCapability>` — so exclusions are declared per task shape in one place. (c) Keep the source heuristic as a belt-and-braces cross-check, extended to any call into services/task.ts's revive surface.
- **证伪方式**：Add a repair option whose apply calls `resumeKick(...)` directly (not `resumeAfterApply`, not `mintNodeRun`, id without 'resurrect') and omit `revivesExecution`. rfc165-agent-launch.test.ts:671 stays green, and applying it to a turn-engine workgroup host task walks straight past both refusals into generic revival.

### LC-11 · P3 · missing-guard · ADJUSTED

**Three single-slot global lifecycle registries silently overwrite on a second registration**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:417-430` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:683-687` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:651-673` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:306-312` · `packages/backend/src/cli/start.ts:465-474` · `packages/backend/src/cli/start.ts:975-977`
- **证据**：`terminalWorkspacePrunePolicy`, `terminalWorkspacePruneEffect` and `terminalTaskHook` are module-level `let … = null` with `register*(fn) { x = fn }` — plain assignment, no accumulation, no already-registered assertion, no name. Each is wired exactly once from cli/start.ts, but tests set and reset them freely (packages/backend/tests/review-cancel-concurrency.test.ts:185, fusion-engine.test.ts:1025, rfc202-lifecycle-exits.test.ts:175/209). The single `terminalTaskHook` slot is occupied by `sealOpenHumanGatesForTask`, i.e. by clarify/review-domain logic; the kernel comment at lines 651-652 already notes the asymmetry ("the single-slot hook above deliberately stays done|canceled-only") which is why RFC-243 had to bypass it and import `notifyTaskTerminal` / `notifyChildBudgetTaskStatus` directly (lines 308-311, 655-673).
- **架构影响**：The registration seam exists to break a lifecycle→clarify import cycle, i.e. it is the kernel's designated extension port — but it holds one subscriber. The next domain wanting a terminal hook either wins the slot (silently disabling the clarify sweep, with zero test coverage of the loss) or does what RFC-243 did and hard-imports into the kernel, which re-couples the thing the port was built to decouple. RFC-294's target has this writer emitting classified committed events (design.md:3022) — a multicast list is the shape that gets there.
- **现有守卫**：none — no assertion that a slot is registered at most once, and no test that both a terminal sweep AND any second consumer run for the same transition.
- **拟建守卫**：Convert all three to named multicast subscriptions (`addTerminalTaskListener(name, fn)` returning an unsubscribe, each invocation individually try/caught as today) and add a boot assertion + test that registering the same name twice throws while distinct names both fire. Then migrate `notifyTaskTerminal` / `notifyChildBudgetTaskStatus` off direct imports onto the same list, so the kernel's post-commit fan-out has exactly one mechanism.
- **证伪方式**：Call `registerTerminalTaskHook(() => {})` a second time anywhere after cli/start.ts:974 and run the suite: green, and every task reaching done/canceled now leaves its clarify rounds and review parks open forever.

### LC-12 · P3 · stale-assertion · ADJUSTED

**Three blind-write sites justify themselves with "async lifecycle helpers cannot join a sync transaction" — the sync transactional kernel entry point has existed since 2026-07-19**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/terminalSweep.ts:92-99` · `packages/backend/src/services/workgroup/lifecycle.ts:348-359` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:231-289` · `packages/backend/src/services/task.ts:5192` · `packages/backend/src/services/workgroup/taskActions.ts:387`
- **证据**：workgroup/lifecycle.ts:348-350 reads "(guarded awaiting_human-only UPDATE inside dbTxSync; async lifecycle helpers cannot join a sync transaction)"; terminalSweep.ts:92-93 the same ("async lifecycle helpers cannot join a sync transaction"). `setNodeRunStatusTx` — "Synchronous transaction companion for business operations that must move a node_run together with other durable rows" — is at lifecycle.ts:237-289 and is already used by services/task.ts:5192 and services/workgroup/taskActions.ts:387. `git log -S setNodeRunStatusTx -- packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts` dates it to cafc80ee4 (2026-07-19); terminalSweep landed in 30b296055 (2026-07-16). The justification was true when written and false three days later.
- **架构影响**：Small in isolation, load-bearing in aggregate: these three comments are the stated reason the node_run kernel has five bypasses, and they are the first thing a reviewer reads when asked "can this go through the helper?". Two of the three (per-row, id-scoped UPDATEs at workgroup/lifecycle.ts:351-357 and clarify/seal.ts:349-351) are a direct one-line swap to `setNodeRunStatusTx`; only terminalSweep's two set-based sweeps (WHERE taskId AND status) genuinely have no per-row-helper equivalent today.
- **现有守卫**：none — no test asserts these sites cannot use the sync helper, and none would notice if they were migrated or if a fourth site copied the stale justification.
- **拟建守卫**：Migrate the two per-row sites to `setNodeRunStatusTx` and delete the stale clause; for terminalSweep, either add a set-based kernel entry (`setNodeRunStatusesTx({tx, taskId, from, to, extra})` carrying the same fence + terminal checks) or record the real reason (set-based sweep). Then the LC-02 allowlist shrinks to `{ 'services/lifecycle.ts': 3, 'services/terminalSweep.ts': 3 }` and the ratchet becomes meaningful.
- **证伪方式**：`rg -n "setNodeRunStatusTx" packages/backend/src` shows the sync helper plus two existing consumers; `git log --diff-filter=A --format=%ad -- packages/backend/src/services/terminalSweep.ts` (2026-07-16) vs `git log -S setNodeRunStatusTx --format=%ad -- packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts` (2026-07-19).

### LC-13 · P3 · stale-assertion · CONFIRMED

**The 2026-08-12 commons audit records the lifecycle family as fully converged ("三条状态机全收敛 lifecycle.ts CAS+转移表 + 源文本棘轮"); two of the three are not**

- **锚点**：`design/system-commons-unification-audit-2026-08-12.md:30` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:828` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:876-879` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:442-491` · `packages/backend/tests/lifecycle-grep-guard.test.ts:86-91` · `packages/backend/tests/scheduler-audit-s14-tasks-status-blind-write-inventory.test.ts:38-40`
- **证据**：The ledger row for 任务生命周期 rates the family 高 and states: "三条状态机全收敛 lifecycle.ts CAS+转移表 + 源文本棘轮（s14 钉恰好 1 处直写）" — i.e. all three machines converged onto CAS + transition table + source ratchet, with s14 pinning exactly one blind write. Verified against source: only merge_state has all three layers actually wired (its writer computes `to` from the table and its ratchet is a hard allowlist). tasks.status has the ratchet but its writer never consults the table (LC-01). node_runs.status has neither the table consulted (LC-01) nor a real ratchet (LC-02) — s14 governs `tasks` only, and the node_run guard is a marker opt-out with 5 live bypasses. The same ledger row does list "若干注释与现实相反" as a residual, so the drift is half-acknowledged; the headline 全收敛 claim is what a future RFC would read and trust.
- **架构影响**：CLAUDE.md designates the repo as the single source of truth for cross-RFC knowledge, and the RFC-294 workflow expects new RFCs to read the prior audit before designing. A "fully converged" rating on the family that owns task and node_run state means the next lifecycle-touching RFC will assume the machines are sealed and will feel free to add a call site with a hand-written allowedFrom — which is exactly how the five illegal-per-table transitions in LC-01 accumulated.
- **现有守卫**：none — audit documents are prose with no executable assertion. Nothing re-derives the ledger's claims from source.
- **拟建守卫**：Correct the ledger row to distinguish the three machines by enforcement layer actually present, and add a machine-checkable coverage test (`lifecycle-enforcement-matrix.test.ts`) asserting, per machine: (a) the CAS writer references its transition table, (b) a hard per-file write allowlist exists, (c) the allowlist is non-empty and occupied. That test's output becomes the ledger row, so the doc can no longer drift from source.
- **证伪方式**：`rg -n "nextTaskStatus|nextNodeRunStatus" packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts` — neither appears inside setTaskStatus or setNodeRunStatus; only `transitionTaskStatusByEvent` (1 external caller) and `transitionNodeRunStatus` use them. `rg -n "rfc053-allow-direct-status-write" packages/backend/src` → 5 hits outside services/lifecycle.ts.

### LC-14 · P3 · duplicate-implementation · CONFIRMED

**Frontend hand-rolls the terminal task set omitting `interrupted`, while importing the shared predicate two files away**

- **锚点**：`packages/frontend/src/routes/tasks.detail.tsx:320-331` · `packages/frontend/src/routes/tasks.detail.tsx:327` · `packages/frontend/src/lib/task-detail-tabs.ts:7` · `packages/frontend/src/lib/task-detail-tabs.ts:350-360` · `packages/frontend/src/components/home/RecentlyDoneList.tsx:12` · `packages/frontend/src/components/home/RecentlyDoneList.tsx:19-20` · `packages/shared/src/lifecycle.ts:203-208`
- **证据**：tasks.detail.tsx:327 computes `const terminal = status === 'done' || status === 'failed' || status === 'canceled'` to decide whether to stop the 15s workgroup-room poll. `TERMINAL_TASK_STATUSES` is `['done','failed','canceled','interrupted']`, and `isTerminalTaskStatus` is already imported inside the same package at lib/task-detail-tabs.ts:7 and components/home/RecentlyDoneList.tsx:12 (the latter with an explicit "flag-audit W0: single source" comment). So a workgroup task that ends `interrupted` never satisfies the stop condition and polls `/api/workgroup-tasks/{id}/room` every 15 seconds for as long as the tab is open.
- **架构影响**：Minor runtime cost, but it is a live counter-example to the flag-audit W0 claim that the frontend consumes the terminal set from a single source — the one place where getting it wrong has a behavioural consequence is the place that hand-rolled it.
- **现有守卫**：none — no lint or test forbids a literal terminal-status disjunction in packages/frontend/src.
- **拟建守卫**：Replace with `isTerminalTaskStatus(status)` and add a grep ratchet over packages/frontend/src forbidding a boolean expression that compares a status to two or more of the four terminal literals (allowlist the shared import). Same rule is worth extending to packages/backend/src.
- **证伪方式**：Open a workgroup task that ended `interrupted` (e.g. after a daemon restart) on /tasks/{id}: the room query keeps refetching at 15s indefinitely. Change line 327 to `isTerminalTaskStatus(status)` and it stops.

### LC-15 · P3 · boundary-blur · ADJUSTED

**Two further CAS+transition-table kernels live outside the commons in incompatible shapes — and the satellite one actually enforces its table, which the shared one does not**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/workgroup/lifecycle.ts:51-76` · `packages/backend/src/services/workgroup/lifecycle.ts:88-102` · `packages/backend/src/services/workgroup/lifecycle.ts:142` · `packages/backend/src/services/workgroup/lifecycle.ts:182` · `packages/backend/src/services/workgroup/lifecycle.ts:386-400` · `packages/backend/src/services/fusion.ts:1292-1321` · `packages/backend/src/services/fusion.ts:891-900` · `packages/backend/src/services/fusion.ts:1284-1287` · `packages/backend/src/services/fusion.ts:1729-1731`
- **证据**：`WORKGROUP_ASSIGNMENT_TRANSITIONS: Record<WorkgroupAssignmentStatus, readonly WorkgroupAssignmentStatus[]>` plus `assertAssignmentTransition`, `casAssignmentStatus` and `casAssignmentStatusTx` reproduce the entire kernel triple for `workgroup_assignments` — but as a status→status adjacency map, not an event ADT, and in backend/services rather than shared. Crucially, BOTH cas functions call `assertAssignmentTransition(from, to)` before writing (lines 142 and 181), so the satellite machine enforces its table while the shared task/node_run machines do not (LC-01). Separately, `casFusionStatus` (fusion.ts:1301-1322) is a third hand-rolled CAS for the `fusions` table with no transition table at all — legality is a caller-passed `fromStatuses` array, and its UPDATE has no `WHERE status = from` predicate (it relies on dbTxSync serialization instead of a CAS predicate).
- **架构影响**：Four status lifecycles, three implementation shapes, one shared. There is no generic `casStatus(table, column, table-of-legal-edges)` primitive, so each new stateful entity re-invents the triple and picks its own strength: event-ADT-but-unenforced (shared), adjacency-and-enforced (workgroup), no-table-and-no-CAS-predicate (fusion). RFC-294 §note at design.md:1624-1626 explicitly keeps workgroup assignments out of the NodeRun state machine, which is a correct domain decision — but it argues for extracting the *mechanism*, not for three mechanisms. Only lifecycle.ts's writes are protected by a source ratchet; the other two tables have none.
- **现有守卫**：none — no ratchet on `.update(workgroupAssignments).set({status})` or `.update(fusions).set({status})` outside their owning modules, and no test asserts fusion transitions against any table (there is none to assert against).
- **拟建守卫**：Extract a generic `guardedStatusCas({ table, idColumn, statusColumn, legal(from,to), from, to, extra })` (sync + async variants) into the platform layer, migrate all three families onto it, and generate the per-table blind-write ratchet from the registration list so a fourth stateful table gets its guard for free instead of by remembering. Minimum viable step: give `fusions` a declared transition table and a `WHERE status = from` CAS predicate.
- **证伪方式**：`sed -n '51,102p' packages/backend/src/services/workgroup/lifecycle.ts` (adjacency table + assert, enforced at both cas entry points) vs `sed -n '442,491p' packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts` (no table reference) vs `sed -n '1301,1322p' packages/backend/src/services/fusion.ts` (no table, no CAS predicate). Add a blind `db.update(fusions).set({status:'done'})` anywhere in backend/src — no test turns red.

## ACL — 资源 ACL / catalog / ref 公共层（14 条：P1 2 / P2 2 / P3 10）

### ACL-01 · P1 · boundary-blur · ADJUSTED

**Five of the twelve ACL resource types have NO owner write gate — view rights are write rights**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:239-255` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:343` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:350` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:357` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:392` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:399` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:406` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:459` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:467` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:475` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:556` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:691` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:704` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:713` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:775` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:783` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:791` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourceAcl.ts:470-482` · `packages/shared/src/schemas/permission.ts:955-998` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/application/commands/actionTemplateCommands.ts:61-71` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/infrastructure/sqliteConfigResourceStore.ts:106-128`
- **证据**：The generic write gate is `requireResourceOwner` (resourceAcl.ts:470-482 = requireResourceView → isResourceOwner → 403). routes/developmentConfig.ts defines its OWN gate instead: `async function requireVisible(...)` at :245-255 which is only `canViewResource(...) || throw NotFoundError`. Every mutation handler for action_template / verification_profile / digital_employee / automation_policy / development_adapter calls requireVisible and nothing else — e.g. `revise: async (actor, id, body) => { await requireVisible(deps, actor, 'action_template', templateStore.getById(id)); reviseActionTemplateDraft(...) }` (:342-347), same shape at :349/:356, :391/:398/:405, :458/:466/:474, :690/:703/:712, :774/:782/:790. The application layer does not compensate: reviseActionTemplateDraft / publishActionTemplate / archiveActionTemplate (modules/development-automation/application/commands/actionTemplateCommands.ts:61-70, 77-125, 128-133) never look at ownerUserId. Meanwhile `action-templates:update`, `verification-profiles:update`, `digital-employees:update`, `automation-policies:update`, `adapter-definitions:update` and their `:archive` twins are all in USER_RESOURCE_WRITES → USER_BASELINE (permission.ts:1053-1069), i.e. held by every ordinary account, under a comment that asserts 'the per-row check is the resource ACL, as with every other type here' — which is exactly what is missing.
- **架构影响**：Breaks the six-resource (now twelve-resource) ACL symmetry at the write half. A `resource_grants` row on these five types silently means 'may edit, publish and archive', not 'may view and use' as it does for the other seven; and any row whose owner flips visibility to 'public' via PUT /acl becomes editable by every logged-in user. Also inverts the RFC-294 direction: instead of the module consuming the shared authority port, the route layer re-derived a weaker gate locally.
- **现有守卫**：none
- **拟建守卫**：AST rule over packages/backend/src/routes/**: for every route whose RouteMeta.permissions contains a `:update` / `:delete` / `:archive` point belonging to an AclResourceType, the handler's transitive call set must contain `requireResourceOwner` (or an explicitly ledgered exemption). Pair it with a behavioural row in the rfc099 matrix asserting 'granted non-owner PUT/publish/archive → 403' for all twelve types.
- **证伪方式**：Seed an action_template owned by alice with visibility 'private', grant bob via PUT /api/code/action-templates/:id/acl, then as bob call PUT /api/code/action-templates/:id with a new draft. If it returns 200 the finding holds; a 403 falsifies it. Same test with visibility 'public' and carol (no grant) is the wider case.

### ACL-02 · P1 · boundary-blur · CONFIRMED

**employee_definitions carries owner/visibility/aclRevision columns but is not an ACL type — the columns are inert and every user reads every employee**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/db/schema.ts:4960-4988` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/application/authoringService.ts:1072-1073` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/infrastructure/sqliteAuthoringStore.ts:502-523` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/infrastructure/sqliteAuthoringStore.ts:530-544` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/composition.ts:396-420` · `packages/backend/src/routes/digitalEmployees.ts:570-580` · `packages/backend/src/routes/digitalEmployees.ts:612-622` · `packages/backend/src/routes/digitalEmployees.ts:624-641` · `packages/backend/src/routes/digitalEmployees.ts:530-548` · `packages/shared/src/schemas/permission.ts:97-100` · `packages/shared/src/schemas/resourceAcl.ts:19-43` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourceAcl.ts:127-142`
- **证据**：employee_definitions (schema.ts:4960-4987) declares ownerUserId, `visibility` NOT NULL DEFAULT 'private', aclRevision NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 and an owner+name unique index — the full RFC-099 column set — but 'employee_definition' is absent from ACL_RESOURCE_TYPES and ACL_TABLES, so no ACL kernel function can ever be applied to it. The store reads both columns into the record (sqliteAuthoringStore.ts:147-157) and then `listEmployeeDefinitions` (:507-524) selects on `archivedAt` only, with no owner/visibility predicate; `getEmployeeDefinition` (:502-505) is a bare id lookup. GET /api/digital-employees (routes/digitalEmployees.ts:568-579) and GET /api/digital-employees/:id (:613-621) are gated only by `digital-employees:read`, a USER_BASELINE point (permission.ts:100). employeeView (composition.ts:410-419) returns `configuration`, the full published `definition` and `workScope` for every row. Note this is a DIFFERENT table from `digital_employees` (schema.ts:4749), which IS an ACL type — the name collision makes the gap easy to miss.
- **架构影响**：A private-by-default row whose privacy is never enforced: the schema records an intent the code does not implement, and every reader of the schema (including future auditors) will assume the ACL applies. `acl_revision` is dead — nothing bumps it, so a future PUT /acl on this type would start from a fabricated fence value.
- **现有守卫**：none
- **拟建守卫**：Schema-reflection guard (same shape as tests/rfc284-resource-policy-schema-guard.test.ts): enumerate every drizzle table that declares BOTH `owner_user_id` and `visibility`, and assert the set equals the set of ACL_TABLES values. A table gains ACL columns ⇒ it must be an AclResourceType, or be listed in an explicit shrinking exemption ledger.
- **证伪方式**：As user alice POST a digital employee, then as user bob (no grant, no admin) GET /api/digital-employees. If bob's response omits alice's row the finding is wrong. `listEmployeeDefinitions` has no actor parameter at all, so it cannot.

### ACL-03 · P2 · missing-guard · ADJUSTED

**The ACL-endpoint enrolment guard is a string-literal regex, so five ACL types are structurally invisible to it**
