- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc099-acl-endpoints-matrix.test.ts:296-308` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc099-acl-endpoints-matrix.test.ts:143-287` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:231-236` · `packages/backend/tests/api-contract-coverage.test.ts:82-95` · `packages/backend/tests/api-contract-coverage.test.ts:206-233` · `packages/backend/tests/contracts/registry.ts:877-888`
- **证据**：The guard scans routes/*.ts with `/mountAclEndpoints\s*\(\s*app\s*,\s*deps\s*,\s*\{[\s\S]{0,400}?type:\s*['\"]([^'\"]+)['\"]/g` and asserts `mounted.sort()` equals the CASES table. routes/developmentConfig.ts:231-236 mounts through a VARIABLE — `mountAclEndpoints(app, deps, { type: cfg.aclType, base: cfg.base, param: 'id', load: ... })` — so the regex never matches it. I re-ran the exact regex over src/routes: it yields exactly 7 hits (agent, capability_template, mcp, plugin, skill, workflow, workgroup) and CASES has exactly those 7 rows, so the assertion is green while action_template / verification_profile / digital_employee / automation_policy / development_adapter have zero cross-user ACL-endpoint coverage. The file's own header (:22-26) states its purpose as 'so adding mountAclEndpoints somewhere new fails here until it is enrolled' — the guard believes it is doing a job it structurally cannot do for factory-mounted types.
- **架构影响**：The one mechanism that was supposed to make ACL symmetry non-optional silently stopped covering 42% of the ACL types the moment someone factored the mount into a helper. Every finding in this family that a matrix row would have caught (ACL-1 included) sails through.
- **现有守卫**：none — the guard IS the thing that is broken
- **拟建守卫**：Replace the source-regex with a runtime enumeration: have mountAclEndpoints append cfg.type to an exported module-level registry, boot the real app in the test, and assert `registeredAclMounts` deep-equals `ACL_RESOURCE_TYPES` AND equals `CASES.map(c=>c.type)`. That makes both 'a type with no mount' and 'a mount with no matrix row' compile-or-test failures regardless of how the mount is spelled.
- **证伪方式**：Run the regex in the test body against packages/backend/src/routes and print the matches; if it returns 12 entries including action_template the finding is wrong. It returns 7.

### ACL-04 · P2 · business-special-case · CONFIRMED

**The generic ACL route mounter hard-codes two resource identities despite already owning a generic post-commit hook**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/routes/resourceAcl.ts:189-202` · `packages/backend/src/routes/resourceAcl.ts:56-68` · `packages/backend/src/routes/resourceAcl.ts:27-32` · `packages/backend/src/routes/workflows.ts:383-388` · `packages/backend/src/routes/workgroups.ts:320-325`
- **证据**：mountAclEndpoints is the one templated PUT /{res}/:id/acl handler, yet it ends with `if (cfg.type === 'workflow') { workflowsBroadcaster.broadcast(WORKFLOWS_CHANNEL, {type:'workflow.acl.updated', workflowId: row.id}) }` and `if (cfg.type === 'workgroup') { workgroupsBroadcaster.broadcast(...) }` — two literal business identities branched on inside the shared mounter, forcing it to import from '@/ws/broadcaster'. The config interface it was handed already declares `afterUpdate?: (resourceId: string) => void | Promise<void>` (:56-57) and `afterWriteInTx?` (:58-68) for exactly this, and the two call sites (workflows.ts:383-388, workgroups.ts:320-325) pass neither — they let the kernel know who they are instead of telling it what to do.
- **架构影响**：Ad-hoc if/switch buried in a generic function — the anti-pattern the repo's registry rule exists to prevent. It is also the template: a third type needing WS invalidation will copy the branch rather than use the hook, and each branch drags a new ws import into a routing kernel that should depend on nothing per-domain. Under RFC-294 this is a cross-context coupling inside what should be a pure transport template.
- **现有守卫**：none — no test forbids new `cfg.type ===` branches, and rfc099-acl-endpoints-matrix.test.ts asserts only HTTP status/payload, not which broadcaster fired
- **拟建守卫**：AST rule: inside packages/backend/src/routes/resourceAcl.ts, forbid any BinaryExpression comparing `cfg.type` (or any AclResourceType-typed identifier) to a string literal. Move both branches into the two call sites' `afterUpdate`. Ratchet the current two out in the same change so the rule starts at zero.
- **证伪方式**：Delete both if-blocks and pass equivalent `afterUpdate` callbacks from workflows.ts / workgroups.ts. If nothing else in the mounter needs cfg.type's identity (it does not — `resource` is already derived through the exhaustive ACL_PERMISSION_PREFIX map at :88-117), the branches were pure special-casing.

### ACL-05 · P3 · stale-assertion · ADJUSTED

**'Only agents and workflows have a builtin column' is stale in five places — capability_templates has it too**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/db/schema.ts:89` · `packages/backend/src/db/schema.ts:483` · `packages/backend/src/db/schema.ts:4621` · `packages/backend/src/services/systemResources.ts:24-28` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/closure.ts:55-62` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/commit.ts:621-625` · `packages/backend/src/services/bundle/refs.ts:75-81` · `packages/shared/src/ref/ast.ts:88` · `packages/shared/src/ref/ast.ts:132-138` · `packages/shared/src/ref/codecs.ts:157-158` · `packages/backend/src/services/capabilityTemplates.ts:423-427`
- **证据**：db/schema.ts has exactly three `builtin: integer('builtin', {mode:'boolean'})` columns: line 89 (agents), 483 (workflows) and 4621 (capabilityTemplates, inside the block whose owner/visibility/aclRevision are at :4616-4620 and whose unique index is `capability_templates_owner_name_unique` at :4626). Five independent hand-copies still say two: systemResources.ts:24-27 ('Only agents + workflows carry the column today'); closure.ts:55-62 ('只有 agents / workflows 两张表有 builtin 列'); commit.ts:625-631 `if (type !== 'agent' && type !== 'workflow') return null` — a runtime early-return that makes a built-in capability template unresolvable by name on import; ast.ts:88 `{k:'builtin'; type:'agent'|'workflow'}` and its zod twin at :136 — so the builtin AST variant cannot express one; codecs.ts:158 `BUNDLE_BUILTIN_RE = /^builtin:(agent|workflow)\/(\S{1,256})$/` — so the wire form cannot encode one.
- **架构影响**：One business fact ('which tables carry framework-built-in identity') is duplicated into a runtime guard, a type union, a zod enum and a regex, with the schema as an unlinked sixth copy. The ast.ts:83-86 comment itself narrates the last time these copies diverged ('正式 codec 拒绝它、payload schema 接受它' → a package whose own parser could not read it). It has diverged again, silently, in the fail-closed direction.
- **现有守卫**：none — tests/rfc271-builtin-resolve.test.ts:302 only asserts the string 'BUNDLE_BUILTIN_RE' exists in codecs.ts; nothing compares any of the five copies to the schema
- **拟建守卫**：Export one `BUILTIN_CAPABLE_TYPES` derived by drizzle reflection (`Object.entries(ACL_TABLES).filter(([,t]) => 'builtin' in getTableColumns(t))`), assert in a schema-reflection test that it equals the literal used by ast.ts / codecs.ts / commit.ts, and build BUNDLE_BUILTIN_RE from it instead of hand-writing the alternation.
- **证伪方式**：grep -n "builtin: integer" packages/backend/src/db/schema.ts — three hits, the third at 4621 inside capabilityTemplates. If it were two the finding is wrong.

### ACL-06 · P3 · missing-guard · ADJUSTED

**OWNER_NAME_UNIQUE_TYPES is a silent-absence Set, and its only test duplicates it by hand at six of eleven entries**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/resourceAcl.ts:146-164` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourceAcl.ts:664-679` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourceAcl.ts:709-723` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc223-owner-transfer.test.ts:27-34` · `packages/backend/src/db/schema.ts:4701` · `packages/backend/src/db/schema.ts:4982-4985`
- **证据**：OWNER_NAME_UNIQUE_TYPES is `ReadonlySet<AclResourceType>` — membership is opt-in, so a new type that HAS an owner+name index but is not registered silently loses the pre-check at :664-679 and the typed-409 mapping at :709-723, degrading a designed 409 into a raw SQLite 500. The registry's own comment (:152-157) documents that this exact miss happened for capability_template. The only test is rfc223-owner-transfer.test.ts, whose `OWNER_SCOPED_TYPES` (:27-34) is a HAND-WRITTEN list of six — agent, skill, mcp, plugin, workgroup, capability_template — omitting the five RFC-310 types that are in the production Set and do carry the index (schema.ts:4701, 4727, 4753, 4779, 4810). employee_definitions carries the index too (schema.ts:4982) and is in neither list.
- **架构影响**：Two hand-maintained lists of the same fact plus a third (the drizzle indexes) with nothing linking any pair. The failure mode is asymmetric and invisible: 'in the Set but no index' is harmless, 'has index but not in the Set' is a 500, and only the test author's memory distinguishes them.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/rfc223-owner-transfer.test.ts:27-34 tests six types from its own literal list; adding a twelfth ACL type with a unique index turns nothing red
- **拟建守卫**：Derive OWNER_NAME_UNIQUE_TYPES by reflecting drizzle's `getTableConfig(table).indexes` for a unique index over (owner_user_id, name), instead of hand-listing; keep the literal only as a schema-reflection assertion (`expect(derived).toEqual(literal)`). Then drive rfc223-owner-transfer.test.ts's matrix off the same constant rather than a local copy.
- **证伪方式**：Add a 13th ACL type whose table has an owner+name unique index, omit it from OWNER_NAME_UNIQUE_TYPES, and run the suite. If any test fails the finding is wrong.

### ACL-07 · P3 · business-special-case · ADJUSTED

**directRefsOf — the export-closure edge extractor — is an if-chain whose default is a silent empty edge set**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/closure.ts:199-259` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/closure.ts:235-240` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/closure.ts:373-375` · `packages/shared/src/schemas/resourceAcl.ts:56-69` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc271-export-gates.test.ts:119-152` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc271-roundtrip.test.ts:447-455`
- **证据**：`directRefsOf(type: BundleResourceType, row)` is four sequential `if (type === 'agent') {...return out}` / `'workflow'` / `'capability_template'` / `'workgroup'` blocks ending in a bare `return out` at :258 that returns the empty array for anything unmatched. The function's own comment at :236-241 names the hazard: 'forgetting that produced a package whose binding pointed at a template that was not in it — a defect the compiler could not see, because the extractor's default is an empty list rather than a missing case.' The consequence is documented in rfc271-roundtrip.test.ts:447-455: an incomplete closure makes serialize.ts fall back to `external:<source-ULID>`, producing a package that imports into the wrong resource or fails on the far instance — silently, at export time.
- **架构影响**：The single walker that decides what a config package contains cannot tell 'this type genuinely has no outgoing refs' (skill/mcp/plugin) from 'nobody wrote this type's case yet'. Every new packageable type starts life with a wrong-but-green closure.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/rfc271-export-gates.test.ts:119-151 asserts the three existing arms behave; nothing asserts the arm set is complete, and the roundtrip lock at rfc271-roundtrip.test.ts:447-500 only enumerates the six original tables' ids
- **拟建守卫**：Convert to `const REF_EXTRACTORS: Record<BundleResourceType, (row) => Ref[]>` with an explicit `() => []` entry for skill/mcp/plugin, so 'this type has no edges' is a written claim and a new BUNDLE_RESOURCE_TYPES entry is a compile error. Add a test asserting `Object.keys(REF_EXTRACTORS).sort()` equals `[...BUNDLE_RESOURCE_TYPES].sort()`.
- **证伪方式**：Add a type to BUNDLE_RESOURCE_TYPES without touching directRefsOf and run typecheck + the full suite. Green ⇒ finding holds.

### ACL-08 · P3 · forgeable-contract · ADJUSTED

**AclRow's optional visibility fails OPEN, and the whole config-package read path enters the ACL kernel through `as never`**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/resourceAcl.ts:70-84` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourceAcl.ts:288-294` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourceAcl.ts:317-330` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/closure.ts:285` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/closure.ts:351` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/export.ts:312` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/export.ts:398` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/preview.ts:415` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/commit.ts:645` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/commit.ts:674`
- **证据**：AclRow declares `visibility?: ResourceVisibility` and `ownerUserId?: string | null` as OPTIONAL (:77-84), and every predicate reads `(row.visibility ?? 'public')` (isVisibleRow :290, canViewResource :413, canViewResourceInTx :432, filterVisibleRows :399, isResourceOwner :460). So a row projection that simply forgets to select `visibility` is indistinguishable from a public row — the fence fails open. The one thing that would still catch it, the type checker, is switched off at all six entry points on the config-package read path: `isVisibleRow(actor, r as never, grants)` (closure.ts:285, :351; export.ts:312, :398; preview.ts:415) and `canViewResource(db, actor, expectType, row as never)` (commit.ts:645). Today those callers pass full `select()` rows so the values are present; nothing keeps that true.
- **架构影响**：The ACL oracle's only structural guarantee — 'you cannot ask me about a row without giving me its visibility' — is deliberately disabled across the entire package export/import surface, and the failure direction is disclosure. A future narrowing of any of those selects to a projection compiles clean and turns a private resource public inside exported packages.
- **现有守卫**：none — no lint rule, AST rule, or test forbids `as never` at an ACL predicate call site, and no test asserts the fail direction of an AclRow with visibility undefined
- **拟建守卫**：Make the two fields REQUIRED on a new `AclRowStrict` and have the predicates take that; provide one explicit `legacyAclRow({id, ownerUserId, visibility})` adapter for the genuinely-untyped Record rows so the widening happens once, named, and greppable. Plus an AST rule: no `as never` / `as unknown as` in an argument position of isVisibleRow / canViewResource / canViewResourceInTx / filterVisibleRows / requireResourceView / requireResourceOwner.
- **证伪方式**：Call `isVisibleRow(strangerActor, {id:'x', ownerUserId:'alice'}, new Set())` — no visibility field. It returns true. Then narrow closure.ts:285's select to omit visibility and typecheck: it passes because of the cast.

### ACL-09 · P3 · duplicate-implementation · ADJUSTED

**A second, semantically divergent visibility oracle lives (tested, exported, unused) inside the development-automation module**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/application/queries/configResourceQueries.ts:1-46` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourceAcl.ts:288-294` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc310-pr1b-action-template.test.ts:175-191`
- **证据**：`isRecordVisible(record, audience)` (:17-24) is a parallel implementation of isVisibleRow: `if (audience.bypassAcl) return true; if (record.visibility === 'public') return true; return record.ownerUserId !== null && record.ownerUserId === audience.actorUserId`. It diverges from the kernel in two ways: (a) it has no grants branch at all — the header comment (:4-6) admits 'resource_grants 精确过滤在 route 集成时统一接 services/resourceAcl 的 filterVisibleRows'; (b) it omits the `hasPrivateResourceAccess` / `resource-acl:private` gate that resourceAcl.ts:291 applies, so an actor lacking that account-range point still sees their own private rows here where the kernel denies. It has no production caller (grep: only its own file), yet it is exported and blessed by tests at rfc310-pr1b-action-template.test.ts:186-188.
- **架构影响**：A dormant second source of truth for the platform's most security-relevant predicate, already carrying two divergences, already covered by green tests that assert the divergent behaviour is correct. Whoever wires it up tomorrow inherits both gaps with test approval.
- **现有守卫**：none — nothing forbids a second visibility predicate; the RFC-282 D1 convergence lock (tests/rfc282-d1-acl-inline-convergence.test.ts) is a behaviour lock on four named legacy sites, not a structural ban
- **拟建守卫**：Either delete it and have the module take a `visibility` port satisfied by resourceAcl at bootstrap (the RFC-294 shape), or make it a thin re-export. Then add a repo-wide AST ratchet: outside services/resourceAcl.ts, no function may contain a `visibility === 'public'` comparison together with an `ownerUserId ===` comparison — the two-line signature of a hand-rolled ACL predicate. Current violations ledgered and shrinking only.
- **证伪方式**：Grep for imports of configResourceQueries in src/ — only the test imports it. Then compare its branch list to resourceAcl.ts:288-294: the `hasPrivateResourceAccess` and `grantedIds.has(row.id)` branches are absent.

### ACL-10 · P3 · duplicate-implementation · ADJUSTED

**The ACL kernel hand-copies both halves of the owner-scoped-name fence it could have imported**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/resourceAcl.ts:664-679` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourceAcl.ts:709-723` · `packages/backend/src/services/ownerScopedName.ts:8-34` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/infrastructure/sqliteConfigResourceStore.ts:26-29`
- **证据**：updateResourceAcl's owner-transfer collision pre-check writes its own predicate — `and(eq(table.ownerUserId, nextOwner), eq(table.name, cur.name), ne(table.id, row.id))` (:668-670) — instead of `ownerScopedNameWhere(table.ownerUserId, table.name, nextOwner, cur.name, {column: table.id, id: row.id})`, which exists for exactly this and is NULL-safe against migration 0118's `COALESCE(owner_user_id,'')` expression indexes. Its catch block then re-writes isOwnerNameUniqueViolation's regex verbatim — `/UNIQUE constraint failed|SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE|constraint failed/i` (:714) is byte-identical to ownerScopedName.ts:30 — but WITHOUT the index-name/table discriminator (:33), so any unique-constraint failure during an ACL PUT is reported as `resource-name-conflict`. A third variant, `message.includes('UNIQUE constraint failed')`, serves the five RFC-310 types at sqliteConfigResourceStore.ts:27-30.
- **架构影响**：Three spellings of one fence; the kernel's copy is both the least NULL-safe and the least discriminating, and it is the copy that runs for all twelve types on the owner-transfer path. A future table that gains a second unique index will start mislabelling its violations as name conflicts.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/rfc223-owner-transfer.test.ts exercises the collision on six types but asserts only the resulting 409 code, so any of the three spellings satisfies it
- **拟建守卫**：Import ownerScopedNameWhere at resourceAcl.ts:668 and give isOwnerNameUniqueViolation a per-type index-name lookup (a Record<AclResourceType, {table,index}> beside ACL_TABLES) so the catch can discriminate. Then a grep ratchet: `UNIQUE constraint failed` may appear only in services/ownerScopedName.ts.
- **证伪方式**：Diff resourceAcl.ts:714's regex literal against ownerScopedName.ts:30's — identical alternation, and the `message.includes(indexName)` half at ownerScopedName.ts:33 is absent from the kernel copy.

### ACL-11 · P3 · duplicate-implementation · CONFIRMED

**INTENT_HANDLE_RE re-encodes INTENT_RESOURCE_TYPES as a regex alternation with no lock between them**

- **锚点**：`packages/shared/src/ref/codecs.ts:29-42` · `packages/shared/src/ref/codecs.ts:211-216` · `packages/shared/src/schemas/resourceAcl.ts:95-103` · `packages/shared/src/schemas/intentChangeset.ts:48-56` · `packages/shared/tests/rfc271-resource-ref.test.ts:269-294` · `packages/backend/tests/docs-node-kind-coverage.test.ts:126-133`
- **证据**：`INTENT_HANDLE_RE = /^res#(agent|skill|mcp|plugin|workflow|workgroup)#([1-9][0-9]{0,5})$/` (codecs.ts:29-30) hand-spells the same six identities that INTENT_RESOURCE_TYPES declares as a const tuple (resourceAcl.ts:95-102). The regex is the actual wire gate — IntentHandleSchema is `.regex(INTENT_HANDLE_RE)` (intentChangeset.ts:52-53) — and decodeIntentRef then casts the capture with `handle[1] as AclResourceType` (codecs.ts:37), so the two lists must agree or the cast lies. The existing source lock (rfc271-resource-ref.test.ts:269-287) only asserts the regex is DEFINED in codecs.ts and re-exported from schemas — it never compares its alternation to INTENT_RESOURCE_TYPES. codecs.ts:211's `BUNDLE_NAME_RE = /^name:(workflow|workgroup)\//` + `named[1] as AclResourceType` (:216) has the same shape while the parallel decodeCallRef (:131) is properly typed `'workflow'|'workgroup'`.
- **架构影响**：Adding a seventh intent-creatable type updates the tuple, the zod enums, the applyChangeset loop (services/intent/applyChangeset.ts:291) and the INTENT.md doc guard — and silently NOT the regex, so the model's handles for that type are rejected at parse with no signal pointing at the regex. The reverse (removing a type) leaves the regex accepting it and the `as` cast minting a value the pipeline believes is intent-creatable.
- **现有守卫**：none for the list equivalence — packages/backend/tests/docs-node-kind-coverage.test.ts:131-133 iterates INTENT_RESOURCE_TYPES against INTENT.md, but nothing iterates it against INTENT_HANDLE_RE
- **拟建守卫**：Build the regex from the constant: `new RegExp('^res#(' + INTENT_RESOURCE_TYPES.join('|') + ')#([1-9][0-9]{0,5})$')`, which also lets decodeIntentRef narrow with asIntentResourceType instead of casting. If the literal must stay for readability, add `for (const t of INTENT_RESOURCE_TYPES) expect(INTENT_HANDLE_RE.test('res#'+t+'#1')).toBe(true)` plus the negative direction over ACL_RESOURCE_TYPES minus INTENT_RESOURCE_TYPES.
- **证伪方式**：Add a type to INTENT_RESOURCE_TYPES and run the shared suite. Green ⇒ the two lists are unlinked.

### ACL-12 · P3 · missing-guard · CONFIRMED

**The package secret-slot indexer's per-type switch silently skips capability_template and has no exhaustiveness check**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/secretInputs.ts:298-388` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/secretInputs.ts:495-504` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/serialize.ts:320-355` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/serialize.ts:160` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/serialize.ts:181` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/serialize.ts:197-198` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourcePackage/serialize.ts:227` · `packages/shared/src/schemas/resourceAcl.ts:56-69`
- **证据**：indexSecretSlots switches on `resourceType: ResourcePackageType` (= BUNDLE_RESOURCE_TYPES, seven entries) with cases for agent, skill, mcp, plugin, workflow and workgroup (:325-386) — capability_template has no case, there is no `default`, and the function body returns void from the switch, so an unhandled type contributes zero secret slots with no error. The export half is currently symmetric (serialize.ts:320-345 emits the capability_template op with `scripts: parseJson(row.scriptsJson, {})` and no redaction), so nothing is broken today — but the two per-type tables are independent hand-maintained lists that must stay in lockstep, and the only signal if they diverge is a package whose secret manifest names a field the importer cannot find.
- **架构影响**：Two mirror-image per-type tables (redact-on-export, locate-on-import) with no shared registry and no compile-time link. The moment template script bodies are redacted — the obvious next step, given they are already fenced by `scripts:author` — the import side stays silently empty.
- **现有守卫**：none — the switch has no `never` exhaustiveness arm, and no test asserts that every BUNDLE_RESOURCE_TYPES member appears in both the redaction and the slot-index tables
- **拟建守卫**：Add `default: { const _exhaustive: never = resourceType; void _exhaustive; break }` so a new packageable type is a compile error here, and pair the two tables into one `Record<BundleResourceType, {redact, locate}>` so 'no secrets in this type' is written once instead of being the absence of two cases.
- **证伪方式**：Add an eighth entry to BUNDLE_RESOURCE_TYPES and typecheck. The switch compiles unchanged.

### ACL-13 · P3 · boundary-blur · ADJUSTED

**The RFC-310 five bypass the ACL kernel's create-default triple, inlining the literal instead**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/resourceAcl.ts:86-124` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/infrastructure/sqliteConfigResourceStore.ts:70-93` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/application/commands/actionTemplateCommands.ts:37-59` · `packages/backend/src/services/capabilityTemplates.ts:305-315` · `packagesets/shared/src/schemas/capabilityTemplate.ts:92` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc231-private-copy.test.ts:318-371`
- **证据**：The kernel exposes DEFAULT_USER_RESOURCE_VISIBILITY / initialPrivateResourceAcl / assertInitialResourceOwner (:86-124), the last of which is the fence that 'HTTP payloads never choose an owner'. Seven services import them (agent, skill, mcp, plugin, workflow, workgroups, bundle/apply). The five RFC-310 stores do not: sqliteConfigResourceStore.create writes `visibility: 'private'` as a bare literal (:78) with ownerUserId taken straight from the caller, and createActionTemplate (:37-58) passes `ownerUserId: input.actorUserId` with no assertInitialResourceOwner call anywhere on the path. Today the route supplies `actor.user.id` so nothing is exploitable — but the fence that would catch a future payload-supplied owner is simply not on this path.
- **架构影响**：The 'creator is owner, private, revision 0' invariant is now expressed twice: once as a named kernel function under a ratchet, once as scattered literals under none. The RFC-231 ratchet's own design assumed one spelling.
- **现有守卫**：none for these five — packages/backend/tests/rfc231-private-copy.test.ts:318-372 is a hard-coded allowlist of six tables scanned across nine named source files; it cannot see inserts into action_templates / verification_profiles / digital_employees / automation_policies / development_adapter_definitions, nor inserts into its six tables from any file outside the nine
- **拟建守卫**：Widen that ratchet from a literal table list to `Object.entries(ACL_TABLES)` and from a fixed sourceFiles list to a walk of src/**, asserting every `.insert(<aclTable>)` site's file contains `initialPrivateResourceAcl` or `initialBuiltinResourceAcl`, with an explicitly shrinking exemption ledger.
- **证伪方式**：grep initialPrivateResourceAcl across src — seven service files, none under modules/. Then read sqliteConfigResourceStore.ts:78: `visibility: 'private',` inline.

### ACL-14 · P3 · stale-assertion · CONFIRMED

**The kernel's own headers still describe a six-type world, including a mount exclusion that does not exist**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/resourceAcl.ts:1-36` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourceAcl.ts:127-142` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourceAcl.ts:388-389` · `packages/shared/src/schemas/resourceAcl.ts:1-8` · `packages/backend/src/routes/resourceAcl.ts:34-40` · `packages/backend/src/routes/resourceAcl.ts:73-87` · `packages/backend/tests/contracts/registry.ts:867-888`
- **证据**：resourceAcl.ts:3 'Six resource types (agent / skill / mcp / plugin / workflow / workgroup)' — ACL_TABLES has twelve (:127-142). shared/schemas/resourceAcl.ts:3 says the same while ACL_RESOURCE_TYPES below it lists twelve. routes/resourceAcl.ts:73-77 'these twelve routes (six resources x GET/PUT) ... leaving it to the six callers' — there are seven mount call sites producing 24 routes. filterVisibleRows' comment (:388-389) 'keeps the five routes uniform'. Most consequential: routes/resourceAcl.ts:34-40 states 'RFC-310 的五类数字员工配置资源已进 AclResourceType，但…挂载配置的键域显式排除它们…等对应 routes 批次落地时从这里移除排除项' — and the very next line is `export type MountedAclResourceType = AclResourceType`, i.e. no exclusion exists and the five ARE mounted (developmentConfig.ts:231). The RFC-284 T12 OCC-fence table at resourceAcl.ts:24-36 likewise registers six of twelve types' fences.
- **架构影响**：The kernel's own documentation is the thing every reviewer reads to decide 'is this symmetric?'. It currently answers a question about six types when twelve exist, and the routes/resourceAcl.ts note actively instructs a future editor to remove an exclusion that isn't there — a change that would be a no-op while feeling like completion. This is the doc-side twin of ACL-2's regex: both encode 'six' into a place that governs twelve.
- **现有守卫**：none — no doc-coverage test covers these headers (the closest, packages/backend/tests/docs-node-kind-coverage.test.ts:126-133, iterates INTENT_RESOURCE_TYPES against INTENT.md only)
- **拟建守卫**：Extend the docs-coverage pattern: a test that reads services/resourceAcl.ts + routes/resourceAcl.ts and asserts every member of ACL_RESOURCE_TYPES appears in the OCC-fence table, and that no header contains a count word ('six'/'five'/'twelve') that disagrees with ACL_RESOURCE_TYPES.length. Delete the MountedAclResourceType exclusion note outright.
- **证伪方式**：Compare ACL_RESOURCE_TYPES.length (12) to each quoted comment. Read routes/resourceAcl.ts:40 for the claimed exclusion — the type alias is the unrestricted union.

## RT — Runtime driver port 与能力声明公共层（15 条：P1 0 / P2 3 / P3 12）

### RT-01 · P2 · escape-hatch · ADJUSTED

**POST /api/runtimes/probe spawns a real child with request-supplied extraArgs and isSandbox WITHOUT the capability gate the registry write path enforces**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/routes/runtimes.ts:38-47` · `packages/backend/src/routes/runtimes.ts:239-252` · `packages/backend/src/routes/runtimes.ts:269-286` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtimeRegistry.ts:208-219` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtimeRegistry.ts:714-722` · `packages/backend/tests/runtime-routes-registry.test.ts:194-212`
- **证据**：routes/runtimes.ts:239-252 parses ProbeBody (routes/runtimes.ts:42,46 — only `z.boolean().optional()` and `z.array(z.string().min(1)).max(16).optional()`) and passes `isSandbox: body.isSandbox === true` and `extraArgs: body.extraArgs` straight into `smokeRuntime({protocol: body.protocol, ...})`. Neither `validateExtraArgs` (runtimeRegistry.ts:209) nor `validateIsSandbox` (runtimeRegistry.ts:714) is called on this path — those are the ONLY places `acceptsExtraArgs` / `acceptsSandboxCompatibilityMarker` are consulted. The same bypass exists on POST /api/runtimes (:270-285): the smoke child is spawned BEFORE `createRuntime` validates, so an unacceptable arg set is executed and only then rejected. Today the breach is inert only because opencode's spawn happens to read neither field (packages/backend/src/services/runtime/opencode/spawn.ts has no IS_SANDBOX write; only claudeCode/spawn.ts:296 sets `env.IS_SANDBOX = '1'`).
- **架构影响**：A capability declaration that is enforced at one of two entry points is not a capability — it is a convention. The declaration's meaning ('only drivers declaring this consume it') is asserted in the error message at runtimeRegistry.ts:218 but is untrue of the probe path, so a future driver that reads ctx.extraArgs/ctx.isSandbox silently gains an unvalidated argv/env channel reachable by any settings:write caller.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/runtime-extra-args.test.ts covers validateExtraArgs and buildClaudeSpawn but never exercises /api/runtimes/probe; packages/backend/tests/runtime-routes-registry.test.ts does not assert a capability rejection on the probe body.
- **拟建守卫**：Call validateExtraArgs(body.protocol, body.extraArgs) and validateIsSandbox(body.protocol, body.isSandbox) at the top of both handlers (before any spawn), and add a route test: POST /api/runtimes/probe {protocol:'opencode', isSandbox:true} and {protocol:'opencode', extraArgs:['--x']} must both 400 with the capability code and must NOT have spawned. Additionally add a source ratchet asserting that every call site of smokeRuntime( in src/routes is preceded in the same function by a validateExtraArgs( call.
- **证伪方式**：Search routes/runtimes.ts for validateExtraArgs / validateIsSandbox. If either appears on the probe or pre-create smoke path, or if a route test asserts a 400 for {protocol:'opencode', isSandbox:true}, this is wrong.

### RT-02 · P2 · missing-guard · CONFIRMED

**The kind-discrimination ratchet's allowlist is a permanent, un-ratcheted exemption — and two of its three entries are already dead**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:230-271` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:239-247` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:264` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:176-187`
- **证据**：The scan skips allowlisted files BEFORE reading them: `if (kindDiscriminationAllowlist.has(rp)) continue` (:264) with the set `{'routes/runtimes.ts','services/runner.ts','cli/start.ts'}` (:239-247). I re-ran the test's own regex over packages/backend/src (excluding services/runtime) with the allowlist disabled: the only file that still matches today is `cli/start.ts`. So `routes/runtimes.ts` and `services/runner.ts` no longer need the exemption, yet the entries persist and permanently pre-approve those two files — a `if (driver.kind === 'opencode')` added to runner.ts tomorrow keeps the suite green. Contrast with the RFC-282 ledgers, which DO ratchet: rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:200-206 fails on a stale entry.
- **架构影响**：runner.ts is the shared execution pump and routes/runtimes.ts is the registry face — the two highest-traffic places a per-runtime branch would be added. The one guard that exists for 'no kind literals outside driver dirs' is blind precisely there, and the blindness is invisible because the exemption never expires.
- **现有守卫**：none — the allowlist itself has no stale check; the file's second test (:272-293) only ratchets the REGEX, not the allowlist.
- **拟建守卫**：Add a stale-ratchet mirroring rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:200-206: collect files that match the regex WITHOUT the allowlist, then assert `allowlist.filter(f => !matched.includes(f)) === []` so an exemption whose site was fixed fails the suite. That immediately prunes routes/runtimes.ts and services/runner.ts and forces cli/start.ts's exemption to carry a live justification.
- **证伪方式**：Delete 'services/runner.ts' from the allowlist and run the test. If it still passes, the entry was dead as claimed. If it fails, runner.ts still discriminates and my scan was wrong.

### RT-03 · P2 · missing-guard · ADJUSTED

**The A2 forbidden-token scan exempts the whole services/runtime/ directory, so the cross-driver commons that LIVE there are structurally unscannable for runtime dialect**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:158-172` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:259` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/stageSkills.ts:44` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/types.ts:102-105` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/spawnCtx.ts:38`
- **证据**：rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:166 `if (f.rel.startsWith('services/runtime/')) continue` before testing FORBIDDEN_TOKENS (:160 `['OPENCODE_CONFIG_CONTENT','--mcp-config','.claude/']`). The exemption is correct for services/runtime/opencode/** and services/runtime/claudeCode/**, but services/runtime/ ALSO contains the runtime-neutral commons: stageSkills.ts, spawnCtx.ts, head.ts, selfCheck.ts, types.ts, injectionIdentity.ts. Three of the findings above live in exactly that unscanned band: stageSkills.ts:44 `'.claude-plugin'`, types.ts:101-105 `opencodePath/claudeCodePath`, spawnCtx.ts:36 `{opencodeCmd?} | {runtimeCmd?}`. The kind-discrimination ratchet has the identical hole (rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:257 `if (rp === 'services/runtime') continue`).
- **架构影响**：Both of the repo's runtime-fence guards treat 'inside services/runtime/' as a synonym for 'is a driver', but the directory is a mixed zone: drivers plus the commons every driver shares. The result is that the ONE region where runtime dialect does the most damage — the shared kernel two drivers both call — is the one region no guard inspects, which is why F1/F7/F13 could land unnoticed.
- **现有守卫**：none — this IS the guard, and its own scope excludes the target.
- **拟建守卫**：Narrow both skips from `services/runtime/` to `services/runtime/opencode/` and `services/runtime/claudeCode/`, and let the shared files (stageSkills.ts, spawnCtx.ts, head.ts, selfCheck.ts, types.ts, injectionIdentity.ts) be scanned by both the FORBIDDEN_TOKENS test and the kind-discrimination regex. Land it with the F1/F7/F13 fixes so the ratchet starts green; add '.claude-plugin' and the /\b(opencode|claudeCode|claude)[A-Z]\w*Path\b/ identifier shape to FORBIDDEN_TOKENS.
- **证伪方式**：Read rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:166 and rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:257. If either skip is already narrowed to the two driver subdirectories, this is wrong.

### RT-04 · P3 · business-special-case · ADJUSTED

**stageSkills — the shared two-runtime staging kernel hardcodes a Claude-Code plugin exclusion and applies it to opencode; its own header describes failure semantics the code no longer has**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/runtime/stageSkills.ts:13-17` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/stageSkills.ts:25-44` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/stageSkills.ts:76-83` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/stageSkills.ts:90-95` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc154-runtime-config-dir.test.ts:455-463` · `packages/backend/tests/claude-skill-injection-2026-08-09.test.ts:143`
- **证据**：stageSkills.ts:44 `const EXCLUDED_SKILL_ENTRY = '.claude-plugin'`, applied unconditionally in the cpSync filter at :79 `if (basename(src) !== EXCLUDED_SKILL_ENTRY) return true`. The justifying comment (:26-42) is explicitly Claude-only: "Claude Code loads a `<skills-dir>/<name>/` entry that contains `.claude-plugin` as a PLUGIN ... measured on 2.1.226". opencode/driver.ts:173 calls the same function, so an opencode-staged skill silently loses any `.claude-plugin` subtree and logs a warning about a runtime that is not running. Separately, the header at :13-18 asserts "Failure semantics differ by caller ... opencode (runner-era) FAILS the spawn on a staging error ... claude stages best-effort" — false in current source: neither claudeCode/config.ts:155 nor :309 passes `{bestEffort:true}`, and `grep -rn bestEffort packages/backend/src` returns only the two definition lines (stageSkills.ts:57, :91). The per-caller escape hatch has zero production callers.
- **架构影响**：The one place where a skill tree crosses into a runtime config dir now encodes one runtime's plugin-loading dialect for all runtimes. A third driver inherits a rule that is meaningless for it, and the natural place to add ITS exclusion is another literal in the same generic filter — the kernel becomes a union of per-runtime denylists instead of a port. The dead `bestEffort` parameter is a pre-opened lane for reintroducing per-caller behaviour divergence.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/claude-skill-injection-2026-08-09.test.ts only asserts the exclusion on the CLAUDE staging path; packages/backend/tests/rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:88-92 locks that stageSkills has one definition and two whitelisted callers, but says nothing about what is inside it. The A2 forbidden-token scan that would catch runtime dialect explicitly skips this file (rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:166 `if (f.rel.startsWith('services/runtime/')) continue`).
- **拟建守卫**：Move the exclusion behind a driver-owned port: add `readonly stagingExclusions?: readonly string[]` (or `filterStagedEntry?(basename): boolean`) to RuntimeDriver, have stageSkills take it as a parameter, and add to packages/backend/tests/rfc282-a3-driver-selfcheck.test.ts a source-text assertion that services/runtime/stageSkills.ts contains no literal matching /['"]\.(claude|opencode)[^'"]*['"]/. Plus a unit test asserting `stageSkills` with the opencode driver's exclusion set COPIES a `.claude-plugin` dir. Delete the `bestEffort` parameter (zero callers) and the stale header paragraph.
- **证伪方式**：Run: grep -n "EXCLUDED_SKILL_ENTRY" packages/backend/src/services/runtime/stageSkills.ts and grep -rn "bestEffort" packages/backend/src. If a production caller passes bestEffort, or if the exclusion is parameterized per driver, this finding is wrong.

### RT-05 · P3 · business-special-case · ADJUSTED

**validateExtraArgs is capability-gated at the door but enforces Claude Code's argv dialect on every driver that opts in**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/runtimeRegistry.ts:213-219` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtimeRegistry.ts:244-262` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/claudeCode/spawn.ts:70-100` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/claudeCode/spawn.ts:222-256` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/index.ts:75` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/claudeCode/driver.ts:475-478`
- **证据**：The gate is correct and capability-driven: runtimeRegistry.ts:215 `if (getRuntimeDriver(protocol).acceptsExtraArgs !== true) throw ...`. The body is not: :254 `if (CLAUDE_PLATFORM_OWNED_FLAGS.has(bareFlag))` — a set defined in claudeCode/spawn.ts:70-100 listing `--append-system-prompt-file`, `--session-id`, `--allowedTools`, `--fork-session` etc., and :246-251 rejects a bare token with the message "would be consumed as the prompt positional", which is claude's argv convention (opencode takes the prompt positionally too but is fail-closed here, so the rule exists only for claude). There is also a second, divergent implementation of the same 'is this a platform-owned flag' predicate at claudeCode/spawn.ts:230-256 with different bare-flag extraction (`arg.startsWith('--') && arg.includes('=')` vs the registry's `token.includes('=')`) plus variadic/valueless handling the registry copy lacks.
- **架构影响**：`acceptsExtraArgs` reads like a port but is really a permission to be validated by claude's rules. A third driver declaring it inherits claude's reserved-flag list and claude's positional convention, so the declaration cannot express that driver's real argv contract — the only way out is another `if (protocol === ...)` inside the validator, i.e. reintroducing exactly the literal branching RFC-143 removed.
- **现有守卫**：none for the dialect. packages/backend/tests/runtime-extra-args.test.ts:82-83 only asserts the two drivers' capability VALUES; the suite's lock (e) proves CLAUDE_PLATFORM_OWNED_FLAGS covers what claudeCode/spawn.ts emits, which is the driver-side invariant, not the shared-validator one. Nothing turns red if a third driver sets acceptsExtraArgs:true.
- **拟建守卫**：Promote the reserved set and the positional rule to driver-owned surface: `readonly platformOwnedFlags?: ReadonlySet<string>` + `readonly bareTokenIsPrompt?: boolean` on RuntimeDriver, and have validateExtraArgs read them. Add a selfCheck implication in services/runtime/selfCheck.ts: `acceptsExtraArgs === true ⇒ platformOwnedFlags is a non-empty set`, with a mutation test in rfc282-a3-driver-selfcheck.test.ts feeding a mock driver that declares acceptsExtraArgs without the set. Add a source assertion that runtimeRegistry.ts imports no identifier matching /^CLAUDE_/.
- **证伪方式**：Add a third fake driver to the DRIVERS map in a test with `acceptsExtraArgs: true` and call validateExtraArgs on it with `['--session-id','x']`. If it is rejected with claude's message, the finding holds; if the driver's own flag set is consulted, it does not.

### RT-06 · P3 · business-special-case · ADJUSTED

**A live kind-discriminating branch in routes/runtime.ts escapes the ratchet on both halves of its regex, and silently answers with the wrong runtime's model list**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/routes/runtime.ts:30-34` · `packages/backend/src/routes/runtime.ts:35-53` · `packages/backend/src/routes/runtime.ts:63-71` · `packages/frontend/src/components/ModelSelect.tsx:55` · `packages/frontend/src/components/ModelSelect.tsx:68-75`
- **证据**：routes/runtime.ts:49-53: `const kind: RuntimeKind = matchedReal ? resolved.protocol : rtParam === 'claude' || rtParam === 'claude-code' ? 'claude-code' : 'opencode'`. The ratchet regex is `/\b(?:runtime|protocol|kind|defaultRuntime)\s*[!=]==\s*['"](?:opencode|claude-code)['"]|\bisClaude\b/` — the variable is `rtParam` (not in the name set) and the alias is `'claude'` (not in the literal set), so the branch is invisible to it, and routes/runtime.ts is NOT in the allowlist. I confirmed by running the exact regex over the tree: routes/runtime.ts is not among the hits. The alias exists because the frontend speaks a third spelling: ModelSelect.tsx:55 `runtime?: 'opencode' | 'claude'` and :73 `? { runtime: 'claude' }`. The else-arm is also a wrong-answer path: an unknown `?runtime=` name returns opencode's model list with HTTP 200 (routes/runtime.ts:63-66), and every failure is reported with the code literal `'opencode-models-failed'` (:66-70) even for a claude binary.
- **架构影响**：Runtime identity has three vocabularies in flight — the registry NAME, the driver KIND, and a frontend legacy alias — and the reconciliation is an ad-hoc ternary at the transport edge rather than a registry lookup. That is the exact shape the RFC-282/143 fences were built to eliminate, and it demonstrates that the fence's vocabulary (four variable names, two literals) is a denylist, not an invariant.
- **现有守卫**：none — rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:230-271 does not match this shape and does not scan packages/frontend at all.
- **拟建守卫**：(1) Broaden the ratchet to a shape rule rather than a name list: flag any `=== '<literal>'` / `!== '<literal>'` where the literal is a member of RUNTIME_KINDS ∪ known aliases, anywhere in packages/backend/src and packages/frontend/src outside services/runtime/**; keep an explicitly-ratcheted allowlist. (2) Delete the alias by making the frontend send the registry name (or the kind) and having the route resolve `?runtime=` through resolveRuntimeByName + isKnownRuntimeKind only, 400-ing on an unknown value instead of falling back to opencode.
- **证伪方式**：Run the test's regex from rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:249-251 against packages/backend/src/routes/runtime.ts. If it matches, the branch is guarded and this finding is wrong.

### RT-07 · P3 · duplicate-implementation · ADJUSTED

**'the default runtime is opencode' has no single source — the bare literal is re-derived in 15 production sites across 7 files**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/runner.ts:566` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtimeRegistry.ts:401` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtimeRegistry.ts:451-468` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtimeRegistry.ts:479` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtimeRegistry.ts:948` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtimeRegistry.ts:1004-1013` · `packages/backend/src/routes/runtimes.ts:174-180` · `packages/backend/src/services/memoryDistiller.ts:798`
- **证据**：Each site re-spells the fallback independently: runner.ts:566 `const runtime: RuntimeKind = opts.runtime ?? 'opencode'`; runtimeRegistry.ts:479 `pick(agentRuntime) ?? pick(defaultRuntime) ?? 'opencode'`; :401 `row.name === (defaultRuntimeName ?? 'opencode')`; :948 the same expression again for the can't-disable rule; :1007-1013 a THIRD, structurally different derivation for deleteRuntime (`const effectiveDefault = cfgResolves ? cfg : 'opencode'`) whose comment admits it must match dispatch "EXACTLY"; routes/runtimes.ts:179-180 a FOURTH for the list face; mcpRuntimeTest.ts:2221 a fifth; memoryDistiller.ts:798/1112/1176 three more. runtimeRegistry.ts:461 even logs `log.warn('runtime-name-unknown-fallback-opencode', ...)` before returning a synthesized opencode row. Meanwhile runtime/index.ts:44-52 advertises RUNTIME_KINDS as the single source "so adding a runtime = register a driver here ... every derived set updates automatically" — which is true for the kind SET and false for the default.
- **架构影响**：Four independent computations of 'the effective default' must agree or the product misbehaves in ways the type system cannot see (deleteRuntime protects a row that setRuntimeEnabled would not, or the list marks a different row as default than dispatch uses). It also hard-binds the platform's neutral execution layer to one business identity: changing the shipped default, or shipping a build without opencode, is a 15-site edit with no failing test to guide it.
- **现有守卫**：none — the `?? 'opencode'` / `: 'opencode'` shape is not matched by the kind-discrimination regex (rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:249-251), which requires an `===`/`!==` comparison, and there is no dedicated default-runtime test.
- **拟建守卫**：Introduce one exported `DEFAULT_RUNTIME_KIND` (and `effectiveDefaultRuntimeName(config, rows)`) in services/runtime/index.ts or runtimeRegistry.ts, route all sites through it, then add a grep ratchet in the same spirit as rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts: no file outside that owner may contain `/(\?\?|:)\s*['"](?:opencode|claude-code)['"]/` , with an explicitly-ratcheted (stale-checked) exception ledger. Add a unit test asserting deleteRuntime / setRuntimeEnabled / runtimeRowToView / resolveAgentRuntime all agree on the effective default for the same (config, rows) input.
- **证伪方式**：grep -rn "?? 'opencode'" packages/backend/src . If a shared constant is threaded through and the literal appears once, this is wrong. Also check whether any test asserts the four effective-default derivations agree.

### RT-08 · P3 · boundary-blur · ADJUSTED

**RuntimeBinaryConfig — the neutral defaultBinary port is parameterized by per-runtime config keys, and the probe-receipt invalidation re-derives protocols from those two literals**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/runtime/types.ts:99-105` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/opencode/driver.ts:370-372` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/claudeCode/driver.ts:538-540` · `packages/backend/src/routes/config.ts:89-95` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtimeRegistry.ts:1096-1099`
- **证据**：types.ts:101-105 `export interface RuntimeBinaryConfig { opencodePath?: string | null; claudeCodePath?: string | null }` — the shared driver-contract module enumerates concrete runtime identities as fields, and each driver reads its own (opencode/driver.ts:371 `config.opencodePath ? [config.opencodePath] : ['opencode']`; claudeCode/driver.ts:539 `config.claudeCodePath ? ... : ['claude']`). routes/config.ts:88-95 then rebuilds the affected-protocol list by hand: `if (nextConfig.opencodePath !== currentConfig.opencodePath) changedBinaryProtocols.push('opencode'); if (nextConfig.claudeCodePath !== ...) changedBinaryProtocols.push('claude-code')`, feeding invalidateInheritedRuntimeProbeReceipts.
- **架构影响**：Registering a driver is supposed to be additive; here it also requires widening a shared contract TYPE, adding a Config field, and remembering a third `if` in a settings route. Forgetting the last one leaves a stale GREEN probe receipt attached to a changed binary — the exact failure mode the invalidation exists to prevent — with nothing to signal it. It is also the only remaining place where the driver port's input shape names the drivers.
- **现有守卫**：none — no test asserts that changedBinaryProtocols covers every RUNTIME_KIND, and the two literals in routes/config.ts are string pushes, not comparisons, so the kind-discrimination regex does not see them.
- **拟建守卫**：Replace RuntimeBinaryConfig with a per-kind lookup owned by the registry: `binaryPathFor(kind, config)` driven by a `Record<RuntimeKind, keyof Config>` table (compile-time exhaustive, same pattern as DEFAULT_CONFIG_DIR_PROFILE at shared/runtimeConfigDir.ts:33-37). Then derive changedBinaryProtocols by iterating RUNTIME_KINDS over that table, and add a test asserting `Object.keys(BINARY_CONFIG_KEY).sort() === RUNTIME_KINDS.sort()` plus a route test that changing each protocol's config key invalidates that protocol's receipts.
- **证伪方式**：Read services/runtime/types.ts:99-105 and routes/config.ts:88-95. If RuntimeBinaryConfig has become an index over RuntimeKind, or if changedBinaryProtocols iterates RUNTIME_KINDS, this is wrong.

### RT-09 · P3 · boundary-blur · ADJUSTED

**The unified inventory read dispatches on capability class but hard-wires opencode's implementation for that class, bypassing the driver.readInventory port selfCheck already guarantees**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/execution/inventoryRead.ts:169-186` · `packages/backend/src/services/execution/inventoryRead.ts:49-77` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/index.ts:77` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/opencode/inventory.ts:193-197` · `packages/backend/src/services/execution/startupVerification.ts:143-155` · `packages/shared/src/schemas/startupVerification.ts:34` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/selfCheck.ts:60-67`
- **证据**：inventoryRead.ts:38-40 states the intent — "本读端是运行时无关层，直接 import opencode 内部会把它重新钉死在一个运行时上" — then :176 does `const snapshot = await getInventorySnapshot(db, taskId, nodeRunId)` for EVERY driver whose capabilities.startupObservation === 'inventory-file', and converts it with `facesFromOpencodeSnapshot` (:49). getInventorySnapshot is opencode/inventory.ts and its behaviour is opencode-specific down to `process.env.OPENCODE_PURE` (opencode/inventory.ts:190-193) and the reason code `'opencode-pure-mode'` (:59). The eslint fence is satisfied only because runtime/index.ts:76 re-exports the opencode function through the neutral entry. The same shape recurs in the verification layer: the sources bag field is literally `claudeInit` (startupVerification.ts:150) and the persisted observation source enum is `z.enum(['claude-init','opencode-inventory'])` (shared/schemas/startupVerification.ts:34). Meanwhile selfCheck.ts:60-67 already forces `startupObservation:'inventory-file' ⇒ readInventory implemented`, so the correct port exists and is unused here.
- **架构影响**：The read end LOOKS registry-driven (a switch on the declared class with a 'none' arm for a third runtime) but the class-to-implementation mapping is 1:1 with a runtime name. A third driver that truthfully declares 'inventory-file' would be read through opencode's DB column, opencode's OPENCODE_PURE env and opencode's reason codes, and the frontend would render a source label from a two-value enum that cannot name it. The declaration becomes a label rather than a dispatch key.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/rfc297-inventory-read.test.ts exercises only the two shipped drivers; rfc282-c0c2-runtime-fence.test.ts:66-86 asserts the switch has three arms but not that each arm goes through a port. The A2 forbidden-token scan skips services/runtime/** and does not scan for `Opencode`/`Claude` in identifier names.
- **拟建守卫**：Route the 'inventory-file' arm through `getRuntimeDriver(kind).readInventory` (already selfCheck-guaranteed) and move facesFromOpencodeSnapshot behind a driver method (`observedFacesFromSnapshot`). Widen StartupObservationSchema.source from a two-value enum to the class name ('inventory-file' | 'init-event') or to the driver kind. Guard: a test that registers a THIRD mock driver declaring startupObservation:'inventory-file' with its own readInventory, and asserts getRuntimeInventory returns that driver's faces — today it would return opencode's.
- **证伪方式**：grep -n "getInventorySnapshot\|facesFromOpencodeSnapshot" packages/backend/src/services/execution/inventoryRead.ts. If those are gone in favour of driver.readInventory, this is wrong.

### RT-10 · P3 · business-special-case · CONFIRMED

**A dirty node_runs.runtime silently reads as opencode in the inventory read end, contradicting the read-path contract RFC-282 决策13 put in tryGetRuntimeDriver**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/execution/inventoryRead.ts:155-158` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/index.ts:41-46` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc282-c0c2-runtime-fence.test.ts:46-51`
- **证据**：inventoryRead.ts:156 `const kind: RuntimeKind = isKnownRuntimeKind(run.runtime) ? run.runtime : 'opencode'`, with the comment justifying only the NULL case ("NULL runtime = 早于 RFC-111 的行"). The predicate is `isKnownRuntimeKind`, so it also swallows a corrupt or future value — the row is then read with `getRuntimeDriver('opencode').capabilities` (:157) and rendered as an opencode inventory. runtime/index.ts:41-46 defines the sanctioned read-path behaviour for exactly this case: "Read/display-path lookup: null for an unknown kind so one dirty DB row degrades that row instead of 500-ing the whole page", and rfc282-c0c2-runtime-fence.test.ts:46-52 locks `tryGetRuntimeDriver('made-up-runtime') === null`. This consumer does not use it.
- **架构影响**：The registry provides two lookup contracts (throw on execution, degrade on display); a consumer that hand-rolls a third contract (silently substitute one specific runtime) reintroduces the pre-RFC-282 failure mode — a wrong runtime's answer presented as fact — in the one face whose job is to tell the operator what actually loaded.
- **现有守卫**：none — the fence test asserts tryGetRuntimeDriver's behaviour but nothing asserts that consumers use it; no test feeds inventoryRead a row with an unknown runtime value.
- **拟建守卫**：Use `tryGetRuntimeDriver(run.runtime)` and return an explicit 'unknown-runtime' observation state (or 404/degrade) for a non-NULL unknown value, keeping the NULL⇒opencode legacy path as an explicit `run.runtime === null` branch. Guard: a test inserting a node_run with runtime='made-up' and asserting getRuntimeInventory does NOT return opencode declaration/faces; plus a grep ratchet forbidding `isKnownRuntimeKind(...) ? ... : '<kind literal>'`.
- **证伪方式**：Read inventoryRead.ts:155-158. If the unknown (non-NULL) case is separated from the NULL legacy case, this is wrong.

### RT-11 · P3 · business-special-case · ADJUSTED

**The shared stale-child kill kernel identifies 'our agent child' with an /opencode|bun/ regex; a live claude child on a row without spawn_binary_path is judged a recycled PID and left running**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/util/process.ts:174-185` · `packages/backend/src/util/process.ts:246-257` · `packages/backend/src/services/runner.ts:1715-1730` · `packages/backend/src/services/scheduler.ts:5060-5067`
- **证据**：util/process.ts:181 `return /opencode|bun/i.test(pidCommandLine(pid))` inside `pidCommandLooksLikeAgentChild`, whose doc (:174-177) says "the pid's command line must look like one of our children (the real `opencode` binary, or `bun` ...). Anything else ⟹ the pid was recycled; leave it alone." killStaleRunProcessTree selects it as the FALLBACK identity gate at :254-256 whenever `spawnBinaryPath` is absent or empty, and `if (!matchesShape) return 'command-mismatch'` (:257) skips the SIGTERM→SIGKILL entirely. schema.ts:1750-1758 documents that spawn_binary_path is "NULL for non-agent runs / rows predating RFC-108". orphans.ts:121-128 then proceeds with its rollback/status flip regardless of the outcome. A live `claude` process matches neither alternative.
- **架构影响**：The one place the platform decides 'is this PID still our writer?' encodes a single runtime's binary name. The failure is silent and asymmetric: the safe-looking verdict ('command-mismatch' = don't signal) is the DANGEROUS one for a non-opencode runtime, because the caller then git-resets a worktree under a live writer. Any third runtime inherits the same blind spot on day one.
- **现有守卫**：none — no test exercises pidCommandLooksLikeAgentChild with a claude-shaped command line, and the regex is a string literal inside a comparison-free expression, so the kind-discrimination ratchet does not see it (I ran the ratchet's regex: util/process.ts is not among the hits).
- **拟建守卫**：Derive the fallback vocabulary from the registry instead of hardcoding: build the alternation from `RUNTIME_KINDS.map(getRuntimeDriver).flatMap(d => d.defaultBinary({}))` plus 'bun', or better, delete the fuzzy fallback and treat a missing spawn_binary_path as 'unproven' (the caller already distinguishes danger outcomes). Guard: a unit test asserting pidCommandLooksLikeAgentChild's pattern accepts every driver's defaultBinary({})[0], which fails automatically when a driver is registered.
- **证伪方式**：Read util/process.ts:174-185 and :250-257. If the fallback derives its names from the driver registry, or if the missing-binary case no longer returns 'command-mismatch', this is wrong.

### RT-12 · P3 · duplicate-implementation · ADJUSTED

**runtimeSmoke re-implements claude's terminal-result detection inline instead of using the driver.parseTerminalResultError port, and applies a merged two-runtime error dialect to every runtime's streams**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/runtimeSmoke.ts:256-264` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtimeSmoke.ts:366-372` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/types.ts:852-856` · `packages/backend/src/services/runner.ts:1461` · `packages/backend/src/services/systemAgentRun.ts:528`
- **证据**：runtimeSmoke.ts:262 `if (line.includes('"type":"result"') || line.includes('"is_error":true')) { lastResultLine = line }` — claude's stream-json wire shape as raw substrings in the runtime-neutral smoke, while the port for exactly this exists (types.ts:851-856 `parseTerminalResultError?(line): string | null`, "Runtimes without such a dialect omit the method") and is used by runner.ts:1461 and systemAgentRun.ts:528. runner.ts:1455-1460 even names the split: the terminal-result parse "was fixed on the smoke path only". Separately :344 `const haystack = \`${stderrText}\n${stdoutText}\`.toLowerCase()` merges both streams for every runtime, and the classifier ordering at :346-350 exists solely for one runtime's wording (:345-347: "claude's region/proxy block reads 'Failed to authenticate. API Error: 403 Request not allowed'"), with AUTH/NETWORK/MODEL signature sets (:68-104) that are a union of both runtimes' phrasings.
- **架构影响**：Runtime failure classification — which drives what an operator is told to fix — has two implementations, one behind a port and one inline, and the inline one is the operator-facing registration/probe path. A driver whose dialect diverges cannot influence the smoke verdict at all; the only way to add a third runtime's wording is to append to the shared regex, which then also fires on the other runtimes' output (the file already documents one such false-positive incident at :78-88).
- **现有守卫**：none for the split — packages/backend/tests/runtime-claude-events.test.ts and runtime-claude-e2e.test.ts assert the DRIVER's is_error handling; nothing asserts that runtimeSmoke has no inline protocol literals. MODEL_FAIL_SIGNATURES is exported "供回归测试直接断言" (:90) but only its content is tested, not its ownership.
- **拟建守卫**：Replace the inline check with `driver.parseTerminalResultError?.(line)` and move the auth/network/model signature sets onto the driver (`readonly failureSignatures?: {auth,network,model}`), classifying only against the running driver's own set. Guard: extend packages/backend/tests/rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts FORBIDDEN_TOKENS (currently ['OPENCODE_CONFIG_CONTENT','--mcp-config','.claude/'] at :160) with '"type":"result"' and '"is_error"' so any stdout-protocol literal outside services/runtime/** fails.
- **证伪方式**：grep -n 'is_error' packages/backend/src/services/runtimeSmoke.ts. If the literal is gone in favour of the driver port, this is wrong.

### RT-13 · P3 · missing-guard · CONFIRMED

**MCP-playground support is a method-presence proxy — the exact shape RFC-282 C2 eliminated for inventory — and its two-method contract is not enforced together**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/mcpRuntimeTest.ts:127-133` · `packages/backend/src/services/mcpRuntimeTest.ts:2232-2238` · `packages/backend/src/services/mcpRuntimeTest.ts:858` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/types.ts:781-792` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/claudeCode/driver.ts:485-488` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc282-a3-driver-selfcheck.test.ts:59-173`
- **证据**：mcpRuntimeTest.ts:132 `return tryGetRuntimeDriver(row.protocol)?.mcpTestSessionReference !== undefined` is the eligibility predicate, and :2233 gates admission on the same presence check. types.ts:783-786 states the rule explicitly: "方法存在 = 该 runtime 支持测试台". This is the same predicate shape RFC-282 C2 removed for inventory — rfc282-c0c2-runtime-fence.test.ts:54-61 asserts `'readInventory !== undefined'` no longer appears in runner.ts or mcpRuntimeTest.ts, but says nothing about mcpTestSessionReference. The paired method is consumed optionally: :858 `const runtimeSessionId = runtime.driver.createMcpTestNativeSessionId?.() ?? null`, so a driver implementing only mcpTestSessionReference is judged eligible and then always runs with nativeSessionId null — which claude's own implementation treats as fatal (claudeCode/driver.ts:486 `if (nativeSessionId === null) throw new Error('mcp-test-native-session-missing')`). selfCheck.ts verifies only startupObservation, declarationFaces and the disabled-resource policy; it never looks at this pair.
- **架构影响**：Two capability idioms coexist — declared stances in `capabilities` (boot-verified) and method presence (unverified) — and only the first is a contract. New capabilities keep landing in the unverified idiom because it costs nothing, so the boot self-check's coverage shrinks relative to the port surface with every RFC.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/rfc282-a3-driver-selfcheck.test.ts:59-173 proves the self-check refuses a missing face, an illegal stance, a missing readInventory and a missing init-event sample, but has no case for the mcp-test pair.
- **拟建守卫**：Add `readonly supportsMcpTest?: true` to RuntimeDriverCapabilities and a selfCheck implication: `supportsMcpTest === true ⇒ typeof createMcpTestNativeSessionId === 'function' && typeof mcpTestSessionReference === 'function'`, plus the inverse (either method present ⇒ the stance declared). Add mutation tests in rfc282-a3-driver-selfcheck.test.ts for a driver implementing only one half, and switch mcpRuntimeTest.ts:132 / :2233 to read the declared stance. More generally, add a selfCheck assertion that every optional method on RuntimeDriver is either covered by an implication or listed in an explicitly-ratcheted 'no-implication' ledger.
- **证伪方式**：grep -n "mcpTestSessionReference" packages/backend/src/services/runtime/selfCheck.ts. If the pair is verified at boot, this is wrong.

### RT-14 · P3 · forgeable-contract · ADJUSTED

**The shared spawn-context translation forges an Agent by cast and carries per-driver knob names in its signature**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/runtime/spawnCtx.ts:21-32` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/spawnCtx.ts:36-48` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/spawnCtx.ts:74-78` · `packages/backend/src/services/runtime/opencode/driver.ts:92-99`
- **证据**：spawnCtx.ts:24-32 `syntheticPersonaAgent` builds `{name, description:'', bodyMd, permission:{}, outputs:[]}` and returns it `as unknown as Agent` (:31) — the cast is required precisely because the object is NOT an Agent (no id, owner, visibility, aclRevision, schemaVersion). toBusinessCtx then feeds it into the real business assembly path at :48 `agent: ctx.injection.agent ?? syntheticPersonaAgent(ctx)`. The same forgery is repeated inside a driver at opencode/driver.ts:92-99 (`as unknown as Parameters<typeof renderOpencodeAgentEntry>[0]`). Additionally the shared translator's signature names both drivers' private knobs: :36 `head: { opencodeCmd?: string[] } | { runtimeCmd?: string[] }` and :76 the same union for toSystemCtx.
- **架构影响**：Any invariant the Agent type is supposed to carry into spawn assembly (ownership, visibility, ACL revision, declared outputs) can be bypassed by constructing the value structurally, and the cast makes that invisible to typecheck and to review. The head union means the shared layer must be edited whenever a driver renames its own private knob, which is the opposite of a port.
- **现有守卫**：none — no test asserts the absence of `as unknown as Agent` in services/runtime/**, and there is no lint rule banning the cast; eslint's no-restricted-imports fence covers imports, not casts.
- **拟建守卫**：Introduce an explicit `PersonaAgent` type (the exact subset the renderers need) and change renderOpencodeAgentEntry / the business assembly to accept `Agent | PersonaAgent`, deleting both casts. Replace the head union with a single neutral `binaryOverride?: readonly string[]` already present on AgentSpawnContext (types.ts:376 documents the collapse) so spawnCtx names no driver knob. Guard: a source assertion in rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts that no file under services/runtime/ contains 'as unknown as Agent' or 'as unknown as Parameters<'.
- **证伪方式**：grep -n 'as unknown as' packages/backend/src/services/runtime/spawnCtx.ts packages/backend/src/services/runtime/opencode/driver.ts. If both casts are gone, this is wrong.

### RT-15 · P3 · stale-assertion · ADJUSTED

**Audit ledger claims 'runtime 字面量执行分支 driver 目录外真零' and 'driver.kind 目录外零引用'; source has at least three such branches today**

- **锚点**：`design/system-commons-unification-audit-2026-08-12.md:27` · `packages/backend/src/cli/start.ts:396-401` · `packages/backend/src/routes/runtime.ts:49-53` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:244-247`
- **证据**：The ledger row at :27 asserts "runtime 字面量执行分支 driver 目录外真零" and :37 repeats "driver.kind 目录外零引用". Source: cli/start.ts:400 `if (config.defaultRuntime === 'claude-code') { const ccDriver = getRuntimeDriver('claude-code') ... }` (also a category error — it compares a runtime NAME against a KIND literal, so a custom row named 'my-claude' with protocol 'claude-code' as the default is never probed); routes/runtime.ts:49-53 the alias ternary (F5); routes/config.ts:88-95 the two-literal changedBinaryProtocols push (F7). The repo's own ratchet acknowledges start.ts by allowlisting it (rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:243-246), which directly contradicts '真零'. In the other direction the ledger's open-item column at :27 and row N2 at :55 record 'selfCheck 缺 inventory-file⇒readInventory 蕴含守卫' as OPEN — that was closed by RFC-284: selfCheck.ts:60-67 and :74-88 now enforce both implications and rfc282-a3-driver-selfcheck.test.ts:115-172 proves it.
- **架构影响**：RFC-294 §2 cites this audit as a direct input and repeats 'runtime driver 已经有单一事实源'. A future RFC that trusts '真零' will size a runtime-related change as additive and skip the three call sites that actually need editing; conversely the stale-open N2 row invites re-doing work that is done. Per CLAUDE.md the ledger is not an oracle, but it is what a new session reads first.
- **现有守卫**：none — no test or CI step reconciles the audit ledger's claims against source; markdown link-check does not verify assertions.
- **拟建守卫**：Convert the two absolute claims into executable form and cite the test from the doc: keep the kind-discrimination ratchet (rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:230-271) as the definition of '真零', give it a stale-ratcheted allowlist (F4) and the widened vocabulary (F5), and change the ledger row to reference the test file plus its current allowlist size rather than the word '真零'. Mark N2 closed with the selfCheck.ts:60-88 anchor.
- **证伪方式**：Read cli/start.ts:400 and routes/runtime.ts:49-53. If neither compares a runtime identity against a literal, the ledger claim holds and this is wrong. Read selfCheck.ts:60-88 — if the implications are absent, N2 is genuinely still open.

## NK — shared 图模型注册表（node-kind / ports / sharding / wrapper）（14 条：P1 0 / P2 3 / P3 11）

### NK-01 · P2 · business-special-case · CONFIRMED

**The `list<T>` handler's validate normalizes EVERY list with the one-item-per-line codec, corrupting `list<markdown>` bodies before persistence**

- **锚点**：`packages/shared/src/outputKinds/list.ts:134-186` · `packages/shared/src/listWire.ts:17-22` · `packages/backend/src/services/envelope.ts:651-667` · `packages/backend/src/services/runner.ts:2243-2245` · `packages/backend/src/services/runner.ts:2304-2306` · `packages/backend/src/services/review.ts:585` · `packages/backend/src/services/scheduler.ts:7767-7769`
- **证据**：list.ts:142 is `const items = splitListItems(rawContent)` with NO item-kind branch, and line 185 returns `{ ok: true, body: items.join('\n'), items: itemResults }`. `splitListItems` (listWire.ts:17-22) trims every line and drops every blank line. The same file's own `bulletSuffix` / `examplePlaceholder` / `buildPromptGuidance` (lines 50-131) DO branch on `list<markdown>` and tell the agent its documents are boundary-framed multi-line bodies. envelope.ts:652-668 returns `result.body` verbatim; runner.ts:2244 does `normalizedContent.set(name, resolved.body)` for every non-pathish kind, and line 2305 writes that into `node_run_outputs`.
- **架构影响**：The kernel that owns 'what does a declared kind mean' has one branch of its behavior written for one caller family (path/string lists) and applied to all. The prompt half of the same handler says 'emit multi-line documents'; the validate half says 'one item per line'. The persisted value is therefore not the value the protocol asked the agent to produce, and every downstream reader (review multi-doc at review.ts:585, fanout shard split at scheduler.ts:7768) reads the mangled form. Concretely: paragraph breaks inside each document are dropped and every line is trimmed, so fenced code blocks and nested lists lose indentation while the boundary lines survive — document COUNT stays right, document CONTENT does not.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/shared/tests/rfc081-list-markdown-wire.test.ts locks the split/join codec round-trip in isolation, and packages/shared/tests/output-kinds-registry.test.ts exercises the list handler only with `list<string>` / `list<path<md>>` (lines 219-250). No test calls the list handler with a `list<markdown>` kind at all.
- **拟建守卫**：Move the codec choice INTO the handler (`ParametricOutputKindHandler.splitItems(parsed, raw)` defaulting to `splitListItems`, with the list handler delegating to its item handler), then add a registry-level property test: for every handler and every kind it `matches`, `validate(x).body` must round-trip through that handler's own declared codec. Plus a grep ratchet: `splitListItems(` / `splitMarkdownDocs(` may only appear inside packages/shared/src/listWire.ts and packages/shared/src/outputKinds/**.
- **证伪方式**：Call `getHandlerForParsedKind(parseKind('list<markdown>')).validate('# A\n\npara one\n\npara two\n<!-- @@aw-doc-boundary@@ -->\n# B', ctx, io)` and inspect `.body`. If it is byte-identical to the input, this finding is wrong.

### NK-02 · P2 · business-special-case · ADJUSTED

**`promotedSourceForWrapper` is an ad-hoc wrapper-kind if-chain with a silent null default, and mislabels unknown wrappers as `wrapper-git`**

- **锚点**：`packages/shared/src/workflowScope.ts:268-303` · `packages/shared/src/workflowScope.ts:329-349` · `packages/shared/src/workflowScope.ts:350-358` · `packages/backend/src/services/workflow.validator.ts:1166` · `packages/shared/tests/wrapper-kind-single-source.test.ts:33-55`
- **证据**：Inside the module that calls itself 'the single structural oracle', boundary promotion is: `if (wrapper.kind === 'wrapper-loop') { …outputBindings… } if (wrapper.kind === 'wrapper-fanout') { …boundary edges… } // wrapper-git deliberately exposes only its own generated git_diff … return null`. There is no `satisfies Record<WrapperKind, …>` and no `never` guard — a plain if-chain with an implicit default. Separately, two failure returns stamp a business identity as a placeholder: line 335 `wrapperKind: wrapper?.kind ?? 'wrapper-git'` and line 347 `wrapperKind: 'wrapper-git'` for the 'parent id is not a wrapper node' case. That field is rendered verbatim into user-facing diagnostics at workflow.validator.ts:1166/1913/2234 and scheduler.ts:10054.
- **架构影响**：WRAPPER_NODE_KINDS is a real single source for membership, but the SEMANTICS attached to each member are not table-driven. A fourth wrapper kind joins NODE_KIND and WRAPPER_NODE_KINDS, compiles everywhere, passes the wrapper-single-source test (which only checks the derived membership property), then fails closed with `wrapper-output-not-exposed` on every cross-boundary read — a silent capability hole rather than a compile error. The `'wrapper-git'` placeholder additionally makes the error message assert a wrapper type the graph does not contain, sending readers to the wrong node.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/shared/tests/wrapper-kind-single-source.test.ts:37-48 lists ten files it ratchets and workflowScope.ts is not among them; its regex (line 50) requires a three-way literal union on one construct, so a two-branch if-chain like this one is out of scope by design.
- **拟建守卫**：Convert to `const WRAPPER_BOUNDARY_PROMOTERS = { 'wrapper-git': …, 'wrapper-loop': …, 'wrapper-fanout': … } as const satisfies Record<(typeof WRAPPER_NODE_KINDS)[number], Promoter>` so a new wrapper kind fails to compile (wrapper-git's entry becomes an explicit `() => null` carrying its rationale). Make `wrapperKind` in the failure result `NodeKind | null` so 'unknown' is representable instead of borrowed from git.
- **证伪方式**：Add a fourth member to WRAPPER_NODE_KINDS and NODE_KIND locally and run `bun run typecheck`. If workflowScope.ts reports an error, the exhaustiveness is real and this finding is wrong; today only node-kind-behavior.ts, nodePorts.ts, workflow-node-references.ts, workflowNodeGeometry.ts, runLiveness.ts and the three frontend tables report.

### NK-03 · P2 · missing-guard · CONFIRMED

**The RFC-147 channel-port literal ratchet scans only backend+shared; the frontend already carries two live instances it was written to forbid**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc147-system-channel-ports.test.ts:219-252` · `packages/frontend/src/components/canvas/dropTarget.ts:126-135` · `packages/frontend/src/components/workflow-editor/ConnectionDialog.tsx:156-166` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc146-kind-predicate-guard.test.ts:19-23`
- **证据**：The ratchet's roots are hardcoded to two packages: `const roots = [['backend', resolve(import.meta.dir, '..', 'src')], ['shared', resolve(import.meta.dir, '..', '..', 'shared', 'src')]] as const` (lines 223-226), under a test title claiming '五端口字面量比较式全仓禁绝' — a repo-wide ban. The frontend is not scanned, and it contains `if (sourceHandle === '__clarify__') return null` (dropTarget.ts:133) and `if (sourceNode.kind === 'clarify-cross-agent' && fields.sourcePortName === 'to_designer')` (ConnectionDialog.tsx:160). Compare packages/backend/tests/rfc146-kind-predicate-guard.test.ts:19-23, which correctly walks all three roots.
- **架构影响**：The guard the repo relies on to keep channel-port semantics in one place is blind to a third of the codebase, and the blind spot is the surface where users author these edges. The two existing instances are the seed: each is a private restatement of registry knowledge that will not be updated when the registry changes, and the ratchet's green status actively signals that no such restatements exist.
- **现有守卫**：none for frontend — the ratchet is the guard and it does not look there.
