- **拟建守卫**：Add `['frontend', resolve(import.meta.dir, '..', '..', 'frontend', 'src')]` to the roots array and fix the two hits by importing the shared constants, or by routing through `isSystemChannelEdge` / the registry's `specFor`. Invariant unchanged; coverage becomes what the test already claims.
- **证伪方式**：`rg -n "[!=]== *'(__clarify__|__clarify_response__|__external_feedback__|to_designer|to_questioner)'" packages/frontend/src`. Zero hits means the frontend is clean today — it is not, there are two.

### NK-04 · P3 · stale-assertion · ADJUSTED

**`NODE_KIND_BEHAVIORS.isProcess` is a fake dimension — zero production consumers, and the table's own doc claims otherwise**

- **锚点**：`packages/shared/src/node-kind-behavior.ts:57-64` · `packages/shared/src/node-kind-behavior.ts:13-14` · `packages/shared/src/node-kind-behavior.ts:245-247` · `packages/backend/tests/node-kind-behavior-table.test.ts:125-139` · `packages/backend/tests/node-kind-behavior-table.test.ts:108-122` · `packages/backend/src/services/task.ts:5380` · `packages/backend/src/services/scheduler.ts:2428` · `packages/backend/src/services/stuckTaskDetector.ts:548`
- **证据**：The dimension's doc says: `Process kinds spawn real work … Consumed via isProcessNodeKind (schemas/workflow.ts — RFC-146 made it table-backed …) and by extension everywhere that predicate is used (validator, canvas, retry cascade agreement tests)`. Source disagrees: `rg -n "isProcessNodeKind" packages/` returns only node-kind-behavior.ts itself plus three TEST files (shared/tests/wrapper-fanout-schema.test.ts, backend/tests/node-kind-behavior-table.test.ts, backend/tests/scheduler-wrapper-fanout-routing.test.ts). `rg -n "\.isProcess\b"` outside tests returns exactly one hit — line 246, the predicate's own body. The validator does not call it; the canvas does not call it. The other three dimensions do have real consumers (retryCascade → task.ts:5380; settlesWithoutRow → scheduler.ts:2429 + stuckTaskDetector.ts:548; isAgent → ~10 sites).
- **架构影响**：This is exactly the failure mode RFC-146 wrote the table's admission rule to prevent, and for which it DELETED four dimensions ('a fake SSOT — the table said what should happen while kind-blind status-driven code did the real work'). A dimension nothing reads is a value that silently diverges from actual behavior; the next RFC that adds a kind fills in `isProcess` believing it is load-bearing, and the next RFC that needs the concept re-derives it from an or-chain because grep for the predicate shows only tests. The table's stated invariant no longer describes the table.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/node-kind-behavior-table.test.ts:80-89 asserts `isProcessNodeKind(k) === NODE_KIND_BEHAVIORS[k].isProcess`, i.e. it locks the predicate's agreement with the table, not that anything consumes it. Adding another consumerless dimension tomorrow turns nothing red; the same test file's key-set and dimension-name locks (lines 27-29, 125-139) would simply be updated alongside it.
- **拟建守卫**：A source-scan ratchet in packages/backend/tests: for every key of `NodeKindBehavior` and for every derived predicate exported from node-kind-behavior.ts, assert at least one reference exists under packages/{backend,frontend,shared}/src EXCLUDING node-kind-behavior.ts itself. Invariant: 'every dimension of the behavior table has at least one production reader.' Mechanically the same shape as the existing rfc146-kind-predicate-guard walker, which already enumerates the three src roots.
- **证伪方式**：`rg -n 'isProcessNodeKind|\.isProcess\b' packages/backend/src packages/frontend/src packages/shared/src`. If any hit outside packages/shared/src/node-kind-behavior.ts appears, this finding is wrong.

### NK-05 · P3 · duplicate-implementation · ADJUSTED

**`isInlineMarkdownItemKind` claims to be the single anti-drift predicate but is hand-inlined three more times inside the list handler**

- **锚点**：`packages/shared/src/reviewMultiDoc.ts:12-22` · `packages/shared/src/outputKinds/list.ts:50-53` · `packages/shared/src/outputKinds/list.ts:61-64` · `packages/shared/src/outputKinds/list.ts:96-98` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc103-fanout-kind-aware-split.test.ts:55-62`
- **证据**：reviewMultiDoc.ts:12-19 states the predicate exists so review and fanout 'can't drift; fanout was hand-rolling .split('\n') and shredding list<markdown> documents per line'. Inside outputKinds/list.ts the identical expression is written out three times: lines 51-52 `parsed.kind === 'list' && parsed.item.kind === 'base' && parsed.item.name === 'markdown'`, lines 62-63 the same, line 97 `k?.kind === 'list' && k.item.kind === 'base' && k.item.name === 'markdown'`. list.ts imports only from `../kindParser` and `../listWire` — importing reviewMultiDoc would create no cycle (reviewMultiDoc itself imports only kindParser).
- **架构影响**：A generic kernel (the list handler) hardcodes one item kind's identity instead of asking the item handler, and does so in a module whose sibling predicate was extracted specifically to stop that. Six copies of one rule across two modules is the pre-consolidation state the RFC-103 extraction was supposed to end; a future inline kind (e.g. `list<json>` with its own framing) must be added in six places, and the three inside list.ts are the ones a grep for the predicate name will not find.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/rfc103-fanout-kind-aware-split.test.ts:57-58 asserts only that scheduler.ts contains the literal text `isInlineMarkdownItemKind(itemKind)`. Nothing forbids a fourth inline copy, and nothing scans outputKinds/.
- **拟建守卫**：Grep ratchet over packages/{shared,backend,frontend}/src: the regex `\.item\.kind === 'base'[\s\S]{0,80}\.name === 'markdown'` may appear only in packages/shared/src/reviewMultiDoc.ts. Invariant: 'the inline-markdown item test has exactly one spelling.' Same shape as the existing rfc147 five-literal ratchet.
- **证伪方式**：`rg -n "item.name === 'markdown'" packages/shared/src`. If outputKinds/list.ts is absent from the hits, the duplication has been fixed.

### NK-06 · P3 · duplicate-implementation · ADJUSTED

**Two output-kind registries co-exist; the dead one is fully exported from the barrel and its documented type-level exhaustiveness is now vacuous**

- **锚点**：`packages/shared/src/outputKinds/index.ts:22-38` · `packages/shared/src/outputKinds/index.ts:31-34` · `packages/shared/src/schemas/review.ts:52-69` · `packages/shared/src/outputKinds/registry.ts:14-22` · `packages/shared/src/index.ts:70`
- **证据**：index.ts:28-37 reads: `const h = HANDLERS[kind]; if (!h) { // Defense-in-depth: AgentOutputKind is a string-literal union, so the type system already prevents missing entries. … }`. But schemas/review.ts:52-69 defines `AgentOutputKindSchema = z.string().refine(...).refine(...)` and `export type AgentOutputKind = z.infer<typeof AgentOutputKindSchema>` — i.e. `string`. So `Readonly<Record<AgentOutputKind, OutputKindHandler>>` is `Record<string, …>` and enforces nothing; the `!h` throw is the ONLY protection. Separately, `rg -n "getOutputKindHandler|groupPortsByKind|composePerKindRepairBlocks|markdownFileHandler" packages/ --glob '!**/*.test.*'` outside outputKinds/ returns zero production hits — the entire legacy Record, plus string.ts / markdown.ts / markdownFile.ts, is reachable only from its own unit tests, while remaining exported via index.ts and the shared barrel (packages/shared/src/index.ts:70 `export * from './outputKinds'`).
- **架构影响**：Any module in any package can `import { getOutputKindHandler } from '@agent-workflow/shared'` and get a three-kind registry that does not know `path<ext>`, `list<T>` or `signal` — a second source of truth for the kernel that decides port validation, prompt guidance and repair blocks. It looks like the registry (same directory, same barrel, same doc voice) and throws at runtime for every parametric kind the product actually uses. registry.ts:18-19 records the intent to delete it as a 'follow-up' that has not happened.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/shared/tests/output-kinds-handler-interface.test.ts, compose-per-kind-repair-blocks.test.ts, build-protocol-block-via-handlers.test.ts and envelope-followup-port-validation.test.ts all still import the legacy helpers, which keeps them alive and would keep them green if a production caller started using them tomorrow.
- **拟建守卫**：Delete the legacy Record and markdownFile.ts / string.ts / markdown.ts, migrating those tests onto PARAMETRIC_HANDLERS. If deletion must wait, add an import ratchet: `getOutputKindHandler|groupPortsByKind|composePerKindRepairBlocks` may not appear under packages/{backend,frontend}/src nor anywhere in packages/shared/src except outputKinds/index.ts. Also replace the stale comment with a test asserting `AgentOutputKindSchema` is a widened string schema, so nobody re-derives 'the type system prevents missing entries'.
- **证伪方式**：`rg -n 'getOutputKindHandler' packages --glob '!**/*.test.*'` — a hit outside packages/shared/src/outputKinds/index.ts falsifies 'zero production consumers'. For the type claim: typecheck a line `const x: AgentOutputKind = 'anything-at-all'` — it compiles.

### NK-07 · P3 · escape-hatch · ADJUSTED

**`shardingRegistry` is a mutable module-global with an exported writer, so any caller can rewrite persisted shard identity at runtime**

- **锚点**：`packages/shared/src/shardingRegistry.ts:32` · `packages/shared/src/shardingRegistry.ts:45-47` · `packages/shared/src/shardingRegistry.ts:66-73` · `packages/shared/src/index.ts:102` · `packages/backend/src/services/scheduler.ts:7803-7817` · `packages/shared/src/outputKinds/index.ts:9-13`
- **证据**：`const REGISTRY = new Map<string, KeyOfFn>()` (line 32) with `export function registerKeyOf(itemKind, keyOf) { REGISTRY.set(stringifyKind(itemKind), keyOf) }` (45-47) and `export function clearShardingRegistry(): void { REGISTRY.clear(); installDefaults() }` (70-73). The only stated protection is the comment on line 68: 'Tests use this to avoid cross-test pollution; production code should never call it.' packages/shared/src/index.ts:102 re-exports the whole module, so both writers are public API of `@agent-workflow/shared`. The value they control is consumed at scheduler.ts:7805-7817, where `keyOf(value, idx, itemKind)` produces the `shardKey` persisted on `node_runs` and later used for shard reuse, aggregator find-by-shardKey, and resume dedup.
- **架构影响**：A shared kernel that owns a PERSISTED identity exposes an unguarded mutation hook. A single `registerKeyOf` call from a feature module (or a stray import in the compiled single binary) silently changes how shards are keyed for every task in the process — new rows stop matching the shardKeys already on disk, so resume reuse, the `#idx` collision disambiguation at scheduler.ts:7814 and aggregator lookup all mis-bind. This is the 'escape hatch that lets one caller bypass the kernel's invariant' shape, with no compile-time or test-time cost to using it.
- **现有守卫**：none — the only test touching these functions is packages/shared/tests/sharding-registry.test.ts, which exercises registerKeyOf/clearShardingRegistry as supported behaviour. No lint rule, dep rule or grep ratchet restricts callers.
- **拟建守卫**：Either make the table a frozen const (`const REGISTRY: ReadonlyMap<string, KeyOfFn>` built once from a literal, with tests reading `resolveKeyOf` only), or keep the mutators but add a source ratchet: `registerKeyOf(` / `clearShardingRegistry(` may appear only under packages/*/tests/**. Invariant: 'shard-key derivation is fixed at build time.' Same shape as the existing rfc284-microhelpers unique-idiom locks.
- **证伪方式**：`rg -n 'registerKeyOf|clearShardingRegistry' packages`. If every hit is under tests/ AND the functions are not re-exported from packages/shared/src/index.ts, the escape hatch is closed.

### NK-08 · P3 · boundary-blur · ADJUSTED

**Clarify node-side channel ports `questions` / `answers` are outside SYSTEM_CHANNEL_PORTS, outside the literal ratchet, and the registry↔declaredPorts drift lock only runs in one direction**

- **锚点**：`packages/shared/src/nodePorts.ts:271-286` · `packages/shared/src/schemas/workflow.ts:682-683` · `packages/shared/src/schemas/workflow.ts:755` · `packages/shared/src/systemChannelPorts.ts:61-87` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc147-system-channel-ports.test.ts:159-184` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc147-system-channel-ports.test.ts:219-252`
- **证据**：nodePorts.ts:275-276 declares the clarify node's ports as raw literals — `systemInputs: [{ name: 'questions' }], systemOutputs: [{ name: 'answers' }]` — in the same file that imports CLARIFY_SOURCE_PORT_NAME, CLARIFY_RESPONSE_TARGET_PORT_NAME and the two cross-clarify constants (nodePorts.ts:32-40). A constant for the first already exists and is unused here: schemas/workflow.ts:755 `export const CROSS_CLARIFY_INPUT_PORT_NAME = 'questions' as const`. `answers` has no constant at all. The validator spells both raw about twelve times (workflow.validator.ts:2362, 2367, 2369, 2408, 2436, 2438, 2475, 2490, 2492, 2716, 2761, 3460). SYSTEM_CHANNEL_PORTS (systemChannelPorts.ts:61-87) contains exactly five keys, neither of them. The drift lock at rfc147-system-channel-ports.test.ts:167 iterates `Object.keys(SYSTEM_CHANNEL_PORTS)` and checks each is declared in nodePorts — never the reverse.
- **架构影响**：The registry's header claims it is 'the single source for which port names are framework channel ports'. Half the clarify channel — the node-side half — lives outside it as free-floating literals, so the two halves of one channel are governed by different mechanisms and can drift independently, which is precisely the drift the registry was built after. The one-directional lock means any future system port added only to declaredPorts inherits the same unguarded status by default.
- **现有守卫**：none for these two names. packages/backend/tests/rfc147-system-channel-ports.test.ts:219-252 ratchets literal comparisons for the five agent-side names ONLY (its regex at line 228-229 enumerates `__clarify__|__clarify_response__|__external_feedback__|to_designer|to_questioner`), and the registry↔declaredPorts lock at lines 159-184 walks registry→declaredPorts only.
- **拟建守卫**：(a) Add `questions` / `answers` as registry rows, or promote them to exported constants and extend the ratchet regex to cover them; (b) invert the drift lock as well: for every port appearing in any kind's `systemInputs`/`systemOutputs` from PORT_DERIVERS, assert a SYSTEM_CHANNEL_PORTS row exists — invariant 'declaredPorts system groups are a subset of registry keys', the missing direction that would have caught this at write time.
- **证伪方式**：`rg -n "'questions'|'answers'" packages/shared/src/nodePorts.ts packages/backend/src/services/workflow.validator.ts`. If the literals are gone (replaced by constants) or the names now appear in SYSTEM_CHANNEL_PORTS, this is fixed.

### NK-09 · P3 · missing-guard · ADJUSTED

**The runtime-builtin token switch has no exhaustiveness over its own registry: a new `agent-prompt` builtin silently falls through to the inbound-port lookup**

- **锚点**：`packages/shared/src/prompt.ts:562-608` · `packages/shared/src/prompt.ts:610-621` · `packages/shared/src/prompt.ts:425` · `packages/shared/src/prompt.ts:488-489` · `packages/shared/src/runtimeBuiltins.ts:54-160` · `packages/shared/tests/rfc295-runtime-parameter-authority.test.ts:50-64`
- **证据**：`BUILTIN_VARS` is derived from the registry (prompt.ts:425 `export const BUILTIN_VARS: ReadonlySet<string> = AGENT_PROMPT_BUILTIN_NAMES`). Rendering is `if (BUILTIN_VARS.has(name)) { switch (name) { case '__repo_path__': … case '__repo_group__': … } }` with NO `default` and no `never` guard (lines 562-607). Control then reaches 608-621: the DEPRECATED check, then `const v = input.inputs[name]; return fence(name, v)`. So a registry token with no case renders whatever an inbound port of the same name carries. The comment immediately above (610-616) documents this exact hazard for retired tokens: 'they must NOT fall through to the input lookup: a saved workflow with an inbound port that happens to share the retired name … would otherwise render upstream content where years of prompts had an empty string (impl-gate re-review high)'.
- **架构影响**：The registry (RUNTIME_BUILTIN_PARAMETERS) is the stated authority for discoverable runtime parameters, and the validator, the frontend picker (runtime-parameters/catalog.ts:142) and the workgroup producer all project from it. The renderer alone is not a projection — it is a hand-written switch that happens to cover the current set. Adding a parameter is a one-line table edit that typechecks, passes the authority test, appears in the UI picker, passes prompt-template validation, and then renders an unrelated port's content into the agent prompt.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/shared/tests/rfc295-runtime-parameter-authority.test.ts:54-56 asserts only set equality between BUILTIN_VARS / AGENT_PROMPT_BUILTIN_NAMES / CALL_WORKGROUP_BUILTIN_NAMES and the registry projections. Both sides derive from the same table, so the assertions hold by construction and say nothing about renderer coverage.
- **拟建守卫**：Either give the switch a `default: { const _x: never = name as never }` after narrowing `name` to `RuntimeBuiltinName` (compile-time), or add a runtime property test: for every name in `runtimeBuiltinParametersFor('agent-prompt')`, `renderUserPrompt` on a template `{{name}}` with an inbound port of the SAME name carrying a sentinel must not emit the sentinel. The second form also re-locks the RFC-148 hazard the adjacent comment describes.
- **证伪方式**：Add a token to RUNTIME_BUILTIN_PARAMETERS with `semantics['agent-prompt']` and no case in prompt.ts, then run `bun run typecheck && bun run --filter @agent-workflow/shared test`. If anything goes red, this finding is wrong.

### NK-10 · P3 · duplicate-implementation · ADJUSTED

**`privilegedNodeAccessOf` re-derives the privileged-kind→permission mapping as an if-chain shadowing the table declared two lines above it**

- **锚点**：`packages/frontend/src/hooks/usePrivilegedNodes.ts:31-35` · `packages/frontend/src/hooks/usePrivilegedNodes.ts:60-65` · `packages/frontend/src/hooks/usePrivilegedNodes.ts:66-78` · `packages/frontend/src/hooks/usePrivilegedNodes.ts:9-12` · `packages/frontend/tests/rfc270-palette-permission.test.tsx:36-96`
- **证据**：Lines 32-35 declare the table: `export const PRIVILEGED_NODE_KINDS = { script: 'scripts:author', 'code-host-call': 'code-host-calls:author' } as const satisfies Partial<Record<NodeKind, string>>`. Lines 60-64 then re-enumerate the same membership by hand: `const grantOf = (kind: string): boolean | null => { if (kind === 'script') return grants.canAuthorScripts; if (kind === 'code-host-call') return grants.canAuthorCodeHost; return null }`, and line 65 `const isProtectedKind = (kind) => grantOf(kind) === false` is what drives palette greying, Inspector placeholders and canvas drag/delete protection (via `protectedNodeIds`, lines 69-71). The table itself is used only for the display string at line 74. `Partial<Record<…>>` means the table is not exhaustive either, by design.
- **架构影响**：A permission surface where the registry and the decision function are two independent lists. Adding a third privileged kind to PRIVILEGED_NODE_KINDS compiles, shows a 'requires permission' label in the palette, and `grantOf` returns `null` for it, so `isProtectedKind` is false and the node stays draggable, deletable and fully visible for a user without the grant. The file's own header states the module exists because the three consumers 'must use the SAME criterion, otherwise you drift into half-open combinations like: can't drag it out of the palette but can delete it on the canvas' — exactly the state a third kind would produce.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/frontend/tests/rfc270-palette-permission.test.tsx is the only file referencing these symbols and it exercises the two existing kinds; adding a third table entry turns nothing red.
- **拟建守卫**：Key `PRIVILEGED_NODE_KINDS` to a `keyof PrivilegedNodeGrants` alongside the permission string so one object drives both, making a new row name its grant field at compile time. Then add a test iterating `Object.keys(PRIVILEGED_NODE_KINDS)` asserting each kind is `isProtectedKind` when its grant is false — invariant 'every declared privileged kind is actually protected'.
- **证伪方式**：Add `'call-workflow': 'workflows:author'` to PRIVILEGED_NODE_KINDS and run the frontend suite. If nothing fails and `isProtectedKind('call-workflow')` returns false with all grants false, the finding holds.

### NK-11 · P3 · stale-assertion · CONFIRMED

**`code-round` still occupies a full row in the behavior table describing an execution chain RFC-310 deleted**

- **锚点**：`packages/shared/src/node-kind-behavior.ts:203-221` · `packages/backend/src/services/scheduler.ts:5664-5673` · `packages/backend/src/services/runLiveness.ts:127-136` · `packages/backend/tests/node-kind-behavior-table.test.ts:31-78`
- **证据**：node-kind-behavior.ts:203-206 asserts in the present tense: 'RFC-304 — the single synthesized node of a code-capability round. It drives a whole stage sequence (program / script / ai / invoke steps) inside one node_run, so it is process-bearing and cascades on retry exactly like script and code-host-call', and lines 211-215 justify `isAgent: false` by 'individual kind:"ai" stages inside it DO spawn agent runs'. scheduler.ts:5667-5672 says the opposite: `// RFC-310 PR-10 T104 — code-round 执行链已删除` returning `{ kind: 'failed', summary: 'code-round execution was retired by RFC-310; use development missions', message: 'code-round-retired' }`. runLiveness.ts:127-136 likewise still reasons about 'most of a round's wall-clock is spent in kind:"program" stages … platform code running in-process'.
- **架构影响**：The kernel meant to be the authoritative description of per-kind behavior documents a mechanism that no longer exists, in three places, in the voice of current fact. `retryCascade: 'mint-placeholder'` on a kind that can only fail means a retry cascade mints placeholder rows for a node guaranteed to fail — harmless today only because the kind is synthesized-only, but the row is an active instruction, not a comment. A reader deciding how to model a new synthesized kind will copy a pattern that was withdrawn.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/node-kind-behavior-table.test.ts:31-78 value-locks only nine of the fourteen kinds (agent-single, three wrappers, review, the clarify pair, input, output); call-workflow, call-workgroup, script, code-host-call and code-round have no per-row value assertion. The structural test at lines 108-122 would catch a NEW process kind, but nothing re-examines a retired one.
- **拟建守卫**：Either remove `code-round` from NODE_KIND with its migration story, or add an explicit `retired: true` dimension with a real consumer — and extend the value-lock loop to iterate ALL of NODE_KIND rather than a hand-picked nine, so every row's intent is pinned and a retirement forces the row to be revisited.
- **证伪方式**：`grep -n "code-round" packages/backend/src/services/scheduler.ts` — if a real dispatch branch (not the `code-round-retired` failure) exists, the table's description is current and this finding is wrong.

### NK-12 · P3 · boundary-blur · CONFIRMED

**`git_diff` — a framework-minted port name — is a bare literal in three packages while its sibling `__done__` is an exported constant**

- **锚点**：`packages/shared/src/nodePorts.ts:235-238` · `packages/backend/src/services/scheduler.ts:9814` · `packages/frontend/src/lib/workflow-starters.ts:284-288` · `packages/shared/src/wrapperFanout.ts:169-175` · `packages/shared/tests/rfc199-git-diff-port-contract.test.ts:4-21`
- **证据**：nodePorts.ts:237 declares `dataOutputs: [{ name: 'git_diff', kind: 'list<path<*>>' }]`. scheduler.ts:9814 writes it back independently: `await upsertWrapperOutput(db, wrapperRunId, 'git_diff', paths.join('\n'))`. workflow-starters.ts:286 wires an edge on `portName: 'git_diff'`. Three literals, no constant. Contrast wrapperFanout.ts:169-175, whose comment explicitly says the sibling port got a const 'so call sites in other PRs can grep / pattern-match against the canonical spelling' — and scheduler.ts:8059 plus WrapperNodes.tsx:229/285 all import `FANOUT_DONE_PORT_NAME`.
- **架构影响**：The two ports the framework itself mints are governed by two different disciplines inside the same wrapper family. The declaration and the producer are coupled only by a string typed twice in two packages, so a rename in the registry leaves the scheduler writing to a port no consumer declares — the wrapper completes and its output row is unreachable, with no type error.
- **现有守卫**：partial, one side only — packages/shared/tests/rfc199-git-diff-port-contract.test.ts:12-20 value-locks the DECLARED side (`expect(port).toEqual({ name: 'git_diff', kind: 'list<path<*>>' })`). Nothing ties scheduler.ts:9814 or workflow-starters.ts:286 to that name; a rename on the producer side is caught only indirectly by wrapper-git integration tests, and I did not enumerate those exhaustively.
- **拟建守卫**：Export `GIT_DIFF_PORT_NAME = 'git_diff'` next to `FANOUT_DONE_PORT_NAME` and add the same-shaped grep ratchet used for the five channel ports: the literal `'git_diff'` may appear only in its defining module. Invariant: 'every framework-minted port name has exactly one spelling.'
- **证伪方式**：`rg -n "'git_diff'" packages --glob '!**/*.test.*'` — currently three hits across three packages; one hit means it is fixed.

### NK-13 · P3 · stale-assertion · CONFIRMED

**docs/dev-gotchas.md's NodeKind checklist names a file that no longer contains a compile-time exhaustiveness point**

- **锚点**：`docs/dev-gotchas.md:624` · `packages/frontend/src/components/canvas/wrapperFit.ts:1-30` · `packages/shared/src/workflowNodeGeometry.ts:50-67` · `packages/backend/src/services/runLiveness.ts:148-151`
- **证据**：dev-gotchas.md:624 enumerates the eight compile-time exhaustiveness points and ends with `frontend/canvas/wrapperFit.ts`. That file today opens `// RFC-302 — wrapper geometry is canonical in @agent-workflow/shared. This frontend adapter owns only chrome-derived minimum sizes …` and is a 107-line re-export shim; `grep -n "NodeKind\|satisfies\|never" packages/frontend/src/components/canvas/wrapperFit.ts` returns nothing. The guard moved to packages/shared/src/workflowNodeGeometry.ts:50 `export const DEFAULT_NODE_SIZE_BY_KIND: Record<NodeKind, WorkflowPlacementSize> = { … }` (still genuinely exhaustive — the annotated Record makes a missing key an error). I verified the other seven still hold, including runLiveness.ts:148-152's real `const exhaustive: never = kind`.
- **架构影响**：The checklist is the artefact contributors follow when extending NODE_KIND, and this repo's own docs-node-kind-coverage.test.ts:22-23 states the governing principle: 'a doc that claims completeness and is not complete is worse than one that claims nothing, because readers stop looking.' The list is right about the count but wrong about one location; a contributor who greps the named file and finds nothing will conclude either that the list rotted (and stop trusting the other seven) or that the guard was removed.
- **现有守卫**：none — docs-node-kind-coverage.test.ts guards docs/workflow-yaml.md's per-kind sections and its stale-count claim, and rfc234-intent-doc.test.ts guards intentDoc, but nothing asserts that the eight files named in dev-gotchas.md:624 still contain an exhaustiveness construct.
- **拟建守卫**：A test that reads dev-gotchas.md:624, extracts the file paths, and asserts each contains one of `satisfies Record<NodeKind`, `: Record<NodeKind`, or `: never = kind`. Invariant: 'the exhaustiveness checklist names files that actually carry the guard.' Cheap, and it fails the day a guard is relocated rather than months later.
- **证伪方式**：`grep -n "NodeKind" packages/frontend/src/components/canvas/wrapperFit.ts` — a `satisfies Record<NodeKind, …>` there falsifies this.

### NK-14 · P3 · stale-assertion · CONFIRMED

**Shared output-kind kernels still carry 'PR-A scope: not yet wired / PR-D will wire it' notes for code that has been in production for months**

- **锚点**：`packages/shared/src/shardingRegistry.ts:23-25` · `packages/shared/src/outputKinds/list.ts:21-22` · `packages/shared/src/outputKinds/signal.ts:17-20` · `packages/shared/src/outputKinds/registry.ts:6-22` · `packages/shared/src/kindParser.ts:175-179`
- **证据**：shardingRegistry.ts:23-24: 'PR-A scope: registry + resolveKeyOf. Not yet wired into scheduler (PR-D)' — it is wired, scheduler.ts:7805. list.ts:21-22: 'PR-A scope: registered into PARAMETRIC_HANDLERS as the new ListHandler; not yet called by runtime. PR-D wires it into envelope + scheduler' — envelope.ts:652 calls it on every declared port. signal.ts:17-20: 'AgentOutputKind schema does NOT yet whitelist "signal" as a base name (PR-B adds it). In PR-A the only way to reach this handler is via direct test or via the wrapper-fanout __done__ auto-mint path' — kindParser.ts:175-179 REGISTERED_BASE_KINDS already contains 'signal'. registry.ts:6-12 says 'PR-A 阶段：这是一个 sibling 注册表 … 不接现有 runtime', immediately followed at lines 14-19 by a correction stating the opposite; the superseded paragraph was left in place above it.
- **架构影响**：Four of this family's kernels open with a scope note that misstates whether they are live. The concrete cost is auditability: the repo's rule is that assertions about behavior must be re-verified against source, and these are the first thing a reader sees. 'Not yet called by runtime' is also load-bearing in a specific way — it is exactly the sentence that would let a reviewer wave through a change to list.ts's validate (see F2) as test-only.
- **现有守卫**：none — comment freshness is unguarded by construction here; no test reads these headers.
- **拟建守卫**：Delete the superseded paragraphs. The generalizable move is the one docs-node-kind-coverage.test.ts already applies to prose claiming completeness: when a module header asserts 'X has no runtime callers', back it with the import ratchet that makes it true (F4's proposed guard), so the header becomes checkable rather than aspirational.
- **证伪方式**：For each: `rg -n 'resolveKeyOf|getHandlerForParsedKind' packages/backend/src` and `rg -n "'signal'" packages/shared/src/kindParser.ts`. Production hits falsify the 'not yet wired' claims.

## TP — Transport / composition root / platform 公共层（14 条：P1 0 / P2 6 / P3 8）

### TP-01 · P2 · missing-guard · ADJUSTED

**Contract-coverage scanner is blind to ~40 computed-path endpoints, and its own "blind spots are frozen" meta-guard cannot see them either**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:139-232` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:284-285` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:364-365` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:413-414` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:657-658` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:725-726` · `packages/backend/tests/api-contract-coverage.test.ts:33-34` · `packages/backend/tests/api-contract-coverage.test.ts:77-79` · `packages/backend/tests/api-contract-coverage.test.ts:129-160` · `packages/backend/tests/api-contract-coverage.test.ts:215-247` · `packages/backend/src/routes/digitalEmployees.ts:382-386` · `packages/backend/src/server.ts:408`
- **证据**：REGISTER_ROUTE_RE requires `path: '<literal>'`; developmentConfig.ts registers its 6-route family with `path: cfg.base` / `path: `${cfg.base}/:id/publish`` and mounts it 5 times (bases at :285 action-templates, :365 verification-profiles, :414 digital-employees, :658 automation-policies, :726 development-adapters), plus mountAclEndpoints with a computed base at :231. I re-ran the test's own two regexes over src/routes: developmentConfig.ts has 15 registerRoute calls, 9 discovered, 0 flagged as blind spots; digitalEmployees.ts 32 calls, 31 discovered, 0 flagged. That is 5x6 generic + 5x2 ACL + 1 = ~41 real endpoints invisible. Cross-check: tests/contracts/registry.ts holds 421 entries and contains no '/api/code/action-templates', '/api/code/verification-profiles', '/api/integrations/development-adapters' path at all (only :208 POST /api/code/automation-policies/preview-decision, a literal route). The blind-spot detector `reReg` fails on these too because its `[^}]*?` cannot cross the `}` inside `${cfg.base}`, so the meta-test at :235-247 asserts `discoverNonLiteralMounts()` equals exactly the 2 resourceAcl.ts entries and passes — reporting "all blind spots known" while 41 endpoints are unseen. The test header itself names this failure mode: "A guard that cannot see a route reports 'all registered' — the failure mode is silent completeness."
- **架构影响**：The parametrised mountConfigResource family is exactly the shape RFC-294 wants (one registry, five instantiations) — but the repo's contract guard is a text scanner tuned to the old hand-written shape, so moving TOWARD the target architecture silently removes contract coverage. Authorization is still fine (these routes do go through registerRoute, so assertRouteMetaCoverage sees them), but 401/shape/wire coverage in tests/contracts/registry.ts is not required for them, and each new parametrised family widens the hole with a green gate.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/api-contract-coverage.test.ts:235-247 is the guard that was supposed to catch this and is itself blind to the pattern (verified by re-running its regexes).
- **拟建守卫**：Replace the regex scanner with a runtime oracle: build the app (createApp with an in-memory db, as rfc305-architecture-lock.test.ts:440-447 already does), then assert `allRouteMeta()` (routes/registry.ts:107) is a superset-free bijection onto tests/contracts/registry.ts ENDPOINTS, with an explicit shrink-only exception list. Invariant: every route the framework actually mounted has a contract entry — impossible to evade with a computed path, a helper, or a file outside src/routes/.
- **证伪方式**：Run `bun test packages/backend/tests/api-contract-coverage.test.ts` (green today), then grep tests/contracts/registry.ts for '/api/code/action-templates' (absent) while grepping routes/developmentConfig.ts:143,157,177,192,207,221 (six registered routes, instantiated five times at :285,:365,:414,:658,:726). If the guard were doing its job, either the entries would exist or the test would be red.

### TP-02 · P2 · escape-hatch · ADJUSTED

**Route-gate startup self-check has two unratcheted exemption holes: EXEMPT_MOUNTS and the blanket method='ALL' filter**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/routes/registry.ts:291-301` · `packages/backend/src/routes/registry.ts:318-331` · `packages/backend/src/routes/registry.ts:334-356` · `packages/backend/src/mcp/server.ts:211` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc247-route-coverage.test.ts:81-90`
- **证据**：routeMetaCoverage filters mounted routes with `.filter((r) => r.method.toUpperCase() !== 'ALL')` and `.filter((r) => !EXEMPT_MOUNTS.has(r.path))` before checking for undeclared routes. The ALL filter is justified as "middleware is not an endpoint", but mcp/server.ts:211 `app.all('/api/mcp', handler)` proves an ALL mount can be a real, request-handling endpoint — the authors knew this and belt-and-braced it into EXEMPT_MOUNTS (registry.ts:322-330). Any future `app.all('/api/x', handler)` or `app.use('/api/x/*', gate)` therefore runs without a RouteMeta declaration and `assertRouteMetaCoverage` will not refuse to boot. Separately, EXEMPT_MOUNTS is a plain module-private Set with no test: `rg TOKEN/EXEMPT_MOUNTS packages/backend` returns source hits only (registry.ts:296,299,318,343 and a comment in mcp/server.ts:164) and zero test files.
- **架构影响**：The whole value proposition of RFC-247 is 'a route physically cannot exist without a declaration, and the daemon refuses to boot otherwise' (registry.ts:18-20,256-260). Both filters convert that from an invariant into a convention: silencing the boot check needs one line in a Set, or simply mounting with a verb the check ignores. This is the one place in this family where a hole is directly security-relevant.
- **现有守卫**：none for either hole. packages/backend/tests/route-gate-single-source.test.ts:39-48 bans 7 retired identifier NAMES only; packages/backend/tests/rfc247-route-registry.test.ts:63-266 tests declaration validation and gate semantics but never EXEMPT_MOUNTS or ALL mounts.
- **拟建守卫**：Two assertions in one test: (1) freeze EXEMPT_MOUNTS by exporting it and asserting `expect([...EXEMPT_MOUNTS].sort()).toEqual(['*','/api/mcp'])` — growing it becomes a deliberate, reviewed edit; (2) after createApp, assert every mounted route whose path starts with '/api/' and whose method is 'ALL' is in EXEMPT_MOUNTS, i.e. drop the blanket ALL filter for /api/* and let middleware be distinguished by path prefix wildcard instead of by verb.
- **证伪方式**：Add `app.all('/api/probe', (c) => c.json({}))` to server.ts and run any backend test that builds an app: it boots green and answers, with no RouteMeta and no gate. Then add a path to EXEMPT_MOUNTS and run `bun run test:backend` — nothing turns red.

### TP-03 · P2 · boundary-blur · ADJUSTED

**WS transport commons reads another bounded context's private table with hand-written raw SQL, and no dep rule covers ws/ -> db at all**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/ws/registry.ts:1154-1187` · `packages/backend/src/ws/registry.ts:54` · `packages/backend/src/ws/registry.ts:57-64` · `packages/backend/src/ws/registry.ts:396-452` · `packages/backend/src/ws/registry.ts:935-947` · `packages/backend/src/modules/identity-access/infrastructure/sqliteUserAccessRepository.ts:45-73` · `.dependency-cruiser.cjs:87-95` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:18`
- **证据**：authorityRevisionCurrent does `db.$client.query('SELECT status, access_revision FROM users WHERE id = ? LIMIT 1').get(...)` from inside the generic sendJson path. Cross-checked: this is the only reference to `access_revision` outside packages/backend/src/modules/identity-access/infrastructure/ and db/schema.ts — i.e. the transport hand-rolls a read the identity-access module already owns (SQLiteUserAccessRepository / ResolveAuthority, modules/identity-access/composition.ts:59-62). The same file also imports 6 domain tables directly (`memories, nodeRunEvents, nodeRuns, tasks, workflows, workgroups` from '@/db/schema') and runs Drizzle selects against them at :396-404, :416-433, :435-452, :536-547, :809-813. The `no-routes-to-db` rule in .dependency-cruiser.cjs:87-95 is scoped `from: ^packages/backend/src/routes/` — ws/ and mcp/ are not covered by any rule in the forbidden list (lines 41-148).
- **架构影响**：RFC-294's direction is transport -> application command/query -> domain, with cross-module access only through exact public contracts. The WS kernel is a transport that goes straight to Drizzle for six domains and to raw SQL for a seventh module's storage. The raw string is worse than the Drizzle calls: a column rename in identity-access typechecks fine here and fails at runtime in the authorization fence, and the RFC-294 preflight AST scanner cannot see it because ws/ is outside MODULES_ROOT and a SQL string is not an import edge.
- **现有守卫**：none — no dep-cruiser rule matches `from: ws/`, and packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:18 scopes the AST ratchet to packages/backend/src/modules/.
- **拟建守卫**：Extend the dep rule family with `no-transport-to-db` covering `^packages/backend/src/(ws|mcp)/` -> `^packages/backend/src/db/` (dependencyTypesNot: type-only), ledgering today's single ws/registry.ts edge in scripts/depcheck.ts KNOWN_VIOLATIONS with a removeWhen; plus a grep ratchet asserting `.$client.query(` appears in zero files under src/ws and src/routes (today: zero in routes, one in ws).
- **证伪方式**：`rg '\.\$client' packages/backend/src` -> 9 hits, exactly one under ws/ (registry.ts:1166). `rg 'access_revision' packages/backend/src` -> only db/schema.ts, modules/identity-access/infrastructure/*, and ws/registry.ts:1156,1167,1180. Add `import { agents } from '@/db/schema'` plus a select to ws/registry.ts and run `bun run depcheck`: green.

### TP-04 · P2 · boundary-blur · CONFIRMED

**mountApiRoutes is both the route table and the assembler, is invoked twice per process, and silently rebinds a process-wide participant**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/server.ts:305-308` · `packages/backend/src/server.ts:397-402` · `packages/backend/src/server.ts:457-545` · `packages/backend/src/server.ts:524-539` · `packages/backend/src/mcp/dispatch.ts:68-85` · `packages/backend/src/mcp/server.ts:149-159` · `packages/backend/src/modules/integration/composition.ts:16-31` · `packages/backend/src/cli/start.ts:747-754` · `packages/backend/src/cli/start.ts:809` · `packages/backend/src/services/webhook/webhookDispatch.ts:607-615`
- **证据**：mountApiRoutes(app, deps) composes createEmployeeInputArtifactStore, buildDevelopmentDeliveryDeps, composeApprovalGatewayRunner, composeDevelopmentEmployeeWorkspace, composeExecutionContract, composeDigitalEmployee, composeTaskCatalog before mounting anything (server.ts:458-545). createDispatcher calls the same function on a second Hono app (dispatch.ts:82), built lazily on the FIRST MCP request (mcp/server.ts:155-159). So a daemon that receives one MCP call holds two independent compositions of those modules. In between, server.ts:524-536 calls `deps.digitalEmployeeWorkStart.bind({...})` capturing THAT composition's runtime — and bind is `bound = participant` with no once-guard (integration/composition.ts:28-31). cli/start.ts:747 creates the deferred and :754 hands `.participant` to the webhook dispatcher, so after the first MCP request every webhook/event-driven work start routes through the MCP dispatcher's private digital-employee runtime instead of the REST one. It also throws hard on a business condition in the app factory: `if (digitalEmployee.runtime === null) throw new Error('task catalog requires the digital employee runtime')` (server.ts:537-539).
- **架构影响**：RFC-294 makes bootstrap the only assembler and the app factory a pure transport adapter. Here the transport adapter IS the assembler, so 'compose once' is an accident of call count rather than a property. All the stores involved are SQLite-backed so I could not prove data divergence today — but the silent last-write-wins rebind means the identity of the runtime executing platform work start depends on whether an MCP request has ever arrived, which nothing states, tests, or logs.
- **现有守卫**：none — no test asserts mountApiRoutes is called once, that it performs no composition, or that DeferredDigitalEmployeeWorkStart.bind is called at most once.
- **拟建守卫**：Two mechanisms: (1) make bind idempotent-or-loud — `if (bound !== null) throw new Error('work-start participant already bound')` in integration/composition.ts, which turns today's silent rebind into a red test immediately; (2) an AST ratchet asserting src/server.ts's mountApiRoutes body contains zero `compose*(`/`create*Store(` calls, with the composition lifted into createApp (or cli/start.ts) and passed to mountApiRoutes as an already-built contract object, the way mountTaskCatalogRoutes/mountEventCenterRoutes/mountDigitalEmployeeRoutes already receive theirs (server.ts:574,582,584).
- **证伪方式**：Add a counter to createDeferredDigitalEmployeeWorkStart.bind and drive one REST request plus one POST /api/mcp against a built app: bind fires twice, with two different closure targets. Or add `console.count` to composeDigitalEmployee and observe two compositions per process.

### TP-05 · P2 · business-special-case · ADJUSTED

**A permission point named for one employee type ('development-missions:*') gates type-agnostic digital-employee routes**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/routes/digitalEmployees.ts:51-59` · `packages/backend/src/routes/digitalEmployees.ts:98-106` · `packages/backend/src/routes/digitalEmployees.ts:152-158` · `packages/backend/src/routes/digitalEmployees.ts:171-177` · `packages/shared/src/schemas/permission.ts:100-228` · `packages/backend/src/server.ts:478-484` · `packages/backend/src/routes/registry.ts:283-311` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc305-architecture-lock.test.ts:439-457`
- **证据**：`POST /api/digital-employee-input-uploads` and `DELETE /api/digital-employee-input-uploads/:uploadRef` — routes on the generic digital-employee module, mounted from a module contract (mountDigitalEmployeeRoutes(app, digitalEmployee), server.ts:584) — declare `permissions: ['development-missions:launch']`. Same for work launch (:157) and interaction (:176,:199). The module itself is type-parametric: composeDigitalEmployee takes `typePackages: [developmentEmployeeTypePackage]` as a list (server.ts:481), and routes/digitalEmployees.ts:113-123 lists employee TYPES generically. So the authorization vocabulary consumed by the generic route gate hard-codes the identity of the one employee type that exists today.
- **架构影响**：The route gate's whole point is that the declaration is the contract. When the point name encodes a business identity that the route is deliberately agnostic to, adding a second employee type forces either (a) reusing a mis-named point, so granting 'development' authority silently grants the new type, or (b) minting parallel points and editing every generic route — the classic per-domain special case smuggled into a shared kernel, here through naming rather than through an if.
- **现有守卫**：none — routes/registry.ts:287-311 checks only that every ROUTE_BACKED_POINT is referenced by some route and vice versa; it has no notion of whether a point's name matches the domain of the routes that cite it. packages/backend/tests/rfc305-architecture-lock.test.ts:439-457 locks out role-based predicates but not point naming.
- **拟建守卫**：Add to the startup self-check (or a test over allRouteMeta()) an ownership assertion: every point cited by a route whose path prefix is P must belong to a declared point-domain registered for P, with a shrink-only exception table. Today that table would contain exactly the 4 `/api/digital-employee*` routes citing `development-missions:*`, making the debt visible and blocking a sixth instance.
- **证伪方式**：Add a second employee type package to server.ts:481 and try to authorize its launch: it needs `development-missions:launch`, and no test or self-check objects. Cross-check the point catalog: `rg "development-missions" packages/shared/src/schemas/permission.ts`.

### TP-06 · P2 · missing-guard · CONFIRMED

**WS channel exhaustiveness locks are half hand-maintained; the RFC-312 presence channel already escaped three of them**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc152-ws-channel-registry.test.ts:103-109` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc152-ws-channel-registry.test.ts:216-238` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc152-ws-channel-registry.test.ts:240-258` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc152-ws-paths-interlock.test.ts:17-35` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc152-ws-paths-interlock.test.ts:49-59` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc152-ws-paths-interlock.test.ts:66-88` · `packages/backend/src/ws/server.ts:181-187` · `packages/backend/src/ws/server.ts:291-313`
- **证据**：WS_CHANNELS key-set exhaustion is real (registry test :103-109 vs ALL_KINDS, and WsChannelRegistry is a mapped type so revalidation is compile-required). But three assertions use hand-written sample arrays and presence is missing from all three: rfc152-ws-channel-registry.test.ts:216-238 (parseWsChannel round-trip: no '/ws/presence'), :241-253 (the pathRe no-overlap samples: no presence entry), rfc152-ws-paths-interlock.test.ts:49-59 (static-path parse: no WS_PATHS.presence) and :67-79 (no-overlap samples: no presence). The paths-interlock bijection test at :17-35 WAS updated to include 'presence' — proving the author touched the file and the sample arrays still did not force an update. Separately, TOKEN_ALLOWED_WS_CHANNELS (ws/server.ts:304-313) is typed `ReadonlySet<string>`, not keyed on WsChannelKind, has zero test references anywhere in the repo (`rg TOKEN_ALLOWED_WS_CHANNELS` -> 2 source hits, 0 tests, and 'token-forbidden-channel' appears only at ws/server.ts:183), and its docstring says "Two are deliberately excluded" while four channels are actually excluded (repo-import, intent-sessions, authority, presence).
- **架构影响**：The registry is the single source of truth for channel wire surface, but the guards that make that meaningful (no path overlap, every path parses, token reachability decided per channel) degrade into stale sample lists as channels are added. A future channel whose pathRe accidentally shadows an earlier one — the exact bug the no-overlap test exists to catch — would not be caught, because the new channel need never appear in the samples. And a typo'd string in TOKEN_ALLOWED_WS_CHANNELS silently closes a channel to tokens with no compile error.
- **现有守卫**：partial and demonstrably leaky — packages/backend/tests/rfc152-ws-channel-registry.test.ts:103-109 forces the key set only; the three sample-driven assertions do not, as presence proves. None for TOKEN_ALLOWED_WS_CHANNELS.
- **拟建守卫**：Derive the samples instead of listing them: give each ChannelSpec a `sampleParams` (or reuse WS_PATHS) so the no-overlap and round-trip tests iterate WS_CHANNEL_KINDS and fail on a channel with no sample; and retype TOKEN_ALLOWED_WS_CHANNELS as an exhaustive `Record<WsChannelKind, boolean>` (or `ReadonlySet<WsChannelKind>` plus a test asserting the set of excluded kinds equals a frozen list), so adding a channel is a compile-time decision about token reachability rather than a silent default-deny plus a stale comment.
- **证伪方式**：Open rfc152-ws-channel-registry.test.ts:241-253 and rfc152-ws-paths-interlock.test.ts:67-79 — neither array contains presence, yet ALL_KINDS at :87-100 does. Add a 13th channel with `pathRe: /^\/ws\/tasks$/` (a deliberate overlap with tasks-list) and only the key-set test forces you to touch anything.

### TP-07 · P3 · missing-guard · ADJUSTED

**routes->db ratchet is import-shaped, so a route can query the database with zero @/db import and stay invisible**

- **锚点**：`scripts/depcheck.ts:216-282` · `scripts/depcheck.ts:403-410` · `.dependency-cruiser.cjs:87-95` · `packages/backend/src/routes/webhookDeliveries.ts:136` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentMissions.ts:208`
- **证据**：The ledger is genuinely accurate today — I verified the 15 KNOWN_VIOLATIONS entries match exactly the 15 route files with a value import of '@/db/schema' (auth, clarify, health, intentSessions, oidc-auth, port-artifacts, repos, reviews, taskClarifyDirective, taskFeedback, taskQuestions, tasks, webhookDeliveries, webhooks, worktree-files), out of 57 route files; routes/resourceAcl.ts:18 and routes/resourcePackages.ts:17 import @/db type-only and are correctly exempt. But the query capability arrives through `deps.db` (AppDeps.db, server.ts:180) and `sql` from 'drizzle-orm', neither of which is an @/db edge: routes/webhookDeliveries.ts:136 already does `await deps.db.all<{p:string}>(sql\`…\`)`. A route that only used that form would import nothing from @/db and no rule would fire. Confirmed reachable: routes/developmentMissions.ts uses `deps.db.` with no @/db value import today.
- **架构影响**：The ratchet says 'shrink only', and it does shrink the import edge — but the debt can migrate rather than shrink: converting a ledgered file's Drizzle calls to raw `sql` templates would make its ledger entry go STALE (forcing its removal, since stale entries fail the gate) and leave the direct DB access completely unguarded. The measured number (15/57, 26%) is therefore a floor on the real routes->DB surface, not a ceiling.
- **现有守卫**：partial — scripts/depcheck.ts:221-282 catches a NEW route FILE acquiring an @/db value import (that is a real, working ratchet). Nothing catches an existing or new file querying through deps.db with drizzle-orm operators only.
- **拟建守卫**：Add a grep/AST ratchet over src/routes/*.ts: no `deps.db.` / `.db.select(` / `.db.insert(` / `.db.update(` / `.db.delete(` / `.db.all(` / `.db.run(` call sites outside a shrink-only allowlist keyed by file:line, mirroring the shape of packages/backend/tests/routes-no-cast.test.ts:50-60. That asserts the invariant the dep rule intends (routes do not talk to persistence) instead of the proxy for it (routes do not import the schema module).
- **证伪方式**：In any route file with no @/db import, add `const rows = await deps.db.all(sql\`SELECT 1\`)` and run `bun run depcheck` + `bun run test:backend`: both green.

### TP-08 · P3 · boundary-blur · ADJUSTED

**AppDeps is a god-object with 6 declared test-only fields and 6 single-consumer fields, and nothing ratchets either**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/server.ts:139-152` · `packages/backend/src/server.ts:154-243` · `packages/backend/src/server.ts:209` · `packages/backend/src/server.ts:215` · `packages/backend/src/server.ts:222` · `packages/backend/src/server.ts:230-242` · `packages/backend/src/server.ts:574-584` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:23-39` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc284-env-flags-registry.test.ts:31-44`
- **证据**：AppDeps declares six fields whose own docstrings say production never supplies them: runtimeDiagnosticTestDependencies (:226-230, itself containing beforeRuntimeProbeCache :141-145 and probeTimeoutMsForTest :146-151), intentTestDependencies (:231-234), mcpRuntimeTestDependencies (:235-241), workflowExactOperationHook (:216-225), codeHostFetch (:204-209, 'Production omits it'), buildScheduleLaunch (:210-215, 'Production omits it'). Reference counts across routes/ + server.ts + mcp/: codeHostFetch 1, buildScheduleLaunch 1, workflowExactOperationHook 1, daemonInfoPath 1, opencodeVersion 1, dbVersion 1. 72 route-mount signatures declare `deps: AppDeps`, against 4 mounts that take an exact module contract (server.ts:574,582,583,584). The RFC-294 preflight already treats AppDeps as poison for module public surfaces (FORBIDDEN_TYPE_NAME, preflight test :23-39) — but only inside packages/backend/src/modules/.
- **架构影响**：Every route module receives the full DbClient, SecretBox, dispatcher, and every in-process test seam whether or not it needs them, which is precisely the dependency shape RFC-294 replaces with exact public contracts. The test seams are contractualised (no env switch can select them, which is genuinely better than the env-flag seams the 2026-08-12 audit flagged as N29 violations) — but the count can only grow, and each new one enlarges the production type that 72 call sites depend on.
- **现有守卫**：none for this file. The analogous problem for environment-variable seams IS ratcheted (docs/env-flags.md + packages/backend/tests/rfc284-env-flags-registry.test.ts:31-45 fail on any unregistered AGENT_WORKFLOW_*/AW_* token); the in-process seams on AppDeps have no registry and no counter.
- **拟建守卫**：Mirror the env-flag ratchet: an AST test that reads the AppDeps declaration, extracts every optional property whose JSDoc contains a test-seam marker, and asserts the exact set equals a frozen list (shrink-only) — plus a second assertion freezing the count of `deps: AppDeps` route-mount signatures at 72 with `toBeLessThanOrEqual`, so the migration to exact contracts can only move one way.
- **证伪方式**：Add `fooTestHook?: () => void` to AppDeps and a new `mountFooRoutes(app, deps: AppDeps)`; run `bun run gate:local` — green.

### TP-09 · P3 · stale-assertion · ADJUSTED

**Security-relevant comment in the WS upgrade path asserts a defect that was closed, and contradicts the same file 130 lines earlier**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/ws/server.ts:291-303` · `packages/backend/src/ws/server.ts:167-169` · `packages/backend/src/ws/registry.ts:746-767` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc152-ws-channel-registry.test.ts:181-184`
- **证据**：ws/server.ts:296-300 states: "`repo-import` — its spec states it has 'no gate of any kind' (RFC-152 D4 leftover); anyone who guesses a batchId sees another user's import. That is an existing defect". The same file at :167-169 says the opposite: "`repo-import` is owner-gated since RFC-285 B6② (the RFC-152 D4 leftover is closed)". Source of truth: ws/registry.ts:762-766 installs an upgradeGate requiring `owner === actor.user.id || hasResourceAclBypass(actor)` and refusing with an existence-preserving `batch-not-found`, and rfc152-ws-channel-registry.test.ts:183 asserts `WS_CHANNELS['repo-import'].upgradeGate` is defined.
- **架构影响**：CLAUDE.md makes in-repo comments the review evidence a new session must verify against source. A stale comment that says a live authorization surface is unguarded is worse than no comment: it invites either a redundant 'fix' or a reviewer waving through a real regression as 'known'. It also sits inside the justification for TOKEN_ALLOWED_WS_CHANNELS, so the reason repo-import is excluded from token access is now recorded as false.
- **现有守卫**：none — no guard compares gate-related prose against the registry. (This one is cheap to fix by hand, but the CLASS is unguarded: the 2026-08-12 audit §4-9 had to correct seven such comment/reality inversions by hand.)
- **拟建守卫**：Cheapest durable mechanism: assert in rfc152-ws-channel-registry.test.ts that ws/server.ts source does NOT contain the strings 'no gate of any kind' or 'existing defect' (a banned-phrase ratchet in the same style as packages/backend/tests/route-gate-single-source.test.ts:79-104). Better: move the per-channel token-access reason INTO the ChannelSpec next to the upgradeGate, so the prose and the mechanism cannot be edited apart.
- **证伪方式**：Read ws/server.ts:167-169 and :296-300 side by side, then ws/registry.ts:762-766. One of the two comments is false and the source says which.

### TP-10 · P3 · business-special-case · ADJUSTED

**Generic HTTP error translator duck-types one domain's error-code literal**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/util/errors.ts:133-155` · `packages/backend/src/util/errors.ts:157-161` · `packages/backend/src/server.ts:410` · `packages/backend/src/mcp/dispatch.ts:85` · `packages/shared/src/lifecycle.ts:62` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:205-270`
- **证据**：errorHandler — the single onError for REST (server.ts:410), MCP dispatch (dispatch.ts:85) and, in spirit, the WS refusal body — has one hand-written non-DomainError branch: `if (isIllegalNodeRunTransition(err)) return c.json({...}, 422)`, where the predicate is `err instanceof Error && (err as {code?: unknown}).code === 'illegal-node-run-transition'`. That is an ad-hoc if on a specific business error string from the task-execution domain, sitting inside the transport commons, not a registry dispatch. The comment explains the constraint honestly (the shared lifecycle module cannot extend the backend's DomainError), which is exactly why the fix is a registry rather than a second if.
- **架构影响**：There is nothing structurally special about this domain: any shared-layer module that wants a non-500 HTTP mapping today must add another duck-typed if here. Two such branches and the commons has an unowned, unordered chain of business predicates — the shape the repo elsewhere replaces with an exhaustive table (see mcp/tools.ts:574-682 RESOURCE_ROUTES, ws/registry.ts:580-951 WS_CHANNELS).
- **现有守卫**：none — no test constrains the number or shape of non-DomainError branches in errorHandler.
- **拟建守卫**：Replace the if with a registry: `const SHARED_ERROR_STATUS: Record<string, ContentfulStatusCode>` keyed by the shared layer's error `code` (populated from packages/shared), consulted once; then a source ratchet asserting util/errors.ts contains exactly one `instanceof DomainError` branch plus one registry lookup, and zero other `err as {` casts. Invariant: a new shared error code is registered as data, never as another branch in the translator.
- **证伪方式**：Add a second `isIllegalXyz(err)` predicate and branch to errorHandler and run `bun run gate:local`: green. Also note the branch IS reachable — shared/lifecycle.ts throws a plain Error with that code precisely because it cannot import backend types.

### TP-11 · P3 · duplicate-implementation · CONFIRMED

**Three independent re-implementations of the claim-with-lease outbox protocol; platform/ owns no outbox kernel**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/modules/task-execution/infrastructure/sqliteTaskLifecycleEventPublisher.ts:36-100` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/infrastructure/sqliteRuntimeStore.ts:835-915` · `packages/backend/src/modules/event-center/infrastructure/sqliteEventStore.ts:973-1032` · `packages/backend/src/db/schema.ts:5128-5135` · `packages/backend/src/db/schema.ts:5598-5600` · `packages/backend/src/platform/persistence/transactionScope.ts:1-9`
- **证据**：Same protocol, three hand-rolled copies: select due rows where `state='pending' OR (state='claimed' AND claimExpiresAt <= now)`, CAS-claim on (id, state, attemptCount), set `claimExpiresAt = now + leaseMs`, then complete/retry/dead-letter. task-execution does it over task_lifecycle_event_outbox (:38-78 claim, :81-100 complete), digital-employee over employee_os_outbox (isNull/lte claimExpiresAt at :845-846, lease at :862,:880, release at :892,:913), event-center over eventDeliveries (:980-981 due predicate, :1006 lease, :1025 release). `packages/backend/src/platform/` contains exactly two files, both about transaction scope — there is no shared outbox/claim primitive to reuse.
- **架构影响**：Lease expiry, attempt accounting and dead-lettering are the parts of an outbox that are easy to get subtly wrong and hard to test per-copy; three copies means three places a claim-expiry off-by-one or a missing attemptCount guard can live independently. Unlike the other findings here this is not a special case hiding INSIDE a commons — it is a commons that was never extracted, which the question's rules count as boundary blur (a second and third source of truth).
- **现有守卫**：none — no test or dep rule constrains where claim/lease logic may be implemented, and there is no kernel to point new code at.
- **拟建守卫**：Extract `platform/persistence/outbox.ts` exposing claimOne/complete/retry/deadLetter over a small port (table accessor + clock + leaseMs), migrate the three call sites, then add a grep ratchet: `claimExpiresAt` may be written only in platform/persistence/outbox.ts and db/schema.ts (shrink-only allowlist for the migration window). Invariant: exactly one implementation of the claim protocol.
- **证伪方式**：`rg -l claimExpiresAt packages/backend/src` -> db/schema.ts plus three module infrastructure files in three different bounded contexts. Diff the three claim blocks: same predicate, same CAS, three spellings.

### TP-12 · P3 · stale-assertion · ADJUSTED

**DAEMON_CADENCE claims to be the single background-cadence registry, but ~6 loops keep their own literals and membership is unguarded**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/daemonCadence.ts:1-40` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc284-daemon-cadence.test.ts:6-28` · `packages/backend/src/services/backupScheduler.ts:194` · `packages/backend/src/services/taskArchive.ts:573` · `packages/backend/src/services/maintenanceRetention.ts:185` · `packages/backend/src/services/pluginGenerationGc.ts:16` · `packages/backend/src/services/memoryDistillScheduler.ts:431` · `packages/backend/src/services/eventsArchive.ts:19` · `packages/backend/src/services/eventsArchive.ts:25`
- **证据**：daemonCadence.ts:1 declares itself "daemon 后台节奏唯一注册表" and its header explains the pre-RFC-284 pain ("要翻 9 个文件… start.ts 两处连常量名都没有的裸 1h"). 14 entries are consumed by 11 call sites. But 30 `setInterval(` sites exist under packages/backend/src, and these keep their own periods: backupScheduler.ts:194 `setInterval(() => safePrune('prune tick'), 3_600_000)` — a naked literal of exactly the shape the registry was created to eliminate; taskArchive.ts:573 `intervalMs: number = 3_600_000`; maintenanceRetention.ts:185 `intervalMs: number = 3_600_000`; pluginGenerationGc.ts:16 `const DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS = 60 * 60_000`; memoryDistillScheduler.ts:431 `options.intervalMs ?? 1000`; scheduledTaskScheduler.ts:261 `opts.intervalMs ?? SCHEDULE_TICK_MS`. (eventsArchive.ts:441 does import HOUR_MS from the module — partial adoption.)
- **架构影响**：The operational question the registry exists to answer — 'worst case, how long until state X is swept' — still requires grepping. The registry's existence is worse than neutral for a reader who trusts the header, because it looks authoritative while covering under half the loops.
- **现有守卫**：packages/backend/tests/rfc284-daemon-cadence.test.ts:6-28 pins the VALUE of every entry already in the table (a real anti-tuning lock), but asserts nothing about membership: a new setInterval with an inline literal adds no entry and turns nothing red.
- **拟建守卫**：A source ratchet in the style of packages/backend/tests/rfc284-env-flags-registry.test.ts:31-45: scan src for `setInterval(` and require the second argument to be a `DAEMON_CADENCE.*` member reference or a parameter fed from one, with a shrink-only file:line allowlist seeded with today's ~6 offenders. Invariant: every recurring daemon loop's period is named in one table.
- **证伪方式**：`rg -n 'setInterval\(' packages/backend/src | wc -l` -> 30; `rg -n DAEMON_CADENCE packages/backend/src | grep -v daemonCadence.ts | wc -l` -> 11 consumption sites. Then read backupScheduler.ts:194.

### TP-13 · P3 · escape-hatch · CONFIRMED

**Double cast in a WS frame gate converts a compile-time union guarantee into a silent frame drop**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/ws/registry.ts:493-517` · `packages/backend/src/ws/registry.ts:509-512` · `packages/shared/src/schemas/ws.ts:246-252` · `packages/backend/tests/routes-no-cast.test.ts:24-40`
- **证据**：extractTaskIdFromListMessage handles 'lifecycle.alert' with `typeof (msg as unknown as {taskId?: string}).taskId === 'string' ? (msg as unknown as {taskId: string}).taskId : null`, commented "Defensive narrowing — payload shape may evolve". The shared discriminated union already makes it non-optional: shared/src/schemas/ws.ts:246-252 declares `z.object({ type: z.literal('lifecycle.alert'), taskId: z.string(), … })`. So the cast is not narrowing anything TypeScript cannot infer — it is discarding an inference. Consequence: if a future edit removes `taskId` from that variant, this site does not fail to compile; it returns null, and the caller's contract (registry.ts:494-496, and the gate at :651-652) is to DROP the frame. A wire-shape regression becomes an invisible delivery regression in an ACL filter.
- **架构影响**：Small in blast radius, exact in kind: an `as unknown as` inside a security gate that trades a red build for a silent behaviour change. Repo-wide there are 107 `as unknown as` occurrences in packages/backend/src with no lint rule (eslint.config.js has only @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any at 'warn', line 75).
- **现有守卫**：none for ws/. packages/backend/tests/routes-no-cast.test.ts:24-40 is exactly the right guard shape — an allowlisted cast ratchet — but it is scoped to `src/routes` only (ROUTES_DIR at line 24).
- **拟建守卫**：Delete the cast (the union already gives `msg.taskId: string`) and extend routes-no-cast.test.ts's scanner to `src/ws` and `src/mcp` with the same ALLOWED_CAST_TARGETS + shrink-only FILE_LINE_ALLOWLIST, seeded with the one legitimate documented erasure at ws/registry.ts:992 (`WS_CHANNELS[kind] as unknown as ErasedChannelSpec`).
- **证伪方式**：Remove `taskId` from the 'lifecycle.alert' variant in shared/src/schemas/ws.ts:246-252 and run `bun run typecheck`: ws/registry.ts:509-513 still compiles, and the frame is now silently dropped by the tasks-list gate.

### TP-14 · P3 · boundary-blur · CONFIRMED

**One channel's concept is hard-wired into the channel-agnostic connection lifecycle, outside the ratchet's scope**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/ws/registry.ts:208-217` · `packages/backend/src/ws/registry.ts:344-352` · `packages/backend/src/ws/registry.ts:355-385` · `packages/backend/src/ws/registry.ts:928-949` · `packages/backend/src/ws/connections.ts:79-81` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc152-ws-task-channel.test.ts:192-203`
- **证据**：WsConnectionData — the struct every channel shares — carries a presence-specific `releasePresence?: () => void` field (:209-217), and the generic `untrackConnection` calls `releasePresence(ws)` unconditionally for every connection of every channel (connections.ts:79-82). Contrast the same RFC's own stated principle, written 20 lines below in the same file: resync is "声明成通道自己的数据（与 upgradeGate / frameGate 同构），而不是在复核循环里写 kind === 'presence' 分支" (registry.ts:344-347). Presence release did not get that treatment. Meanwhile the ratchet that enforces 'no per-channel anything in the transport skeleton' reads exactly one file: `readFileSync(resolve(import.meta.dir,'..','src','ws','server.ts'))` (rfc152-ws-task-channel.test.ts:193) — connections.ts and the pipeline half of registry.ts are unscanned.
- **架构影响**：The channel-agnostic skeleton is now three files (server.ts, connections.ts, and gatedSubscribe/openWsChannel/sendJson inside registry.ts) while the guard covers one. The precedent set by releasePresence is 'per-connection resource release is a hardcoded call in the generic path', so a second channel needing cleanup adds a second hardcoded call rather than a spec field — regrowing the hand-copied per-channel branches RFC-152 removed.
- **现有守卫**：partial — packages/backend/tests/rfc152-ws-task-channel.test.ts:195-203 bans `case '<kind>'` / `kind === '<kind>'` / `.kind ===` but only in ws/server.ts, and a named function call like `releasePresence(ws)` matches none of its patterns anywhere.
- **拟建守卫**：Generalise the spec: replace `releasePresence` with an optional `ChannelSpec.onClose?(ws)` (symmetric with the existing `onOpenExtra`), then widen the ratchet to scan ws/server.ts + ws/connections.ts + the pipeline section of ws/registry.ts for any identifier containing a channel kind from WS_CHANNEL_KINDS, with an allowlist limited to the WS_CHANNELS literal itself.
- **证伪方式**：`rg -n releasePresence packages/backend/src/ws` -> registry.ts:217,362-366,384 and connections.ts:81, the last inside a function whose docstring calls itself channel-agnostic. Then add `if (ws.data.channel.kind === 'memories') …` to connections.ts and run rfc152-ws-task-channel.test.ts: green, because it never reads that file.

## DE — 数字员工三 context 与执行合同公共层（13 条：P1 0 / P2 9 / P3 4）

### DE-01 · P2 · boundary-blur · ADJUSTED

**The generic Digital Employee OS context queries development-automation's own Mission tables directly**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/composition/writerCutover.ts:4-10` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/composition/writerCutover.ts:21` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/composition/writerCutover.ts:23-31` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/composition/writerCutover.ts:123-193` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/composition.ts:179` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/composition.ts:519` · `design/RFC-294-backend-layered-target-architecture/proposal.md:152-153` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:238-268`
- **证据**：writerCutover.ts:4-10 imports `developmentApprovalSagas, developmentMissionLinks, developmentMissions, developmentMrClaims` from '@/db/schema' and runs four Drizzle queries against them (countOpenLegacyMissions at :23-31; analyzeDigitalEmployeeMigration at :123-169 joins missions -> mr claims -> mission links -> approval sagas, and filters on the development-owned status literals `['approved','rejected','expired','unavailable']` at :21). Every other consumer of those four tables lives in modules/development-automation/infrastructure/*. The result shape is then re-exported on the generic module's own query surface: composition.ts:179 `getMigrationStatus(): ReturnType<typeof analyzeDigitalEmployeeMigration>`.
- **架构影响**：RFC-294 proposal.md:150-151 and :164-165 forbid exactly this ('不能以"复用方便"为由共享 Drizzle table' / '四者不得以共享表、内部 import 或 bootstrap if type === development 重新耦合'). The generic OS now cannot be composed, migrated or reasoned about without the development schema present, and a change to development mission columns silently breaks a digital-employee query. It also leaks a development read model out through DigitalEmployeeQueries.
- **现有守卫**：none — rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:74-101 ledgers only INBOUND `@/modules/digital-employee/*` import edges; a table imported from the global `@/db/schema` namespace is invisible to every import-based guard in the repo, and the genericTypeLiteralBan at :104-131 matches only the string literal 'development', never the identifier `developmentMissions`.
- **拟建守卫**：AST/table-ownership ratchet: derive a table->owning-module map (one Drizzle table symbol may be referenced from exactly one modules/<context>/ subtree, plus db/schema.ts and migrations), assert `tableOwnershipViolations()` toEqual([]) with an explicit expiring debt list. Concretely for this case: define `LegacyMissionDrainPort { openMissionCount(): number; drainReport(): ... }` in digital-employee/composition/required-ports.ts, implement it in development-automation, and let the ratchet forbid any `development*` table symbol under modules/digital-employee/.
- **证伪方式**：grep -n 'developmentMissions\|developmentMrClaims\|developmentApprovalSagas\|developmentMissionLinks' packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/composition/writerCutover.ts — if it returns nothing, the coupling was removed. Then confirm the four symbols are referenced only under modules/development-automation/.

### DE-02 · P2 · boundary-blur · ADJUSTED

**development-automation reads the Digital Employee OS's private reaction-round table, including its internal planJson and state enum**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/infrastructure/sqliteRuntimeStore.ts:19-33` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/composition/digitalEmployeeWorkspace.ts:11` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/composition/digitalEmployeeWorkspace.ts:599-606` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/composition/digitalEmployeePlatformWorkItems.ts:12` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/composition/digitalEmployeePlatformWorkItems.ts:1402-1413` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:230-268`
- **证据**：`employeeReactionRounds` is owned by digital-employee (sqliteRuntimeStore.ts:19-33 imports it alongside the other 13 employee* tables and is the only writer: createRound/retryRound/settleRound). development-automation reads it directly from two files: digitalEmployeeWorkspace.ts:598-606 selects `employeeReactionRounds.caseId` and `employeeReactionRounds.planJson` (the OS's internal frozen plan document) by roundRef; digitalEmployeePlatformWorkItems.ts:1401-1413 selects rounds filtered by `eq(employeeReactionRounds.workItemRef,'repair-conflict')` and `eq(employeeReactionRounds.state,'completed')`, i.e. it reads the OS's private state machine enum AND a business work-item literal from another context's table. This is the mirror image of F1 — the two contexts read each other's tables in both directions.
- **架构影响**：planJson is the OS's internal, unversioned document; nothing stops digital-employee from reshaping it, and nothing would tell development-automation. The state literal 'completed' and the round lifecycle become a de-facto public contract that has no public/ declaration, no schema and no owner. This is the '共享表' re-coupling RFC-294 proposal.md:164-165 names explicitly.
- **现有守卫**：none — rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:1253-1261 pins cross-context INTERNAL IMPORT edges to an exact two-row debt list, but `employeeReactionRounds` arrives via '@/db/schema', which the crossContextViolations() rule at :238-268 does not treat as a module edge at all.
- **拟建守卫**：Same table-ownership ratchet as F1, applied symmetrically: any table symbol whose name matches an owner prefix registered by context X may be referenced only from modules/X/** (+ db/schema.ts + migrations + tests). Replace the two reads with a typed query on digital-employee/public/queries (e.g. `EmployeeReactionRoundQueryPort.planFor(roundRef)` returning a declared DTO, and `lastSettledRound(caseId, workItemRef)`).
- **证伪方式**：rg -l 'employeeReactionRounds' packages/backend/src — today returns three files across two modules; the coupling is gone when it returns only modules/digital-employee/infrastructure/sqliteRuntimeStore.ts (plus db/schema.ts).

### DE-03 · P2 · business-special-case · ADJUSTED

**The generic OS retry kernel escalates to a fresh scene by string-matching an error code that development-automation builds by concatenation**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/application/runtimeService.ts:2015-2021` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/composition/digitalEmployeeWorkspace.ts:739-744` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/infrastructure/workspaceValidator.ts:59-84` · `packages/backend/src/modules/task-execution/composition/required-ports.ts:22` · `packages/backend/src/modules/task-execution/composition/digitalEmployeeExecution.ts:428-437`
- **证据**：runtimeService.ts:2015 `const boundaryFailure = errorCode.startsWith('workspace-boundary-')`, and :2016-2021 uses it to jump the attempt ordinal forward to the next fresh-scene boundary instead of the next same-scene attempt. The only producer of that prefix is digitalEmployeeWorkspace.ts:743 `errorCode: \`workspace-${verdict.kind}-${verdict.code}\`` where verdict.kind is 'boundary'|'semantic' (workspaceValidator.ts:70-85). The two ends are joined by nothing but a hand-typed prefix that one side builds with template interpolation from an enum the other side never sees; it travels through the untyped `errorCode: string` on task-execution's DigitalEmployeeWorkspacePort (digitalEmployeeExecution.ts:436) and through ReactionExecutionSnapshot.errorCode. A sibling code family is also silently swallowed: digitalEmployeeWorkspace.ts:709 emits `workspace-${conflictInspection.code}` (no 'boundary'/'semantic' segment), so those never trigger the escalation.
- **架构影响**：The retry policy of the generic OS — a platform-level behaviour — is decided by a business module's error-string spelling. Renaming `kind: 'boundary'` to anything else in workspaceValidator.ts, or reordering the template in digitalEmployeeWorkspace.ts:743, silently downgrades every boundary violation to a same-scene retry: the OS keeps re-running an agent inside a workspace it has already proven is contaminated. The failure is invisible (retries still happen, just in the wrong scene).
- **现有守卫**：none — rg 'workspace-boundary' over packages/backend/tests returns only rfc281-*.test.ts, which is the unrelated opencode permission-synthesis feature. tests/rfc310-employee-workspace-delivery.test.ts exercises fresh-scene *preparation* (:288-298, :734-740) but never asserts that a boundary errorCode causes the escalation, so both the producer and the consumer can drift freely.
- **拟建守卫**：(a) Replace the string sniff with a closed field on the port: `DigitalEmployeeWorkspacePort.validate -> { ok:false; errorClass:'boundary'|'semantic'|'infrastructure'; errorCode; errorDetail }`, thread errorClass through ReactionExecutionSnapshot, and branch on the union so TS exhaustiveness catches a new class. (b) Until then, a pairing test that imports the real workspace composer, forces a boundary verdict, feeds the resulting errorCode into #retryOrFailExecution and asserts attemptOrdinal jumps to the fresh-scene boundary — one test that turns red if either side renames.
- **证伪方式**：Change `kind: 'boundary'` to `kind: 'fence'` in workspaceValidator.ts:76 and run the full backend suite. If nothing goes red, the contract is unguarded exactly as reported.

### DE-04 · P2 · business-special-case · CONFIRMED

**The generic Event Center response-rule kernel branches on a business target kind and hardcodes a development permission id**
