- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/modules/event-center/application/eventResponseRules.ts:58-62` · `packages/backend/src/modules/event-center/application/eventResponseRules.ts:99` · `packages/backend/src/modules/event-center/application/eventResponseRules.ts:118` · `packages/backend/src/modules/event-center/application/eventResponseRules.ts:157-166` · `packages/backend/src/modules/event-center/domain/responseRule.ts:9-50` · `packages/backend/src/routes/eventCenter.ts:237` · `packages/backend/src/routes/eventCenter.ts:258` · `packages/backend/src/routes/eventCenter.ts:277`
- **证据**：eventResponseRules.ts:161-165: `if (draft.target.kind === 'digital-employee' && !principal.canLaunchDigitalEmployee) throw new ForbiddenError('forbidden','missing permission: development-missions:launch',{requiredPermission:'development-missions:launch'})`. The permission id is a development-automation name (shared/src/schemas/permission.ts:218, and routes/developmentMissions.ts:275) embedded in a bounded context whose declared owner is 'event and source catalog, response rule, subscription...'. The principal DTO itself is shaped around that one target: `canLaunchDigitalEmployee: boolean` at :61. The other three target kinds in the same union (workflow / agent / workgroup, responseRule.ts:9-50) get no launch-permission check in this kernel at all.
- **架构影响**：It is an ad-hoc if buried in a generic function, not a registry: there is no table mapping target.kind -> required permission and no compile-time exhaustiveness. Adding a fifth target kind that needs a permission produces a silently unauthorised launch path — the code compiles, the tests pass, and the rule fires with no check. It also pins an event-center error message to a development permission spelling, so renaming the permission leaves a wrong human-readable message behind.
- **现有守卫**：none for a NEW instance. The existing branch IS covered end-to-end (packages/backend/tests/rfc315-event-automation-permissions.test.ts:264-322 asserts 403 + code 'forbidden' on both create and update for a digital-employee target), so the deny path is not untested — but nothing enumerates target kinds against required permissions, so a fifth kind added tomorrow turns nothing red.
- **拟建守卫**：Replace the if with an exhaustive registry in event-center/domain/responseRule.ts: `const TARGET_LAUNCH_PERMISSION: Record<EventResponseTarget['kind'], LaunchPermissionRef|null>` plus a `satisfies` / never-check so a new union member fails typecheck; move the concrete permission id in from the composition root (event-center should receive it, not name it). Add a test that iterates every key of that Record and asserts a rule with that target is refused for a principal lacking the mapped permission.
- **证伪方式**：Add a fifth variant to eventResponseTargetSchema (responseRule.ts:9-50) with its own refId and run the backend suite; responseTargetTemplateTexts at :121-133 may complain via TS, but #requireTargetLaunchPermission will not — proving the permission mapping is unguarded.

### DE-05 · P2 · escape-hatch · ADJUSTED

**A magic 'platform' slot string lets a type package bypass the OS's tool-binding invariant, with no shared constant on either side**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/application/runtimeService.ts:1800-1826` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/application/runtimeService.ts:1895` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/application/runtimeService.ts:1969-1986` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/composition/employeeTypePackage.ts:3511` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/composition/required-ports.ts:90-97`
- **证据**：runtimeService.ts:1800 `const platformSelected = item.nodeKind === 'business-tool' && selectedSlotRef === 'platform'`. That flag then negates both invariants immediately below: :1801-1809 skips the 'selected slot must exist' check (`!platformSelected &&`), and :1820-1825 skips the 'no exact published tool for this slot' check. The value is produced by the type package as a bare literal: employeeTypePackage.ts:3511 `slotRef: kind === 'external-id' ? request.defaultSlotRef : 'platform'`. The two literals are never derived from a shared constant. The escape then lands on `platformWorkItems`, itself an OPTIONAL port (composition.ts:246, required-ports.ts:90-97) whose default (composition.ts:443-456) returns status 'blocked' with a hand-written summary.
- **架构影响**：Any registered type package can opt one work item out of 'every business-tool round resolves to an exact published tool revision' by returning a four-syllable string from a JSON callback the OS deliberately treats as untrusted (it zod-parses the shape at :1776-1791 but not the value's meaning). Rename 'platform' on either side and the effect is not an error but a different code path: the OS starts demanding a tool binding that does not exist, or stops demanding one that should.
- **现有守卫**：none — the string appears in two modules with no shared symbol; rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts's literal ban covers only 'development'.
- **拟建守卫**：Export `PLATFORM_WORK_ITEM_SLOT_REF` from digital-employee/public/types.ts and require both sides to import it; better, make the codec return a closed union `{ kind:'slot'; slotRef } | { kind:'platform-work-item' }` so 'platform' stops being a value in the slot namespace at all. Add a source-text ratchet: the literal `'platform'` may not appear in a slot-comparison position in runtimeService.ts.
- **证伪方式**：Change employeeTypePackage.ts:3511's `'platform'` to `'platform-tool'` and run the backend suite. If tests still pass (or fail only with an unrelated 'unknown slot' message), the coupling is exactly as unguarded as reported.

### DE-06 · P2 · duplicate-implementation · ADJUSTED

**terminalKind is an unvalidated free string with three divergent classification tables, one dead literal and one spelling typo**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/application/adapters/task-catalog-adapter.ts:16-41` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/domain/mission.ts:16-30` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/application/runtimeService.ts:1433-1437` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/application/ports/runtimeStore.ts:249` · `packages/backend/src/routes/digitalEmployees.ts:237-242` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/composition/employeeTypePackage.ts:4319-4322` · `packages/frontend/src/components/digital-employees/outcomes.ts:14-25` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc310-task-catalog.test.ts:218`
- **证据**：The value is minted in four unrelated places — employeeTypePackage.ts:4321 `terminalKind = mergeRequest.status` ('merged'|'closed'), sqliteRuntimeStore.ts:1414 defaults to 'completed', runtimeService.ts:1434 mints 'deadline-exceeded', and routes/digitalEmployees.ts:239 accepts ANY `z.string().min(1)` from HTTP — and then classified by three independent hand-rolled tables that do not agree: (1) task-catalog-adapter.ts:39 `terminalKind === 'canceled' || terminalKind === 'closed-unmerged'` — 'closed-unmerged' is a legacy development Mission terminal kind (development-automation/domain/mission.ts:18) that the OS never produces, so the real 'closed' value falls through and is reported to the task catalog as status 'done'; (2) runtimeService.ts:1437 `['closed','cancelled','failed','blocked'].includes(terminalKind)` — note 'cancelled' (double L) while every other site in the repo writes 'canceled', so a case terminated as 'canceled' is scored 'satisfied' by the collaboration join; (3) outcomes.ts:15-21 buckets 'merged' / 'completed-no-change' / 'no-change-confirmed'. Separately, task-catalog-adapter.ts:16-28 maps TaskStatus->case state and :35-41 maps case state->TaskStatus as two unrelated tables, so filtering the catalog by status=canceled returns terminal cases whose reported status is 'done'.
- **架构影响**：There is no owner and no schema for a value that drives task-catalog status, collaboration join outcome, and two UI buckets. Every consumer re-derives the vocabulary from memory, and two of the three current derivations are already wrong. Because the HTTP terminate route accepts an arbitrary string, an operator can mint a terminalKind no table knows.
- **现有守卫**：none — no test enumerates terminalKind values, and no shared type exists. rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:104-131 reads task-catalog-adapter.ts and outcomes.ts (they are inside the scanned generic roots) but only searches them for the literal 'development'.
- **拟建守卫**：Declare a closed union in digital-employee/public/types.ts (`EmployeeCaseTerminalKind`), validate the terminate route against it, and express the three classifications as exhaustive `Record<EmployeeCaseTerminalKind, ...>` tables so adding a kind fails typecheck in all three. Add a round-trip property test asserting caseStatesFromTaskStatuses/taskStatus are mutually consistent.
- **证伪方式**：rg -n "terminalKind === '" packages/backend/src packages/frontend/src — today returns the three divergent tables above. Then grep 'closed-unmerged' under modules/digital-employee: it appears only in the adapter, never in any producer, confirming the dead branch.

### DE-07 · P2 · stale-assertion · CONFIRMED

**The RFC-310 architecture lock's literal ban is a single word, so business special-cases sit inside the very files it opens**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:104-131` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:125` · `design/RFC-310-rule-driven-development-digital-employee/os-architecture-manifest.json:71` · `packages/frontend/src/routes/employee-cases.$caseId.tsx:106-118` · `packages/frontend/src/routes/employee-cases.$caseId.tsx:126-140` · `packages/frontend/src/components/digital-employees/outcomes.ts:14-25`
- **证据**：The test builds `const literal = new RegExp(\`['\"]${manifest.genericTypeLiteralBan}['\"]\`)` with genericTypeLiteralBan = 'development' (manifest.json:70) and asserts no scanned generic file contains it. employee-cases.$caseId.tsx is in that scanned list (test :117), and at :113 it reads `const merged = terminalKind === 'merged'` and at :131-136 renders the literal copy 'MR 已合入' / 'MR merged'. 'merged' and 'MR' are source-control/development business concepts; the file is green. Same for components/digital-employees/outcomes.ts:15 ('merged', 'completed-no-change', 'no-change-confirmed'). The ban is also trivially evaded by a template literal, an imported constant, `startsWith('develop')`, or the identifier form (`developmentMissions` in F1).
- **架构影响**：The manifest's third guarantee — 'generic OS core and canvas never branch on the development employee type' — reads much stronger than it is. Everyone downstream (including the RFC-294 proposal at :164-165) treats it as the enforcement of the no-recoupling rule; in reality it blocks one exact spelling of one word. Business identity is already present in three of the files it scans.
- **现有守卫**：the test itself, but it is the finding: it turns red only for the literal 'development' in quotes, so a NEW business special-case using any other vocabulary ('merged', 'mr', 'pipeline', 'repair-conflict', 'closed-unmerged') adds cleanly.
- **拟建守卫**：Turn genericTypeLiteralBan into a ledger, not a word: (a) an exact allow-list of business vocabulary permitted in the generic roots, asserted with toEqual so both an unknown literal and a removed one fail; (b) derive the ban set from the registered type packages' descriptors (every typeId, every workItemRef, every contextTypeId a type package declares becomes a banned literal in the generic roots) so it grows automatically with the domain; (c) match identifiers as well as quoted strings.
- **证伪方式**：Add `const x = 'repair-conflict'` to packages/frontend/src/components/digital-employees/outcomes.ts and run tests/rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts — it stays green, proving the ban's width.

### DE-08 · P2 · duplicate-implementation · CONFIRMED

**task-execution re-declares the execution-contract implementation union instead of importing the kernel's exported schema**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/modules/task-execution/composition/digitalEmployeeExecution.ts:11-16` · `packages/backend/src/modules/task-execution/composition/digitalEmployeeExecution.ts:35-59` · `packages/backend/src/modules/execution-contract/domain/model.ts:348-365` · `packages/backend/src/modules/execution-contract/public/types.ts:12-22`
- **证据**：digitalEmployeeExecution.ts:37-59 hand-writes `exactRefSchema`, `agentImplementationSchema`, `workflowImplementationSchema`, `programImplementationSchema` and `implementationSchema = z.discriminatedUnion('kind',[...])` — a field-for-field copy of executionContractImplementationSchema (model.ts:352-365), including the same `/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/` digest regex and the same `z.enum(['bash','node','python'])`. The file already imports from '@/modules/execution-contract/public/types' at :10-16 (it pulls EXECUTION_CONTRACT_SCRIPT_INPUT_PORT, buildExecutionContractAgentPrompt and the participant type from exactly that entrypoint), so the single source of truth is one identifier away — but executionContractImplementationSchema, though exported at public/types.ts:18, is not taken.
- **架构影响**：The contract kernel's own definition of 'what may execute a contract' now has two authorities. Widening the union or tightening a field in execution-contract validates at authoring time but is silently rejected at launch time by the stale copy (or vice versa: the copy accepts an implementation the validator would refuse). This is a second source of truth for a kernel type, which RFC-294 classifies as boundary blur even when the kernel itself is clean.
- **现有守卫**：none — the two schemas are structurally identical today, so nothing observes their divergence; there is no test that parses a fixture through both.
- **拟建守卫**：Delete lines 37-59 and import `executionContractImplementationSchema` from '@/modules/execution-contract/public/types' (already an allowed manifest edge). Add an AST ratchet in rfc294-architecture-preflight: a module may not declare a `z.discriminatedUnion` whose variant literal set equals one already exported from another context's public surface; or minimally a source-text guard asserting the string `z.literal('program')` appears exactly once under packages/backend/src/modules.
- **证伪方式**：Add a fourth variant (e.g. kind:'mcp-tool') to execution-contract/domain/model.ts:352 and run the backend suite — authoring accepts it, digitalEmployeeExecution.ts:229's implementationSchema.parse throws at launch, and no test names the cause.

### DE-09 · P2 · boundary-blur · ADJUSTED

**Both architecture ratchets only see module-to-module edges, so routes/ and services/ deep-import module internals freely and the public/ surfaces are bypassed**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:120-133` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:238-243` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:47-53` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:127-147` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentConfig.ts:22-71` · `packages/backend/src/routes/code.ts:22-30` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/public/types.ts:1-15` · `eslint.config.js:107-121` · `design/RFC-294-backend-layered-target-architecture/plan.md:82`
- **证据**：rfc294-architecture-preflight's productionModuleUnits() (:120-133) visits only MODULES_ROOT, and crossContextViolations (:238-243) skips any file whose moduleLocation() is null — so a route or service is never a source of a violation. rfc310-architecture-lock ledgers only the consumers of `composition` and `composition/required-ports` (:47-53, :127-147) — deep imports into domain/application/engine/infrastructure are not enumerated at all. The result in source: routes/developmentConfig.ts:60-71 imports engine/policy/evaluatePolicy, engine/policy/workSelection, domain/facts, domain/factCell, domain/decision, domain/predicate, domain/digitalEmployee, domain/journeyProjection plus four infrastructure/sqlite* stores (:42-52); routes/developmentMissions.ts imports 14 internal paths; services/developmentDeliveryDeps.ts:16-18 imports application/ports/reconcilerPorts + two domain files; meanwhile development-automation/public/types.ts is 15 lines exporting one codec. code-capability is the same story: routes/code.ts and routes/capabilityTemplates.ts and services/capabilityTemplates.ts and services/demoSeed.ts all deep-import domain/ and application/, while public/queries.ts:1-18 documents itself as the exact five-name surface 'so nothing internal leaks'.
- **架构影响**：The exact-public-surface contract that RFC-294 §G2 rests on is enforced only against a caller class that barely exists (module-to-module, currently 2 debt rows). The caller class that actually holds the coupling — transport and legacy services — is unmeasured, so the modules' public/ directories are decorative for two of the five contexts in this family. Any migration that later tries to change a domain type has to chase route files that no ledger names.
- **现有守卫**：none. rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:1253-1261 pins a two-row cross-context debt list but structurally cannot observe routes/services; rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:145-146 pins composition consumers only. rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:74-101 DOES scan all of BACKEND_SRC for inbound edges — but only for the three OS contexts, which is why they are clean and these two are not.
- **拟建守卫**：Two mechanisms, either sufficient: (a) extend the RFC-310 inbound-manifest pattern (which already works and is green) to development-automation and code-capability — an exact externalImports ledger asserted with toEqual; (b) an eslint no-restricted-imports group in packages/backend/eslint.config.js:117-121 (the file already carries the RFC-282 runtime-fence precedent, mutation-proved by tests/rfc282-a1-eslint-boundary.test.ts) banning '@/modules/*/domain/*', '@/modules/*/application/*', '@/modules/*/engine/*', '@/modules/*/infrastructure/*' from packages/backend/src/{routes,services}/**, with an explicit expiring exemption list.
- **证伪方式**：rg -n '@/modules/development-automation/(domain|application|engine|infrastructure)' packages/backend/src/routes packages/backend/src/services — today ~25 edges. Run tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts and tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts: both green.

### DE-10 · P3 · forgeable-contract · ADJUSTED

**The execution-contract fixture prober validates program executors against a different environment than the one production gives them**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/modules/execution-contract/infrastructure/taskExecutionAdapter.ts:208-216` · `packages/backend/src/modules/task-execution/composition/digitalEmployeeExecution.ts:314-326` · `packages/backend/src/modules/execution-contract/domain/model.ts:489-492` · `packages/backend/src/modules/execution-contract/application/executionContractService.ts:135-136`
- **证据**：The fixture prober is the gate that decides whether a program implementation is 'valid' (executionContractService.ts:135-136 pushes its checks into the receipt). It runs the script with exactly one env var: taskExecutionAdapter.ts:212-215 `env: { DIGITAL_EMPLOYEE_TOOL_PARAMETERS_JSON: parameterValuesJson }`. Production runs the same script with three: digitalEmployeeExecution.ts:319-322 sets `DIGITAL_EMPLOYEE_TOOL_PARAMETERS_JSON`, `DIGITAL_EMPLOYEE_TOOL_CONNECTION_REF_JSON` and `DIGITAL_EMPLOYEE_TOOL_SLOT`. None of the three names is a shared constant — all six occurrences are bare string literals in two modules. Contrast the sibling members of the same contract family, which ARE single-sourced and correctly used by both sides: EXECUTION_CONTRACT_SCRIPT_INPUT_PORT / _ENV / _FILE_ENV (model.ts:489-492, used at taskExecutionAdapter.ts:222 and digitalEmployeeExecution.ts:243/316).
- **架构影响**：A 'valid' receipt is not evidence the program will work: a program that reads DIGITAL_EMPLOYEE_TOOL_SLOT or the connection ref passes the fixture with those unset and only fails in a live case. Worse, the drift is one-way silent — renaming the env in the runtime host still leaves the fixture green. This defeats the stated purpose of the fixture probe in the manifest ('fixture probes and exact output receipts') and makes the receipt forgeable in the sense that it certifies a contract the runtime does not honour.
- **现有守卫**：none — the two literals live in different modules with no shared symbol and no test asserts they are equal or that the fixture env equals the runtime env.
- **拟建守卫**：Export the three names as constants beside EXECUTION_CONTRACT_SCRIPT_INPUT_ENV in execution-contract/domain/model.ts, re-export via public/types.ts, and add a source-text ratchet asserting the string 'DIGITAL_EMPLOYEE_TOOL_' appears in exactly one production file. Stronger: hoist a single `buildContractProgramEnv(plan)` used by both the prober and the host, and a test that diffs `Object.keys(fixtureEnv)` against `Object.keys(runtimeEnv)`.
- **证伪方式**：rg -n 'DIGITAL_EMPLOYEE_TOOL_' packages/backend/src — today two files, six occurrences, zero constants. Fixed when the names resolve to one exported constant each.

### DE-11 · P3 · escape-hatch · ADJUSTED

**Two more Digital Employee ports are optional-with-fallback, and one of the fallbacks disables the platform-tool immutability invariant**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:133-157` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:146-151` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/composition.ts:240` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/composition.ts:246` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/composition.ts:331-340` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/composition.ts:443-456` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/application/authoringService.ts:219` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/application/authoringService.ts:303-312` · `packages/backend/src/modules/digital-employee/application/ports/authoringStore.ts:53-57`
- **证据**：The guard test at :133-167 hard-locks exactly one port as non-optional (`expect(composition).not.toContain('executionContracts?:')` for three files). Its neighbours in the same options object are optional with silent defaults: composition.ts:240 `platformTools?:` -> composition.ts:331-340 returns `{ list:()=>[], getRevision:()=>null, isPlatformTool:()=>false }` (the same shape as EMPTY_DIGITAL_EMPLOYEE_PLATFORM_TOOL_CATALOG, authoringStore.ts:53-57, which authoringService.ts:219 also falls back to). authoringService.ts:303-312 is the only enforcement of 'platform tools are immutable' — `if (this.#platformTools.isPlatformTool(input.toolId)) throw ConflictError('employee-platform-tool-readonly')`. With the fallback in place that check is unconditionally false, so every platform tool becomes editable. composition.ts:246 `platformWorkItems?:` similarly defaults to a hand-written 'blocked' envelope (:443-456). Both bootstraps do pass the real ports today (server.ts:482, cli/start.ts:1190), so this is a latent hatch, not a live break.
- **架构影响**：The kernel advertises an immutability invariant that is contingent on an optional constructor argument. Any future assembler, test harness or second bootstrap that omits platformTools silently converts platform-owned tool revisions into user-editable drafts — the exact 'optional parameter that lets one caller bypass the kernel's invariant' shape the guard was written to prevent for executionContracts.
- **现有守卫**：none — rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:157-167 bans only the string 'executionContracts?:' in three named files; 'platformTools?:' and 'platformWorkItems?:' sit two lines away in the same file and are green.
- **拟建守卫**：Generalise the ratchet: read ComposeDigitalEmployeeOptions with the TS AST and assert the set of optional members equals an explicit reviewed ledger (toEqual), so making any port optional — or newly optionalising one — requires editing the ledger. Separately, make platformTools required (both bootstraps already pass it) and delete EMPTY_DIGITAL_EMPLOYEE_PLATFORM_TOOL_CATALOG, or move the immutability check to a place that cannot be nulled out.
- **证伪方式**：Delete `platformTools:` from packages/backend/src/server.ts:482 and run the backend suite: it typechecks, the app composes, and platform tools become editable with no test failing.

### DE-12 · P3 · missing-guard · ADJUSTED

**The code-capability retirement ratchet is module-scoped, and a live production writer to the retired round tables survives outside it**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:274-287` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:296-319` · `packages/backend/src/services/demoSeed.ts:249-297` · `packages/backend/src/cli/start.ts:662-665` · `packages/backend/src/modules/code-capability/application/codeMatrixQuery.ts` · `design/RFC-294-backend-layered-target-architecture/proposal.md:157-159`
- **证据**：The ratchet at :276-287 walks `legacyRoot = src/modules/code-capability` and asserts no line matches /\.(?:insert|update|delete)\(/ (one documented exemption: application/templateUpstreamStatus.ts, which does write at :213). The T108 ratchet at :296-319 additionally asserts six writer-private tables have zero consumers anywhere. Neither covers the three round tables that remain readable: packages/backend/src/services/demoSeed.ts inserts into codeWorkItems (:250-264), codeWorkRounds (:266-278) and codeRoundStages (:283-296), and cli/start.ts:663-664 calls seedDemoContent(db) on every daemon boot. The writer is outside modules/code-capability, so the module-scoped scan cannot see it, and codeWorkItems/codeWorkRounds/codeRoundStages are not in the six-table list.
- **架构影响**：RFC-294 proposal.md:157-159 describes code-capability as a '历史查询/兼容资源岛' whose only live write exception is capability-template sync. In source there is a second one. More importantly the ratchet's shape means a NEW writer added anywhere under routes/ or services/ tomorrow — the exact 'writer 悄悄复活' signal T104/T105 says it exists to catch — turns nothing red, because the guard's blind spot is precisely where a revived writer would naturally be written.
- **现有守卫**：none for writes outside the module root. tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:276-287 covers inside-module writes only; :296-319 covers six other tables only.
- **拟建守卫**：Flip the ratchet from 'no write verbs inside the module' to 'no write verbs against these table symbols anywhere': enumerate the code-capability table set (codeFindings, codeWorkItems, codeWorkRounds, codeRoundStages, codeAiAttempts, codeTriggerDeliveries + the six already covered), scan all of packages/backend/src, and assert the set of files containing `.insert(`/`.update(`/`.delete(` against them equals an explicit exemption ledger (capabilityTemplates sync + a decision on demoSeed).
- **证伪方式**：rg -n 'codeWorkItems|codeWorkRounds|codeRoundStages' packages/backend/src/services/demoSeed.ts — three .insert() calls at :250, :267, :285. Then run tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts: green.

### DE-13 · P3 · escape-hatch · CONFIRMED

**The implicit-declaration hook is an optional kernel parameter that soft-fails malformed declarations into a development-specific legacy table**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/modules/execution-contract/composition.ts:13-15` · `packages/backend/src/modules/execution-contract/infrastructure/taskExecutionAdapter.ts:24` · `packages/backend/src/modules/execution-contract/infrastructure/taskExecutionAdapter.ts:82-106` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/composition/employeeTypePackage.ts:1412-1436` · `packages/backend/src/modules/execution-contract/public/commands.ts:5-22` · `packages/backend/src/modules/execution-contract/domain/model.ts:105-116` · `packages/backend/src/services/agent.ts:143` · `packages/backend/src/services/agent.ts:375`
- **证据**：Two problems in the same field. (1) Soft-fail: taskExecutionAdapter.ts:94-105 does `declarationsSchema.safeParse(agent.frontmatterExtra.executionContracts)` and then `declaredContractRefs: declared.success ? declared.data : fallbackDeclarations` — so an agent whose executionContracts field is PRESENT BUT MALFORMED does not fail validation, it silently degrades to developmentImplicitAgentContractDeclarations, which maps `frontmatterExtra.digitalEmployeeTemplate` through the hardcoded legacy table at employeeTypePackage.ts:1412-1425. (2) Two validators for one field: the save-path reconciler uses a hand-rolled predicate (public/commands.ts:5-22: contractId is a non-empty string, version a positive integer) while the read-path uses zod's machineIdSchema (model.ts:105-109: /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]*$/, max 240). The hook is also declared optional in the kernel (composition.ts:13-15, defaulted to `() => []` at taskExecutionAdapter.ts:86) although both bootstraps pass it (server.ts:475, cli/start.ts:1184).
- **架构影响**：Concrete divergence: save an agent with executionContracts=[{contractId:'!!!',version:1}]. services/agent.ts:143 -> reconcileCreatedAgentExecutionContractPorts sees a valid declaration and appends the managed 'agent-result' output port; the validator then rejects the same field, reports zero declared contracts, and falls back to the legacy digitalEmployeeTemplate projection — so the agent's declared contract identity comes from a compatibility table it never declared, while its port layout says otherwise. The 'agent-contract-declared' check (executionContractService.ts:151-159) is then answering about a different declaration than the one on disk.
- **现有守卫**：none — no test feeds a malformed executionContracts value through both the reconciler and the validator, and no test asserts the two predicates agree.
- **拟建守卫**：Use executionContractRefSchema as the single predicate on both paths (replace the hand-rolled checks in public/commands.ts:5-22 with `z.array(executionContractRefSchema).min(1).safeParse(...)`), and make the fallback conditional on ABSENCE only: `declared.success ? declared.data : (field === undefined ? fallback : throw ValidationError)`. Add a property test asserting hasExecutionContractDeclarations(x) === declarationsSchema.safeParse(x).success over a fixture set including '!!!', a 300-char id, and version 0.
- **证伪方式**：In a scratch test, call reconcileCreatedAgentExecutionContractPorts with frontmatterExtra.executionContracts=[{contractId:'!!!',version:1}] (outputs gains 'agent-result') and then declarationsSchema.safeParse on the same value (success:false) — the two predicates disagree on a value the product accepts.

## FE — 前端公共组件库与数据层（11 条：P1 0 / P2 2 / P3 9）

### FE-01 · P2 · duplicate-implementation · CONFIRMED

**JoinModeField hand-rolls the `.segmented` control instead of using <Segmented>, and its buttons carry a class that does not exist in CSS**

- **锚点**：`packages/frontend/src/components/canvas/inspector/JoinModeField.tsx:59-79` · `packages/frontend/src/components/canvas/inspector/AgentSingleEdit.tsx:87` · `packages/frontend/src/components/Segmented.tsx:80-190` · `packages/frontend/src/components/Segmented.tsx:160-176` · `packages/frontend/src/styles.css:5240-5286` · `packages/frontend/src/styles.css:9714` · `packages/frontend/tests/ux-source-ratchets.test.ts:131-234` · `packages/frontend/tests/rfc286-f1-dead-class-extinction.test.ts:36-57`
- **证据**：JoinModeField renders `<div className="segmented" role="group">` with two `<button className={current === 'any' ? 'is-active' : ''} aria-pressed={...}>`. The shared primitive renders `role="radiogroup"` + `role="radio"` + `aria-checked` + roving `tabIndex` + Arrow/Home/End nav, and its options carry `segmented__option` / `segmented__option--active` (Segmented.tsx:160-176). styles.css defines `.segmented`, `.segmented__option`, `.segmented__option--active` (5240, 5260, 5278) and the 36px min-height rule keyed on `.segmented__option` (22375) — it defines NO `.segmented .is-active` and no bare-button rule. So the fork's buttons are literally unstyled inside a styled pill container, and lose radio semantics + keyboard navigation.
- **架构影响**：The reuse rule the repo treats as a product-level invariant (`.segmented` = one component) now has a live escapee inside canvas/inspector, which is the exact place the next inspector field will be copy-pasted from. It also re-opens the dead-class bug class RFC-286 F1 was created to close.
- **现有守卫**：none — tests/ux-source-ratchets.test.ts checks role=dialog / input / textarea / table only; tests/rfc286-f1-dead-class-extinction.test.ts:36-56 hard-codes exactly three class names (`error-text`, `checkbox-row`, `form-error`); and `rg -l 'JoinModeField|node-join-mode' packages/frontend/tests` returns nothing — the component has zero tests of any kind.
- **拟建守卫**：AST ratchet in tests/ux-source-ratchets.test.ts: for every JSX element whose className string contains the token `segmented`, assert the element is `<Segmented …>` (an allowlist Map keyed by file, like ROLE_DIALOG_ALLOWLIST, for any justified exception). Pair it with a generalized dead-class ratchet (see F-DEADCLASS) so `is-active` inside a `.segmented` subtree cannot pass.
- **证伪方式**：Open JoinModeField.tsx:59-79 and grep styles.css for `is-active` in a `.segmented` context (`rg 'segmented.*is-active|is-active.*segmented' packages/frontend/src/styles.css` → 0 hits). If a `.segmented button` or `.segmented .is-active` rule exists somewhere I missed, the styling half of this finding is wrong; the a11y half (role=group+aria-pressed vs radiogroup+radio, no arrow keys, no roving tabindex) stands regardless.

### FE-02 · P2 · missing-guard · CONFIRMED

**Five className families are applied in live code but defined nowhere in CSS — the exact bug class RFC-286 F1 fixed, with the F1 guard hard-coded to three names so it cannot see them**

- **锚点**：`packages/frontend/src/components/code/DevelopmentConfigEditor.tsx:169` · `packages/frontend/src/routes/code.missions.new.tsx:416` · `packages/frontend/src/components/canvas/inspector/ScriptEdit.tsx:298` · `packages/frontend/src/components/agents/DependencyTreePreview.tsx:114` · `packages/frontend/src/components/webhooks/DeliveriesPanel.tsx:539` · `packages/frontend/src/components/NodeDetailDrawer.tsx:322-328` · `packages/frontend/src/styles.css:7076-7085` · `packages/frontend/src/styles.css:11574` · `packages/frontend/src/styles.css:15492-15557` · `packages/frontend/tests/rfc286-f1-dead-class-extinction.test.ts:36-57` · `packages/frontend/tests/task-output-panel-source-guards.test.ts:22-26`
- **证据**：`grep -c` in packages/frontend/src/styles.css returns 0 for `page__subtitle` (5 call sites: routes/code.policies.tsx:72, code.config.detail.tsx:213, code.missions.new.tsx:416 and :926, code.policies.$id.tsx:243), 0 for `form-section__hint` (9 call sites across components/code/DevelopmentConfigEditor.tsx:169,240,310,599,803, DigitalEmployeePlaybookEditor.tsx:302,417,1275, routes/code.config.detail.tsx:873 — note `.form-section__title` and `.form-section__body` DO exist at styles.css:7076,7085, so the missing one is not a naming illusion), and 0 for `inspector-hint` (7 call sites incl. ScriptEdit.tsx:298,305,329 and settings/CodeHostsSection.tsx:223,255). `task-output-card__body` (NodeDetailDrawer.tsx:322,328; webhooks/DeliveriesPanel.tsx:539) appears in styles.css exactly once — inside a comment at line 11574 — because tests/task-output-panel-source-guards.test.ts:24 actively asserts `.task-output-card__body {` must NOT exist. `dep-tree__empty` / `dep-tree__error` (DependencyTreePreview.tsx:103,114,122,128,136,141) are undefined while eleven sibling `.dep-tree__*` rules are (styles.css:15479-15557); `dep-tree__error` carries `role="alert"`, i.e. an error UI rendered as unstyled bare text.
- **架构影响**：CSS is the one shared kernel with no compiler. RFC-286 F1 diagnosed exactly this ('渲染出来是无样式裸文本（真实 bug）') and shipped a guard that enumerates three literal class names instead of asserting the invariant, so the class of bug it names reappeared within days in the newest feature area (components/code/* landed 2026-08-19, after the 2026-08-12 audit).
- **现有守卫**：none for a new instance. tests/rfc286-f1-dead-class-extinction.test.ts:36 iterates `['error-text','checkbox-row']` plus one regex for `form-error`; tests/task-output-panel-source-guards.test.ts:24 pins the CSS deletion but never checks whether anything still applies the class; tests/styles-tokens.test.ts and tests/theme-css-ratchet.test.ts validate CSS variables, never class existence.
- **拟建守卫**：Replace the three-name allowlist with the invariant: walk src/**/*.tsx, collect every static className token (plus tokens inside template literals, the two-form scan F1 already implements), and assert each token is defined as a selector in styles.css or prose.css — with a small explicit allowlist for third-party/library tokens (`nodrag`, `nopan`, `nowheel`, xyflow) and a snapshot of the current offenders that may only shrink. This is F1's own scanner with the literal list swapped for the rule.
- **证伪方式**：`grep -c 'page__subtitle' packages/frontend/src/styles.css` etc. A hit >0 for any of the five names, or a rule reaching them through a parent selector (none of these are element-typed), falsifies that entry. `task-output-card__body`'s only CSS hit is a comment — verify by `sed -n '11570,11580p'`.

### FE-03 · P3 · duplicate-implementation · ADJUSTED

**TASK_QUERY_KEYS is a single source of truth only for the three WS hooks; every consuming useQuery/invalidate still spells the same key as a literal, and the guard only watches the producing side**

- **锚点**：`packages/frontend/src/lib/query-keys.ts:1-23` · `packages/frontend/tests/task-sync-rules.test.ts:137-149` · `packages/frontend/src/routes/tasks.detail.tsx:213-274` · `packages/frontend/src/components/NodeDetailDrawer.tsx:107-128` · `packages/frontend/src/components/tasks/WorkflowSyncBanner.tsx:55-58` · `packages/frontend/src/components/inventory/RuntimeInventorySection.tsx:39` · `packages/frontend/src/components/inventory/StartupVerificationBanner.tsx:25` · `design/system-commons-unification-audit-2026-08-12.md:124`
- **证据**：`TASK_QUERY_KEYS.nodeRuns(id)` returns `['tasks', id, 'node-runs']` (query-keys.ts:22). NodeDetailDrawer.tsx:107 writes `queryKey: ['tasks', taskId, 'node-runs', nodeRunId, 'events']`, :127 invalidates `['tasks', taskId, 'node-runs']`, WorkflowSyncBanner.tsx:56-58 and DynamicWorkflowPanel.tsx:93-94 do the same, RuntimeInventorySection.tsx:39 and StartupVerificationBanner.tsx:25 extend the same literal prefix. The RFC-286 F4 lock (task-sync-rules.test.ts:138-148) reads only `src/hooks/useTaskSync.ts`, `useTasksSync.ts`, `useClarifyWs.ts` and rejects literal keys there. Its own comment states the failure mode — '字面 key 与 route 侧靠肉眼同步，改一边即静默失联（只剩轮询兜底）' — and then guards exactly one of the two sides.
- **架构影响**：The invariant that matters is 'producer key === consumer key'. Guarding only the producer converts a symmetric contract into an asymmetric one: renaming a segment inside query-keys.ts passes every test while silently detaching ~10 consumer surfaces from live WS invalidation, degrading them to the 15s polling fallback with no red anywhere.
- **现有守卫**：partial — packages/frontend/tests/task-sync-rules.test.ts:136-149 turns red only if a literal is added inside one of the three hook files. Adding a NEW literal `['tasks', …]` consumer anywhere else is green.
- **拟建守卫**：Extend the same zero-literal scan to the consuming side: any `queryKey:` / `invalidateQueries({ queryKey:` whose first array element is a string in {tasks, reviews, clarify, task-questions, task-clarify-directives} must come from the factory. Grandfather today's offenders as an EXACT per-file occurrence allowlist (the two-way honesty form already used in tests/async-state-gate-source-guard.test.ts) so the count can only go down.
- **证伪方式**：Read task-sync-rules.test.ts:138 (`for (const hook of ['useTaskSync.ts','useTasksSync.ts','useClarifyWs.ts'])`) — if the loop or another test file also scans components/ and routes/, the guard is not one-sided. `rg "queryKey: \['tasks'" packages/frontend/src --glob '!**/*.test.*'` lists the unguarded consumers.

### FE-04 · P3 · forgeable-contract · ADJUSTED

**ErrorDetails decides 'safe to disclose resource names' from a plain object key name, so the ACL disclosure authority is structurally forgeable by any emitter**

- **锚点**：`packages/frontend/src/components/ErrorDetails.tsx:10-16` · `packages/frontend/src/components/ErrorDetails.tsx:104-117` · `packages/backend/src/services/resourceAcl.ts:336-367` · `packages/backend/src/services/skill.ts:460-463` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc203-refs-acl.test.ts:79-286` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc202-lifecycle-exits.test.ts:396-435` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc223-reverse-delete-races.test.ts:58-61` · `packages/frontend/tests/rfc203-error-details.test.tsx:53-83`
- **证据**：ErrorDetails.tsx:104 is `const visible = namedArray(d.visibleScheduled) ?? namedArray(d.visible)`; when that shape parses, :106-117 renders the referencing resources BY NAME. The only thing separating 'principal-filtered' from 'raw' is the key spelling. The authorized producer is `DisclosedRefs { visible, hiddenCount }` (resourceAcl.ts:336-353), spread into ConflictError details at skill.ts:463, plugin.ts:240,262, mcp.ts:250,284, agent.ts:688,716. Any future emitter that attaches `{ visible: rows.map(r => ({id, name})) }` without going through `discloseRefs*` gets other users' private resource names rendered, and the component's own header comment calls this the 'ACL iron rule (Codex design-gate P1)'.
- **架构影响**：A security invariant is carried by a naming convention across a package boundary. There is no nominal type, no marker field, no runtime proof — exactly the 'caller can construct the kernel's authority value structurally' shape. The blast radius is cross-tenant name disclosure in a delete/rename refusal dialog.
- **现有守卫**：partial and in the wrong direction — tests/rfc203-error-details.test.tsx:63-83 proves the five KNOWN legacy keys (`referencedBy`/`scheduledTaskIds`/`workflows`/`agents`/`taskIds`) render count-only, and :53-62 proves the principal-aware shape renders names. Nothing turns red when a new emitter names a raw array `visible`, nor when a future ErrorDetails branch renders names from another key. `rg -l discloseRefs packages/backend/tests` → no test file.
- **拟建守卫**：Two halves. Backend: a source ratchet asserting the literal key `visible:` may appear in a thrown-error `details` object ONLY inside services/resourceAcl.ts (i.e. every other occurrence must be a spread of a `DisclosedRefs` value) — or, better, brand it (`__disclosed: 'refs'` written only by discloseRefsSync) and have ErrorDetails.tsx:104 require the brand. Frontend: a source lock asserting ErrorDetails.tsx contains exactly one name-rendering branch and that its predicate references the brand.
- **证伪方式**：Read ErrorDetails.tsx:104-117 — if the branch checks anything beyond key shape (a brand, a type guard imported from shared) the finding is wrong. Then `rg 'visible:' packages/backend/src/services --glob '!**/*.test.*'` to see whether any emitter besides discloseRefs*/workflow.ts:745/workgroups.ts:935 already produces the key; today none does, which is why this is a latent contract hole rather than a live leak.

### FE-05 · P3 · duplicate-implementation · CONFIRMED

**WorkflowStarterDialog re-implements <ChoiceCards> as a raw radiogroup and drops its roving-tabindex contract**

- **锚点**：`packages/frontend/src/components/workflow-editor/WorkflowStarterDialog.tsx:310-332` · `packages/frontend/src/components/ChoiceCards.tsx:41-118` · `packages/frontend/src/styles.css:9426-9454` · `packages/frontend/tests/ux-source-ratchets.test.ts:131-234`
- **证据**：WorkflowStarterDialog renders `<div className="workflow-starter__catalog" role="radiogroup">` containing `<button type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={selected} className={`workflow-starter__card …`}>` with `<strong>` label + `<span>` description — structurally the same widget ChoiceCards owns (label + description + selected card). The fork sets NO `tabIndex`, so every card is a tab stop (ChoiceCards.tsx:49-52 computes a single roving tab stop precisely because 'a native disabled button is skipped by Tab, which would make the whole group keyboard-unreachable'), and implements no Arrow-key movement (ChoiceCards.tsx:53-70).
- **架构影响**：Two radiogroup-of-cards implementations with divergent keyboard contracts; the primitive's a11y fixes (Codex P2 in ChoiceCards) do not reach the editor's starter picker, and the next card picker has two templates to copy from.
- **现有守卫**：none — tests/ux-source-ratchets.test.ts has no role="radiogroup"/role="radio" rule; tests/workflow-starter-dialog.test.tsx exercises behaviour, not primitive provenance.
- **拟建守卫**：Add a role ratchet to tests/ux-source-ratchets.test.ts alongside the role="dialog" one: any JSX with `role="radiogroup"` or `role="radio"` must live in components/Segmented.tsx or components/ChoiceCards.tsx, or in a documented allowlist Map entry.
- **证伪方式**：Read WorkflowStarterDialog.tsx:318-330 and check for a `tabIndex` attribute or an `onKeyDown` on the cards — I found neither. If ChoiceCards cannot express this catalog (it takes `label: string`, not ReactNode), the correct fix is extending the primitive, but the finding that a second implementation exists still stands.

### FE-06 · P3 · business-special-case · ADJUSTED

**ModelSelect's provider ordering hard-codes opencode, and its runtime-switching prop plus a second query key are dead API surface**

- **锚点**：`packages/frontend/src/components/ModelSelect.tsx:206-221` · `packages/frontend/src/components/ModelSelect.tsx:34` · `packages/frontend/src/components/ModelSelect.tsx:55` · `packages/frontend/src/components/RuntimeList.tsx:555-556` · `packages/frontend/tests/model-select.test.tsx:55-70` · `packages/frontend/tests/runtime-claude-frontend.test.tsx:28-29` · `packages/frontend/tests/runtime-claude-frontend.test.tsx:116-135`
- **证据**：`groupByProvider` (ModelSelect.tsx:206) is a generic exported helper whose comparator is `const aIsOpencode = a.startsWith('opencode')` (:216-219) with the comment 'opencode CLI sorts opencode* providers first, then alphabetical. Mirror.' It is applied unconditionally to whatever `/api/runtime/models` returns, including the curated Claude Code list and any custom registered runtime. Separately, `runtime?: 'opencode' | 'claude'` (:55) and `RUNTIME_CLAUDE_MODELS_QUERY_KEY` (:34) have zero external consumers: `rg 'ModelSelect' packages/frontend/src` yields exactly one call site, RuntimeList.tsx:556, which passes only `runtimeName`. AgentForm.tsx:300 records 'AgentForm no longer renders ModelSelect.'
- **架构影响**：A runtime-agnostic picker encodes one runtime's presentation rule for all runtimes — the 'generic helper correct only for one caller family' pattern. The dead `runtime`/claude-key branch is a live-looking second code path that a future caller will reach for, resurrecting a duplicate cache entry for the same endpoint.
- **现有守卫**：none — tests/model-select.test.tsx:55-69 asserts groupByProvider's opencode-first behaviour, i.e. the existing test PINS the special case rather than bounding it. Nothing flags the unreachable prop (lint/depcheck do not see unused optional props or unused exported consts).
- **拟建守卫**：Move the ordering rule out of the component into the runtime descriptor (`orderProviders?: (a,b)=>number` supplied per registered runtime, defaulting to alphabetical) and assert in test that ModelSelect.tsx contains no runtime-name literal. Separately, delete the `runtime` prop + RUNTIME_CLAUDE_MODELS_QUERY_KEY and add them to a 'retired API cannot return' string ratchet (the shape tests/settings-card-surfaces.test.ts already uses for retired chrome).
- **证伪方式**：`rg 'ModelSelect' packages/frontend/src --glob '!**/*.test.*'` — if a second call site passes `runtime={'claude'}`, the dead-surface half is wrong. Read ModelSelect.tsx:213-220 for the comparator; if the sort were driven by data from the endpoint response rather than a literal, the special-case half is wrong.

### FE-07 · P3 · business-special-case · CONFIRMED

**The shared dialog chrome carries a hardcoded dark-mode color override for one specific feature's primary button**

- **锚点**：`packages/frontend/src/styles.css:13758-13783` · `packages/frontend/tests/ux-source-ratchets.test.ts:214` · `packages/frontend/tests/theme-css-ratchet.test.ts:59-93`
- **证据**：`:root[data-theme='dark'] .dialog__overlay:has(.agent-import) .btn--primary { background: #2759a5; border-color: #2759a5; }` plus its `:hover` and the duplicated `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark)` pair. These are raw hex values that override the tokenized `--accent-fill`/`--on-accent` contract of `.btn--primary` for exactly one feature, identified by a body-content class (`.agent-import`) that the Dialog primitive does not own and does not know about.
- **架构影响**：The design-system's button variant stops being a single source of truth for its own color the moment a feature can override it by name from inside the shared dialog selector; and the override is expressed in raw hex, outside the token layer that styles-tokens.test.ts exists to protect.
- **现有守卫**：none — tests/theme-css-ratchet.test.ts:60-72 only asserts every `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark)` block is scoped to `:root:not([data-theme])` (which this override satisfies) and :74-93 that no `var()` is undefined; tests/styles-tokens.test.ts asserts token declarations exist, never that colors flow through them.
- **拟建守卫**：CSS ratchet: no declaration matching `background|color|border-color: #hex` may appear in a rule whose selector contains `.btn`, `.status-chip`, `.form-input`, `.select__`, or `.dialog__` — those must use `var(--…)`. Grandfather today's offenders as an exact-count allowlist so the number can only fall.
- **证伪方式**：`sed -n '13763,13782p' packages/frontend/src/styles.css`. If the hex values were replaced by tokens, or if `.agent-import` were a Dialog `panelClassName` variant rather than a body class, the finding weakens (the latter would make it F-CSS-DIALOG-HATCH instead).

### FE-08 · P3 · escape-hatch · CONFIRMED

**Four feature-name literals now appear inside the shared `.dialog__overlay` rule surface, re-specifying overlay geometry the Dialog primitive owns**

- **锚点**：`packages/frontend/src/styles.css:8530-8534` · `packages/frontend/src/styles.css:10145-10147` · `packages/frontend/src/styles.css:23013-23017` · `packages/frontend/src/styles.css:26386-26388` · `packages/frontend/src/components/Dialog.tsx:18` · `packages/frontend/src/components/Dialog.tsx:55` · `packages/frontend/src/components/Dialog.tsx:373-374` · `packages/frontend/tests/ux-source-ratchets.test.ts:211-234` · `packages/frontend/tests/overlay-ux-inventory.test.ts:367-379`
- **证据**：`.dialog__overlay:has(> .workflow-editor-surface-dialog)`, `.dialog__overlay:has(> .workflow-validation-dialog)`, `.dialog__overlay:has(.mobile-nav-dialog)` and `.dialog__overlay:has(> .script-code-editor-dialog)` each reset the overlay's `padding` / `align-items` / `justify-content`. The primitive exposes only `size: 'sm'|'md'|'lg'|'full'` (Dialog.tsx:17) and a free-form `panelClassName` (Dialog.tsx:56-57) that is documented as applying to the PANEL — yet callers use it as a hook to restyle the OVERLAY, which is chrome the caller does not own. Two of the four are the same variant twice ('edge-to-edge, zero gutter': validation + script editor) and two more are the same variant twice ('full-height side sheet': editor surface + mobile nav).
- **架构影响**：The dialog size enum has an unbounded, per-feature shadow vocabulary living in the stylesheet. Every new sheet/edge-to-edge dialog adds a fifth and sixth feature name to the shared kernel's own selector instead of a fifth value to its enum, so the primitive's geometry contract can never be reasoned about from its TypeScript surface.
- **现有守卫**：none — tests/ux-source-ratchets.test.ts:211-234 bans a fixed list of RETIRED class names but nothing forbids new `.dialog__overlay:has(.<feature>)` rules; no test enumerates the Dialog size vocabulary.
- **拟建守卫**：Promote the two recurring shapes into the enum (`size: 'sheet-start' | 'sheet-end'` and `gutter: 'none'`), then add a CSS ratchet: `.dialog__overlay` may only be qualified by `.dialog--<size>` selectors — any `:has()` on a non-`dialog--*` class is a violation, with an exact-count allowlist for the residue.
- **证伪方式**：`rg '\.dialog__overlay' packages/frontend/src/styles.css` and read each hit. If the `:has()` selectors keyed on `dialog--*` modifiers rather than feature class names, the escape hatch would be closed and this finding void.

### FE-09 · P3 · duplicate-implementation · CONFIRMED

**TaskOutputPanel re-implements TabBar's roving-tabindex + arrow-key contract because the primitive has no vertical orientation**

- **锚点**：`packages/frontend/src/components/TaskOutputPanel.tsx:93-166` · `packages/frontend/src/components/TabBar.tsx:27` · `packages/frontend/src/components/TabBar.tsx:69-86` · `packages/frontend/src/components/TabBar.tsx:141-420` · `packages/frontend/tests/task-output-panel-source-guards.test.ts:33-42`
- **证据**：TaskOutputPanel defines its own `moveFocus`/`handleTabKeyDown` (`ArrowDown`/`ArrowUp`/`Home`/`End`/Space/Enter, tabRefs map, rovingIndex state) at :93-124 and renders `role="tablist" aria-orientation="vertical"` with `.task-outputs-panel__option` buttons at :130-165. TabBar owns the identical contract (`activation` mode, roving tabindex, tabDomIds) but ships only a horizontal `.tabs` chrome with overflow scroll controls and exposes no `orientation` prop (TabBarBaseProps, TabBar.tsx:68-86).
- **架构影响**：Two tab keyboard implementations to keep in sync; the second one is pinned in place by a guard (task-output-panel-source-guards.test.ts:33-42 asserts the hand-rolled roles must stay), so the fork is now contractually protected rather than scheduled for convergence.
- **现有守卫**：none for a NEW vertical tablist elsewhere; the existing guard protects THIS fork rather than preventing another. tests/ux-source-ratchets.test.ts has no tablist rule.
- **拟建守卫**：Add `orientation?: 'horizontal' | 'vertical'` to TabBar, migrate this panel, then extend the ux-source-ratchets role scan to require any `role="tablist"` to originate in components/TabBar.tsx (allowlist Map for justified exceptions, same shape as ROLE_DIALOG_ALLOWLIST).
- **证伪方式**：`rg 'orientation' packages/frontend/src/components/TabBar.tsx` — if the prop exists the fork is gratuitous rather than forced; either way it is a second implementation. Read TaskOutputPanel.tsx:99-124 to confirm the key handling is hand-written, not delegated.

### FE-10 · P3 · duplicate-implementation · CONFIRMED

**Three independent implementations of the same `{items, nextCursor}` infinite-query pattern, and the newest 'unified' one has a single caller**

- **锚点**：`packages/frontend/src/hooks/usePagedList.ts:1-37` · `packages/frontend/src/hooks/useTaskOperationsPage.ts:12-44` · `packages/frontend/src/routes/intent.tsx:178` · `packages/frontend/src/routes/repos.tsx:198`
- **证据**：usePagedList.ts:1-8 states outright '与 useTaskOperationsPage 同构(该 hook 先于本原语存在,暂各自成文;后续 intent/deliveries 等接入时收敛到这里)'. `rg 'useInfiniteQuery' packages/frontend/src` returns exactly three production call sites: usePagedList.ts:27, useTaskOperationsPage.ts:18, routes/intent.tsx:178 — each repeating `initialPageParam: null`, `getNextPageParam: last => last.nextCursor ?? undefined`, `refetchOnWindowFocus: false`. `rg 'usePagedList'` shows one consumer, routes/repos.tsx:198.
- **架构影响**：The primitive was created to be the single source of truth and instead became a third variant. Pagination semantics (end-of-list detection, keepPreviousData, maxPages) can now drift per surface, and 'the shared one' has the weakest claim by adoption.
- **现有守卫**：none — nothing prevents a fourth `useInfiniteQuery` call site, and nothing marks the two pre-existing ones as debt in a machine-checkable place.
- **拟建守卫**：Source ratchet: `useInfiniteQuery` may be imported only by hooks/usePagedList.ts, with an exact-count allowlist grandfathering useTaskOperationsPage.ts and routes/intent.tsx that may only shrink (the two-way honesty form from tests/async-state-gate-source-guard.test.ts).
- **证伪方式**：`rg -n 'useInfiniteQuery' packages/frontend/src --glob '!**/*.test.*'`. If only usePagedList appears, the convergence already happened and this is stale.

### FE-11 · P3 · stale-assertion · CONFIRMED

**The 2026-08-12 commons audit's frontend row ('六大高危面…实测 100% 归一零逃逸', 'Segmented 37/TabBar 17 手拼零') is now false in source**

- **锚点**：`design/system-commons-unification-audit-2026-08-12.md:31` · `design/system-commons-unification-audit-2026-08-12.md:41` · `design/system-commons-unification-audit-2026-08-12.md:69` · `packages/frontend/src/components/canvas/inspector/JoinModeField.tsx:59-79` · `packages/frontend/src/components/workflow-editor/ConnectionDialog.tsx:455-540` · `packages/frontend/src/components/canvas/inspector/ClarifyEdit.tsx:72-94` · `packages/frontend/src/components/code/DevelopmentConfigEditor.tsx:169`
- **证据**：Line 31 scores the frontend '高' with '六大高危面（modal/tabs/segmented/骨架/WS/chip）实测 100% 归一零逃逸'; line 41 spells out 'Segmented 37/TabBar 17 手拼零' and lists dead classes as exactly three names (N16, line 69). Source today: JoinModeField.tsx:59-79 is a hand-rolled segmented control (added 1a7da7c04, 2026-08-17 — five days after the audit baseline), and five additional undefined class families are live (F-DEADCLASS), the largest of them introduced with components/code/* in a762a7075 (2026-08-19). Neither the audit nor design/plan.md records the drift.
- **架构影响**：Per repo doctrine the ledger lags the code, and this row is exactly the kind of '已归一, 零逃逸' claim a future RFC will cite to skip re-verification — the audit doc is named as an input contract for RFC-284…289. A stale 'zero escapees' line is worse than no line: it converts an open regression into a closed question.
- **现有守卫**：none — no test or CI step re-checks any claim in design/system-commons-unification-audit-2026-08-12.md; docs and source are unlinked.
- **拟建守卫**：For each numeric/absolute claim the audit makes about the frontend ('手拼零', 'bare fetch 一处', 'new WebSocket 唯一'), land the corresponding source ratchet in packages/frontend/tests and cite the test path in the doc row. A claim without a named guard file should not be written as a fact in the ledger.
- **证伪方式**：`git log --diff-filter=A -1 -- packages/frontend/src/components/canvas/inspector/JoinModeField.tsx` (1a7da7c04, 2026-08-17) versus the audit's stated baseline (≈e7361b02, 2026-08-12). If the audit had a later baseline, the claim was wrong when written rather than stale; either way the row does not match HEAD.

## G — 守卫机制自身（元审计）（12 条：P1 3 / P2 6 / P3 3）

### G-01 · P1 · missing-guard · UNVERIFIED

**The RFC-294 preflight only scans modules/ as the FROM side — legacy services/routes/cli deep-imports into module internals are invisible to every mechanism**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:18` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:120-133` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:240-242` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentMissions.ts:34-49` · `packages/backend/src/cli/start.ts:16-17`
- **证据**：productionModuleUnits() walks only MODULES_ROOT (= packages/backend/src/modules), and crossContextViolations does `const from = moduleLocation(unit.path); if (from === null) continue` — so a file that is not itself inside modules/ is never even a candidate edge source. dependency-cruiser has no rule mentioning `modules/` at all (.dependency-cruiser.cjs:41-149), and eslint.config.js:30-46 fences only services/runtime/{opencode,claudeCode}. Reality: routes/developmentMissions.ts imports `@/modules/development-automation/infrastructure/gitBaselineReader`, `.../infrastructure/sqliteAdmissionLookup`, `.../infrastructure/sqliteMissionStore`, `.../infrastructure/missionReadModels`, `.../application/commands/launchMission`, `.../domain/pipelineManifest`; cli/start.ts imports `.../infrastructure/sqliteMissionStore` and `@/modules/task-execution/infrastructure/sqliteTaskLifecycleEventPublisher`. RFC-294 design.md:112-115 explicitly says bootstrap must not deep-import `modules/*/infrastructure/**`.
- **架构影响**：The single most-used direction of travel during the migration — old layer reaching into a new module — is completely unguarded. Every new RFC that lands a module can wire it up through infrastructure deep imports and the whole guard stack stays green, so the module boundary that the preflight enforces among modules is a boundary only among modules, not a boundary the rest of the daemon has to respect.
- **现有守卫**：none for development-automation, task-execution, task-catalog, code-capability, intent, source-control, work-start. Only identity-access (rfc305-architecture-lock.test.ts:354-376) and digital-employee/event-center/execution-contract (rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:73-101 against os-architecture-manifest.json) have a full inbound-consumer ledger.
- **拟建守卫**：Extend rfc294-architecture-preflight to a second scan set: walk ALL of packages/backend/src, and for every file outside modules/ that names `@/modules/<ctx>/…`, require the target to be `public/{commands,queries,participants,events,types}` or `composition` (bootstrap only), with the current offenders pinned as an exact toEqual debt list identical in discipline to the existing 2-row cross-context ledger. Alternatively add a dependency-cruiser rule `no-outside-to-module-internals` from `^packages/backend/src/(services|routes|cli|ws|mcp|auth|util)/` to `^packages/backend/src/modules/[^/]+/(?!public/|composition)` and ledger the current edges in scripts/depcheck.ts.
- **证伪方式**：Add `import { EvidenceStore } from '@/modules/development-automation/infrastructure/evidenceStore'` to any file under packages/backend/src/services/ and run the full gate (`bun run depcheck` + `bun test packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts packages/backend/tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts`). Everything stays green.

### G-02 · P1 · boundary-blur · UNVERIFIED

**Nothing stops a bounded-context module from importing the legacy services/ god modules — 33 such edges exist and only 2 of 12 contexts have an outbound fence**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/modules/task-execution/application/applySourceTerminationEffect.ts:25-27` · `packages/backend/src/modules/task-execution/application/resolveNodeActivation.ts:24` · `packages/backend/src/modules/identity-access/infrastructure/sqliteUserAccessRepository.ts:14` · `packages/backend/src/modules/event-center/infrastructure/customEventObserverProgram.ts:6-7` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:244-245`
- **证据**：crossContextViolations only considers edges whose TARGET resolves to another module (`const target = targetLocation(edge); if (target === null || target.context === from.context) continue`), so `@/services/*` targets return null and are dropped. Counting `from '@/services/` inside modules/: task-execution 19, integration 6, execution-contract 3, event-center 2, code-capability 2, identity-access 1. Concrete violations of RFC-294 layering: modules/task-execution/application/applySourceTerminationEffect.ts imports `@/services/task`, `@/services/lifecycle` and `@/services/reviewMutationCoordinator` from the APPLICATION layer; modules/identity-access/infrastructure/... imports `@/services/mcpRuntimeTestTransitions`. Only integration (rfc303-architecture-lock.test.ts:23-33, banning services/{task,gc,scheduler,execution}) and development-automation (rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:175-186, banning hono/@/routes/@/server/@/ws/@/mcp/@/db per layer) have any outbound fence, and neither is generalised.
- **架构影响**：A module can take a value dependency on the very god modules RFC-294 exists to dissolve, and its `public/` surface can stay pristine while its application layer is transitively bound to services/scheduler.ts and services/task.ts. That is boundary blur in the direction the target architecture cares most about, and it is the mechanism by which the task/scheduler cycle family (17 ledgered no-circular entries) can propagate into the new modules.
- **现有守卫**：partial only: rfc303-architecture-lock.test.ts:23-33 covers modules/integration/{domain,application,infrastructure}; rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:175-186 covers modules/development-automation. Ten contexts have none.
- **拟建守卫**：Add a per-layer outbound rule to rfc294-architecture-preflight (or a dependency-cruiser rule `no-module-to-legacy-services`) asserting that no file under modules/*/{domain,application,engine,public} imports `@/services/**`, `@/routes/**`, `@/ws/**`, `@/mcp/**` or hono, with today's 33 edges pinned as an exact, per-file toEqual debt list carrying why + removeWhen in the depcheck ledger style.
- **证伪方式**：Add `import { runTask } from '@/services/scheduler'` to packages/backend/src/modules/task-catalog/application/*.ts and run the full gate. Nothing reds (depcheck's no-circular only fires if a cycle actually closes).

### G-03 · P1 · boundary-blur · UNVERIFIED

**Module shape / intra-layering / composition-purity locks exist for only 5 of 12 bounded contexts; task-execution — the RFC-294 execution kernel — has none and carries a non-canonical inbound/ layer**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:29-53` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:101-125` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:313-334` · `packages/backend/src/modules/task-execution/inbound/directTaskInitiator.ts:1` · `design/RFC-294-backend-layered-target-architecture/design.md:105-115`
- **证据**：The closed top-level-directory set, the intra-module layering rules (domain purity, application/engine/public not touching infrastructure or transport, required-ports composition-only) and the 'composition entrypoint has no business branching and no db import' rule are all bound to `MODULE_ROOT = modules/development-automation` (rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:27). rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts adds a manifest for three more contexts, and rfc305 adds one for identity-access. Nothing covers task-execution, task-catalog, code-capability, intent, source-control or work-start. Consequence visible today: modules/task-execution has a top-level `inbound/` directory holding directTaskInitiator.ts, which is not one of RFC-294's per-module layers (design.md:105-108 places inbound adapters at the app-level `adapters/inbound/{http,mcp,…}`, not inside a module); modules/intent contains only `domain/`; modules/task-catalog has no `domain/`; modules/work-start is an EMPTY `public/` directory with zero files. The only globally-enforced structural rule is the public-entrypoint filename check inside publicSurfaceViolations (rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:516-518).
- **架构影响**：RFC-294 fixes the execution chain as TaskEngine → WrapperRuntime → NodeExecutor → ExecutionKernel inside task-execution, but that context has no structural guard at all: a new top-level layer, an application file importing its own infrastructure, or a composition.ts that queries the DB and branches on business identity would all land green. The one module with a full lock is the newest one; the kernel is unguarded.
- **现有守卫**：none for task-execution / task-catalog / code-capability / intent / source-control / work-start. rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts covers development-automation only; rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts covers three contexts; rfc305-architecture-lock.test.ts:303-352 covers identity-access public exports.
- **拟建守卫**：Generalise rfc310-architecture-lock into a data-driven per-context manifest (one JSON like os-architecture-manifest.json, keyed by every directory under modules/) enforcing: (a) top-level dirs ⊆ {domain, application, engine, ports, infrastructure, public, composition} + composition.ts, with today's `inbound/` pinned as an explicit ledgered deviation; (b) the intra-module layering matrix; (c) composition.ts contains no `if/switch/for/while` at statement start and no @/db value import; (d) a non-empty public/ (which would immediately flag work-start).
- **证伪方式**：Create packages/backend/src/modules/task-execution/adapters/foo.ts importing `hono` and `@/db/schema` from what should be a pure layer, and run the full backend suite. No architecture test names task-execution's directory shape, so it stays green.

### G-04 · P2 · missing-guard · UNVERIFIED

**RFC-217 G5 mode-branch ratchet has no stale detection and an Infinity escape hatch — three free business-branch slots exist right now**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc217-architecture-locks.test.ts:176-210` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc217-architecture-locks.test.ts:193-194` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc217-architecture-locks.test.ts:207-208`
- **证据**：The assertion is `expect(count).toBeLessThanOrEqual(cap)` with `const cap = SNAPSHOT[rel] ?? (rel.startsWith('strategies/') ? Infinity : 0)`. Because it is `<=` and not `===`, a count that has DROPPED below its snapshot leaves the slack permanently reusable. Measured today: SNAPSHOT says dwActions.ts:2 / room.ts:1, actual `mode === '` counts are 0 and 0 (verified by grep). That is 3 workgroup-mode business branches that can be re-added with zero test turning red. Separately, every file under services/workgroup/strategies/ has cap = Infinity, so unbounded ad-hoc `mode === '…'` branching inside the strategy files is permitted — the opposite of the registry-dispatch pattern the lock was written to enforce.
- **架构影响**：The one ratchet that directly targets 'business special case inside a shared kernel' (workgroup mode scatter, the exact regression that RFC-217 T3b collapsed from 40+ sites) silently loosens over time. Every other exact-count ratchet in this repo (depcheck stale check, rfc284 spawn stale check, rfc282 both-direction counts, s14/rfc144 exact counts) fails on a stale entry; this one does not, making it the weakest member of an otherwise consistent family.
- **现有守卫**：none — nothing detects that a rfc217 SNAPSHOT row is now higher than reality, and nothing bounds strategies/**.
- **拟建守卫**：Change the assertion to exact equality against the snapshot (`expect(count).toBe(cap)`) so both growth and un-pruned slack fail, exactly as scripts/depcheck.ts:403 (stale) and rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet.test.ts:145-148 already do; replace the `Infinity` arm with an explicit per-strategy-file cap, or better, assert that strategy files select behaviour from a `satisfies Record<WorkgroupMode, Strategy>` table rather than comparing the literal at all.
- **证伪方式**：Run `grep -c "mode === '" packages/backend/src/services/workgroup/dwActions.ts packages/backend/src/services/workgroup/room.ts` — both return 0 while the snapshot claims 2 and 1. Then add two `if (mode === 'dynamic_workflow')` branches to dwActions.ts and run `bun test packages/backend/tests/rfc217-architecture-locks.test.ts`: it stays green.

### G-05 · P2 · escape-hatch · UNVERIFIED

**The god-surface budget and the public type-taint scanner both under-measure: any type imported from outside modules/ scores as one leaf and is never traversed**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:1063-1080` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:271-290` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:40-41` · `packages/backend/src/modules/integration/public/participants.ts:1-30`
- **证据**：shapeStats resolves a TypeReferenceNode via declarationForTypeReference → resolveUnit, and resolveUnit only searches `units` (which is productionModuleUnits(), i.e. modules/** only). When resolution fails it falls through to `return { methods: 0, topLevelFields: 0, transitiveLeaves: 1, unionVariants: 1 }`. Therefore a public contract that embeds a large DTO from @agent-workflow/shared or @/platform is scored as ONE transitive leaf against a budget of 24. Live example: modules/integration/public/participants.ts:1 imports `StartAgentTask, StartTask, StartWorkgroupTask` from @agent-workflow/shared and embeds them as `payload` inside WorkStartTarget — the whole request DTO is one leaf. The same blind spot exists in publicSurfaceViolations: FORBIDDEN_TYPE_IMPORT (:40-41) lists @/{auth,cli,config,db,mcp,routes,server,services,util,ws} but NOT @/platform or @/embed, and resolveUnit cannot follow those specifiers either, so a public type referencing `@/platform/persistence/transactionScope` is neither banned nor traversed. That same file also carries `Readonly<Record<string, string>>` in a cross-context participant — `Record` is not in FORBIDDEN_TYPE_NAME, so an open map passes the public-surface check.
- **架构影响**：The two rules whose whole purpose is to stop a mega-port or an adapter type leaking through a public entrypoint have a systematic under-count exactly at the seam where DTOs actually enter — the shared package and the platform layer. A wire request schema can become a cross-context contract without the god-surface ratchet noticing, which is how 'public surface' and 'HTTP wire' quietly become the same thing again.
- **现有守卫**：none — nothing bounds the size of a shared-package type embedded in a module public contract, and nothing bans @/platform in a public type.
- **拟建守卫**：Include packages/shared/src/** and packages/backend/src/platform/** in the preflight's `units` set for RESOLUTION purposes only (keep modules/** as the rule subject), so shapeStats and the taint walk traverse into them; add @/platform and @/embed to FORBIDDEN_TYPE_IMPORT; add `Record` (with a non-literal key) to FORBIDDEN_TYPE_NAME for public entrypoints, mirroring the `Record<string, unknown>` ban already enforced for development-automation required-ports at rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:220.
- **证伪方式**：Add `export type Mega = Readonly<{ payload: import('@agent-workflow/shared').StartTask }>` to any modules/*/public/types.ts and run `bun test packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts` — godSurfaceViolations reports nothing because StartTask scores 1 leaf.

### G-06 · P2 · missing-guard · UNVERIFIED

**Frontend design-system enforcement is a per-file rollout whitelist, not a ratchet — a brand-new bespoke dialog, empty state or select reds nothing**

- **锚点**：`packages/frontend/tests/dialog-grep.test.ts:16-60` · `packages/frontend/tests/data-table-callsite.test.ts:17-45` · `packages/frontend/tests/empty-loading-callsite.test.ts:13-30` · `packages/frontend/tests/ux-source-ratchets.test.ts:83-127`
- **证据**：dialog-grep.test.ts asserts only that three named files (AgentImportDialog.tsx, repos/BatchImportDialog.tsx, routes/reviews.detail.tsx) render <Dialog> and no longer carry their own legacy `__overlay`/`__panel` classNames. data-table-callsite.test.ts names four files. empty-loading-callsite.test.ts names three routes. None of them scans the rest of packages/frontend/src for a NEW bespoke overlay/panel/empty-state. The only whole-frontend scans are in ux-source-ratchets.test.ts, and they cover exactly five shapes (alert/prompt/confirm, role="dialog", bare text <input>, bare <textarea>, native <table>) — all three of which carry hand-editable file allowlists with no stale detection. Nothing anywhere bans a native `<select>` in JSX (verified: `grep -rn '<select' packages/frontend/src` returns only comments and CSS), even though CLAUDE.md §Frontend UI consistency makes that an explicit prohibition, and nothing bans a `<button>` without a `.btn` class or a new `.xxx__overlay` selector added to styles.css.
- **架构影响**：The design-system commons (Dialog, Select, Form primitives, ChipsInput, EmptyState/LoadingState, .btn, .segmented) are enforced retroactively — the guards prove past retrofits stayed retrofitted, but do not stop the next feature from becoming a visual island. That inverts the intent of the repo rule, which classifies a non-reused surface as a product-level bug.
- **现有守卫**：partial: ux-source-ratchets.test.ts:154-210 covers role="dialog", bare <input>, bare <textarea>, native <table> repo-wide. No mechanism covers native <select>, bespoke overlay/panel classNames, bare `<div className="muted">` empty states, or non-.btn buttons.
- **拟建守卫**：Add to ux-source-ratchets.test.ts three more repo-wide AST rules with the same allowlist+stale discipline: (a) `isIntrinsic(node, 'select')` is forbidden outside components/Select.tsx and MultiSelect.tsx; (b) any JSX className string literal matching /__(overlay|panel|backdrop|modal)\b/ is forbidden outside components/Dialog.tsx; (c) a styles.css scan asserting no NEW `.*__overlay|.*__panel` selector beyond a pinned list. Add a stale check to all allowlists (every allowlisted file must still actually contain the pattern), copying rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:176-187.
- **证伪方式**：Create packages/frontend/src/components/Foo.tsx rendering `<div className="foo__overlay"><div className="foo__panel"><select><option/></select></div></div>` and run `bun run test:frontend`. Green.

### G-07 · P2 · missing-guard · UNVERIFIED

**Whole-src ratchets claim mutation verification in prose only — just four mechanisms carry an in-repo negative fixture**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc217-architecture-locks.test.ts:23` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:16-18` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet.test.ts:16-17` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:332-357`
- **证据**：rfc217 states 'Every lock here has been mutation-verified (break it → this file reds)'; rfc310 states '变异实证（2026-08-18，开发期手工做过一轮）'; rfc284-spawn states '变异实证（写入时验证过）'. None of the three ships a fixture that re-proves the matcher today. The mechanisms that DO ship one are: rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:332-357 (known-bad fixtures through the same matchers, plus a comment-stripping negative case), rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:1140-1248 (five mutated source-unit fixtures), rfc282-a1-eslint-boundary.test.ts:33-98 (the real ESLint config against bad code), and route-ux-inventory.test.ts:802-851 (parser fixtures including a must-throw case). That is 4 of roughly 20 architecture mechanisms.
- **架构影响**：These ratchets are regex/AST scanners over evolving source. When the production code shape drifts (a `.set({\n status:` reformatted, a `Bun.spawn` behind a helper alias, a differently-spelled import), the scanner stops matching and the ratchet reports zero offenders — indistinguishable from compliance. Without a fixture that must stay red, the decay is silent and permanent.
- **现有守卫**：none — no meta-test asserts that architecture-lock files contain a negative fixture, and rfc287-t1-scheduler-source-lock-inventory.test.ts (the guard-of-guards) only inventories which tests read scheduler.ts, not whether their matchers still bite.
- **拟建守卫**：Require every whole-src scanner to export its matcher and add a sanity describe feeding one known-bad and one known-good fixture through it — the exact pattern already in rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:332-357. Then extend rfc287-t1-scheduler-source-lock-inventory.test.ts into a general guard-of-guards: for every test file whose name matches /architecture|boundary|ratchet|lock|guard/, assert the file contains at least one assertion that would fail on a fabricated violation (e.g. a `describe('sanity'` block), maintained as an exact two-way ledger.
