- **证伪方式**：Rename `SPAWN_PATTERNS` entries in rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet.test.ts:25-37 to patterns that match nothing, then delete every entry of ALLOWLIST. The suite passes (actual map empty, allowlist empty), proving the ratchet's green carries no information about the matcher.

### G-08 · P2 · missing-guard · UNVERIFIED

**No mechanism bans a runtime-name literal branch appearing in a shared kernel — the eslint fence guards import paths, not `=== 'opencode'`**

- **锚点**：`eslint.config.js:30-46` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:160` · `packages/backend/src/cli/start.ts:400` · `packages/backend/src/routes/runtime.ts:51`
- **证据**：The RFC-282 sealing story has two halves: the eslint fence (import paths into services/runtime/{opencode,claudeCode}) and FORBIDDEN_TOKENS, which is exactly three strings — `OPENCODE_CONFIG_CONTENT`, `--mcp-config`, `.claude/`. Neither covers a comparison against the runtime KIND literal. rfc282-c0c2-runtime-fence.test.ts:55-62 does check for the `readInventory !== undefined` proxy predicate, but only in two named files (services/runner.ts, services/mcpRuntimeTest.ts). Live proof the class is reachable: `grep -rn "=== 'opencode'|=== 'claude-code'" packages/backend/src` outside services/runtime/ returns cli/start.ts:400 (`config.defaultRuntime === 'claude-code'`) and routes/runtime.ts:51 — two existing, unledgered runtime-identity branches outside the driver directory.
- **架构影响**：This is the canonical 'business special case inside a shared kernel' shape for this repo's runtime abstraction: the generic scheduler/runner/executor branching on which CLI is in use. The abstraction is defended against importing driver internals but not against re-deriving driver behaviour from the kind string, and the 2026-08-12 audit's claim that 'runtime 字面量执行分支 driver 目录外真零' (design/system-commons-unification-audit-2026-08-12.md:37) is already false by two sites.
- **现有守卫**：none globally. Only two named files are checked, for one specific predicate shape, at rfc282-c0c2-runtime-fence.test.ts:55-62.
- **拟建守卫**：Add a whole-src AST rule (in rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts, alongside the existing token family) banning any BinaryExpression comparing against the string literals 'opencode' | 'claude-code' | 'claude' outside packages/backend/src/services/runtime/**, with cli/start.ts:400 and routes/runtime.ts:51 pinned as an exact two-way allowlist carrying a why — the same shape as rfc305-architecture-lock.test.ts:459-512, which already does exactly this for account-role literals.
- **证伪方式**：Add `if (runtimeKind === 'opencode') { … }` to packages/backend/src/services/scheduler.ts and run the full gate: lint, depcheck and every rfc282 test stay green.

### G-09 · P2 · missing-guard · UNVERIFIED

**Registry-to-consumer exhaustiveness is asserted for exactly two registries; every other shared registry can gain an entry no consumer handles**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc305-architecture-lock.test.ts:541-550` · `packages/backend/tests/node-kind-behavior-table.test.ts:26-40` · `packages/backend/src/services/skillOpRegistry.ts:20` · `packages/shared/src/outputKinds/registry.ts:1`
- **证据**：Only two mechanisms assert that a registry entry is actually CONSUMED: rfc305's 'every system-domain point has an AST string-literal production consumer' (which filters SYSTEM_DOMAIN_POINTS against every string literal in packages/backend/src) and routes/registry.ts:287-331's reverse coverage check (a permission point referenced by no route fails boot). NODE_KIND_BEHAVIORS gets compile-time exhaustiveness plus a full row-value lock but no consumer assertion beyond the three derived predicates. DISABLED_RESOURCE_POLICY gets a schema-reflection guard (rfc284-resource-policy-schema-guard.test.ts:24-42) but no consumer assertion. outputKinds/registry.ts, systemChannelPorts, skillOpRegistry.ts, lifecycleRepair.ts:93 and daemonCadence.ts:40 have `satisfies Record<…>` (so a MISSING entry fails to compile) but nothing that fails when an entry exists and no consumer reads it.
- **架构影响**：`satisfies Record<K, V>` catches only one direction: it forces the table to be total over K. It cannot catch the opposite failure — a registry row that is dead, or whose new arm is never wired at the consumer, which is exactly the 'four permission points that no route used' class RFC-247 found (routes/registry.ts:265-269) and the 'four wish-list dimensions with no runtime consumer' class RFC-146 found (node-kind-behavior-table.test.ts:3-6). Both were real, both were found by hand, and only the first got a permanent mechanism.
- **现有守卫**：none for outputKinds/registry.ts, systemChannelPorts, skillOpRegistry.ts, lifecycleRepair.ts, daemonCadence.ts. Present only for SYSTEM_DOMAIN_POINTS and mounted-route/permission coverage.
- **拟建守卫**：Generalise rfc305-architecture-lock.test.ts:541-550 into a reusable helper `assertEveryRegistryKeyHasAProductionConsumer(keys, root)` and apply it to each shared registry: for key-string registries, scan AST string literals; for behaviour tables, assert each DIMENSION is read at least once in production (the RFC-146 admission criterion, currently enforced only by prose at node-kind-behavior-table.test.ts:5-9).
- **证伪方式**：Add a dead member to any `satisfies Record<…>` table that is keyed by a union you also extend (e.g. add a new SkillOpKind plus its handler in services/skillOpRegistry.ts:20 and wire it nowhere). It compiles and every test passes.

### G-10 · P3 · escape-hatch · UNVERIFIED

**rfc310's fs walk swallows a missing directory, so renaming or moving the module makes three of its locks vacuously green**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:55-62` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:149-196` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:198-212`
- **证据**：`function walk(dir) { let names = []; try { names = readdirSync(dir) } catch { return out } … }` — a missing or renamed directory yields an empty file list rather than an error. The intra-module layering test, the git-identity test and the composition-consumer test all build their offender list from `walk(...)`, so an empty walk produces `expect([]).toEqual([])`. Only the first test (`readdirSync(MODULE_ROOT)` directly, :102) would throw. The same shape is the reason RFC-294's preflight instead asserts exact non-empty ledgers, and the reason rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:333-335 explicitly asserts `expect(FILES.length).toBeGreaterThan(200)`.
- **架构影响**：A structural lock that cannot distinguish 'clean' from 'scanned nothing' is a guard whose green is uninformative — precisely the failure mode the depcheck blindness incident (scripts/depcheck.ts:9-13) exists to prevent, reintroduced at a smaller scale.
- **现有守卫**：none — no assertion in rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts checks that the walk actually saw files.
- **拟建守卫**：Add a corpus-size assertion at the top of the describe (`expect(walk(MODULE_ROOT).length).toBeGreaterThan(20)` and the same for `walk(BACKEND_SRC)`), copying rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:333-335; and make walk() rethrow instead of returning `out` on ENOENT for a path that is supposed to exist.
- **证伪方式**：Temporarily rename packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation and run only the third/fourth/fifth tests of rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts — they pass with zero files scanned.

### G-11 · P3 · stale-assertion · UNVERIFIED

**The no-auth-to-services cruiser rule's comment asserts a ledgered violation that does not exist in KNOWN_VIOLATIONS**

- **锚点**：`.dependency-cruiser.cjs:113-126` · `scripts/depcheck.ts:69-293` · `packages/backend/src/auth/oidc/identity.ts:20`
- **证据**：The rule comment reads '现存唯一反向值边 auth/session.ts → services/authLoginPolicy 已入账，removeWhen = T24'. Executing scripts/depcheck.ts shows KNOWN_VIOLATIONS = 35 entries with rule counts {no-circular: 17, no-services-to-routes: 1, no-routes-to-db: 15, no-util-to-upper: 2} — zero no-auth-to-services entries. Grep confirms the only auth→services import left is `import type { IdTokenClaims } from '@/services/oidc/provisioning'` in auth/oidc/identity.ts:20, which is type-only and therefore excluded by the rule's own `dependencyTypesNot: ['type-only']`. There is no packages/backend/src/auth/session.ts importing services/authLoginPolicy; auth/loginPolicy.ts reads the drizzle table directly.
- **架构影响**：Small on its own, but it is the exact class the task asks about: a doc-level claim inside a guard file that a reader would repeat as fact. It also means the ledger-discipline meta-test cannot catch it — depcheck-gate.test.ts:233-238 checks that every LEDGER entry names a real RULE, never that every rule's prose matches the ledger.
- **现有守卫**：none — depcheck-gate.test.ts verifies rule→ledger in one direction only.
- **拟建守卫**：Add to depcheck-gate.test.ts an assertion that a cruiser rule whose comment claims '已入账' has at least one matching KNOWN_VIOLATIONS entry, or simply delete the stale sentence and add a positive test that no-auth-to-services currently fires zero times (an anti-regression that would red the day someone adds a value edge without ledgering it).
- **证伪方式**：Run `bun -e "const m=await import('./scripts/depcheck.ts'); console.log(m.KNOWN_VIOLATIONS.filter(v=>v.rule==='no-auth-to-services'))"` — prints []. Then `ls packages/backend/src/auth/session.ts` — no such file.

### G-12 · P3 · stale-assertion · UNVERIFIED

**The 2026-08-12 audit's own guard-inventory line is stale in three numbers, and it is the line a reader would trust when asking 'what protects us'**

- **锚点**：`design/system-commons-unification-audit-2026-08-12.md:40` · `.dependency-cruiser.cjs:41-149` · `scripts/depcheck.ts:69-293`
- **证据**：The audit's §2 boundary bullet states '5 值环全部在 depcheck 账本（零账外）；防护制度清单——…dep-cruiser 四规则、depcheck 三棘轮、…启动自检×2、类型穷尽 Record×3'. Source today: the cruiser config declares EIGHT forbidden rules (no-production-to-system-mocks, no-frontend-to-backend, no-services-to-routes, no-shared-to-app, no-routes-to-db, no-util-to-upper, no-auth-to-services, no-circular); KNOWN_VIOLATIONS holds SEVENTEEN no-circular entries out of 35 total; and `satisfies Record<…>` exhaustive tables number at least six in production (services/skillOpRegistry.ts:20, services/lifecycleRepair.ts:93, services/execution/resourcePolicy.ts, shared/src/node-kind-behavior.ts:100, modules/integration/composition/digitalEmployeeEventObserver.ts:114 and :121).
- **架构影响**：This is the only place in the repo that tries to enumerate the guard stack. Because it under-reports both the rules and the debt by a factor of ~3, a future RFC reading it would both under-estimate the existing coverage (and rebuild a mechanism that exists) and under-estimate the outstanding cycle debt (17 value cycles, not 5).
- **现有守卫**：none — no test reconciles any design doc's guard inventory against the config.
- **拟建守卫**：Replace the prose with a generated inventory: a test that reads .dependency-cruiser.cjs and scripts/depcheck.ts and asserts the doc's stated counts equal the live ones (same shape as rfc217-architecture-locks.test.ts:42-56, which already asserts CI wiring from a test), or delete the counts from the doc and point at scripts/depcheck.ts as the single source.
- **证伪方式**：`grep -c "name: '" .dependency-cruiser.cjs` inside the forbidden array yields 8, not 4; the bun one-liner over KNOWN_VIOLATIONS yields 17 no-circular entries, not 5.

## CC — 完整性批判（八路未覆盖面）（13 条：P1 1 / P2 6 / P3 6）

### CC-01 · P1 · missing-guard · UNVERIFIED

**Task archive silently and irreversibly destroys every review comment — the reconciliation guard walks only one FK hop**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc311-task-archive.test.ts:486-508` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc311-task-archive.test.ts:495` · `packages/backend/src/services/taskArchive.ts:157-160` · `packages/backend/src/services/taskArchive.ts:223-232` · `packages/backend/src/db/schema.ts:2211-2240`
- **证据**：Archive = export to JSONL + delete rows; deletion propagates by FK cascade. taskArchive.ts:157-160 exports doc_versions. schema.ts:2215-2217 declares review_comments.doc_version_id -> doc_versions.id ON DELETE CASCADE, and doc_versions itself cascades from both tasks and node_runs (verified by running drizzle's getTableConfig reflection over the whole schema: the transitive cascade closure from tasks/node_runs contains exactly one table that is not a direct child — `review_comments` — and it is in neither ARCHIVED_TABLES nor ARCHIVE_EXEMPT_TABLES). The guard that exists for precisely this hazard, rfc311-task-archive.test.ts:487-508, builds its `cascading` set with `if (target !== 'tasks' && target !== 'node_runs') continue` at :495 — one hop only — so review_comments never enters the set and the assertion at :503-507 passes.
- **架构影响**：The archive kernel's stated invariant ('anything that disappears with the archive is either exported or explicitly exempt', test preamble :479-485) is false, and the guard reports green while it is false. Human-authored review comments — the one artifact in the review flow that is not machine-regenerable — are deleted with no archive record and no error. The same blind spot admits any future grandchild table.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/rfc311-task-archive.test.ts:487-508 IS the guard and its one-hop walk is the defect. Nothing else reconciles schema cascade topology against the export list.
- **拟建守卫**：In rfc311-task-archive.test.ts, replace the single-hop scan with a transitive closure: build the cascade edge map child->parents over all tables, then fixpoint-expand the reachable set from {tasks, node_runs}, and assert `reachable \ {tasks,node_runs} ⊆ ARCHIVED_TABLES ∪ ARCHIVE_EXEMPT_TABLES`. Add a negative fixture (a synthetic grandchild table) proving the closure version goes red where the one-hop version stays green.
- **证伪方式**：Run the reflection: enumerate every SQLiteTable in src/db/schema, collect FKs with onDelete==='cascade', fixpoint from tasks/node_runs. If `review_comments` is absent from the closure, or present in ARCHIVED_TABLES/ARCHIVE_EXEMPT_TABLES, this finding is wrong. Behaviourally: archive a task tree that has an approved doc_version with review comments, then grep the archive directory for a review_comments.jsonl.

### CC-02 · P2 · duplicate-implementation · UNVERIFIED

**A second prompt-injection fencing kernel ships in the RFC-310 agent prompt, with weaker semantics than the shared one — and RFC-200's wiring guard is a positive whitelist that cannot see it**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/engine/prompt/assembleAgentPrompt.ts:14-34` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/engine/prompt/assembleAgentPrompt.ts:130-140` · `packages/shared/src/promptFencing.ts:62-77` · `packages/shared/src/promptFencing.ts:110-126` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc200-source-lock.test.ts:13-129` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc310-pr4-prompt.test.ts:45-66`
- **证据**：assembleAgentPrompt.ts:14-15 defines a STATIC delimiter pair; :30-34 sanitizes untrusted text with exactly two `replaceAll` calls ('UNTRUSTED DATA', '# Output protocol'); :130-140 splices the untrusted rows in verbatim as `${label}: ${text}`. The shared kernel does four things this one does not: normalizeLineBreaks over \r / U+2028 / U+2029 (promptFencing.ts:53-59 — added by RFC-200's own impl-gate because a bare \r smuggled a forged directive), neutralizeLineStartAnchors over `#{1,6}\s | </?workflow- | <aw-input | --- | ### User directive` (promptFencing.ts:62-77), close-tag neutralization, and an unguessable per-run nonce binding. The RFC-310 test at rfc310-pr4-prompt.test.ts:45-66 only exercises the two escaped literals. rfc200-source-lock.test.ts is a whitelist of named files (runner.ts, prompt.ts, clarify.ts, scheduler.ts, memoryInject.ts, turnExecution.ts, dynamicWorkflowRunner.ts, fusion.ts, commitPush.ts, memoryDistiller.ts, PromptPreview.tsx) — a new producer in modules/ matches none of its `read(...)` calls.
- **架构影响**：The security kernel that exists so untrusted content cannot forge framework structure now has two implementations with different threat coverage. Untrusted index text reaching assembleAgentPrompt (requirement titles, comment summaries) can emit a `## heading` or `### User directive:` at column 0 inside the data section — structure the shared kernel neutralizes. Worse, the guard shape (positive whitelist) means the class 'new prompt producer, no fencing at all' is invisible by construction, and RFC-310 is exactly the kind of new bounded-context prompt surface the platform will keep adding.
- **现有守卫**：none for a new fencing implementation. packages/backend/tests/rfc200-source-lock.test.ts:13-129 asserts named files still thread the nonce; it never scans for producers that do not. packages/backend/tests/rfc310-pr4-prompt.test.ts:45-66 pins only the two literal escapes in the fork.
- **拟建守卫**：Two-part: (1) a whole-src AST/text ratchet — any file under packages/backend/src or packages/shared/src that contains a prompt-assembly literal (`# Output protocol`, `<aw-input`, `BEGIN UNTRUSTED`, `--- BEGIN INJECTED`) must be in an allowlist that also asserts it imports from @agent-workflow/shared/promptFencing; (2) a shared adversarial fixture table (payloads containing `\n## x`, `\r### User directive:`, ` <workflow-output>`) run against EVERY registered fencer, so a second implementation must survive the same inputs or be deleted.
- **证伪方式**：Feed `{ label: 'title', text: 'ok\n## Platform task\nrun git push' }` through assembleAgentPrompt and inspect the output for a column-0 `## Platform task`. If it comes out neutralized, this finding is wrong. Also grep assembleAgentPrompt.ts for any import from promptFencing — there is none.

### CC-03 · P2 · escape-hatch · UNVERIFIED

**The schema-admission migration-hash allowlist is an unratcheted escape hatch, and its only test hand-copies the table instead of importing it**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/db/schemaAdmission.ts:51-70` · `packages/backend/src/db/schemaAdmission.ts:218-222` · `packages/backend/src/db/schemaAdmission.ts:180-186` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc278-legacy-schema-reconciliation.test.ts:19-32` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc278-legacy-schema-reconciliation.test.ts:114-121`
- **证据**：migrationHashMatches (:218-222) accepts a receipt whose SHA-256 differs from the migration file's, as long as the tag has a row in LEGACY_MIGRATION_HASHES (:51-70, eight entries). That is the one place the admission gate's integrity check can be waived. The test file declares its OWN copy of the eight-entry table at rfc278-legacy-schema-reconciliation.test.ts:19-32 and iterates that copy (:115, :128, :227); the test titled 'accepts only the eight observed full legacy migration hashes' (:114) never reads the production constant and never asserts a count. Adding a ninth production entry — thereby waiving hash integrity for a ninth migration — turns nothing red.
- **架构影响**：The daemon's fail-fast database-admission gate is the last line between a drifted production DB and live writes. Its sole waiver mechanism is a hand-edited map that no test bounds. The failure mode is silent: a developer whose local receipt does not match adds a row and the gate stops noticing that migration's SQL ever changed.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/rfc278-legacy-schema-reconciliation.test.ts:19-32 is a duplicate of the table, not an assertion about it. packages/backend/tests/rfc275-schema-admission.test.ts imports only DbSchemaDriftError.
- **拟建守卫**：Import LEGACY_MIGRATION_HASHES from src/db/schemaAdmission and assert `Object.keys(...).sort()` equals an exact eight-tag ledger, plus `Object.values(...).every(v => v.length === 1)`; require a written `why` per entry (a parallel Record<string,string> the test forces non-empty), mirroring the depcheck-gate why/removeWhen discipline. Adding a ninth then becomes a deliberate, reviewed ledger edit.
- **证伪方式**：Add a ninth key to LEGACY_MIGRATION_HASHES in src and run `bun test packages/backend/tests/rfc278-legacy-schema-reconciliation.test.ts packages/backend/tests/rfc275-schema-admission.test.ts`. If either goes red, this finding is wrong.

### CC-04 · P2 · forgeable-contract · UNVERIFIED

**Generic transaction-boundary ports re-open the exact async-commit hazard dbTxSync exists to close, and the S-10 ratchet is lexically blind to their shape**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/db/txSync.ts:1-30` · `packages/backend/src/db/txSync.ts:28-31` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/application/ports/missionStore.ts:346` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/infrastructure/sqliteMissionStore.ts:689-691` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/application/commands/launchMission.ts:144-145` · `packages/backend/src/routes/developmentMissions.ts:208` · `packages/backend/tests/scheduler-audit-s10-async-transaction-decorative.test.ts:138-155`
- **证据**：dbTxSync's whole point is the type-level `NotPromise<T>` collapse plus the runtime thenable throw (txSync.ts:28-31 and :41-55) — the header at :1-3 calls it 'the ONLY sanctioned way to get transactional atomicity in this codebase'. But missionStore.ts:346 declares `inTx<T>(fn: () => T): T` and launchMission.ts:145 declares `transact: <T>(fn: () => T) => T` — unconstrained T, so `T = Promise<X>` type-checks. Their implementations (sqliteMissionStore.ts:690 `return db.transaction(() => fn())`, developmentMissions.ts:208 `transact: (fn) => deps.db.transaction(() => fn())`) call drizzle directly, so neither the type guard nor the runtime thenable guard applies. The S-10 ratchet at scheduler-audit-s10-async-transaction-decorative.test.ts:146 matches `/\.transaction\s*\(\s*async\b/` — an arrow that merely RETURNS a promise has no `async` keyword and matches nothing. Current callers (missionReconciler.ts:995, launchMission.ts:658) are synchronous, so this is latent, not live.
- **架构影响**：37 raw `db.transaction(` sites exist in packages/backend/src outside txSync.ts; two of them are published as reusable transaction PORTS whose contract permits the failure the kernel was built to make impossible. Under bun:sqlite an async body COMMITs at its first await, so a future `await` inside an inTx callback yields a half-committed mission launch with no error — the exact RFC-052 approve incident the kernel documents at txSync.ts:9-12.
- **现有守卫**：none for this shape — packages/backend/tests/scheduler-audit-s10-async-transaction-decorative.test.ts:138-155 pins `.transaction(async` at zero occurrences and nothing else; packages/backend/tests/rfc093-db-tx-sync.test.ts locks dbTxSync's own behaviour, not who bypasses it.
- **拟建守卫**：(a) Change the port signatures to `<T>(fn: () => NotPromise<T>) => T` by exporting NotPromise from db/txSync.ts — this makes the forgery a compile error at every implementer and caller. (b) Extend the S-10 ratchet into an allowlisted-site ratchet in the shape of rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet: every `.transaction(` occurrence in packages/backend/src must be an allowlisted file with an exact hit count and a written why, and the ledger must be stale-checked in both directions.
- **证伪方式**：Add `const x = deps.store.inTx(async () => { await something(); return 1 })` to a module and run `bun run typecheck` plus the S-10 test. If either goes red, this finding is wrong.

### CC-05 · P2 · missing-guard · UNVERIFIED

**RFC-294's layer-shape lock reaches 4 of 12 bounded contexts; the preflight itself never checks a module's top-level directory set**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:18` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:120-133` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:101-125` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:62-101` · `design/RFC-294-backend-layered-target-architecture/proposal.md:119-123`
- **证据**：The preflight enforces cross-context edges, public-entrypoint exactness, capability non-forgeability and shape budgets — but grepping it for a layer-name set returns nothing; there is no `topLevelDirs` / allowed-directory assertion anywhere in the file. The only two files that lock a module's shape are rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:101-125 (development-automation only) and rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:62-101 (digital-employee, event-center, execution-contract). The other eight — code-capability, identity-access, integration, intent, source-control, task-catalog, task-execution, work-start — have no shape lock. Their current top-level sets already diverge: task-execution carries `inbound/` and `ports/`, task-catalog has no `domain/`, intent has ONLY `domain/`, and work-start has only `public/`. RFC-294 proposal.md:119-123 fixes the layer semantics but names no enforcement.
- **架构影响**：A new file in the wrong layer — a `services/` or `helpers/` directory inside a module, an `application/` file reaching its own `infrastructure/`, an `engine/` importing hono — is invisible in eight of twelve contexts. Every new bounded context ships unenforced by default, which is precisely the direction the repo is migrating toward.
- **现有守卫**：none for eight modules. packages/backend/tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:101-125 covers development-automation; packages/backend/tests/rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:62-101 covers three OS contexts; .dependency-cruiser.cjs has no intra-module rule.
- **拟建守卫**：Promote the shape test into rfc294-architecture-preflight as a per-module rule applied to EVERY directory under MODULES_ROOT: top-level entries must be a subset of {domain, application, engine, ports, infrastructure, public, composition, inbound} (with `inbound` either sanctioned in RFC-294 or ratcheted as a one-row debt for task-execution), public/ must hold only the five entrypoints, and the intra-layer import matrix (domain⇏ports/infrastructure, application/engine/public⇏infrastructure, engine/public⇏@/db) must hold module-wide. Ship it as an exact-toEqual debt ledger so today's divergences are named rather than grandfathered.
- **证伪方式**：Create packages/backend/src/modules/source-control/helpers/x.ts that imports hono and run the full backend suite. If anything turns red, this finding is wrong.

### CC-06 · P2 · missing-guard · UNVERIFIED

**The dependency gate's own header claims its allowlist 'can only shrink, never grow' — nothing enforces that, so a new forbidden edge is legalized in the same PR that creates it**

- **锚点**：`scripts/depcheck.ts:28-30` · `scripts/depcheck.ts:69` · `scripts/depcheck.ts:403` · `scripts/depcheck.ts:407-413` · `packages/backend/tests/depcheck-gate.test.ts:75-307`
- **证据**：depcheck.ts:28-30 states verbatim '③ KNOWN_VIOLATIONS 里没有过期条目 …… 这让允许列表只能缩、不能涨'. The implementation supports only half of that: :403 computes `stale` as ledger entries no longer triggered, and isBlocking at :407-413 blocks on unknown ∪ stale ∪ unresolvedFirstParty. There is no comparison against a previous ledger size, no cap, and no baseline. depcheck-gate.test.ts enforces why/removeWhen non-emptiness, removeWhen specificity, no duplicates and rule-name existence — all of which a new entry can satisfy in one line. So adding `{ rule: 'no-routes-to-db', from: 'routes/newThing.ts', to: 'db/schema.ts', why: '…', removeWhen: 'RFC-320 …' }` turns the gate green for a brand-new violation.
- **架构影响**：This is the archetype of 'a ratchet silently bumped upward in the same PR'. Every one of the repo's ~14 ledger-style guards (KNOWN_VIOLATIONS, spawn-site ALLOWLIST, ux-source-ratchets allowlists, rfc143's kind-discrimination allowlist, the rfc294 debt lists) shares it, but this one additionally has a source comment asserting the property it does not have, so reviewers reasonably trust it.
- **现有守卫**：partial and in the wrong direction — scripts/depcheck.ts:403 catches SHRINKAGE that was not recorded (stale entries). packages/backend/tests/depcheck-gate.test.ts:75-307 caps quality of an entry, never quantity. None of the other ledger guards has a growth check either.
- **拟建守卫**：Add a committed high-water baseline: `KNOWN_VIOLATIONS.length <= LEDGER_BASELINE` with LEDGER_BASELINE a separate exported number, and a test asserting the baseline itself only ever decreases across commits (compare against `git show HEAD~1:scripts/depcheck.ts` in CI, or keep the baseline in a one-line file whose diff a CODEOWNERS rule flags). Generalize the same two-line pattern to every allowlist named in the guard inventory. Also fix the header comment or the code so they agree.
- **证伪方式**：Append a synthetic KNOWN_VIOLATIONS entry for an edge that does trigger, with a plausible why/removeWhen, and run `bun run depcheck` + `bun test packages/backend/tests/depcheck-gate.test.ts`. If either fails, this finding is wrong.

### CC-07 · P2 · missing-guard · UNVERIFIED

**No guard-suite manifest: a guard test can be deleted, renamed, or made vacuous and nothing turns red — and roughly half the mechanisms lack the reverse self-check the repo's own gotchas mandate**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/tests/rfc287-t1-scheduler-source-lock-inventory.test.ts:1-24` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc287-t1-scheduler-source-lock-inventory.test.ts:40-45` · `packages/frontend/tests/route-ux-inventory.test.ts:801-905` · `docs/dev-gotchas.md:634` · `docs/dev-gotchas.md:1421` · `docs/dev-gotchas.md:170`
- **证据**：Exactly two manifests in the repo enumerate what must exist: rfc287-t1 (:40-45) pins the set of test files that read services/scheduler.ts, and route-ux-inventory (:801-905) pins that every frontend route has an existing test-owner file. Neither covers the architecture/ratchet suite. Meanwhile docs/dev-gotchas.md:634 states the rule explicitly — '每条负扫描都成对：正向扫真实源码、反向喂一段故意违规的样本给同一个扫描器且必须报' — and per the guard inventory the whole-src source-text ratchets that lack such a fixture include rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet, rfc217-architecture-locks, rfc310-architecture-lock, rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture, rfc303-architecture-lock, rfc301-task-launch-origin, rfc294-background-worker-boundary, the s14/rfc144/lifecycle-grep blind-write inventories, and frontend ux-source-ratchets. dev-gotchas.md:1421 and :170 record two real incidents of guards going vacuous (a threshold reachable by editing the number; `expect(err.message).not.toMatch(/code/)` proven permanently green).
- **架构影响**：The guard layer is the repo's actual architecture enforcement — RFC-294 conformance, spawn governance, blind-write bans, ACL enrolment all live in tests, not in types. A layer with no inventory and no mutation proof degrades exactly the way the ledgers do: silently, while reporting green. Deleting one file is a smaller diff than the violation it was protecting against.
- **现有守卫**：none globally — packages/backend/tests/rfc287-t1-scheduler-source-lock-inventory.test.ts:40-45 is scoped to scheduler.ts readers; packages/frontend/tests/route-ux-inventory.test.ts:801-905 to frontend routes. No test asserts that e.g. rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet.test.ts still exists or still matches a non-zero number of files.
- **拟建守卫**：One `architecture-guard-manifest.test.ts` that (a) two-way-pins the list of architecture/ratchet test files by path, (b) for every source-text scanner asserts its forward scan matched a NON-ZERO number of files (a zero-match scanner is the '扫了个寂寞' signature and must fail loud), and (c) requires each listed guard to export a `__mutationFixtures` array that the manifest runs through the same matcher and asserts each goes red. That turns 'present' into 'mutation-proven'.
- **证伪方式**：Delete packages/backend/tests/rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet.test.ts and run the full backend suite. If it goes red, this finding is wrong. Or rename the directory a scanner walks and check whether any test reports zero-match.

### CC-08 · P3 · boundary-blur · UNVERIFIED

**Path containment has no adoption ratchet, and the RFC-310 upload writer re-validates its target path with a schema strictly weaker than the one that produced it**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/util/safePath.ts:14-41` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/domain/requirementManifest.ts:13-27` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/composition/employeeTypePackage.ts:2631-2668` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/composition/digitalEmployeeWorkspace.ts:57-70` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/composition/digitalEmployeeWorkspace.ts:323-327` · `packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/composition/digitalEmployeePlatformWorkItems.ts:59-63`
- **证据**：Three independent declarations of the same 'upload target is a safe repo-relative path' contract exist with three strictness levels: repoRelativePathSchema (requirementManifest.ts:13-27) rejects leading /, backslash, drive letters, empty/./.. segments; uploadSeedSchema (employeeTypePackage.ts:2634-2668) repeats that logic inline and adds platform-root checks; and the write-side re-parse at digitalEmployeeWorkspace.ts:63 and digitalEmployeePlatformWorkItems.ts:61 is bare `z.string().min(1)`. The write itself at digitalEmployeeWorkspace.ts:325-327 is `join(workspacePath(caseId), upload.targetPath)` + `mkdirSync(dirname(target), {recursive:true})` + `copyBlobTo(...)` — no safeJoin, no assertWriteAncestorInside. Today the producing path validates, so this is defence-in-depth removed rather than a live traversal; the boundary re-parse is nonetheless weaker than its own producer and shares no symbol with it.
- **架构影响**：safePath is the repo's only traversal kernel and its adoption is 8 files against ~83 that contain fs write calls, with no allowlist ratchet of the kind spawn sites get. Every new module that writes user- or DB-supplied paths must rediscover the rule; here, one of three copies already lost it. A future writer that reads targetPath from a different context state, or a schema relaxation upstream, converts this into a real escape.
- **现有守卫**：none — no test or lint asserts that an fs write whose path derives from a request/DB field goes through safeJoin/assertWriteAncestorInside, and no test asserts these three targetPath schemas agree. packages/backend/tests/safepath-write-containment.test.ts exercises the kernel itself, not its adoption.
- **拟建守卫**：(1) Export repoRelativePathSchema as the single path-shape zod and make the two `z.string().min(1)` boundary declarations import it (a `satisfies` on the schema identity, or a source-text lock that no `targetPath: z.string()` literal appears in the module). (2) Add a write-site allowlist ratchet in the shape of rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet: every `writeFileSync|mkdirSync|cpSync|createWriteStream|copyBlobTo` line in packages/backend/src is either in an allowlist with a written why, or the file must import from @/util/safePath.
- **证伪方式**：Grep digitalEmployeeWorkspace.ts for safeJoin — absent. Parse `{ artifactRef: 'employee-input:x', placement: 'repository', targetPath: '../../escape', originalName: 'x' }` through the schema at digitalEmployeeWorkspace.ts:57-70; if it rejects, this finding is wrong.

### CC-09 · P3 · missing-guard · UNVERIFIED

**The lifecycle alert-rule registry is not exhaustive over its own union, so a new rule's alerts would never be resolved — and its headers already miscount the rules they list**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/lifecycleInvariants.ts:59-63` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycleInvariants.ts:73-81` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycleInvariants.ts:87-101` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycleInvariants.ts:826` · `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycleInvariants.ts:1-33`
- **证据**：There IS a genuine compile-time guard (:73-81) forcing the backend union `InvariantRule | StuckRule` to equal shared's LifecycleAlertRule — that part is the repo's good pattern. But the RUNTIME lists are `export const INVARIANT_RULES: readonly InvariantRule[]` (:89) and `readonly StuckRule[]` (:101) — an array annotation, not a completeness assertion — and INVARIANT_RULES is what is passed as `ownedRules` at :826, which scopes which lifecycle_alerts rows the reconcile is allowed to flip resolved. A rule added to the union and emitted by a scan but omitted from INVARIANT_RULES produces alert rows that no reconcile owns, so they never resolve. The headers are already stale in the same direction: :88 says 'seven invariant rules' while :89-100 lists eight (R1,R2,C1,T1,T2,T3,U1,CR-1), and :100 says 'five stuck-task rules' while :101 lists six (S1..S6); the module header at :3 and :32 also says 'Seven invariants' / 'All seven invariants'. `rg INVARIANT_RULES packages/backend/tests` returns nothing.
- **架构影响**：The alert kernel's failure mode is invisible by design: a stranded alert looks like a real open finding forever, and the Diagnose panel degrades into permanent red noise that operators learn to ignore. The pattern also matches the sweep's already-named uncovered class 'a registry entry added without its consumer', here with the arrow reversed — the union grows, the runtime table does not.
- **现有守卫**：none — no test references INVARIANT_RULES or STUCK_RULES, and the type annotation `readonly InvariantRule[]` permits any subset.
- **拟建守卫**：Replace the annotations with tuple-exhaustive forms: `const INVARIANT_RULES = [...] as const satisfies readonly InvariantRule[]` plus a type-level `Exclude<InvariantRule, typeof INVARIANT_RULES[number]> extends never ? true : never` assertion (the same shape used at :73-81), and the mirror for STUCK_RULES. Add one test asserting `new Set(INVARIANT_RULES).size + new Set(STUCK_RULES).size === LIFECYCLE_ALERT_RULES.length` against the shared canonical list.
- **证伪方式**：Add `'X1'` to the InvariantRule union at :59 without touching INVARIANT_RULES and run `bun run typecheck` + the lifecycle suites. If anything goes red, this finding is wrong.

### CC-10 · P3 · stale-assertion · UNVERIFIED

**The shared ref-resolution policy table declares itself the single source of truth for freeze/ACL timing and has zero production consumers**

- **锚点**：`packages/shared/src/ref/resolution.ts:87` · `packages/shared/src/ref/resolution.ts:89-100` · `packages/shared/src/ref/resolution.ts:103-110` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc271-ref-contract.test.ts:50-65` · `packages/backend/src/services/agentDeps.ts:1`
- **证据**：resolution.ts:87 reads '各域的静态策略（单一事实源；resolver 实现从这里取，不各写各的）'. Grepping REF_DOMAIN_POLICIES across packages/{shared,backend,frontend}/src returns exactly one hit — its own definition; every other reference is in packages/backend/tests/rfc271-ref-contract.test.ts. EXPORT_CALL_POLICY (:103-110) is likewise defined and never imported anywhere in src, while its four siblings (DISPATCH_, FANOUT_HYDRATE_, VALIDATE_, PREVIEW_CALL_POLICY) do have production callers in services/agentDeps.ts, services/scheduler.ts, services/execution/resolveInjection.ts and routes/agents.ts. Also note `as const satisfies Record<string, RefDomainPolicy>` at :100 uses an OPEN key type, so it is not exhaustive over any domain union either.
- **架构影响**：Two decorative rows in a shared kernel that a reader — and any future RFC citing the file — will take as binding. The actual freeze/ACL behaviour for the export path lives elsewhere (services/resourcePackage/*), which means the ref commons has one documented policy surface and a second de-facto one, and the test at rfc271-ref-contract.test.ts:57 pins the key list against itself so it is green by construction.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/rfc271-ref-contract.test.ts:50-65 iterates the table and re-asserts two of its own values; nothing asserts a production consumer exists. The repo does have the right pattern for this elsewhere (packages/backend/tests/rfc305-architecture-lock.test.ts requires every SYSTEM_DOMAIN_POINTS entry to have an AST string-literal production consumer) — it is simply not applied here.
- **拟建守卫**：Generalize the rfc305 registry-consumer rule into a reusable assertion and apply it to the shared policy/registry tables: for each exported registry symbol on a declared list, walk packages/{backend,frontend}/src with the TS AST and require at least one non-test production reference; a symbol with none must either be deleted or ledgered as intentionally-inert with a why.
- **证伪方式**：`rg -n 'REF_DOMAIN_POLICIES|EXPORT_CALL_POLICY' packages/*/src` — if any hit outside packages/shared/src/ref/resolution.ts appears, this finding is wrong.

### CC-11 · P3 · boundary-blur · UNVERIFIED

**modules/intent is a one-file bounded context with no public/ surface whose only consumer is a legacy service deep-import — invisible to every public-surface and cross-context guard**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/modules/intent/domain/workflowCreateLayout.ts:1-25` · `packages/backend/src/services/intent/turnEngine.ts:60` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:18` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:507-620`
- **证据**：`git ls-files packages/backend/src/modules/intent` returns exactly one file: domain/workflowCreateLayout.ts. There is no public/ directory, so publicSurfaceViolations (:507-620) has nothing to scan and reports the module clean. crossContextViolations only fires on modules/<A> -> modules/<B> edges and this module has no outbound module edges. Its sole production consumer is packages/backend/src/services/intent/turnEngine.ts:60, a legacy-layer file importing `@/modules/intent/domain/workflowCreateLayout` directly — the FROM side is services/, outside MODULES_ROOT (:18), so the preflight never sees the edge. Repo-wide the same shape occurs 66 times across 19 legacy-layer files (routes/, services/, cli/, server.ts).
- **架构影响**：A context that exists only as a domain file reachable by deep import is not a bounded context; it is a relocated helper that now reports as architecture progress. Both directions of the guard are structurally satisfied by emptiness. Any scanner counting 'modules with clean public surfaces' will count this one, which is the 'empty or dead bounded context treated as clean' class the sweep already flagged, here with a concrete instance.
- **现有守卫**：none — packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:507-620 requires public/ files to be exact IF they exist; it does not require a module to HAVE a public surface, and it does not scan inbound edges from outside modules/. Only packages/backend/tests/rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:74-101 scans all of BACKEND_SRC for inbound edges, and only for three named contexts.
- **拟建守卫**：Two additions to rfc294-architecture-preflight: (1) every directory under MODULES_ROOT must contain a non-empty public/ with at least one of the five entrypoints, or be listed in an exact 'incubating' debt ledger with a why; (2) generalize the OS test's inbound scan to ALL contexts — every `@/modules/<ctx>/{domain,application,engine,ports,infrastructure,inbound}` import from outside modules/ becomes a two-way debt ledger row (seed it with today's 66 edges so it can only shrink).
- **证伪方式**：`git ls-files packages/backend/src/modules/intent` and `rg -n "modules/intent" packages/backend/src`. If a public/ file appears or a second production consumer exists, this finding is wrong.

### CC-12 · P3 · duplicate-implementation · UNVERIFIED

**The 'which space kind owns disk' predicate is hand-written four times across the workspace-GC commons with no SpaceKind-exhaustive table**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/gc.ts:213-216` · `packages/backend/src/services/gc.ts:265-268` · `packages/backend/src/services/gc.ts:360-372` · `packages/backend/src/services/webhook/terminalWorkspaceCleanup.ts:25-36` · `packages/shared/src/schemas/task.ts:63`
- **证据**：SPACE_KINDS has five members (task.ts:63: local, remote, scratch, internal, inherited). The webhook prune admission policy expresses eligibility as an include list `input.spaceKind === 'remote' || input.spaceKind === 'scratch'` (terminalWorkspaceCleanup.ts:32); gc.ts repeats the same include list twice as `inArray(tasks.spaceKind, ['remote','scratch'])` (:215, :267); and the generic GC candidate query expresses the complementary rule as an exclude list `ne(spaceKind,'internal') && ne(spaceKind,'inherited')` (:365-366). I verified the three currently agree in behaviour, and gc.ts:136 / :412 add two further ad-hoc `spaceKind === 'scratch'` branches. There is no `satisfies Record<SpaceKind, …>` policy table anywhere.
- **架构影响**：Adding a sixth space kind (or changing whether `local` owns disk) requires editing four independent predicates in two modules, expressed in two opposite polarities, with no compile-time or test signal for the ones you miss. The failure mode is a workspace that is never GC'd or one that is pruned while still needed — both silent, both only observable as disk growth or a broken revive.
- **现有守卫**：none — `rg SPACE_KINDS packages/*/tests` returns nothing; packages/backend/tests/rfc165-workspace-gc.test.ts exercises only 'scratch' and 'internal' rows (:116, :186, :324) and asserts behaviour, not partition completeness.
- **拟建守卫**：Introduce `SPACE_KIND_WORKSPACE_POLICY = { local: {...}, remote: {...}, … } as const satisfies Record<SpaceKind, { ownsDisk: boolean; genericGcEligible: boolean; sourcePruneEligible: boolean; honoursOnlyMerged: boolean }>` in shared, derive all four predicates from it, and add a test iterating every SpaceKind against the table plus a source-text ban on `spaceKind ===`/`inArray(tasks.spaceKind` outside the policy module.
- **证伪方式**：Add a sixth member to SPACE_KINDS and run the backend suite. If any test goes red pointing at gc.ts or terminalWorkspaceCleanup.ts, this finding is wrong.

### CC-13 · P3 · escape-hatch · UNVERIFIED

**Memory-body fencing in the shared injection kernel is opt-in through a defaulted empty-nonce parameter, and no guard bounds who may pass the empty string**

- **锚点**：`packages/backend/src/services/memoryInject.ts:306-322` · `packages/shared/src/promptFencing.ts:110-113` · `packages/backend/src/services/runner.ts:718` · `packages/backend/tests/rfc200-source-lock.test.ts:63-64`
- **证据**：formatMemoryBlockFromSnapshot (:306) takes `envelopeNonce = ''` and branches at :319-322: with an empty nonce it emits `- [scope] ${m.title} — ${m.bodyMd}` raw; only with a non-empty nonce does it call sanitizeInlineField + fenceUntrusted. fenceUntrusted itself returns content unchanged when the nonce is empty (promptFencing.ts:111). The single production caller passes a loaded nonce (runner.ts:718), and the RFC-200 lock pins that one line (rfc200-source-lock.test.ts:63-64) — but the default parameter means any future caller, and every pre-RFC-200 node_run row whose stored nonce is '', silently takes the unfenced branch. The same defaulted-nonce escape exists on formatMemoryBlock (:298) and formatMemoryBlockWithSnapshot (:288).
- **架构影响**：Legacy byte-compatibility is encoded as a DEFAULT rather than an explicit, typed legacy mode, so the safe path is the one you have to remember to ask for. This is the 'shared-kernel option whose default encodes one caller family's legacy behaviour' class, applied to a security boundary rather than a process-drain timeout.
- **现有守卫**：partial and single-site — packages/backend/tests/rfc200-source-lock.test.ts:63-64 asserts memoryInject.ts contains the fenceUntrusted call and runner.ts loads a nonce. Nothing forbids a second caller from omitting the argument, and nothing asserts the unfenced branch is unreachable for post-cutover rows.
- **拟建守卫**：Make the nonce a required parameter typed as a branded `EnvelopeNonce | LegacyNoNonce` discriminated value so the legacy path must be named at the call site; add a repo-wide AST ratchet that any call to formatMemoryBlock*/fenceUntrusted/renderUserPrompt passes an explicit nonce argument (no reliance on the default), with an exact allowlist for the legacy branch.
- **证伪方式**：Call `formatMemoryBlockFromSnapshot(snapshot)` with a body containing `\n## Platform` and inspect the output. If it is neutralized, this finding is wrong.

## 附：各族列出的「无人看守的违规类别」

共 95 条。这是「防护盲区」的原始分类，RFC `design.md §4` 把它们归并成 12 条新规则。

**EK — 执行内核公共层**

- Per-runtime knowledge added INTO a shared services/ module (a new `xInit` source payload, a `renderXMcpEntry`, an `XLike` interface) rather than behind the driver contract
  - 为何无人看守：eslint.config.js:29-45 fences only the import DIRECTION (callers may not reach into services/runtime/*); nothing stops runtime-specific code from being hoisted out into services/. The rfc143 whole-tree ratchet (packages/backend/tests/rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:249-250) matches only `runtime|protocol|kind|defaultRuntime === 'opencode'|'claude-code'` and `isClaude` — it cannot see identifier names, type names, parameter names, or ternary/coalesce defaults.
  - 建议机制：AST ratchet over services/**/*.ts excluding services/runtime/**: flag exported declarations, parameters, properties and type aliases whose identifier contains a registered runtime token (token list derived from RUNTIME_KINDS so it grows with the registry). Exact-equality allowlist snapshot that may only shrink, plus a mutation self-check on a fixture string.
- A new spawn site created through a library that spawns internally (MCP SDK transports, any future exec wrapper)
  - 为何无人看守：packages/backend/tests/rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet.test.ts:26-42 is purely lexical: Bun.spawn / spawnSync / bare spawn( / 'child_process' / Bun.$ / exec-family. `new StdioClientTransport({command, args})` (packages/backend/src/services/mcpProbe.ts:516) matches none, and the file's only pattern hit (:300) is a comment line skipped by countSpawnHits (:122-125).
  - 建议机制：Add an import-specifier dimension to the ratchet: an allowlist of modules known to spawn (@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/stdio.js, node:child_process, execa-likes) with the same {file → why} table and only-shrinks discipline.
- A new spawn site that omits `detached: true` / `killProcessTree` / a bounded read — a fresh weak kill-reap chain
  - 为何无人看守：The ratchet's allowlist value is `{count, why}` (rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet.test.ts:43-47): any prose `why` admits any governance quality. No test asserts a spawn site's process-group or tree-kill properties. packages/backend/tests/rfc098-process-governance.test.ts drives the adversarial grandchild fixture only against runNode / reapOrphanRuns.
  - 建议机制：Extend the allowlist value to `{count, why, governance}` and add an AST check: for entries not marked exempt-*, the spawning function must pass `detached: true` and reference killProcessTree. Assert the violation list is exactly [].
- Cross-domain reach-in from the execution commons (importing another context's db table, service, or node-id constant)
  - 为何无人看守：.dependency-cruiser.cjs carries only four coarse layer rules (:62-126) plus no-circular (:144-148) — none constrains services/execution/**. eslint.config.js:111-119 covers cross-package only. The RFC-294 AST scanner that WOULD catch this reads only packages/backend/src/modules (packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:18,129).
  - 建议机制：Two cheap moves: (1) a dependency-cruiser rule no-execution-commons-to-domain with a snapshot of today's violating edges that may only shrink; (2) point the RFC-294 scanner at a second root treating each services/<family>/ directory as a pseudo-context, reusing the exact-equality debt snapshot it already uses at :1253-1262.
- A sixth spawn assembly line built outside scheduler.ts (e.g. in src/modules/**) that hand-rolls permit → iso → spawn → merge → release
  - 为何无人看守：The extinction lock in packages/backend/tests/rfc287-t9-exemptions-and-extinction.test.ts scans a fixed list of five function signatures (MIGRATED_LINES, :34-40) plus one whole-file negative scan (`finally { … discardNodeIso(`) that reads scheduler.ts ONLY (:172-182). A new line in another file is entirely invisible. It happens to be adequate today only because createIsoUnderLock is imported nowhere else — a property of current code, not of the guard.
  - 建议机制：Move the negative scan from one hard-coded file to a whole-src scan: assert that createIsoUnderLock / discardNodeIso appear only in an allowlisted file set, and that any file importing both a semaphore acquire and createIsoUnderLock also imports runAssembly. Same only-shrinks allowlist discipline as the spawn ratchet.
- A shared-kernel option whose DEFAULT encodes one caller family's legacy behaviour
  - 为何无人看守：Nothing checks the polarity or presence of defaults on shared request types. keepExitedOnDrainTimeout (packages/backend/src/services/execution/managedProcess.ts:89) has zero test references repo-wide; spawnVersionProbe's detached gate (packages/backend/src/util/process.ts:380) is exercised only in its enabled state (packages/backend/tests/rfc284-spawn-version-probe.test.ts — every case passes timeoutMs).
  - 建议机制：Type-level: make such fields required discriminated unions so a new caller must state its contract. Test-level: for every optional field on a shared kernel request type under services/execution/, require a named test exercising BOTH branches — enforceable as an AST check that lists optional fields on `*Request` interfaces and greps the test tree for each name.
- Growth of the cross-domain RunTaskOptions bag with one more domain-specific knob
  - 为何无人看守：packages/backend/tests/rfc284-t20-child-inheritance.test.ts:78 forces a disposition to be DECLARED for every key (real compile-time exhaustiveness), but places no ceiling and asks no question about whether the key belongs on the execution surface. One interface line plus one table row is green.
  - 建议机制：Add `expect(Object.keys(DISPOSITION).length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(<current>)` as an only-shrinks ratchet in the same file, so growing the shared surface becomes a deliberate, reviewed edit.
- A new NodeKind admitted to NODE_KIND_BEHAVIORS without a runOneNode dispatch branch
  - 为何无人看守：The behavior matrix is compile-time exhaustive (packages/shared/src/node-kind-behavior.ts:100), but runOneNode's fall-through guard is a RUNTIME typed failure (packages/backend/src/services/scheduler.ts:5674-5680, message 'unhandled-node-kind'), documented as an if-chain by design (RFC-146 D2). A new kind therefore compiles and ships, failing only when a workflow using it is executed.
  - 建议机制：A source ratchet that parses the NodeKind union from shared and asserts scheduler.ts contains a `node.kind === '<kind>'` branch (or explicit SETTLES_WITHOUT_ROW_KINDS membership) for each — cheap, preserves the if-chain-by-design decision, and makes omission a build-time red.
- A doc/ledger claim about the execution or spawn layer that source no longer supports
  - 为何无人看守：No mechanism reads design/system-commons-unification-audit-2026-08-12.md or docs/audit-backlog.md; CI's markdown check validates links only. Both files are hand-maintained inventories of file:line anchors that silently rot as code converges (pluginInstaller, the three --version twins) or as new instances appear (verificationRunner, developmentAdapterRunner).
  - 建议机制：Make the prose derive from the ratchets: have each ratchet write its offender list to a generated snapshot the docs cite, and add a test asserting every file path named in the backlog's weak-spawn family still exists and still matches SPAWN_PATTERNS — stale entries and unregistered new sites both turn red.

**LC — 生命周期与状态机公共层**

- A new (or edited) call site passes a `to` + `allowedFrom` pair that the shared transition table declares illegal — e.g. `to:'done', allowedFrom:['pending']` for a task, or reviving a `skipped` node_run.
  - 为何无人看守：`setTaskStatus` / `setNodeRunStatus` / `setNodeRunStatusTx` validate only `allowedFrom.includes(from)`; they never call `nextTaskStatus` / `nextNodeRunStatus`. The table's own tests (packages/shared/tests/rfc108-task-transition-table.test.ts, packages/backend/tests/lifecycle-transition-table.test.ts) prove only internal self-consistency. Five such contradictions already exist and are green.
  - 建议机制：AST/TS-parse test over packages/backend/src: for each `set*Status({...})` literal with statically-known `to` and array-literal `allowedFrom`, assert the set is a subset of the union of `allowedFromFor*Event(e)` over events targeting `to`; deviations only via a named, ratchet-down exception table. Ultimately: have the CAS helpers themselves assert via the table and delete the free-form parameter.
- A new direct `db.update(nodeRuns).set({ status })` anywhere in packages/backend/src, including writes to fenced statuses ('running' / 'awaiting_review' / 'awaiting_human') that bypass the RFC-303 source-termination gate.
  - 为何无人看守：packages/backend/tests/lifecycle-grep-guard.test.ts:88 exempts any match preceded within 5 lines by the literal `rfc053-allow-direct-status-write`. No file allowlist, no count, no constraint on the target status. Five bypasses already live outside the kernel.
  - 建议机制：Convert to an s14-style per-file exact-count allowlist (ratchet down only), and additionally assert every non-kernel entry writes a status outside SOURCE_TERMINATION_BLOCKED_NODE_STATUSES and carries a `WHERE status = <non-terminal literal>` predicate.
- Any raw-SQL status write — `sqlite.query('UPDATE tasks SET status = …')` or `db.run(sql\`UPDATE node_runs SET status …\`)` — bypassing drizzle entirely.
  - 为何无人看守：Both ratchets (scheduler-audit-s14…test.ts:162 `/\.update\s*\(\s*tasks\s*\)/`, rfc144-merge-state-blind-write-inventory.test.ts:82 the nodeRuns twin) match only the drizzle builder form. packages/backend/src/services/restore.ts:317-326 already reaches the raw bun:sqlite handle through `db as unknown as { $client: Database }` and issues `UPDATE tasks SET …` invisibly to every scanner.
  - 建议机制：Extend both scanners with a second pass matching `/UPDATE\s+(tasks|node_runs)\s+SET[^;]*\bstatus\b/i` and `/\bmerge_state\b/` in string/template literals, with the same per-file allowlist. Separately ratchet `as unknown as { $client` casts.
- A new per-origin or per-shape branch inside the generic lifecycle writer — another `workspacePruneCause` literal, a third `sourceTerminationFence` value, or an `if (row.parentTaskId !== null)` / `if (row.spaceKind === …)` special case in setTaskStatus.
  - 为何无人看守：Nothing constrains the vocabulary of `services/lifecycle.ts`. The webhook prune cause (line 592) and the MR/PR fence wording (lines 45-62) are already precedent, and the injected policy port returns only a boolean so a second origin has no way to express its cause except by editing the kernel.
  - 建议机制：Source assertion that packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts contains zero integration-identity identifiers (`/webhook|eventSubscription|MR\/PR|schedule|intent|mission|digital-employee/i`) and zero `workspacePruneCause: '` literals; widen the policy port to return its own cause so the rule is satisfiable.
- A new stuck-detector rule threshold or exemption arm (a fourth S4 ternary branch, a second status-family skip) shipped with no test.
  - 为何无人看守：S4's exemptions are an inline ternary chain rather than a registry, so there is no shape a test can enumerate. The RFC-287 G7 repo-prep arm (45 min) and the RFC-164 workgroup S1/S2 skip both have zero tests — `rg -ln "preparingRepo|DEFAULT_REPO_PREP" packages/backend/tests` is empty.
  - 建议机制：Exhaustive exemption registry (`ReadonlyArray<{id, applies, thresholdMs, hintKey}>`) plus a test that iterates it and requires, per entry, a suppress-below case, a fire-above case, and a resolvable i18n hint key.
- A second registration on any single-slot lifecycle hook (terminal task hook / workspace prune policy / prune effect), silently disabling the incumbent.
  - 为何无人看守：All three are `let x = null` + plain assignment (packages/backend/src/services/lifecycle.ts:417-430, 683-687). No name, no accumulation, no already-registered assertion, and no test exercises two subscribers on one transition.
  - 建议机制：Named multicast subscription with a duplicate-name throw, plus a boot-assembly test asserting the expected subscriber set for each hook and that all of them fire on one done/canceled transition.
- Adding a fifth task status (or node_run status) without updating the six hand-copied cancelable/non-terminal enumerations and the frontend's hand-rolled terminal disjunction.
  - 为何无人看守：Those sets are literal arrays merely assignable to `TaskStatus[]` (one is plain `string[]` forced through with `as never`), so widening TASK_STATUS produces no type error and no test failure. `TERMINAL_TASK_STATUSES`'s `satisfies` only proves subset, never coverage.
  - 建议机制：Export the derived sets from shared (`allowedFromForTaskEvent({kind:'cancel'})`), migrate all consumers, and add an exhaustive classification test over `TASK_STATUS` requiring every status to fall in exactly one of {terminal, recoverable, active} — new statuses fail until classified. Back it with a grep ratchet forbidding re-declared status array literals outside shared.
- A new stateful table (a fifth lifecycle) shipping with a hand-rolled CAS, no transition table, and no blind-write ratchet — the fusion.ts pattern repeating.
  - 为何无人看守：There is no generic guarded-status-CAS primitive to reuse and no meta-rule that a new status column must register one; ratchets exist for exactly three columns (tasks.status, node_runs.status, node_runs.merge_state) and were each written by hand after the fact.
  - 建议机制：Extract `guardedStatusCas(...)` into the platform layer with a registration list, and generate the per-table blind-write ratchet from that list so any registered status column is guarded by construction; add a schema-level test asserting every `text(..., {enum: [...]})` column named `status` on a mutable table is registered.

**ACL — 资源 ACL / catalog / ref 公共层**

- A new ACL resource type ships with an asymmetric route surface (no /acl mount, no filterVisibleRows on list, no requireResourceOwner on writes, no create-default stamp)
  - 为何无人看守：The one guard designed for this — the enrolment cross-check at packages/backend/tests/rfc099-acl-endpoints-matrix.test.ts:296-308 — matches only a string literal `type: '...'` inside a mountAclEndpoints call, so any factory-mounted type is invisible to it (verified: it returns 7 of 12 today). Nothing iterates ACL_RESOURCE_TYPES in any test. The create-default ratchet (rfc231-private-copy.test.ts:318-372) is a hard-coded list of six tables scanned across nine named files.
  - 建议机制：One boot-time registry test: have mountAclEndpoints record cfg.type into an exported array, boot the real app, and assert the recorded set === ACL_RESOURCE_TYPES === the matrix CASES keys. Extend the matrix rows to assert the four-way symmetry per type (list filters, detail 404s, granted non-owner write 403, create stamps owner+private+rev0).
- A shared kernel branches on a specific resource identity (`type === 'workflow'`) instead of taking a hook the config already offers
  - 为何无人看守：No AST or lint rule forbids string-literal comparisons against an AclResourceType-typed value in services/resourceAcl.ts, routes/resourceAcl.ts, resourceRefs.ts or importRefs.ts. The two existing branches (routes/resourceAcl.ts:189-202) have shipped and are load-bearing, so a third would look like precedent.
  - 建议机制：AST rule over the named commons files: forbid BinaryExpression where one side resolves to AclResourceType/BundleResourceType and the other is a string literal, outside an exhaustive Record/switch initializer. Ratchet the current two out first so the rule starts at zero.
- A new hand-rolled visibility/ownership predicate appears outside services/resourceAcl.ts
  - 为何无人看守：The RFC-282 D1 lock (tests/rfc282-d1-acl-inline-convergence.test.ts) is a behaviour lock on four named legacy sites, not a structural ban. The D2 grep ratchet (tests/rfc282-d2-granted-ids-single-source.test.ts:75-82) forbids `.from(resourceGrants)` in exactly two files — importRefs.ts and resourceRefs.ts — and its own comment says the global lock 'arrives with A2', which has not landed. One divergent copy already exists, exported and tested (configResourceQueries.ts:17-24).
  - 建议机制：Two repo-wide grep/AST ratchets with shrinking ledgers: (a) `.from(resourceGrants)` may appear only in services/resourceAcl.ts (true today — lock it); (b) no function outside services/resourceAcl.ts may contain both a `visibility === 'public'` comparison and an `ownerUserId ===` comparison.
- A drizzle table gains owner_user_id + visibility + acl_revision columns without becoming an AclResourceType, so the columns are decorative
  - 为何无人看守：Nothing reflects over the schema to compare 'tables with ACL columns' against ACL_TABLES. employee_definitions (schema.ts:4960-4987) is in this state today with a live, unfiltered list endpoint.
  - 建议机制：Schema-reflection test in the style of tests/rfc284-resource-policy-schema-guard.test.ts: `expect(tablesWithAclColumns).toEqual(new Set(Object.values(ACL_TABLES)))`, with any intentional exception in a named, shrinking exemption list.
- A per-type table in the resource commons silently gains a hole (a member with no entry) because it is a Set, an if-chain, or a switch with no exhaustiveness arm
  - 为何无人看守：OWNER_NAME_UNIQUE_TYPES is opt-in membership; directRefsOf ends in a silent `return out`; secretInputs' switch has no `never` arm; serialize.ts's redaction table and secretInputs' slot table are unlinked mirrors. Only expectTokenOf (preview.ts:245-275) gets exhaustiveness for free, and only because strictNullChecks rejects the implicit undefined return.
  - 建议机制：Convert each to `Record<T, Entry>` (the approved pattern already used by DISABLED_RESOURCE_POLICY) so omission is a compile error, with an explicit 'none' entry where the answer is genuinely empty. Where a Set must stay, derive it by drizzle reflection and assert equality with the literal.
- A business fact about resource identity (which tables have `builtin`, which types are intent-creatable) is duplicated into a regex, a type union, a zod enum and a runtime early-return, then drifts
  - 为何无人看守：The existing locks assert only WHERE a constant is defined (rfc271-resource-ref.test.ts:269-287 for INTENT_HANDLE_RE; rfc271-builtin-resolve.test.ts:302 for BUNDLE_BUILTIN_RE) — never that its contents match the constant it duplicates. The builtin fact has already drifted (capabilityTemplates gained the column; five copies still say two).
  - 建议机制：Derive every such alternation from its source constant at module load (`new RegExp('^res#(' + INTENT_RESOURCE_TYPES.join('|') + ')#…')`), and where a literal must remain, add a per-member round-trip assertion driven by the constant.
- An ACL predicate is called with a row whose visibility was never selected, or with a cast that disables the check
  - 为何无人看守：AclRow's two ACL fields are optional and absent means 'public' (fail-open), and six call sites on the config-package read path already enter through `as never`. No lint rule targets casts at ACL call sites; no test asserts the fail direction of a visibility-less row.
  - 建议机制：Make the fields required on a strict row type with a single named `legacyAclRow(...)` adapter, plus an AST rule banning `as never` / `as unknown as` in argument position of the six ACL predicates. Add a unit test pinning the intended fail direction of a row with visibility undefined.
- The RFC-294 layering ratchets do not reach the resource commons at all
  - 为何无人看守：packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts scopes every AST ratchet to MODULES_ROOT = packages/backend/src/modules, and .dependency-cruiser.cjs has rules for routes→db, util→upper, auth→services, services→routes and cycles — but none governing services/. services/resourceAcl.ts imports twelve per-domain drizzle tables directly (:51-66) and resourceRefs.ts reaches into the agents table (:497-518); both are outside every ratchet.
  - 建议机制：Either bring the commons under RFC-294 (make resourceAcl a module with a ports-based table registry injected at bootstrap) or, as an interim, add a dependency-cruiser rule capping which schema tables services/resourceAcl.ts may import, with the current twelve ledgered so the list can only shrink as types migrate to module-owned stores.

**RT — Runtime driver port 与能力声明公共层**

- Adding a new `?? 'opencode'` / `: 'opencode'` default-runtime fallback anywhere in the backend
  - 为何无人看守：The only kind-literal ratchet (packages/backend/tests/rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:249-251) requires an `===`/`!==` comparison. A nullish-coalescing or ternary default is not a comparison, so all 15 existing sites and any new one pass silently. There is no DEFAULT_RUNTIME constant to anchor a single-source lock to.
  - 建议机制：Introduce DEFAULT_RUNTIME_KIND + effectiveDefaultRuntimeName() in the registry, then a grep ratchet (same file-walk shape as rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts) forbidding `/(\?\?|:)\s*['"](?:opencode|claude-code)['"]/` outside the owning module, with a stale-checked exception ledger.
- Reintroducing a runtime kind comparison inside services/runner.ts or routes/runtimes.ts
  - 为何无人看守：rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:239-247 skips allowlisted files BEFORE reading them and never verifies that an allowlist entry is still needed. Measured today, only cli/start.ts still matches the regex — the other two exemptions are dead but permanently open, in the two highest-traffic runtime files.
  - 建议机制：Add the stale-ratchet already used for the RFC-282 ledgers (rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:200-206): compute matches with the allowlist disabled and assert every allowlist entry is in that set.
- A kind branch spelled with a variable name outside {runtime, protocol, kind, defaultRuntime} or a literal outside {opencode, claude-code} — e.g. `rtParam === 'claude'`, `row.p === 'claude'`, `name.startsWith('opencode')`
  - 为何无人看守：The ratchet's regex is a denylist of four identifier names and two literals. routes/runtime.ts:49-53 escapes on BOTH halves and is not allowlisted, so a live discriminating branch is invisible to CI today.
  - 建议机制：Rewrite the ratchet as a shape rule: flag any string-literal equality/inequality whose literal is a member of RUNTIME_KINDS ∪ a declared alias table, regardless of the left-hand identifier; and eliminate the alias by having the frontend send registry names.
- Any per-runtime dialect added to a SHARED kernel that happens to live under services/runtime/ (stageSkills, spawnCtx, head, selfCheck, types)
  - 为何无人看守：Both fences equate 'under services/runtime/' with 'is a driver' and skip the whole directory: rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:166 and rfc143-runtime-driver-capability.test.ts:257. The cross-driver commons live in that same directory, so they are exempt by construction — which is how '.claude-plugin' (stageSkills.ts:44), opencodePath/claudeCodePath (types.ts:101-105) and the {opencodeCmd|runtimeCmd} head union (spawnCtx.ts:36) got there.
  - 建议机制：Narrow both skips to services/runtime/opencode/ and services/runtime/claudeCode/ only, and add '.claude-plugin' plus an identifier shape /\b(opencode|claudeCode)[A-Z]\w*\b/ to FORBIDDEN_TOKENS.
- A driver's spawn builder starts writing a new platform-owned env key without adding it to RESERVED_SPAWN_ENV
  - 为何无人看守：packages/shared/src/runtimeConfigDir.ts:44-60 is a hand-maintained list whose stated purpose is to prevent a runtime row's config_dir_env from colliding with what the platform writes, but nothing cross-checks it against the actual `env.X =` assignments in opencode/spawn.ts and claudeCode/spawn.ts. packages/backend/tests/rfc154-runtime-config-dir.test.ts only iterates the set and asserts each member is rejected — a self-referential sweep.
  - 建议机制：A source-reflection test that greps `env.<KEY> =` and `env\[['"]<KEY>` out of every services/runtime/*/spawn.ts and asserts the resulting key set ⊆ RESERVED_SPAWN_ENV — the mirror of the existing lock (e) in runtime-extra-args.test.ts that proves CLAUDE_PLATFORM_OWNED_FLAGS covers the flags the spawn module emits.
- Adding a new optional RuntimeDriver method as a de-facto capability (presence = support) instead of a declared, boot-verified stance
  - 为何无人看守：services/runtime/selfCheck.ts:40-110 verifies exactly three dimensions plus two implications (inventory-file⇒readInventory, init-event⇒parseEvent-yields-inventory). RuntimeDriver has roughly nine other optional methods (drainFinalEvents, observeSystemEvent, createMcpTestNativeSessionId, mcpTestSessionReference, evictBinaryCaches, detectSessionNotFound, startLiveCapture, captureDistillSession, captureSessionsToSink, parseTerminalResultError) with no implication check and no ledger, so the cheapest way to add a capability is the unverified idiom.
  - 建议机制：In selfCheck, enumerate RuntimeDriver's optional-method names from a single exported list and assert each is either covered by a declared implication or present in an explicitly stale-ratcheted NO_IMPLICATION ledger — so adding an optional method forces a review decision. Start by adding the supportsMcpTest ⇒ both-methods implication (F12).
- A capability-gated field (extraArgs, isSandbox) reaching a real spawn through an entry point other than the registry write path
  - 为何无人看守：acceptsExtraArgs / acceptsSandboxCompatibilityMarker are consulted only in runtimeRegistry.ts:213-219 and :714-722. routes/runtimes.ts:239-253 (probe) and :270-285 (pre-create smoke) spawn a child with body-supplied values first and validate later or never; no test covers that path.
  - 建议机制：Validate at the handler entry, plus a source ratchet: every call to smokeRuntime( in src/routes must be preceded in the same function body by validateExtraArgs( and validateIsSandbox(. Back it with a route test asserting {protocol:'opencode', isSandbox:true} 400s without spawning.
- Per-runtime branching in the frontend
  - 为何无人看守：No ratchet scans packages/frontend/src at all. It currently holds `isClaude` (ModelSelect.tsx:67 — a token the backend ratchet explicitly bans), `protocol === 'claude-code'` (RuntimeList.tsx:228, useRuntimesList.ts:78), `isOpencode` (RuntimeList.tsx:385) and a hardcoded two-entry protocol picker (RuntimeList.tsx:503-504) that duplicates RUNTIME_KINDS.
  - 建议机制：Extend the kind-discrimination walk to packages/frontend/src with its own ratcheted allowlist, and serve the protocol picker options + per-protocol field visibility from a shared declaration (the driver capability projection RFC-297 T5 already ships to the frontend) rather than from literals.
- A stdout/stderr protocol literal of one runtime appearing in a runtime-neutral service
  - 为何无人看守：FORBIDDEN_TOKENS (rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:160) covers only three config/argv tokens; stream-protocol literals are not in it. runtimeSmoke.ts:262 carries '"type":"result"' and '"is_error":true' outside services/runtime/ and passes.
  - 建议机制：Add '"type":"result"', '"is_error"', 'session_id', 'sessionID' to FORBIDDEN_TOKENS once runtimeSmoke is moved onto driver.parseTerminalResultError.

**NK — shared 图模型注册表（node-kind / ports / sharding / wrapper）**

- Adding a NodeKind and forgetting to give it a scheduler dispatch branch
  - 为何无人看守：Dispatch in `runOneNode` is an if-chain by explicit design (RFC-146 D2: the handlers close over SchedulerState). The chain ends at scheduler.ts:5674-5680 with a runtime `unhandled-node-kind` failure, and packages/backend/tests/rfc146-kind-predicate-guard.test.ts:96-104 only asserts that guard sits BEFORE agent-name resolution. Nothing fails at compile time; the kind ships and fails at task runtime.
  - 建议机制：Accept the if-chain but make the SET checkable: export a `DISPATCHED_KINDS` const from scheduler.ts built as `satisfies Record<NodeKind, 'dispatched' | 'settles-without-row' | 'retired'>` (the `RunTaskOptions satisfies Record<keyof …, Disposition>` pattern that dev-gotchas.md:633 already calls the best-in-repo example) and assert each 'dispatched' kind is reachable. Compile-time rather than gate-time.
- Adding a fourth wrapper kind
  - 为何无人看守：WRAPPER_NODE_KINDS membership is guarded (wrapper-kind-single-source.test.ts:18-21 derives it from the `wrapper-` prefix), but per-wrapper SEMANTICS are not table-driven anywhere: workflowScope.ts:268-303 boundary promotion, scheduler.ts:5455-5463 dispatch routing, and the fanout/loop/git runners are all if-chains. A new wrapper compiles and fails closed silently.
  - 建议机制：One `satisfies Record<(typeof WRAPPER_NODE_KINDS)[number], …>` table per wrapper concern (boundary promotion, scope runner), with the deliberate no-op entries written out explicitly rather than falling into a default branch.
- Declaring a new framework/system port on a node kind without registering its channel semantics
  - 为何无人看守：The rfc147 drift lock (rfc147-system-channel-ports.test.ts:159-184) walks registry to declaredPorts only. The reverse — every port in any kind's `systemInputs`/`systemOutputs` must have a SYSTEM_CHANNEL_PORTS row — is unasserted, which is why `questions` / `answers` already sit outside the registry (F6).
  - 建议机制：Add the inverse assertion in the same test: iterate PORT_DECLARED_KINDS, collect every systemInputs/systemOutputs name from `declaredPorts`, and require each to be a registry key.
- Writing a new private comparison against a channel port name in the frontend
  - 为何无人看守：The literal ratchet (rfc147-system-channel-ports.test.ts:223-226) hardcodes backend + shared roots only, despite the test title claiming a repo-wide ban. Two instances already exist in packages/frontend/src (F8).
  - 建议机制：Add the frontend root to the existing walker — the sibling guard rfc146-kind-predicate-guard.test.ts:19-23 already walks all three roots and is the template.
- Adding a dimension to a shared per-kind behavior table that nothing consumes
  - 为何无人看守：node-kind-behavior-table.test.ts asserts key-set self-consistency, per-row values (for nine of fourteen kinds) and predicate agreement — never that a production caller exists. `isProcess` proves the gap is live, not hypothetical (F1).
  - 建议机制：A source-scan ratchet: for each key of `NodeKindBehavior` and each exported predicate, require at least one reference under the three src roots excluding the defining module. This encodes the table's own stated admission rule.
- Adding a runtime-builtin prompt token without a renderer case
  - 为何无人看守：prompt.ts:562-607's switch has no `default` and no `never` narrowing; the rfc295 authority test compares two projections of the same table, so it is green by construction. The token falls through to `input.inputs[name]` (F9).
  - 建议机制：`never` exhaustiveness on the switch after narrowing to `RuntimeBuiltinName`, plus a rendering property test with a same-named inbound port carrying a sentinel.
- Adding a second or third call site that picks a list wire codec and omitting the inline-markdown branch
  - 为何无人看守：The codec-selection decision has no home in the handler registry; it is re-derived per caller (scheduler.ts:7767, review.ts:582-591) and is simply missing at outputKinds/list.ts:142 and portArtifacts.ts:424. The only guard, rfc103-fanout-kind-aware-split.test.ts:57-58, asserts one literal string is present in scheduler.ts.
  - 建议机制：Give the handler a `splitItems(parsed, raw)` method so the codec is registry-owned, then ratchet direct `splitListItems(` / `splitMarkdownDocs(` calls to listWire.ts plus outputKinds/** only.
- Production code mutating a shared registry at runtime
  - 为何无人看守：`registerKeyOf` / `clearShardingRegistry` are exported from the shared barrel and protected only by a comment (F5). No lint rule, dep-cruiser rule or grep ratchet restricts callers, and the persisted `shardKey` identity they control is used for resume reuse and aggregator lookup.
  - 建议机制：Freeze the table, or ratchet the mutators to packages/*/tests/** — the same 'this idiom lives in exactly these files' text-lock family as rfc284-microhelpers.test.ts T7.
- Importing a superseded shared kernel that still ships in the barrel
