  - 为何无人看守：The legacy `HANDLERS` Record and its three handler files have zero production callers but are exported from `@agent-workflow/shared` and kept alive by their own tests (F4). Nothing distinguishes 'the registry' from 'the withdrawn registry' at an import site.
  - 建议机制：Delete, or ratchet `getOutputKindHandler|groupPortsByKind|composePerKindRepairBlocks|markdownFileHandler` out of every src tree except the defining module. Generalizes to: any deprecated-but-exported kernel needs an import ratchet, not a comment.
- Adding a privileged node kind to the table without wiring the decision function
  - 为何无人看守：`PRIVILEGED_NODE_KINDS` is `Partial<Record<NodeKind, string>>` and the enforcing `grantOf` is an independent if-chain (F10). A third row yields a labelled-but-unprotected node across all three canvas surfaces with no test red.
  - 建议机制：Key the table to a `keyof PrivilegedNodeGrants` so one object drives both, plus a test iterating `Object.keys(PRIVILEGED_NODE_KINDS)` asserting `isProtectedKind` is true when the corresponding grant is false.
- Relocating a compile-time exhaustiveness point without updating the contributor checklist
  - 为何无人看守：dev-gotchas.md:624's eight-file list is prose; nothing verifies the named files still carry the construct, and one entry is already stale (F14).
  - 建议机制：Parse the paths out of that line and assert each file matches `satisfies Record<NodeKind|: Record<NodeKind|: never = kind` — the same 'the doc claims completeness, so the claim gets a test' move docs-node-kind-coverage.test.ts already makes for docs/workflow-yaml.md.

**TP — Transport / composition root / platform 公共层**

- A non-`routes/` transport (ws/, mcp/, or a module inbound adapter) importing @/db or hand-writing SQL against another context's table
  - 为何无人看守：.dependency-cruiser.cjs's no-routes-to-db is anchored `from: ^packages/backend/src/routes/` and no rule in the forbidden list (lines 41-148) matches ws/ or mcp/. The RFC-294 AST preflight is scoped to packages/backend/src/modules/ (rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:18). Raw SQL strings are invisible to both mechanisms by construction. ws/registry.ts already exercises the gap at :57-64 (6 tables) and :1166 (raw users SELECT).
  - 建议机制：Add a `no-transport-to-db` dep rule for `^packages/backend/src/(ws|mcp)/` -> `^packages/backend/src/db/` (dependencyTypesNot type-only), ledger the one existing edge in scripts/depcheck.ts, and add a grep ratchet forbidding `.$client.query(` outside db/ and cli/.
- A route reaching the database without importing @/db — via `deps.db` plus drizzle-orm `sql` templates
  - 为何无人看守：The ratchet is import-shaped. AppDeps.db (server.ts:180) hands every route a live DbClient and `sql` comes from the npm package, so the whole data path exists with zero first-party @/db edge. routes/webhookDeliveries.ts:136 shows the exact call shape already in the tree.
  - 建议机制：Call-site grep/AST ratchet over src/routes for `deps.db.` and `.db.(select|insert|update|delete|all|run)(`, with a shrink-only file:line allowlist in the style of packages/backend/tests/routes-no-cast.test.ts:50-60.
- An /api endpoint that is never seen by the startup route-gate self-check — mounted with app.all/app.use, or silenced with a new EXEMPT_MOUNTS entry
  - 为何无人看守：routeMetaCoverage drops every method='ALL' mount and every EXEMPT_MOUNTS path before comparing (routes/registry.ts:298-299). EXEMPT_MOUNTS has zero test references. The comment at :292-297 argues the ALL filter is structural, but mcp/server.ts:211 proves an ALL mount can be a real endpoint.
  - 建议机制：Freeze EXEMPT_MOUNTS with an exact-equality assertion, and stop filtering ALL for /api/* paths — distinguish middleware by wildcard path rather than by verb, so an ALL endpoint must be exempted deliberately.
- A new endpoint with a computed path, or registered outside src/routes/, escaping the contract registry
  - 为何无人看守：api-contract-coverage.test.ts discovers routes by two regexes over src/routes/*.ts that both require a string-literal path; the meta-guard meant to freeze blind spots (:235-247) uses a regex whose `[^}]*?` cannot cross the `}` inside a `${…}` template, so computed-path registrations are invisible to the detector as well as to the discovery. ~41 endpoints are in this state today (5x mountConfigResource + its ACL mounts + digitalEmployees.ts:386), plus /api/whoami in server.ts (documented exception at :162-166, but nothing stops a second one).
  - 建议机制：Replace text scanning with a runtime oracle: build the app and diff allRouteMeta() against tests/contracts/registry.ts ENDPOINTS, with a shrink-only exception list.
- A new test-only seam field on AppDeps, or a new route mount taking AppDeps wholesale instead of an exact module contract
  - 为何无人看守：No registry and no counter exist. The equivalent problem for environment-variable seams IS ratcheted (docs/env-flags.md + rfc284-env-flags-registry.test.ts:31-45), and RFC-294's preflight already forbids AppDeps in module public surfaces — but neither mechanism looks at the AppDeps declaration itself or at the 72 `deps: AppDeps` route signatures.
  - 建议机制：AST test that extracts AppDeps' optional test-seam properties and asserts them equal a frozen list, plus `expect(appDepsRouteMountCount).toBeLessThanOrEqual(72)` so the migration to exact contracts is one-way.
- Per-channel policy or per-channel lifecycle logic added to the WS transport skeleton in a shape other than `case 'kind'` / `kind === 'kind'`, or in a skeleton file other than ws/server.ts
  - 为何无人看守：rfc152-ws-task-channel.test.ts:193 reads only ws/server.ts, and its banned patterns (:195-203) are branch-shaped. A `ReadonlySet<string>` keyed by channel kind (ws/server.ts:304-313) and a named call like `releasePresence(ws)` in ws/connections.ts:81 both slip through, and both exist today.
  - 建议机制：Widen the ratchet to ws/server.ts + ws/connections.ts + registry.ts's pipeline section, banning any identifier or string literal equal to a WS_CHANNEL_KINDS member outside the WS_CHANNELS object literal; move token reachability and close-time cleanup into ChannelSpec fields.
- A new WS channel that overlaps an existing pathRe, or whose path never round-trips through parseWsChannel
  - 为何无人看守：The key-set exhaustion lock (rfc152-ws-channel-registry.test.ts:103-109) forces the registry keys and the mapped type forces `revalidation`, but the three no-overlap / round-trip assertions iterate hand-written sample arrays. Presence (RFC-312) is absent from all three, proving a new channel is never forced in.
  - 建议机制：Derive samples from the specs (add `sampleParams` to ChannelSpec, or iterate WS_PATHS) so the tests loop over WS_CHANNEL_KINDS and fail on a channel with no sample.
- A second duck-typed business-error branch in the generic HTTP error translator
  - 为何无人看守：util/errors.ts:143,157-161 is an ad-hoc if with no surrounding constraint; no test counts or shapes the non-DomainError branches of errorHandler.
  - 建议机制：Registry lookup keyed by the shared layer's error `code`, plus a source ratchet asserting errorHandler contains one DomainError branch, one registry lookup, and zero other `err as {` casts.
- A fourth hand-rolled claim-with-lease outbox drain in a new bounded context
  - 为何无人看守：No shared kernel exists to point at (packages/backend/src/platform/ holds only the two transaction-scope files), so there is nothing to enforce reuse against; the three existing copies live in three different modules' infrastructure directories, each internally consistent.
  - 建议机制：Extract platform/persistence/outbox.ts, migrate the three call sites, then ratchet: `claimExpiresAt` may only be written in that file and db/schema.ts.
- A new recurring daemon loop whose period is an inline literal rather than a DAEMON_CADENCE member
  - 为何无人看守：rfc284-daemon-cadence.test.ts:6-28 pins values of existing members only; membership is unguarded, which is why ~6 loops (backupScheduler.ts:194 etc.) already sit outside the table that calls itself 唯一注册表.
  - 建议机制：Scan src for `setInterval(` and require the period argument to trace to DAEMON_CADENCE, with a shrink-only file:line allowlist — the same mechanism rfc284-env-flags-registry.test.ts uses for env tokens.
- A permission point named after one business/product identity gating routes that are deliberately identity-agnostic
  - 为何无人看守：routes/registry.ts:287-311 checks only point<->route mutual existence. Nothing relates a point's domain to the domain of the routes citing it, so `development-missions:launch` gating `/api/digital-employee-input-uploads` is invisible.
  - 建议机制：Extend the startup self-check with a point-ownership table (path prefix -> allowed point domains) plus a shrink-only exception list seeded with the 4 existing digital-employee routes.
- A second call of the composition root, or a silent re-bind of a process-wide late-bound participant
  - 为何无人看守：mountApiRoutes both composes and mounts, and createDispatcher calls it again (mcp/dispatch.ts:82); DeferredDigitalEmployeeWorkStart.bind is `bound = participant` with no once-guard (modules/integration/composition.ts:28-31). No test asserts single assembly or single bind.
  - 建议机制：Make bind throw on re-bind (turns today's silent rebind into an immediate red test), and add an AST ratchet that src/server.ts's mountApiRoutes body contains zero compose*/create*Store calls — composition moves to createApp/cli/start.ts and arrives as an already-built contract.
- A new `as unknown as` that discards a real compile-time guarantee, anywhere outside src/routes
  - 为何无人看守：packages/backend/tests/routes-no-cast.test.ts is the right mechanism but is scoped to ROUTES_DIR (line 24); eslint.config.js has no cast rule. 107 `as unknown as` sites exist in packages/backend/src.
  - 建议机制：Extend the routes-no-cast scanner to src/ws and src/mcp first (the two transports in this family), with the documented erasure at ws/registry.ts:992 as the seed allowlist entry, then widen further.

**DE — 数字员工三 context 与执行合同公共层**

- Two contexts sharing a Drizzle table (either direction). All tables live in the single `@/db/schema` namespace, so a module reading another module's private table is indistinguishable from reading its own.
  - 为何无人看守：Every architecture guard in the repo reasons over IMPORT SPECIFIERS. rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:238-268 classifies edges by '@/modules/<context>/...' path; rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:74-101 matches the same prefix; rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:127-147 ledgers two exact specifiers. A table symbol imported from '@/db/schema' matches none of them. This is why F1 and F2 — the two most direct violations of RFC-294's '不得以共享表重新耦合' — are both invisible.
  - 建议机制：A table-ownership manifest + AST ratchet: map each exported symbol in db/schema.ts to exactly one owning modules/<context>/ subtree (plus db/, migrations/, tests/), scan all of packages/backend/src for references, and assert the violation set toEqual an explicit expiring debt list. The RFC-310 manifest is the right precedent — it is green and accurate; it simply needs a tables section beside externalImports.
- A generic kernel branching on a business STRING that is not the word 'development' — an error-code prefix, a terminal kind, a slot name, a work-item ref, a port name, a permission id.
  - 为何无人看守：The only literal ban in the family is genericTypeLiteralBan='development' (os-architecture-manifest.json:70, applied at rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:126-131). It is one word, quoted-form only. F3 ('workspace-boundary-'), F6 ('platform'), F7 ('closed-unmerged'/'cancelled'/'merged') and F8 all sit inside roots the test already walks.
  - 建议机制：Derive the ban set from data instead of hardcoding one word: at test time, load every registered EmployeeTypePackageRegistration descriptor and every ExecutionContractRegistration guide, extract their typeIds / workItemRefs / contextTypeIds / contractIds / slotRefs, and assert none of those strings appears as a quoted literal or identifier inside the generic roots. That grows automatically as the domain grows and would have caught all four findings.
- A cross-context contract carried by a bare string built on one side and pattern-matched on the other (prefix, suffix, magic value, regex over prompt text).
  - 为何无人看守：No guard inspects string CONSTRUCTION vs string CONSUMPTION. F3 (concatenated `workspace-${kind}-${code}` vs startsWith), F4 (three env names duplicated as literals), F6 ('platform' minted in one module, compared in another), and the prompt-marker round-trip at task-execution/composition/digitalEmployeeExecution.ts:168 vs :400-427 (EXPECTED_ANALYSIS_PLAN_PATH_JSON written into free text and recovered by regex) are all in this class. Note the family already demonstrates the fix: EXECUTION_CONTRACT_SCRIPT_INPUT_PORT/_ENV/_FILE_ENV are exported constants used correctly by both sides.
  - 建议机制：(a) Convert each to a closed union or an exported constant on the owning context's public surface, so TS exhaustiveness/identity does the work. (b) Backstop with a source-text ratchet: an inventory of cross-context magic strings, asserted to appear in exactly one production file each. (c) For each pair, one pairing test that drives the real producer into the real consumer.
- A required capability being made optional-with-fallback, in any port other than executionContracts.
  - 为何无人看守：rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:157-167 bans the exact substring 'executionContracts?:' in three named files. Its neighbours platformTools?: (composition.ts:240) and platformWorkItems?: (composition.ts:246) are green, and the third host of the same participant (task-execution/composition/digitalEmployeeExecution.ts:182) is not scanned at all — so making THAT one optional also turns nothing red.
  - 建议机制：Read ComposeDigitalEmployeeOptions and composeDigitalEmployeeExecution's deps with the TS AST and assert the set of optional members equals an explicit reviewed ledger (toEqual, so both a new optional and a removed one fail). Add digitalEmployeeExecution.ts to the file list the participant check already walks.
- Structural forgery of a cross-context capability whose type name ends in Participant or Port.
  - 为何无人看守：capabilityForgeViolations (rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:760-802) requires a brand only for types that match SENSITIVE_TYPE (:22, Actor|Authority|Capability|Claim|Token|WorkerIdentity|Tx) AND are declared in public/participants. The RFC-310 contexts name their capabilities *Participant and put two of them in public/types.ts, so the guard passes vacuously. `rg 'unique symbol' packages/backend/src/modules` returns zero hits across all three OS contexts.
  - 建议机制：Require the brand for every exported type in public/participants regardless of name suffix, and add Participant|Port to SENSITIVE_TYPE; then move ExecutionContractParticipant (execution-contract/public/types.ts:32) and DigitalEmployeePlatformToolCatalogParticipant (digital-employee/public/types.ts:65) into their contexts' public/participants.ts and brand them so only composeX() can mint one.
- A route, service, cli or server file deep-importing a module's domain/application/engine/infrastructure, bypassing public/.
  - 为何无人看守：rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:120-133 walks only MODULES_ROOT, and crossContextViolations() skips non-module sources; rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:127-147 ledgers only two exact composition specifiers. Only the three OS contexts have an all-of-src inbound ledger (rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:74-101) — which is exactly why those three are clean and development-automation (~25 deep edges) and code-capability (~8) are not.
  - 建议机制：Extend the RFC-310 inbound-manifest pattern to the remaining contexts (an exact externalImports ledger asserted with toEqual), and/or add a no-restricted-imports group to packages/backend/eslint.config.js:117-121 banning '@/modules/*/{domain,application,engine,infrastructure}/*' from packages/backend/src/{routes,services}/** — the file already carries an equivalent runtime fence (RFC-282) that is mutation-proved by tests/rfc282-a1-eslint-boundary.test.ts, so both the mechanism and its proof pattern already exist in-repo.
- A second copy of a kernel schema or classification table appearing outside the kernel.
  - 为何无人看守：Nothing compares declarations across modules. F9 (implementationSchema duplicated in task-execution) and F7 (three divergent terminalKind tables plus two non-round-tripping status maps in one file) are both invisible because each copy is internally consistent and separately tested.
  - 建议机制：AST ratchet in rfc294-architecture-preflight: index every z.discriminatedUnion / z.enum variant-literal set exported from a public/ surface, then flag any module declaring a union with an identical literal set. For classification tables, express them as `Record<ClosedUnion, Bucket>` in one owned file so a new member fails typecheck at every consumer.
- A new response-rule target kind, delivery subscriber kind, or terminal kind added without its corresponding permission / consumer / classification.
  - 为何无人看守：All three are runtime-registry or free-string dispatch with no compile-time exhaustiveness: eventResponseRules.ts:161 is a single if over one of four kinds; eventCenterService.ts:679-690 finds a consumer at runtime and dead-letters when none matches; terminalKind is `z.string().min(1)` at routes/digitalEmployees.ts:239. Adding a member is always type-safe and always silently under-handled. (The event-center delivery-consumer registry is otherwise the repo's approved GOOD pattern — the gap is only the missing compile-time exhaustiveness over EventSubscriber['kind'].)
  - 建议机制：For each: an exhaustive `Record<Kind, T>` in the owning domain file plus a `satisfies`/never-check, and a test that iterates Object.keys of that Record and asserts each key is handled end-to-end (permission enforced / consumer registered / bucket assigned). That converts 'silently unhandled' into a compile error plus a red test.

**FE — 前端公共组件库与数据层**

- A native `<select>` (or any other primitive the CLAUDE.md list names but ux-source-ratchets does not scan: ChipsInput chip logic, `.segmented` markup, `role="radiogroup"`, `role="tablist"`, native `<details>`/`<dialog>`) appears in feature code.
  - 为何无人看守：tests/ux-source-ratchets.test.ts covers exactly five rules — alert/prompt/confirm (:132), role="dialog" (:154), text-like `<input>` (:169), `<textarea>` (:183), `<table>`/TableViewport (:195) — plus a fixed retired-class string list (:211). Native `<select>` happens to be at zero today purely by discipline; nothing observes it. The forks found in this audit (F-SEG-FORK, F-CHOICE-FORK, F-TAB-VERTICAL) all sit in the uncovered rules.
  - 建议机制：Extend the existing AST walker in ux-source-ratchets.test.ts with one rule per CLAUDE.md primitive, each backed by an allowlist Map keyed by file with a written justification (the ROLE_DIALOG_ALLOWLIST shape). Minimum set: intrinsic `<select>` → banned outright; `role="radiogroup"|"radio"` → only Segmented.tsx / ChoiceCards.tsx; `role="tablist"|"tab"` → only TabBar.tsx; className token `segmented` → only on a `<Segmented>` element.
- A className is applied in TSX that no CSS selector defines — error/hint UI rendering as unstyled bare text.
  - 为何无人看守：tests/rfc286-f1-dead-class-extinction.test.ts encodes three literal class names rather than the invariant, and tests/styles-tokens.test.ts / theme-css-ratchet.test.ts validate CSS variables, never class existence. Five families are currently dead (F-DEADCLASS), one of which another guard (task-output-panel-source-guards.test.ts:24) actively keeps undefined.
  - 建议机制：Generalize F1's own two-form scanner: collect every static and template-literal className token across src/**/*.{ts,tsx}, subtract the selector set parsed out of styles.css + prose.css, subtract a small explicit third-party allowlist (xyflow's nodrag/nopan/nowheel), and assert the remainder is a snapshot that may only shrink.
- A new consumer spells a WS-synced queryKey as a string literal instead of the factory, silently detaching it from live invalidation.
  - 为何无人看守：The RFC-286 F4 lock (tests/task-sync-rules.test.ts:136-149) reads only three hook files by name. The consuming half of the producer/consumer contract — routes/ and components/ — is entirely unscanned, and ~10 literal sites exist today.
  - 建议机制：Same regex scan applied to the whole of src with an EXACT per-file occurrence allowlist (the two-directional honesty form already proven in tests/async-state-gate-source-guard.test.ts) so both growth and stale entries turn red.
- Intra-frontend layer inversion: a shared primitive or the transport/data layer imports a feature module (or a route imports another route's internals).
  - 为何无人看守：.dependency-cruiser.cjs only encodes cross-package rules (no-frontend-to-backend etc.); eslint.config.js:122-139 restricts only backend packages and backend-only deps; scripts/depcheck.ts's KNOWN_VIOLATIONS ledger is entirely backend paths (`const F` is declared at :61 and never used in a rule). Nothing would flag hooks/useWebSocket.ts importing hooks/useActor.ts (F-WS-AUTHORITY), or a future components/Dialog.tsx importing lib/task-status.ts.
  - 建议机制：Add frontend layer rules to .dependency-cruiser.cjs mirroring the backend's: `components/**` (top-level primitives) and `api/**` may not import `routes/**` or feature-scoped `components/<feature>/**`; `hooks/useWebSocket.ts` and `api/client.ts` may not import `hooks/use*` other than shared infra. Register today's exceptions in depcheck.ts KNOWN_VIOLATIONS with a `removeWhen` pointing at an RFC, exactly as the backend does.
- A raw color/spacing literal is written into a rule that targets a shared design-system class, or a shared kernel's selector is qualified by a feature-specific class name.
  - 为何无人看守：tests/styles-tokens.test.ts asserts tokens are declared; tests/theme-css-ratchet.test.ts asserts dark blocks are scoped and `var()` targets exist. Neither asserts that shared-class rules USE tokens, nor that `.dialog__overlay` / `.btn--*` are not re-specified per feature. F-CSS-AGENTIMPORT and F-CSS-DIALOG-HATCH both pass every current CSS test.
  - 建议机制：Two CSS ratchets over styles.css: (1) no `#hex` / `rgb(` literal in any declaration whose selector contains `.btn`, `.form-input`, `.status-chip`, `.select__`, `.dialog__`, `.segmented__` — must be `var(--…)`; (2) `.dialog__overlay` may only be qualified by `.dialog--*` modifiers. Both with a shrink-only allowlist of today's offenders.
- A security-relevant disclosure decision in a shared renderer is made from an unbranded, structurally-constructible payload shape.
  - 为何无人看守：tests/rfc203-error-details.test.tsx enumerates the five known legacy keys and the one principal-aware key; it cannot express 'no future branch may render names from an unproven shape', and no backend test constrains who may emit a `visible` key in error details.
  - 建议机制：Brand the authorized value (`discloseRefsSync` writes a marker field nothing else writes) and have ErrorDetails.tsx require the brand, so the guard becomes the type system plus one backend source ratchet asserting the brand literal appears only in services/resourceAcl.ts.
- A new component ships with zero tests.
  - 为何无人看守：CLAUDE.md's Test-with-every-change rule is prose only; `bun run gate:local` has no per-file coverage floor. JoinModeField.tsx (RFC-306, 2026-08-17) has no test file at all, which is why its primitive fork and its undefined `is-active` class both landed green.
  - 建议机制：Not a coverage-percentage gate (noisy). A narrower ratchet: every file under src/components/**/ that renders JSX must be referenced by at least one file under tests/ or src/**/__tests__/, with a shrink-only allowlist of today's untested components.

**G — 守卫机制自身（元审计）**

- A legacy layer file (services/, routes/, cli/, ws/, mcp/, auth/, util/) deep-imports a bounded-context module's internals — domain/, application/, engine/, ports/, infrastructure/, inbound/ — instead of its public/ or composition entrypoint.
  - 为何无人看守：rfc294-architecture-preflight only walks packages/backend/src/modules (MODULES_ROOT at rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:18) and skips any file whose moduleLocation() is null (:241-242), so a non-module FROM side never becomes an edge candidate. dependency-cruiser declares no rule mentioning modules/ (.dependency-cruiser.cjs:41-149). eslint's no-restricted-imports fences only services/runtime/{opencode,claudeCode} (eslint.config.js:30-46). Consumer ledgers exist for identity-access (rfc305-architecture-lock.test.ts:354-376) and three OS contexts (os-architecture-manifest.json), but rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:127-147 ledgers only the `composition` and `composition/required-ports` specifiers for development-automation, leaving its infrastructure/ and application/ consumers unguarded (routes/developmentMissions.ts:34-49, cli/start.ts:16-17).
  - 建议机制：Second scan set in rfc294-architecture-preflight covering all of packages/backend/src as the FROM side, or a dependency-cruiser rule `no-outside-to-module-internals` (from ^packages/backend/src/(services|routes|cli|ws|mcp|auth|util)/ to ^packages/backend/src/modules/[^/]+/(?!public/|composition)), with today's edges pinned in scripts/depcheck.ts KNOWN_VIOLATIONS so the list can only shrink.
- A bounded-context module's domain/application/engine/public layer takes a value dependency on a legacy god module (@/services/task, @/services/scheduler, @/services/lifecycle, …).
  - 为何无人看守：crossContextViolations discards edges whose target does not resolve inside modules/ (rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:244-245), so @/services/* targets are simply invisible to the preflight. Per-module outbound fences exist only for integration (rfc303-architecture-lock.test.ts:23-33) and development-automation (rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:175-186). 33 such edges exist today, including modules/task-execution/application/applySourceTerminationEffect.ts:25-27 importing @/services/task and @/services/lifecycle.
  - 建议机制：A per-layer outbound rule in the preflight (or a cruiser rule `no-module-to-legacy-services`) asserting modules/*/{domain,application,engine,public} import nothing from @/services, @/routes, @/ws, @/mcp or hono, with the current 33 edges pinned as an exact per-file toEqual debt list carrying why + removeWhen.
- A module grows a new top-level directory outside RFC-294's layer set, or a module's application/engine/public layer reaches into its own infrastructure, or its composition.ts starts branching on business identity / querying the DB — in any context other than development-automation.
  - 为何无人看守：The closed top-level set, the intra-module layering matrix and the composition-purity rule are all bound to MODULE_ROOT = modules/development-automation (rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:27, :101-125, :149-196, :313-334). Only the public/ FILENAME check is global (publicSurfaceViolations at rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:516-518). Evidence the gap is already live: modules/task-execution/inbound/ is a non-canonical layer (RFC-294 design.md:105-108 puts inbound adapters at adapters/inbound/**), modules/intent has only domain/, and modules/work-start is an empty public/ directory.
  - 建议机制：A single data-driven manifest (one JSON keyed by every directory under modules/, in the style of design/RFC-310-.../os-architecture-manifest.json) enforcing top-level shape, the layering matrix, composition purity and a non-empty public/ for every context, with the current deviations (task-execution/inbound, intent, task-catalog, work-start) pinned as explicit ledgered rows.
- A NEW business branch is added inside a shared kernel outside services/workgroup — e.g. a `kind === '…'` / `origin === '…'` / `status === '…'` special case inside services/lifecycle.ts, services/scheduler.ts, services/dispatchFrontier.ts, services/execution/executor.ts or services/task.ts.
  - 为何无人看守：The only per-file branch-count ratchet in the repo is rfc217-architecture-locks.test.ts:176-210, and its scan root is `packages/backend/src/services/workgroup` (WG at :174) with a cap of 0 for any unlisted file in that directory — everything outside services/workgroup has no branch budget at all. The state-machine guards (scheduler-audit-s14, rfc144, lifecycle-grep-guard) constrain WRITE sites, not branch counts, so a new `if (task.launchOrigin === 'webhook')` inside setTaskStatus reds nothing.
  - 建议机制：Extend the rfc217 G5 ratchet to a repo-wide per-file discriminator-comparison budget over packages/backend/src/services/** and packages/backend/src/modules/**, counting comparisons against known business-identity literals (node kinds, task statuses, launch origins, runtime kinds, employee types, resource types) with today's counts pinned exactly (=== not <=) so both growth and un-pruned slack fail.
- A new entry is added to a shared registry (outputKinds/registry.ts, systemChannelPorts, skillOpRegistry, lifecycleRepair option table, daemonCadence) and no consumer handles it — the reverse of the direction `satisfies Record<K,V>` protects.
  - 为何无人看守：`satisfies Record<K,V>` only forces the table to be TOTAL over K; it cannot see a row nothing reads. Registry-to-consumer exhaustiveness is asserted in exactly two places: rfc305-architecture-lock.test.ts:541-550 (every SYSTEM_DOMAIN_POINTS entry has an AST string-literal production consumer) and routes/registry.ts:287-331 (a permission point referenced by no route fails boot). Every other registry has the compile-time half only.
  - 建议机制：Extract rfc305's check into a shared helper `assertEveryRegistryKeyHasAProductionConsumer(keys, root)` and apply it per registry; for behaviour tables (NODE_KIND_BEHAVIORS-style) assert each DIMENSION has at least one production read, turning RFC-146's prose admission criterion (node-kind-behavior-table.test.ts:5-9) into an executable rule.
- A generic/shared kernel branches on the runtime IDENTITY literal ('opencode' / 'claude-code' / 'claude') rather than on a declared driver capability.
  - 为何无人看守：The RFC-282 sealing is import-path-shaped (eslint.config.js:30-46) plus a three-string token list (rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:160). Neither sees a comparison against the kind literal. rfc282-c0c2-runtime-fence.test.ts:55-62 checks one specific proxy predicate in two named files only. Two unledgered branches already exist outside the driver directory (cli/start.ts:400, routes/runtime.ts:51), contradicting the audit's 'runtime 字面量执行分支 driver 目录外真零' claim at design/system-commons-unification-audit-2026-08-12.md:37.
  - 建议机制：A whole-src AST rule banning BinaryExpression comparisons against runtime-kind literals outside packages/backend/src/services/runtime/**, with an exact two-way allowlist — structurally identical to rfc305-architecture-lock.test.ts:459-512, which already enforces this for account-role literals.
- A mega-DTO or an adapter type from OUTSIDE modules/ (@agent-workflow/shared, @/platform, @/embed) is embedded in a cross-context public contract; and open `Record<string, K>` maps appear in a public participant.
  - 为何无人看守：shapeStats scores any type reference it cannot resolve inside modules/ as one transitive leaf (rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:1080) and resolveUnit only searches module files (:271-290); FORBIDDEN_TYPE_IMPORT (:40-41) omits @/platform and @/embed; FORBIDDEN_TYPE_NAME (:23-39) omits Record. Live example: modules/integration/public/participants.ts:1-30 embeds shared's StartTask/StartAgentTask/StartWorkgroupTask as `payload` and uses `Readonly<Record<string, string>>` in a cross-context target type.
  - 建议机制：Add packages/shared/src/** and packages/backend/src/platform/** to the preflight's resolution corpus (rule subject stays modules/**); add @/platform and @/embed to FORBIDDEN_TYPE_IMPORT; add non-literal-keyed Record to the public-entrypoint ban, mirroring the ban already applied to development-automation required-ports at rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:220.
- A NEW frontend surface uses bespoke chrome instead of the shared design system — a native <select>, a hand-rolled `__overlay`/`__panel` modal, a bare `<div className="muted">` empty state, a <button> without .btn.
  - 为何无人看守：The Dialog / data-table / EmptyState-LoadingState / btn-variants guards are POSITIVE whitelists naming 3-4 existing files each (dialog-grep.test.ts:16-60, data-table-callsite.test.ts:17-45, empty-loading-callsite.test.ts:13-30, btn-variants-callsite.test.ts:39-62); they prove past retrofits stayed retrofitted and say nothing about new files. The only whole-frontend AST scans (ux-source-ratchets.test.ts:131-238) cover five shapes and no native <select> rule exists at all.
  - 建议机制：Three additional repo-wide AST/CSS rules in ux-source-ratchets.test.ts: ban intrinsic <select> outside components/Select.tsx and MultiSelect.tsx; ban JSX className literals matching /__(overlay|panel|backdrop|modal)/ outside components/Dialog.tsx; pin the set of `.*__overlay|.*__panel` selectors in styles.css. Add a stale check to every existing allowlist (each allowlisted file must still contain the pattern), copying rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:176-187.
- A source-text / regex ratchet silently stops matching because the production code was reformatted or the helper was renamed — the guard reports zero offenders and its green becomes uninformative.
  - 为何无人看守：Only four mechanisms ship an in-repo negative fixture (rfc282-single-implementation-lock.test.ts:332-357, rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:1140-1248, rfc282-a1-eslint-boundary.test.ts:33-98, route-ux-inventory.test.ts:802-851). The rest — rfc217, rfc310, rfc284-spawn, rfc303, rfc301, s14, rfc144, lifecycle-grep-guard, rfc313, rfc285-b6 and the ~100 scheduler source locks inventoried at rfc287-t1-scheduler-source-lock-inventory.test.ts:40-148 — assert manual verification in a comment only. rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:55-62 additionally swallows a missing directory and returns an empty file list.
  - 建议机制：(1) A corpus-size assertion in every whole-src scanner (`expect(FILES.length).toBeGreaterThan(N)`), as rfc282 already does at :333-335. (2) A mandatory `describe('sanity: the matchers actually catch violations')` block per architecture lock, feeding one known-bad and one known-good fixture through the exported matcher. (3) Promote rfc287-t1-scheduler-source-lock-inventory into a general guard-of-guards: an exact two-way ledger of every /architecture|boundary|ratchet|lock|guard/ test file together with whether it carries such a sanity block.
- An empty or dead bounded context, or a context whose public surface has no consumers, persists in modules/ and is silently treated as 'clean' by every scanner.
  - 为何无人看守：packages/backend/src/modules/work-start/public/ contains zero files today. Every module-level mechanism is offender-list-shaped (`expect(offenders).toEqual([])`), so an empty context scores perfectly: crossContextViolations sees no edges, publicSurfaceViolations sees no entrypoint files to reject, godSurfaceViolations sees no roots. Nothing asserts a module is non-empty or that its public entrypoints have at least one consumer.
  - 建议机制：In the generalised per-context manifest, require every registered context to declare at least one public entrypoint file and at least one consumer edge (or be explicitly marked `status: 'skeleton'` with a removeWhen), so a dead module must be either finished or deleted rather than sitting green.

**CC — 完整性批判（八路未覆盖面）**

- A table that cascades away with a delete/archive/prune operation through TWO OR MORE foreign-key hops, so single-hop schema-reflection guards report it as unreachable
  - 为何无人看守：packages/backend/tests/rfc311-task-archive.test.ts:495 short-circuits on any FK whose immediate target is not tasks/node_runs; no other guard in the repo computes a transitive FK closure. The same one-hop assumption is embedded in every 'writer-private table has zero consumers' check.
  - 建议机制：A shared test helper `cascadeClosure(schema, roots)` doing a fixpoint over cascade edges, reused by the archive guard, the task-delete guard and any future retention sweep; each caller asserts closure ⊆ its own coverage set, with a synthetic grandchild fixture proving the closure version reds where one-hop does not.
- A NEW prompt producer (or a second fencing implementation) that renders untrusted content without crossing the shared prompt-injection kernel
  - 为何无人看守：packages/backend/tests/rfc200-source-lock.test.ts is a positive whitelist of ~11 named files; it asserts what those files contain and never scans the tree for producers that lack a nonce or lack fenceUntrusted. modules/** is entirely outside its reach.
  - 建议机制：A whole-src scan: any file containing prompt-structure literals ('# Output protocol', '<aw-input', 'BEGIN UNTRUSTED', '--- BEGIN INJECTED', 'workflow-output') must import the shared fencing module or be in a ledgered exemption; plus one adversarial payload table run against every registered fencer.
- A source-text or grep ratchet whose forward scan currently matches ZERO files — the '扫了个寂寞' vacuous guard the repo has already been bitten by twice
  - 为何无人看守：No mechanism asserts that a scanner's forward pass is non-empty. A renamed directory, a reformatted production file, or a renamed helper turns the ratchet permanently green and its green is then cited as evidence. docs/dev-gotchas.md:634 mandates the fix; roughly half the mechanisms in the inventory do not implement it.
  - 建议机制：Every scanner asserts `filesScanned > 0` and, where it has a positive expectation, `matches.length > 0`; plus a paired negative fixture fed through the SAME matcher that must report. Enforce via a manifest test that requires each guard file to export `__mutationFixtures`.
- Deletion or renaming of a guard test file, or gutting one to a vacuous assertion
  - 为何无人看守：Only two manifests enumerate required tests: packages/backend/tests/rfc287-t1-scheduler-source-lock-inventory.test.ts:40-45 (scheduler readers) and packages/frontend/tests/route-ux-inventory.test.ts:801-905 (route test owners). The architecture/ratchet suite has none, so `git rm` of a ratchet is a smaller, greener diff than the violation it prevented.
  - 建议机制：A two-way architecture-guard manifest test pinning guard file paths by name, mirroring the rfc287-t1 pattern; deletion becomes a deliberate manifest edit requiring a written disposition (retarget / vacuous-delete / exempt), as docs/dev-gotchas.md:1421 already prescribes for retired subsystems.
- A ledger/allowlist ratchet GROWN in the same PR that creates the violation it legalizes
  - 为何无人看守：Every ledger in the repo (KNOWN_VIOLATIONS, spawn-site ALLOWLIST, ux-source-ratchets file allowlists, rfc143's kind-discrimination allowlist, the rfc294 debt lists, LEGACY_MIGRATION_HASHES) has stale-detection (shrink without record) but no growth check. scripts/depcheck.ts:28-30 even asserts the property in prose while :403-413 implements only half of it.
  - 建议机制：A committed high-water baseline count per ledger, asserted `<=` and itself only ever decreasing (diffed against HEAD~1 in CI or held in a single-line CODEOWNERS-protected file). Two lines per ledger; converts every 'accepted debt' list into a genuine one-way ratchet.
- A legacy-layer file (routes/, services/, cli/, ws/, mcp/, auth/, util/, server.ts) deep-importing a bounded context's internals — quantified: 66 edges across 19 files today
  - 为何无人看守：packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:18 fixes MODULES_ROOT as the FROM side, so any edge originating outside modules/ is structurally unseen; .dependency-cruiser.cjs has no rule for it. Only packages/backend/tests/rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:74-101 scans inbound edges, for three contexts.
  - 建议机制：Generalize the OS test's inbound scan to every context in modules/, as a two-way exact ledger seeded with today's 66 edges so it can only shrink — the same shape and blast radius as the existing rfc305 identity-access consumer ledger.
- A new file added in the wrong layer, or an intra-module layer inversion, in any of the eight contexts that have no shape lock
  - 为何无人看守：Layer-shape enforcement exists only in packages/backend/tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:101-125 (one module) and rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:62-101 (three modules). The RFC-294 preflight has no top-level-directory or intra-layer rule at all. New contexts therefore ship unenforced by default.
  - 建议机制：Move the shape + intra-layer matrix into rfc294-architecture-preflight as a per-module rule over every directory under MODULES_ROOT, with today's divergences (task-execution's inbound/, intent's missing public/, task-catalog's missing domain/) named in an exact debt ledger rather than grandfathered by silence.
- A registry/table entry whose CONSUMER is missing — the union or schema grows, the runtime dispatch table does not (and its inverse: a runtime table with no production consumer at all)
  - 为何无人看守：`satisfies Record<K,V>` protects table→union but not union→table when the table is annotated as `readonly K[]` (lifecycleInvariants.ts:89, :101) or keyed `Record<string, …>` (ref/resolution.ts:100). The repo has exactly one guard of the right shape — the SYSTEM_DOMAIN_POINTS consumer check in packages/backend/tests/rfc305-architecture-lock.test.ts:541-550 — and it is applied to one registry.
  - 建议机制：Promote the rfc305 check into a reusable AST helper `assertEveryRegistryEntryHasAProductionConsumer(symbol)` and apply it to the declared registry list (NODE_KIND_BEHAVIORS, outputKinds registry, SYSTEM_CHANNEL_PORTS, DAEMON_CADENCE, INVARIANT_RULES/STUCK_RULES, REF_DOMAIN_POLICIES, DISABLED_RESOURCE_POLICY, skillOpRegistry); pair it with tuple-exhaustive `as const satisfies` + an `Exclude<Union, Table[number]> extends never` assertion on every runtime list derived from a union.
- A new fs write whose path derives from a request body, a DB column or a cross-context envelope, without crossing the path-containment kernel
  - 为何无人看守：safePath adoption is 8 files against ~83 containing fs write calls, and there is no site allowlist analogous to packages/backend/tests/rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet.test.ts:43-110 for spawns. Path-shape zod schemas are also re-declared per boundary with divergent strictness.
  - 建议机制：A write-site ratchet in the spawn-site shape: every writeFileSync/mkdirSync/cpSync/renameSync/createWriteStream/copyBlobTo line in packages/backend/src is either allowlisted with a why or its file must import @/util/safePath; plus a single exported repo-relative-path zod that a source-text lock forbids re-declaring.
- A raw `db.transaction(` used instead of dbTxSync, or a transaction-boundary PORT typed permissively enough to accept an async body
  - 为何无人看守：packages/backend/tests/scheduler-audit-s10-async-transaction-decorative.test.ts:146 matches the lexical form `.transaction(async` only. An arrow that merely RETURNS a promise (`() => fn()` where fn is async) matches nothing, and a port typed `<T>(fn: () => T) => T` accepts `T = Promise<X>` at compile time. 37 raw sites exist outside the kernel.
  - 建议机制：Export NotPromise from db/txSync.ts and constrain every transaction-boundary port signature with it (compile-time, non-forgeable); convert the S-10 lexical ban into an allowlisted `.transaction(` site ratchet with exact counts and stale detection in both directions.
- A shared kernel's SECURITY behaviour made opt-in through a defaulted parameter (empty nonce, absent flag) rather than an explicitly-named legacy mode
  - 为何无人看守：Defaults are invisible to import graphs, to AST cast bans, and to source-text ratchets — the call site simply has one fewer argument. The digest already found the same shape on managedProcess drain timeouts; here it is on the prompt-fencing nonce (memoryInject.ts:306, :288, :298 and promptFencing.ts:111).
  - 建议机制：Ban defaults on security-relevant kernel parameters: brand the value (`EnvelopeNonce | LegacyNoNonce`) so the legacy path must be named, and add an AST rule that every call to the listed kernel functions passes the argument explicitly, with an exact allowlist for the legacy branch.
- A doc / RFC / audit-ledger / module-header claim about architecture that source no longer supports
  - 为何无人看守：Nothing in CI reads a markdown claim and re-derives it from source; the only mechanical markdown check is the link checker, which validates URLs. Every one of the eight family audits independently reported stale-assertion findings, and this pass adds four more (schemaAdmission's implied hatch bound, ref/resolution's single-source claim, lifecycleInvariants' seven/five counts, depcheck.ts:28-30's only-shrinks claim). Source comments inside kernels are equally unguarded.
  - 建议机制：For the small set of claims that are mechanically checkable, move them out of prose into executable assertions (a counted list, an exported registry, a `satisfies`). For the rest, adopt the docs-node-kind-coverage pattern: a test that reads the doc, extracts its enumerated claims, and iterates them against the source symbols they name.

**G — 守卫机制自身（元审计）**

- A legacy layer file (services/, routes/, cli/, ws/, mcp/, auth/, util/) deep-imports a bounded-context module's internals — domain/, application/, engine/, ports/, infrastructure/, inbound/ — instead of its public/ or composition entrypoint.
  - 为何无人看守：rfc294-architecture-preflight only walks packages/backend/src/modules (MODULES_ROOT at rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:18) and skips any file whose moduleLocation() is null (:241-242), so a non-module FROM side never becomes an edge candidate. dependency-cruiser declares no rule mentioning modules/ (.dependency-cruiser.cjs:41-149). eslint's no-restricted-imports fences only services/runtime/{opencode,claudeCode} (eslint.config.js:30-46). Consumer ledgers exist for identity-access (rfc305-architecture-lock.test.ts:354-376) and three OS contexts (os-architecture-manifest.json), but rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:127-147 ledgers only the `composition` and `composition/required-ports` specifiers for development-automation, leaving its infrastructure/ and application/ consumers unguarded (routes/developmentMissions.ts:34-49, cli/start.ts:16-17).
  - 建议机制：Second scan set in rfc294-architecture-preflight covering all of packages/backend/src as the FROM side, or a dependency-cruiser rule `no-outside-to-module-internals` (from ^packages/backend/src/(services|routes|cli|ws|mcp|auth|util)/ to ^packages/backend/src/modules/[^/]+/(?!public/|composition)), with today's edges pinned in scripts/depcheck.ts KNOWN_VIOLATIONS so the list can only shrink.
- A bounded-context module's domain/application/engine/public layer takes a value dependency on a legacy god module (@/services/task, @/services/scheduler, @/services/lifecycle, …).
  - 为何无人看守：crossContextViolations discards edges whose target does not resolve inside modules/ (rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:244-245), so @/services/* targets are simply invisible to the preflight. Per-module outbound fences exist only for integration (rfc303-architecture-lock.test.ts:23-33) and development-automation (rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:175-186). 33 such edges exist today, including modules/task-execution/application/applySourceTerminationEffect.ts:25-27 importing @/services/task and @/services/lifecycle.
  - 建议机制：A per-layer outbound rule in the preflight (or a cruiser rule `no-module-to-legacy-services`) asserting modules/*/{domain,application,engine,public} import nothing from @/services, @/routes, @/ws, @/mcp or hono, with the current 33 edges pinned as an exact per-file toEqual debt list carrying why + removeWhen.
- A module grows a new top-level directory outside RFC-294's layer set, or a module's application/engine/public layer reaches into its own infrastructure, or its composition.ts starts branching on business identity / querying the DB — in any context other than development-automation.
  - 为何无人看守：The closed top-level set, the intra-module layering matrix and the composition-purity rule are all bound to MODULE_ROOT = modules/development-automation (rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:27, :101-125, :149-196, :313-334). Only the public/ FILENAME check is global (publicSurfaceViolations at rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:516-518). Evidence the gap is already live: modules/task-execution/inbound/ is a non-canonical layer (RFC-294 design.md:105-108 puts inbound adapters at adapters/inbound/**), modules/intent has only domain/, and modules/work-start is an empty public/ directory.
  - 建议机制：A single data-driven manifest (one JSON keyed by every directory under modules/, in the style of design/RFC-310-.../os-architecture-manifest.json) enforcing top-level shape, the layering matrix, composition purity and a non-empty public/ for every context, with the current deviations (task-execution/inbound, intent, task-catalog, work-start) pinned as explicit ledgered rows.
- A NEW business branch is added inside a shared kernel outside services/workgroup — e.g. a `kind === '…'` / `origin === '…'` / `status === '…'` special case inside services/lifecycle.ts, services/scheduler.ts, services/dispatchFrontier.ts, services/execution/executor.ts or services/task.ts.
  - 为何无人看守：The only per-file branch-count ratchet in the repo is rfc217-architecture-locks.test.ts:176-210, and its scan root is `packages/backend/src/services/workgroup` (WG at :174) with a cap of 0 for any unlisted file in that directory — everything outside services/workgroup has no branch budget at all. The state-machine guards (scheduler-audit-s14, rfc144, lifecycle-grep-guard) constrain WRITE sites, not branch counts, so a new `if (task.launchOrigin === 'webhook')` inside setTaskStatus reds nothing.
  - 建议机制：Extend the rfc217 G5 ratchet to a repo-wide per-file discriminator-comparison budget over packages/backend/src/services/** and packages/backend/src/modules/**, counting comparisons against known business-identity literals (node kinds, task statuses, launch origins, runtime kinds, employee types, resource types) with today's counts pinned exactly (=== not <=) so both growth and un-pruned slack fail.
- A new entry is added to a shared registry (outputKinds/registry.ts, systemChannelPorts, skillOpRegistry, lifecycleRepair option table, daemonCadence) and no consumer handles it — the reverse of the direction `satisfies Record<K,V>` protects.
  - 为何无人看守：`satisfies Record<K,V>` only forces the table to be TOTAL over K; it cannot see a row nothing reads. Registry-to-consumer exhaustiveness is asserted in exactly two places: rfc305-architecture-lock.test.ts:541-550 (every SYSTEM_DOMAIN_POINTS entry has an AST string-literal production consumer) and routes/registry.ts:287-331 (a permission point referenced by no route fails boot). Every other registry has the compile-time half only.
  - 建议机制：Extract rfc305's check into a shared helper `assertEveryRegistryKeyHasAProductionConsumer(keys, root)` and apply it per registry; for behaviour tables (NODE_KIND_BEHAVIORS-style) assert each DIMENSION has at least one production read, turning RFC-146's prose admission criterion (node-kind-behavior-table.test.ts:5-9) into an executable rule.
- A generic/shared kernel branches on the runtime IDENTITY literal ('opencode' / 'claude-code' / 'claude') rather than on a declared driver capability.

Neither sees a comparison against the kind literal. rfc282-c0c2-runtime-fence.test.ts:55-62 checks one specific proxy predicate in two named files only.   - 为何无人看守：packages/backend/tests/rfc200-source-lock.test.ts is a positive whitelist of ~11 named files; it asserts what those files contain and never scans the tree for producers that lack a nonce or lack fenceUntrusted. modules/** is entirely outside its reach.
  - 建议机制：A whole-src scan: any file containing prompt-structure literals ('# Output protocol', '<aw-input', 'BEGIN UNTRUSTED', '--- BEGIN INJECTED', 'workflow-output') must import the shared fencing module or be in a ledgered exemption; plus one adversarial payload table run against every registered fencer.
- A source-text or grep ratchet whose forward scan currently matches ZERO files — the '扫了个寂寞' vacuous guard the repo has already been bitten by twice
  - 为何无人看守：No mechanism asserts that a scanner's forward pass is non-empty. A renamed directory, a reformatted production file, or a renamed helper turns the ratchet permanently green and its green is then cited as evidence. docs/dev-gotchas.md:634 mandates the fix; roughly half the mechanisms in the inventory do not implement it.
  - 建议机制：Every scanner asserts `filesScanned > 0` and, where it has a positive expectation, `matches.length > 0`; plus a paired negative fixture fed through the SAME matcher that must report. Enforce via a manifest test that requires each guard file to export `__mutationFixtures`.
- Deletion or renaming of a guard test file, or gutting one to a vacuous assertion
  - 为何无人看守：Only two manifests enumerate required tests: packages/backend/tests/rfc287-t1-scheduler-source-lock-inventory.test.ts:40-45 (scheduler readers) and packages/frontend/tests/route-ux-inventory.test.ts:801-905 (route test owners). The architecture/ratchet suite has none, so `git rm` of a ratchet is a smaller, greener diff than the violation it prevented.
  - 建议机制：A two-way architecture-guard manifest test pinning guard file paths by name, mirroring the rfc287-t1 pattern; deletion becomes a deliberate manifest edit requiring a written disposition (retarget / vacuous-delete / exempt), as docs/dev-gotchas.md:1421 already prescribes for retired subsystems.
- A ledger/allowlist ratchet GROWN in the same PR that creates the violation it legalizes
  - 为何无人看守：Every ledger in the repo (KNOWN_VIOLATIONS, spawn-site ALLOWLIST, ux-source-ratchets file allowlists, rfc143's kind-discrimination allowlist, the rfc294 debt lists, LEGACY_MIGRATION_HASHES) has stale-detection (shrink without record) but no growth check. scripts/depcheck.ts:28-30 even asserts the property in prose while :403-413 implements only half of it.
  - 建议机制：A committed high-water baseline count per ledger, asserted `<=` and itself only ever decreasing (diffed against HEAD~1 in CI or held in a single-line CODEOWNERS-protected file). Two lines per ledger; converts every 'accepted debt' list into a genuine one-way ratchet.
- A legacy-layer file (routes/, services/, cli/, ws/, mcp/, auth/, util/, server.ts) deep-importing a bounded context's internals — quantified: 66 edges across 19 files today
  - 为何无人看守：packages/backend/tests/rfc294-architecture-preflight.test.ts:18 fixes MODULES_ROOT as the FROM side, so any edge originating outside modules/ is structurally unseen; .dependency-cruiser.cjs has no rule for it. Only packages/backend/tests/rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:74-101 scans inbound edges, for three contexts.
  - 建议机制：Generalize the OS test's inbound scan to every context in modules/, as a two-way exact ledger seeded with today's 66 edges so it can only shrink — the same shape and blast radius as the existing rfc305 identity-access consumer ledger.
- A new file added in the wrong layer, or an intra-module layer inversion, in any of the eight contexts that have no shape lock
  - 为何无人看守：Layer-shape enforcement exists only in packages/backend/tests/rfc310-architecture-lock.test.ts:101-125 (one module) and rfc310-digital-employee-os-architecture.test.ts:62-101 (three modules). The RFC-294 preflight has no top-level-directory or intra-layer rule at all. New contexts therefore ship unenforced by default.
  - 建议机制：Move the shape + intra-layer matrix into rfc294-architecture-preflight as a per-module rule over every directory under MODULES_ROOT, with today's divergences (task-execution's inbound/, intent's missing public/, task-catalog's missing domain/) named in an exact debt ledger rather than grandfathered by silence.
- A registry/table entry whose CONSUMER is missing — the union or schema grows, the runtime dispatch table does not (and its inverse: a runtime table with no production consumer at all)
  - 为何无人看守：`satisfies Record<K,V>` protects table→union but not union→table when the table is annotated as `readonly K[]` (lifecycleInvariants.ts:89, :101) or keyed `Record<string, …>` (ref/resolution.ts:100). The repo has exactly one guard of the right shape — the SYSTEM_DOMAIN_POINTS consumer check in packages/backend/tests/rfc305-architecture-lock.test.ts:541-550 — and it is applied to one registry.
  - 建议机制：Promote the rfc305 check into a reusable AST helper `assertEveryRegistryEntryHasAProductionConsumer(symbol)` and apply it to the declared registry list (NODE_KIND_BEHAVIORS, outputKinds registry, SYSTEM_CHANNEL_PORTS, DAEMON_CADENCE, INVARIANT_RULES/STUCK_RULES, REF_DOMAIN_POLICIES, DISABLED_RESOURCE_POLICY, skillOpRegistry); pair it with tuple-exhaustive `as const satisfies` + an `Exclude<Union, Table[number]> extends never` assertion on every runtime list derived from a union.
- A new fs write whose path derives from a request body, a DB column or a cross-context envelope, without crossing the path-containment kernel
  - 为何无人看守：safePath adoption is 8 files against ~83 containing fs write calls, and there is no site allowlist analogous to packages/backend/tests/rfc284-spawn-site-ratchet.test.ts:43-110 for spawns. Path-shape zod schemas are also re-declared per boundary with divergent strictness.
  - 建议机制：A write-site ratchet in the spawn-site shape: every writeFileSync/mkdirSync/cpSync/renameSync/createWriteStream/copyBlobTo line in packages/backend/src is either allowlisted with a why or its file must import @/util/safePath; plus a single exported repo-relative-path zod that a source-text lock forbids re-declaring.
- A raw `db.transaction(` used instead of dbTxSync, or a transaction-boundary PORT typed permissively enough to accept an async body
  - 为何无人看守：packages/backend/tests/scheduler-audit-s10-async-transaction-decorative.test.ts:146 matches the lexical form `.transaction(async` only. An arrow that merely RETURNS a promise (`() => fn()` where fn is async) matches nothing, and a port typed `<T>(fn: () => T) => T` accepts `T = Promise<X>` at compile time. 37 raw sites exist outside the kernel.
  - 建议机制：Export NotPromise from db/txSync.ts and constrain every transaction-boundary port signature with it (compile-time, non-forgeable); convert the S-10 lexical ban into an allowlisted `.transaction(` site ratchet with exact counts and stale detection in both directions.
- A shared kernel's SECURITY behaviour made opt-in through a defaulted parameter (empty nonce, absent flag) rather than an explicitly-named legacy mode
  - 为何无人看守：Defaults are invisible to import graphs, to AST cast bans, and to source-text ratchets — the call site simply has one fewer argument. The digest already found the same shape on managedProcess drain timeouts; here it is on the prompt-fencing nonce (memoryInject.ts:306, :288, :298 and promptFencing.ts:111).
  - 建议机制：Ban defaults on security-relevant kernel parameters: brand the value (`EnvelopeNonce | LegacyNoNonce`) so the legacy path must be named, and add an AST rule that every call to the listed kernel functions passes the argument explicitly, with an exact allowlist for the legacy branch.
- A doc / RFC / audit-ledger / module-header claim about architecture that source no longer supports
  - 为何无人看守：Nothing in CI reads a markdown claim and re-derives it from source; the only mechanical markdown check is the link checker, which validates URLs. Every one of the eight family audits independently reported stale-assertion findings, and this pass adds four more (schemaAdmission's implied hatch bound, ref/resolution's single-source claim, lifecycleInvariants' seven/five counts, depcheck.ts:28-30's only-shrinks claim). Source comments inside kernels are equally unguarded.
  - 建议机制：For the small set of claims that are mechanically checkable, move them out of prose into executable assertions (a counted list, an exported registry, a `satisfies`). For the rest, adopt the docs-node-kind-coverage pattern: a test that reads the doc, extracts its enumerated claims, and iterates them against the source symbols they name.

## 附：各族「本次审计未覆盖但已识别」的公共内核（完整性批判产出）

- **Migration / physical-schema admission kernel** `packages/backend/src/db/schemaAdmission.ts:1-6`
  - 角色：Fail-fast gate run on every openDb(): validates the complete migration-receipt prefix, then replays the chain and compares the resulting physical SQLite schema against the live one before the daemon exposes any service.
  - 单一事实源声明是否成立：Claims (header :1-6) to be the single admission point that proves receipts and physical schema describe the same database. Mostly holds — but it carries a per-migration hash-alias escape hatch (LEGACY_MIGRATION_HASHES :51-70, consumed at :218-222) whose only test hand-copies the table rather than importing it, so the hatch can grow silently. AUDITED BY NOBODY in the eight-family sweep.
- **Task-archive cascade-table registry** `packages/backend/src/services/taskArchive.ts:223-232`
  - 角色：ARCHIVED_TABLES + ARCHIVE_EXEMPT_TABLES declare which tables must be exported to JSONL before a terminal task tree is deleted from the DB (archive = export + delete, deletion by FK cascade).
  - 单一事实源声明是否成立：Claims (comment at :223, and the guard's own preamble at packages/backend/tests/rfc311-task-archive.test.ts:479-485) that every table that vanishes with the archive is either exported or explicitly exempt. DOES NOT HOLD: the guard walks exactly one FK hop, and review_comments (a transitive cascade descendant) is in neither list. AUDITED BY NOBODY.
- **Prompt-injection fencing kernel** `packages/shared/src/promptFencing.ts:1-12`
  - 角色：RFC-200 untrusted-content boundary: per-run unguessable nonce, aw-input fences, close-tag neutralization, Unicode line-break normalization, line-start anchor neutralization (headings / directives / envelopes).
  - 单一事实源声明是否成立：Claims to be the shared choke point every prompt producer crosses. DOES NOT HOLD: a second, independent fencing implementation ships in packages/backend/src/modules/development-automation/engine/prompt/assembleAgentPrompt.ts:14-34 with a static delimiter and two literal replaceAll escapes. The RFC-200 wiring guard (packages/backend/tests/rfc200-source-lock.test.ts:13-129) is a positive whitelist of named files, so it cannot see a new producer. AUDITED BY NOBODY (node-kind family touched prompt.ts token rendering only).
- **Memory injection kernel** `packages/backend/src/services/memoryInject.ts:1-22`
  - 角色：Loads approved memories for the 5 scopes, clips by per-scope budget, renders the single '## Learned context' block appended to the primary agent's inline prompt, and produces the persisted snapshot.
  - 单一事实源声明是否成立：Claims DEFAULT_INJECTION_BUDGET (:376) is the single source of the budget and that the block shape is grep-guarded. Real gap: the per-scope query fan at :133-205 is five hand-written arms with no exhaustiveness over the scope union, and the fencing of memory bodies is opt-in via a defaulted `envelopeNonce = ''` parameter (:306-322) — an empty nonce renders bodies unfenced. AUDITED BY NOBODY.
- **Path-containment kernel** `packages/backend/src/util/safePath.ts:1-3`
  - 角色：safeJoin / realpathInside / assertWriteAncestorInside — the repo's only lexical+symlink traversal defence for filesystem reads and writes rooted at a managed directory.
  - 单一事实源声明是否成立：Header claims 'every fs read/write inside a skill must land strictly under the skill's root'. Adoption is 8 files (skill.ts, skillVersion.ts, skillTree.ts, worktreeFiles.ts, upload.ts, runtime/claudeCode/config.ts, programArtifactStore.ts) out of ~83 backend files containing an fs write call, and there is no allowlist ratchet on write sites the way there is on spawn sites. AUDITED BY NOBODY.
- **Resource-ref resolution policy kernel** `packages/shared/src/ref/resolution.ts:87-100`
  - 角色：REF_DOMAIN_POLICIES declares freeze/ACL timing per ref domain; the CALL_POLICY constants below it declare onMissing / failureOwner per call purpose.
  - 单一事实源声明是否成立：Comment at :87 claims '各域的静态策略（单一事实源；resolver 实现从这里取，不各写各的）'. DOES NOT HOLD: REF_DOMAIN_POLICIES has ZERO production consumers (only packages/backend/tests/rfc271-ref-contract.test.ts reads it), and EXPORT_CALL_POLICY (:103-110) likewise. Partially audited (ACL family covered ref/codecs.ts) but resolution.ts itself was not.
- **Lifecycle alert-rule registry** `packages/backend/src/services/lifecycleInvariants.ts:87-101`
  - 角色：INVARIANT_RULES / STUCK_RULES are the runtime lists passed as `ownedRules` to reconcileLifecycleAlerts, deciding which lifecycle_alerts rows a scan is allowed to resolve.
  - 单一事实源声明是否成立：Comments call them the 'canonical list'. There IS a real compile-time union-equality guard between backend and shared rule unions (:73-81), but the runtime ARRAYS are typed `readonly InvariantRule[]` — not a completeness assertion — so a rule can exist in the union and be absent from ownedRules, stranding its alerts unresolved forever. Headers are also already stale (':87 says seven, lists eight'; ':101 says five, lists six'). AUDITED BY NOBODY (lifecycle family audited lifecycle.ts CAS/transition tables).
- **Transaction-atomicity kernel** `packages/backend/src/db/txSync.ts:1-30`
  - 角色：dbTxSync — type-level (NotPromise<T>) plus runtime rejection of async transaction bodies, which under bun:sqlite COMMIT at their first await and provide zero atomicity.
  - 单一事实源声明是否成立：Header states verbatim 'the ONLY sanctioned way to get transactional atomicity in this codebase'. DOES NOT HOLD: 37 raw `db.transaction(` sites exist in packages/backend/src outside txSync.ts, and two of them are re-exported as generic transaction-boundary PORTS (`inTx<T>(fn: () => T): T`, `transact: <T>(fn: () => T) => T`) with no NotPromise constraint. AUDITED BY NOBODY.
- **Secret redaction kernel** `packages/backend/src/util/redact.ts:1-20`
  - 角色：redactSensitiveString — the single scrub applied to MCP stderr, HTTP bodies and log lines before they are persisted as errorDetailJson.
  - 单一事实源声明是否成立：Header explicitly disclaims being a security boundary (:17-20) — an honest, non-forgeable stance. No adoption ratchet exists: nothing asserts which persistence sites must call it. AUDITED BY NOBODY.
- **Workspace GC / prune-eligibility kernel** `packages/backend/src/services/gc.ts:1-26`
  - 角色：Hourly two-phase tombstone prune of task workspaces (claim → delete → finalize), plus the scratch-orphan reaper.
  - 单一事实源声明是否成立：No explicit single-source claim, and none is warranted: the predicate 'which space kinds own disk and are prunable' is hand-written three times as an include list (gc.ts:215, gc.ts:267, packages/backend/src/services/webhook/terminalWorkspaceCleanup.ts:32) and once as an exclude list (gc.ts:365-366), with no `satisfies Record<SpaceKind, …>` anywhere. The three currently agree — verified — but nothing holds them together. AUDITED BY NOBODY.
- **At-rest sealing kernel** `packages/backend/src/auth/secretBox.ts:1-6`
  - 角色：AES-256-GCM seal/unseal over the daemon key file for OIDC client_secret and other at-rest secrets.
  - 单一事实源声明是否成立：Claims to be the wrapper for secrets at rest. Not verified further in this pass; flagged as unaudited surface — no family audit opened auth/ at all except via the rfc305 identity-access module ratchets, which cover permission axes, not sealing.
- **Cross-context transaction-scope brand** `packages/backend/src/platform/persistence/transactionScope.ts:1-10`
  - 角色：RFC-294's branded, opaque TransactionScope so business modules can share a transaction without receiving the SQLite handle.
  - 单一事实源声明是否成立：Claims (header :1-2) that 'the SQLite handle remains inside persistence adapters'. It is genuinely non-forgeable (unique-symbol brand), but adoption is 4 files (services/userIdentities.ts, auth/loginPolicy.ts, modules/identity-access/infrastructure/sqliteUserAccessRepository.ts, the sqlite adapter); every other cross-layer call still threads a raw DbClient. AUDITED BY NOBODY.
